// Headless smoke test. NOT game code: run.sh copies the project to a scratch folder,
// drops this in as scripts/_Test.cs, registers it as an autoload, and runs it.
// It pokes private methods by reflection, so a rename there breaks this first.
// Stored as .txt so neither Godot nor the typecheck ever compiles it in place.
using Godot;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

public partial class _Test : Node
{
    int fails = 0;
    void Check(bool ok, string what) { GD.Print((ok ? "PASS " : "FAIL ") + what); if (!ok) fails++; }
    async Task Wait(double s) => await ToSignal(GetTree().CreateTimer(s), "timeout");
    // A HERMETIC NETWORK. Every host in this harness used to search the real network for a router
    // and ask a real web service for the public address, so two checks passed in the sandbox the
    // harness was born in and failed on every real machine -- and now that the game maps ports
    // itself, a test run would have opened ports on the real router. Instead: searches go to a
    // loopback port nobody answers, and the public address to a URL that refuses. A scenario that
    // wants routers points them at tools/smoketest/fakeigd.py (see RouterScenarios).
    const int FakeSsdp = 19000, FakeHttp = 19080, FakePmp = 19351;
    static void NoRouterNoInternet()
    {
        Router.Fake = (1, 1);
        Net.PublicIpService = "http://127.0.0.1:1/";
    }
    // Waits in REAL time: the router work runs on its own thread, and at a fixed frame rate a
    // frame-counted wait can end before a single datagram has had time to come back.
    async Task<bool> RealTime(Func<bool> done, double seconds)
    {
        ulong until = Time.GetTicksMsec() + (ulong)(seconds * 1000);
        while (!done() && Time.GetTicksMsec() < until) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        return done();
    }
    // One value from a character's file as it is on disk (the loaded character's by default).
    static Variant OnDisk(string section, string key, Variant def, string id = null)
    {
        var c = new ConfigFile();
        return c.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{id ?? Character.Id}.cfg") == Error.Ok ? c.GetValue(section, key, def) : def;
    }
    static string Fake(string path)
    {
        using var http = new System.Net.Http.HttpClient { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3) };
        try { return http.GetStringAsync($"http://127.0.0.1:{FakeHttp}{path}").GetAwaiter().GetResult(); }
        catch (Exception e) { return "fake routers unreachable: " + e.Message; }
    }

    // PLUG AND PLAY, network by network. Each is a home network a player really has; the second
    // and third are the one this game's own author has (a TP-Link behind the provider's modem), on
    // which Godot's Upnp reported "no router answered" about a router that had answered.
    async Task RouterScenarios()
    {
        Router.Fake = (FakeSsdp, FakePmp); Net.PublicIpService = $"http://127.0.0.1:{FakeHttp}/ip";
        async Task<(Net.Reach reach, string addr, string status, string lan, string log)> Hosting(string scenario, int port)
        {
            Fake($"/scenario/{scenario}");
            Net.I.Host(port);
            string lan = Net.I.LanAddress.Split(':')[0];
            await RealTime(() => Net.I.Reachability != Net.Reach.Checking, 20);
            var (reach, addr, status) = (Net.I.Reachability, Net.I.InternetAddress, Net.I.LastStatus);
            Net.I.GoOffline();
            await RealTime(() => Net.I.NetworkIdle, 10);           // the ports it opened are closed again
            return (reach, addr, status, lan, Fake("/log"));
        }

        var one = await Hosting("single", 27131);
        Check(one.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && one.addr == "203.0.113.7:27131"
              && one.log.Contains($"inner add 27131 {one.lan}") && one.log.Contains("inner delete 27131"),
              $"one router with UPnP: it opens the port to this PC, friends join the public address, and leaving closes it ({one.reach}; {one.log.Replace('\n', '|')})");

        var two = await Hosting("double", 27132);
        Check(two.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && two.addr == "203.0.113.7:27132"
              && two.log.Contains($"inner add 27132 {two.lan}") && two.log.Contains("front add 27132 127.0.4.40")
              && two.log.Contains("inner delete 27132") && two.log.Contains("front delete 27132"),
              $"two routers, both with UPnP: each opens its hop -- the front one to the inner one's internet side -- and both close ({two.reach}; {two.log.Replace('\n', '|')})");

        var quiet = await Hosting("double-quiet", 27133);
        Check(quiet.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && quiet.addr == "203.0.113.7:27133"
              && quiet.status.Contains("forward UDP 27133 to 127.0.4.40") && quiet.log.Contains($"inner add 27133 {quiet.lan}"),
              $"two routers, the front one silent: ours is opened, and the player is told the ONE forward left, to the address it needs ({quiet.reach})");

        var refused = await Hosting("refuse", 27134);
        Check(refused.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && refused.status.Contains("ConflictInMappingEntry") && !refused.log.Contains("add"),
              $"a router that refuses: the player is told what it said ({refused.reach})");

        var none = await Hosting("none", 27135);
        Check(none.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && none.status.Contains("no router answered"),
              $"no UPnP at all: forward the port by hand, and the address to give out ({none.reach})");

        // The routers that speak NAT-PMP or PCP instead (Apple's, and many without UPnP). This one
        // hands out a DIFFERENT outside port than the one asked for, as NAT-PMP may: friends must
        // be given the port it chose, not ours.
        var pmp = await Hosting("pmp", 27136);
        Check(pmp.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && pmp.addr == "203.0.113.7:28136" && pmp.log.Contains("inner pmp add 27136") && pmp.log.Contains("inner pmp delete 27136"),
              $"a NAT-PMP router: opened, friends given the port IT chose, closed on leaving ({pmp.reach} {pmp.addr}; {pmp.log.Replace('\n', '|')})");
        var pcp = await Hosting("pcp", 27137);
        Check(pcp.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && pcp.addr == "203.0.113.7:27137" && pcp.log.Contains("inner pcp add 27137") && pcp.log.Contains("inner pcp delete 27137"),
              $"a PCP router (it answers NAT-PMP with 'unsupported version'): spoken to in PCP, opened and closed ({pcp.reach}; {pcp.log.Replace('\n', '|')})");

        // Carrier-grade NAT: the router opens the port on an internet side the provider shares. No
        // router in front is asked (it is the provider's), and nobody is sent to forward anything.
        var cg = await Hosting("cgnat", 27138);
        Check(cg.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly && !cg.status.Contains("forward UDP") && cg.status.Contains("carrier-grade") && !cg.log.Contains("front"),
              $"carrier-grade NAT: no forwarding advice that cannot work, and a virtual network suggested ({cg.reach})");
        // A VPN on the host PC: the router opened its public address, but the internet sees the VPN's.
        var vpn = await Hosting("vpn", 27139);
        Check(vpn.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && vpn.addr == "198.51.100.9:27139" && vpn.status.Contains("VPN"),
              $"a VPN on this PC: the router's address to try, and the VPN named as the thing to turn off ({vpn.reach} {vpn.addr})");

        NoRouterNoInternet();
    }

    // Where a guest finds the host. Under run.ps1 -Wan the guest is told "wan" and goes through
    // tools/smoketest/wan.py, a relay 1000 ports up that delays, jitters and drops like the internet.
    static bool Wan => OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs().Contains("wan");
    static string HostAt(int port) => $"127.0.0.1:{(Wan ? port + 1000 : port)}";
    Hub H => GetTree().CurrentScene as Hub;
    int Count<T>() where T : Node => Nodes<T>().Count();
    System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<T> Nodes<T>(Node n = null) where T : Node
    {
        n ??= GetTree().Root;
        foreach (var c in n.GetChildren()) { if (c is T t && !t.IsQueuedForDeletion()) yield return t; foreach (var x in Nodes<T>(c)) yield return x; }
    }
    void KeyDown(Key k) => Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = k, PhysicalKeycode = k, Pressed = true });
    void KeyUp(Key k)   => Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = k, PhysicalKeycode = k, Pressed = false });
    void Key(Key k) { KeyDown(k); KeyUp(k); }
    // events enter in WINDOW coordinates
    Vector2 Win(Vector2 canvas) => GetViewport().GetFinalTransform() * canvas;
    Vector2 WorldToWin(Vector2 w) => GetViewport().GetFinalTransform() * (GetViewport().GetCanvasTransform() * w);
    void Click(Vector2 canvas, MouseButton b = MouseButton.Left) { var at = Win(canvas);
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = b, Pressed = true });
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = b, Pressed = false }); }
    void DoubleClick(Vector2 canvas) { var at = Win(canvas);
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = true });
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = false });
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = true, DoubleClick = true });
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = false }); }
    void AimWorld(Vector2 w) { var at = WorldToWin(w); Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at }); }
    // A DROP, as the host sees one: the link to that peer simply gone, no goodbye. Not
    // DisconnectPeer, which is a polite hang-up: ENet empties the peer's channels at once but Godot
    // lists it until the other side answers, and over a slow link the host went on sending into
    // it for a round trip ("Unable to send packet on channel 1, max channels: 0"). Cut at the end
    // of the frame, after everything that sends in it; Godot lets the peer go at the next poll.
    // (Over the simulated internet the hang-up itself can be lost, 2%: the guest then notices by
    // its timeout instead, several seconds later -- hence the host's longer waits there.)
    void Drop(int peer) => Callable.From(() => (Multiplayer.MultiplayerPeer as ENetMultiplayerPeer)?.GetPeer(peer)?.PeerDisconnectNow()).CallDeferred();
    object Priv(object o, string m, params object[] a) => o.GetType().GetMethod(m, BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).Invoke(o, a);
    // How long since another peer's ship last reported: the silence this peer has heard from its owner.
    static float Quiet(PlayerShip s) => (float)typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_netAge", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(s);
    // ENet's throttle on one end of a link, as (interval ms, acceleration, deceleration).
    static (double interval, double up, double down) Throttle(ENetPacketPeer p) =>
        (p.GetStatistic(ENetPacketPeer.PeerStatistic.PacketThrottleInterval),
         p.GetStatistic(ENetPacketPeer.PeerStatistic.PacketThrottleAcceleration),
         p.GetStatistic(ENetPacketPeer.PeerStatistic.PacketThrottleDeceleration));

    // A STEADY STREAM FROM THE HOST, to count what arrives. The host role sends one unreliable,
    // ordered packet ten times a second -- the kind every ship, raider and boss report is -- to every
    // peer it has let in, and a guest counts them. ENet's throttle, left to fall, threw such packets
    // away before they were sent.
    // ON A CHANNEL OF ITS OWN. ENet delivers an ordered unreliable packet only if nothing sent after
    // it on the same channel got there first (peer.c discards it otherwise), and every ship, raider
    // and base report the host sends shares channel 0. The simulated internet reorders datagrams
    // sent milliseconds apart (each is delayed 90 +/- 25 ms on its own), so on channel 0 the ~80
    // other reports a second threw four in ten of this stream away AT THE RECEIVER: 5.8 a second
    // arrived with nothing dropped before sending. The throttle is the PEER's, on every channel, so
    // a channel of its own still measures it, and only it. (The game's own streams have one each
    // now, NetChannels; this one is the next number after that table's last row, never one of its
    // rows, and a check says so. Not further out: a WebRTC session opens only the channels
    // Link.Channels() lists, and a gap is a data channel that carries nothing.)
    const int StreamChannel = NetChannels.BossSounds + 1;
    bool _streaming; double _streamClock; int _streamGot;
    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        if (!_streaming || !Net.IsHost || !Net.IsOnline) return;
        for (_streamClock += delta; _streamClock >= 0.1; _streamClock -= 0.1) Net.ToHeard(0, this, nameof(NetStream));
    }
    [Rpc(MultiplayerApi.RpcMode.Authority, CallLocal = false, TransferMode = MultiplayerPeer.TransferModeEnum.UnreliableOrdered, TransferChannel = StreamChannel)]
    void NetStream() => _streamGot++;

    // ── WEBRTC, R0: THE PLUGIN INSIDE WARSHIPS (docs/plans/network_webrtc.md §10.4 R0) ─────────────
    // Two WebRtcMultiplayerPeers in this one process, polled by hand: a host (server) and a guest
    // (client), one connection each, THE HOST MAKING THE OFFER as in every spike run, and the bundles
    // swapped whole -- sealed, not trickled -- as an invite and its reply will be. Timed on the WALL
    // clock: libjuice's timers are real time, and --fixed-fps game time is not.
    sealed class RtcPair
    {
        public readonly int Id;
        public readonly long ApplyAfterMs;              // the host takes the reply this long after it was made
        public readonly bool Delivered;                 // the guest is handed the invite at all
        public readonly WebRtcMultiplayerPeer Host = new(), Guest = new();
        public readonly WebRtcPeerConnection HostConn = new(), GuestConn = new();
        public readonly Error Served;
        public readonly bool ConnectedAtOnce;
        string _offer, _answer;
        readonly List<(string media, int index, string name)> _offerCands = new(), _answerCands = new();
        public bool OfferSealed, Pasted, ReplySealed, Applied;
        public ulong ReplyAt, AppliedAt, HostSawAt, GuestSawAt, HostLostAt, GuestLostAt;
        public WebRtcPeerConnection.ConnectionState GuestAtApply;   // had the guest's side got through alone (P2)?
        public int Handlers, OffMain;                   // signal handlers run, and how many off the main thread
        // How the bundles cross: whole, as the plugin made them (null), or through a courier that makes
        // each a code and reads it back, as the pair-proxy check does
        readonly Func<Rendezvous.Kind, string, string[], (string sdp, string[] lines)> _carry;
        public RtcPair(int id, long applyAfterMs = 0, bool delivered = true, Func<Rendezvous.Kind, string, string[], (string sdp, string[] lines)> carry = null)
        {
            Id = id; ApplyAfterMs = applyAfterMs; Delivered = delivered; _carry = carry;
            var ice = new Godot.Collections.Dictionary { ["iceServers"] = new Godot.Collections.Array() };
            Served = Host.CreateServer(Link.Channels());
            ConnectedAtOnce = Host.GetConnectionStatus() == MultiplayerPeer.ConnectionStatus.Connected;
            Guest.CreateClient(id, Link.Channels());
            HostConn.Initialize(ice); GuestConn.Initialize(ice);
            HostConn.SessionDescriptionCreated += (type, sdp) => { Seen(); HostConn.SetLocalDescription(type, sdp); _offer = sdp; };
            HostConn.IceCandidateCreated += (m, i, n) => { Seen(); _offerCands.Add((m, (int)i, n)); };
            GuestConn.SessionDescriptionCreated += (type, sdp) => { Seen(); GuestConn.SetLocalDescription(type, sdp); _answer = sdp; };
            GuestConn.IceCandidateCreated += (m, i, n) => { Seen(); _answerCands.Add((m, (int)i, n)); };
            Host.PeerConnected += p => { Seen(); if (p == Id) HostSawAt = Now; };
            Host.PeerDisconnected += p => { Seen(); if (p == Id) HostLostAt = Now; };
            Guest.PeerConnected += p => { Seen(); if (p == 1) GuestSawAt = Now; };
            Guest.PeerDisconnected += p => { Seen(); if (p == 1) GuestLostAt = Now; };
            Host.AddPeer(HostConn, id);
            HostConn.CreateOffer();
        }
        public static ulong Now => Time.GetTicksMsec();
        // the bundles exactly as the plugin made them, for the codec's byte-for-byte check
        public string Offer => _offer;
        public string Answer => _answer;
        public string[] OfferLines => _offerCands.Select(c => c.name).ToArray();
        public string[] AnswerLines => _answerCands.Select(c => c.name).ToArray();
        readonly ulong _born = Now;
        void Seen() { Handlers++; if (OS.GetThreadCallerId() != OS.GetMainThreadId()) OffMain++; }
        public bool Connected => HostSawAt > 0 && GuestSawAt > 0;
        // How far a pair got, for the message of a check it failed: a pair that did not connect once
        // in three bars said only "5000 ms", and nothing of which step it stopped at.
        public string Stage() =>
            $"offer sealed {OfferSealed}, pasted {Pasted}, reply sealed {ReplySealed} at {(ReplySealed ? ReplyAt - _born : 0)} ms, "
            + $"applied {Applied} at {(Applied ? AppliedAt - _born : 0)} ms; host {HostConn.GetConnectionState()} with {_offerCands.Count} candidates, "
            + $"guest {GuestConn.GetConnectionState()} with {_answerCands.Count}; host saw {HostSawAt > 0}, guest saw {GuestSawAt > 0}";
        // One frame: poll both ends, then move the exchange on. The guest "pastes" the invite the
        // frame it is sealed; the host takes the reply ApplyAfterMs after the guest sealed it.
        public void Step()
        {
            Host.Poll(); Guest.Poll();
            if (!OfferSealed && Link.Sealed(HostConn)) OfferSealed = true;
            if (OfferSealed && !Pasted && Delivered)
            {
                Pasted = true;
                Guest.AddPeer(GuestConn, 1);
                Hand(GuestConn, "offer", Rendezvous.Kind.Invite, _offer, _offerCands);
            }
            if (Pasted && !ReplySealed && Link.Sealed(GuestConn)) { ReplySealed = true; ReplyAt = Now; }
            if (ReplySealed && !Applied && Now >= ReplyAt + (ulong)ApplyAfterMs)
            {
                Applied = true; AppliedAt = Now; GuestAtApply = GuestConn.GetConnectionState();
                Hand(HostConn, "answer", Rendezvous.Kind.Reply, _answer, _answerCands);
            }
        }
        // One side's bundle to the other: whole, or through the courier as a code. A code does not carry a
        // candidate's section: it is the template's one, a=mid:0, as R2's Net will give it.
        void Hand(WebRtcPeerConnection to, string type, Rendezvous.Kind kind, string sdp, List<(string media, int index, string name)> cands)
        {
            if (_carry == null)
            {
                to.SetRemoteDescription(type, sdp);
                foreach (var c in cands) to.AddIceCandidate(c.media, c.index, c.name);
                return;
            }
            var (built, lines) = _carry(kind, sdp, cands.Select(c => c.name).ToArray());
            to.SetRemoteDescription(type, built);
            foreach (var l in lines) to.AddIceCandidate("0", 0, l);
        }
        // Every object this pair made, let go of here and now -- not by the collector's finalizer
        // thread (Assets.cs: a C# handle released there can turn into an engine ERROR).
        public void Free()
        {
            Host.Close(); Guest.Close(); HostConn.Close(); GuestConn.Close();
            Host.Dispose(); Guest.Dispose(); HostConn.Dispose(); GuestConn.Dispose();
        }
    }
    async Task<bool> RtcUntil(Func<bool> done, int ms, params RtcPair[] pairs)
    {
        ulong end = RtcPair.Now + (ulong)ms;
        while (!done() && RtcPair.Now < end)
        {
            foreach (var p in pairs) p.Step();
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        return done();
    }
    // One side's data channels, read once and let go of at once (see Free).
    static List<(int id, WebRtcDataChannel.ChannelState state, int life)> RtcChannels(WebRtcMultiplayerPeer mp, int id)
    {
        using var peer = mp.GetPeer(id);
        var seen = new List<(int, WebRtcDataChannel.ChannelState, int)>();
        foreach (var v in peer["channels"].AsGodotArray())
            using (var ch = v.As<WebRtcDataChannel>()) seen.Add((ch.GetId(), ch.GetReadyState(), ch.GetMaxPacketLifeTime()));
        return seen;
    }

    // THE REPLY WINDOW HOLDS (§3.4, §10.4 R0). R0 measured it once (DESIGN.md: every delay up to 60 s
    // connected, so 30 s); this holds the build to it on every solo run. The friend's agent starts its
    // give-up clock at paste; on this path -- an in-process pair over the LAN host candidate, where the
    // host answers the guest's early checks (P2) -- the guest's DTLS client may start before the host
    // has the reply, and a shorter bound than ICE's would show here first. One pair starts at the top
    // of the run, its host takes the reply Link.ReplyWindowS after the guest made it, and it is judged
    // in WebRtcPairs: in the background, so it adds no wall time beyond the run's own length.
    Task<(bool connected, long appliedAfter, long upAfter, string stage)> _replyWindow;
    async Task<(bool connected, long appliedAfter, long upAfter, string stage)> ReplyWindowHolds()
    {
        if (!Link.Available) return (false, 0, -1, "no plugin");
        var p = new RtcPair(Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue), Link.ReplyWindowS * 1000L);
        await RtcUntil(() => p.Connected || (p.Applied && RtcPair.Now > p.AppliedAt + 4000), (Link.ReplyWindowS + 20) * 1000, p);
        var seen = (p.Connected, p.Applied ? (long)p.AppliedAt - (long)p.ReplyAt : -1L,
                    p.Connected && p.Applied ? (long)Math.Max(p.HostSawAt, p.GuestSawAt) - (long)p.AppliedAt : -1L, p.Stage());
        p.Free();
        return seen;
    }

    // ── WEBRTC, R1: THE CODES (network_webrtc.md §4, §10.4 R1) ──────────────────────────────────────
    // THE SPIKE'S REAL BUNDLES (runs\stun offer_2.json and answer_2.json), the internet address replaced
    // by a documentation one (203.0.113.7): what the plugin makes, as literals the codec is held to.
    const string SpikeOffer = "v=0\r\no=rtc 2614788236 0 IN IP4 127.0.0.1\r\ns=-\r\nt=0 0\r\na=group:BUNDLE 0\r\na=msid-semantic:WMS *\r\na=ice-options:ice2,trickle\r\n"
        + "a=fingerprint:sha-256 45:C6:28:38:84:9D:AD:28:5E:93:1A:E8:88:0C:A7:50:12:17:F4:92:DE:24:53:13:AC:1E:F8:85:80:13:20:99\r\n"
        + "m=application 9 UDP/DTLS/SCTP webrtc-datachannel\r\nc=IN IP4 0.0.0.0\r\na=mid:0\r\na=sendrecv\r\na=sctp-port:5000\r\na=max-message-size:262144\r\n"
        + "a=setup:actpass\r\na=ice-ufrag:giTP\r\na=ice-pwd:4g46IhzHEvYFD/0EIUDle1\r\n";
    static readonly string[] SpikeOfferLines = { "candidate:1 1 UDP 2114977791 192.168.0.33 54617 typ host", "candidate:2 1 UDP 1678769919 203.0.113.7 54617 typ srflx raddr 0.0.0.0 rport 0" };
    const string SpikeAnswer = "v=0\r\no=rtc 2634790502 0 IN IP4 127.0.0.1\r\ns=-\r\nt=0 0\r\na=group:BUNDLE 0\r\na=msid-semantic:WMS *\r\na=ice-options:ice2,trickle\r\n"
        + "a=fingerprint:sha-256 07:9A:D7:15:4A:4C:26:4A:5A:22:A8:45:65:01:87:E7:2A:50:2A:E2:39:EC:20:B0:A6:B6:84:3D:E0:D5:8F:B1\r\n"
        + "m=application 9 UDP/DTLS/SCTP webrtc-datachannel\r\nc=IN IP4 0.0.0.0\r\na=mid:0\r\na=sendrecv\r\na=sctp-port:5000\r\na=max-message-size:262144\r\n"
        + "a=setup:active\r\na=ice-ufrag:BOeX\r\na=ice-pwd:ZOIsPAdHc0dSoJIIX6y8wQ\r\n";
    static readonly string[] SpikeAnswerLines = { "candidate:1 1 UDP 2114977791 192.168.0.33 54581 typ host", "candidate:3 1 UDP 1678769663 203.0.113.7 54581 typ srflx raddr 0.0.0.0 rport 0" };

    // A WHOLE COPIED DISCORD MESSAGE around a code: a name holding "wsr" before it, the time, line breaks,
    // the code hand-copied over two lines with a hyphen, and words after it.
    static string Message(string code)
    {
        int cut = Rng.RandiRange(10, code.Length - 10);
        return $"Swsrdna the Brave — Today at {Rng.RandiRange(1, 12)}:{Rng.RandiRange(10, 59)} PM\nhere it is, paste it in:\n{code[..cut]}-\n{code[cut..]}\nthanks! wsr = warships reply btw";
    }
    // A record as one line of text, every field, for comparing two.
    static string Said(Rendezvous.Record r) =>
        $"{r.Kind} setup {r.Setup} nostun {r.NoStun} proto {r.Proto:x8} id {r.Id} guest {r.Guest} why {r.Why} build '{r.Build}' name '{r.Name}' "
        + $"ice {r.Ufrag}/{r.Pwd} fp {Convert.ToHexString(r.Fingerprint)} [{string.Join(" | ", r.Candidates.Select(Rendezvous.Sdp.Line))}]";
    const string CredChars = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
    static string VaryCred(int n) => new(Enumerable.Range(0, n).Select(_ => CredChars[Rng.RandiRange(0, 63)]).ToArray());
    static System.Net.IPAddress VaryIp(bool v6)
    {
        var b = new byte[v6 ? 16 : 4];
        for (int i = 0; i < b.Length; i++) b[i] = (byte)Rng.RandiRange(0, 255);
        if (v6) b[0] = (byte)(0x20 | (b[0] & 0x1F));           // global unicast, 2000::/3
        return new System.Net.IPAddress(b);
    }
    static Rendezvous.Candidate VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via type, System.Net.IPAddress at) =>
        new(type, Rng.RandiRange(0, 255), Rng.Randi(), Rng.RandiRange(1, 65535), at);
    // A record of a kind with only the fields that kind carries (§4.1), every one of them drawn.
    static Rendezvous.Record VaryRecord(Rendezvous.Kind kind)
    {
        var names = new[] { "Harbour8", "Ølaf", "Kasia Ż", "x", "Twelve Chars" };
        var r = new Rendezvous.Record { Kind = kind };
        string build = Rng.RandiRange(0, 1) == 0 ? "dev" : "2026.09.25-1a2b3c4";
        switch (kind)
        {
            case Rendezvous.Kind.Knock: r.Proto = (int)Rng.Randi(); r.Guest = Rng.Randi(); r.Build = build; r.Name = names[Rng.RandiRange(0, names.Length - 1)]; return r;
            case Rendezvous.Kind.Refuse: r.Why = (Rendezvous.Why)Rng.RandiRange(0, 3); r.Proto = (int)Rng.Randi(); r.Build = build; return r;
        }
        r.Setup = kind == Rendezvous.Kind.Invite ? 0 : 1; r.NoStun = Rng.RandiRange(0, 1) == 1;
        r.Id = Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue); r.Name = names[Rng.RandiRange(0, names.Length - 1)];
        if (kind == Rendezvous.Kind.Invite) { r.Proto = (int)Rng.Randi(); r.Build = build; }
        r.Ufrag = VaryCred(4); r.Pwd = VaryCred(22);
        for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++) r.Fingerprint[i] = (byte)Rng.RandiRange(0, 255);
        int n = Rng.RandiRange(1, 5);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) r.Candidates.Add(VaryCandidate((Rendezvous.Via)Rng.RandiRange(0, 3), VaryIp(Rng.RandiRange(0, 2) == 0)));
        return r;
    }

    // ONE BUNDLE THROUGH THE WHOLE CODEC, as a code travels: stripped, packed, written as text, found
    // in a message, rebuilt. The rebuilt SDP must equal the plugin's own byte for byte but the o= id
    // (fresh each time, and meaningless to the far end), and every candidate line byte for byte. "" when
    // it does; otherwise what differs.
    static string ThroughCodec(Rendezvous.Kind kind, string sdp, string[] lines)
    {
        var r = Rendezvous.Sdp.Strip(kind, sdp, lines);
        r.Id = Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue); r.Name = "Harbour8";
        if (kind == Rendezvous.Kind.Invite) { r.Proto = Net.Protocol; r.Build = Game.Build; }
        var back = Rendezvous.Find($"here you go: {Rendezvous.Encode(r)} (thanks)", kind);
        if (back == null) return "the code was not found in its own message";
        var was = sdp.Split("\r\n"); var now = Rendezvous.Sdp.Build(back).Split("\r\n");
        var oLine = new System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex(@"^o=rtc [0-9]+ 0 IN IP4 127\.0\.0\.1$");
        var diff = new List<string>();
        for (int i = 0; i < Math.Max(was.Length, now.Length); i++)
        {
            string x = i < was.Length ? was[i] : "(none)", y = i < now.Length ? now[i] : "(none)";
            if (x != y && !(i == 1 && oLine.IsMatch(x) && oLine.IsMatch(y))) diff.Add($"line {i + 1} \"{x}\" came back \"{y}\"");
        }
        var rebuilt = back.Candidates.Select(Rendezvous.Sdp.Line).ToList();
        for (int i = 0; i < Math.Max(lines.Length, rebuilt.Count); i++)
            if ((i < lines.Length ? lines[i] : "(none)") != (i < rebuilt.Count ? rebuilt[i] : "(none)"))
                diff.Add($"candidate \"{(i < lines.Length ? lines[i] : "(none)")}\" came back \"{(i < rebuilt.Count ? rebuilt[i] : "(none)")}\"");
        return string.Join("; ", diff);
    }
    // What a refusal says, or "" when there was none.
    static string Refusal(Action act)
    {
        try { act(); return ""; }
        catch (FormatException e) { return e.Message; }
    }

    void Codes()
    {
        var kinds = new[] { Rendezvous.Kind.Invite, Rendezvous.Kind.Reply, Rendezvous.Kind.Knock, Rendezvous.Kind.Refuse };
        // EVERY KIND COMES BACK, field for field, three varied records of each
        var trips = new List<string>();
        for (int n = 0; n < 3; n++)
            foreach (var k in kinds)
            {
                var r = VaryRecord(k);
                string back;
                try { back = Said(Rendezvous.Unpack(Rendezvous.Pack(r))); } catch (FormatException e) { back = e.Message; }
                if (back != Said(r)) trips.Add($"{Said(r)} came back {back}");
            }
        Check(trips.Count == 0, $"invite, reply, knock and refuse each come back from Pack and Unpack field for field, three varied records of each ({(trips.Count == 0 ? "12 of 12" : string.Join("; ", trips))})");
        // the wire values the format names (§4.1): the kind nibble, and a refusal's reason byte
        Check((int)Rendezvous.Kind.Invite == 1 && (int)Rendezvous.Kind.Reply == 2 && (int)Rendezvous.Kind.Knock == 3 && (int)Rendezvous.Kind.Refuse == 4
              && (int)Rendezvous.Why.Full == 0 && (int)Rendezvous.Why.Build == 1 && (int)Rendezvous.Why.Rate == 2 && (int)Rendezvous.Why.Closed == 3
              && Rendezvous.Format == 1 && Rendezvous.Pack(VaryRecord(Rendezvous.Kind.Knock))[0] == 0x31,
              "a record's first byte is its kind and the format (a knock: 0x31), and a refusal says full, build, rate or closed as 0-3 (§4.1)");

        // THE SIZES the plan's model gives for this PC's shape (§4.4 A: one LAN address and one
        // server-reflexive, a release build id of 18 characters, an 8-character name)
        var inv = Rendezvous.Sdp.Strip(Rendezvous.Kind.Invite, SpikeOffer, SpikeOfferLines);
        inv.Id = Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue); inv.Proto = Net.Protocol; inv.Build = "2026.09.25-1a2b3c4"; inv.Name = "Harbour8";
        var rep = Rendezvous.Sdp.Strip(Rendezvous.Kind.Reply, SpikeAnswer, SpikeAnswerLines);
        rep.Id = inv.Id; rep.Name = "Harbour8";
        int invB = Rendezvous.Pack(inv).Length, repB = Rendezvous.Pack(rep).Length, invC = Rendezvous.Encode(inv).Length, repC = Rendezvous.Encode(rep).Length;
        Check(invB == 121 && repB == 98 && invC == 197 && repC == 160,
              $"the spike's bundles pack to the plan's sizes: invite 121 B and 197 characters, reply 98 B and 160 (§4.4 A; {invB} B {invC}, {repB} B {repC})");

        // A NAME is clipped to 16 bytes at a character boundary: the ø of "Ølaf, the Ångøra" would end at 17
        var named = VaryRecord(Rendezvous.Kind.Reply); named.Name = "Ølaf, the Ångøra";
        string clipped = Rendezvous.Unpack(Rendezvous.Pack(named)).Name;
        Check(clipped == "Ølaf, the Ång", $"a name is carried as at most 16 bytes, never half a character (\"{clipped}\")");

        // BYTE FOR BYTE: the spike's real bundles, stripped to a code and rebuilt
        string offerDiff = ThroughCodec(Rendezvous.Kind.Invite, SpikeOffer, SpikeOfferLines), answerDiff = ThroughCodec(Rendezvous.Kind.Reply, SpikeAnswer, SpikeAnswerLines);
        Check(offerDiff == "" && answerDiff == "",
              $"the spike's offer and answer, through the codec and back, equal the plugin's own byte for byte but the o= id ({(offerDiff + answerDiff == "" ? "17 lines and 2 candidates each" : offerDiff + " " + answerDiff)})");

        // WHAT A CODE CANNOT CARRY is refused, and the refusal names it
        string unknown = Refusal(() => Rendezvous.Sdp.Strip(Rendezvous.Kind.Invite, SpikeOffer.Replace("a=mid:0\r\n", "a=mid:0\r\na=extmap-allow-mixed\r\n"), SpikeOfferLines));
        Check(unknown.Contains("\"a=extmap-allow-mixed\""), $"an SDP line the template does not know is refused, by name ({unknown})");
        int big = Rng.RandiRange(256, 999);
        string foundation = Refusal(() => Rendezvous.Sdp.Parse($"candidate:{big} 1 UDP 2114977791 192.168.0.33 54617 typ host"));
        Check(foundation.Contains("over 255"), $"a candidate foundation over 255 ({big}) is refused ({foundation})");
        string cred = Refusal(() => Rendezvous.Sdp.Strip(Rendezvous.Kind.Invite, SpikeOffer.Replace("a=ice-ufrag:giTP", "a=ice-ufrag:gi_P"), SpikeOfferLines));
        Check(cred.Contains("'_'"), $"an ICE credential with a character outside base64 is refused, naming it ({cred})");
        string sha1 = Refusal(() => Rendezvous.Sdp.Strip(Rendezvous.Kind.Invite,
            SpikeOffer.Replace("a=fingerprint:sha-256 45:C6:28:38:84:9D:AD:28:5E:93:1A:E8:88:0C:A7:50:12:17:F4:92:DE:24:53:13:AC:1E:F8:85:80:13:20:99", "a=fingerprint:sha-1 45:C6:28:38:84:9D:AD:28:5E:93:1A:E8:88:0C:A7:50:12:17:F4:92"), SpikeOfferLines));
        Check(sha1.Contains("sha-256"), $"a fingerprint that is not sha-256 is refused ({sha1})");

        // THE TEXT (§4.3), three varied codes. A code whose text holds no 0 or no 1 is drawn again, so
        // the I/L/O check always has something to prove.
        var textFails = new List<string>();
        for (int n = 0; n < 3; n++)
        {
            Rendezvous.Record r; string code;
            do { r = VaryRecord(n == 1 ? Rendezvous.Kind.Invite : Rendezvous.Kind.Reply); code = Rendezvous.Encode(r); }
            while (!code[3..].Contains('0') || !code[3..].Contains('1'));
            string Back(string text) => Rendezvous.Find(text) is { } f ? Said(f) : "nothing";
            if (Back(code) != Said(r)) textFails.Add($"round trip: {Back(code)}");
            // a whole copied Discord message (Message)
            string msg = Message(code);
            if (Back(msg) != Said(r)) textFails.Add($"in a message: {Back(msg)}");
            // one changed character, anywhere but the last (whose low bits can be padding)
            int at = Rng.RandiRange(3, code.Length - 2);
            int was = Rendezvous.Crockford.IndexOf(code[at]);
            char other = Rendezvous.Crockford[(was + Rng.RandiRange(1, 31)) % 32];
            string damaged = code[..at] + other + code[(at + 1)..];
            if (Rendezvous.Find(damaged) != null) textFails.Add($"a changed character at {at} ({code[at]} to {other}) still read");
            if (Back(code.ToLowerInvariant()) != Said(r)) textFails.Add("lower case did not read");
            int ones = 0;
            string retyped = new(code.Select((c, i) => i < 3 ? c : c == '1' ? (ones++ % 2 == 0 ? 'I' : 'L') : c == '0' ? 'O' : c).ToArray());
            if (Back(retyped) != Said(r)) textFails.Add("I, L and O for 1, 1 and 0 did not read");
            // the rows: a code is the paste row's, never the address row's, and a damaged one neither's
            foreach (var t in new[] { code, msg })
                if (Rendezvous.PathFor(t) != Rendezvous.Paste || Rendezvous.Address.Claims(t)) textFails.Add($"the {Rendezvous.PathFor(t)?.Id ?? "no"} row claimed a code");
            if (Rendezvous.PathFor(damaged) != null) textFails.Add($"the {Rendezvous.PathFor(damaged).Id} row claimed a damaged code");
        }
        Check(textFails.Count == 0,
              $"a code reads back whole, inside a Discord message, in lower case and with I, L, O for 1, 1, 0; one changed character does not; every code is the paste row's (three varied: {(textFails.Count == 0 ? "all" : string.Join("; ", textFails))})");
        var typed = new[] { $"192.168.{Rng.RandiRange(0, 255)}.{Rng.RandiRange(1, 254)}", $"26.{Rng.RandiRange(0, 255)}.{Rng.RandiRange(0, 255)}.{Rng.RandiRange(1, 254)}:{Rng.RandiRange(1024, 65535)}", "[2001:db8::7]:27016", "gamebox.local" };
        var misread = typed.Where(t => Rendezvous.PathFor(t) != Rendezvous.Address || Rendezvous.Paste.Claims(t)).ToList();
        Check(misread.Count == 0, $"a typed address is the address row's, and never the paste row's ({(misread.Count == 0 ? string.Join(", ", typed) : "misread: " + string.Join(", ", misread))})");
        Check(Rendezvous.Paste.Id == "paste" && !Rendezvous.Paste.Auto && Rendezvous.Paste.Stun && Rendezvous.Paste.Budget == 400
              && Rendezvous.Address.Id == "address" && Rendezvous.Address.Auto && !Rendezvous.Address.Stun && Rendezvous.Address.Budget == 8192,
              "the paste row walks the STUN rows, never signals again by itself, and holds a code to 400 characters; the address row gathers without STUN, retries by itself, and reads 8,192 B");

        // THE FIT RULE (§4.3, §4.4 E): 10 IPv4 host addresses (the LAN, a Radmin one and 8 others), 9
        // global IPv6 on 9 /64s and a server-reflexive: 20 candidates, in a varied order, into 400
        // characters. The rest goes first, then IPv6; the invite's 248 B then hold the three that matter
        // and 4 IPv6 (97 B + 12 an IPv4 + 24 an IPv6, the plan's layout).
        var lan = VaryIp(false); var radmin = VaryIp(false); var srflx = VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Srflx, VaryIp(false));
        var shapeE = new Rendezvous.Record { Kind = Rendezvous.Kind.Invite, Id = 7, Proto = 1, Build = "2026.09.25-1a2b3c4", Name = "Harbour8", Ufrag = VaryCred(4), Pwd = VaryCred(22) };
        shapeE.Candidates.Add(srflx);
        shapeE.Candidates.Add(VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Host, lan));
        shapeE.Candidates.Add(VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Host, radmin));
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) shapeE.Candidates.Add(VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Host, VaryIp(false)));
        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++)
        {
            var v6 = VaryIp(true).GetAddressBytes(); v6[7] = (byte)i;       // 9 different /64s
            shapeE.Candidates.Add(VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Host, new System.Net.IPAddress(v6)));
        }
        shapeE.Candidates = shapeE.Candidates.OrderBy(_ => Rng.Randi()).ToList();
        int fitDropped = Rendezvous.Fit(shapeE, Rendezvous.Paste, new List<System.Net.IPAddress> { radmin }, lan);
        int fitLength = Rendezvous.Encode(shapeE).Length, v6Kept = shapeE.Candidates.Count(c => c.Address.AddressFamily == System.Net.Sockets.AddressFamily.InterNetworkV6);
        Check(fitLength <= 400 && shapeE.Candidates.Contains(srflx) && shapeE.Candidates.Any(c => c.Address.Equals(lan)) && shapeE.Candidates.Any(c => c.Address.Equals(radmin))
              && v6Kept == 4 && shapeE.Candidates.Count == 7 && fitDropped == 13,
              $"20 candidates fit 400 characters with the server-reflexive, the overlay and the LAN address kept, and 4 IPv6 (§4.4 E: {fitLength} characters, {shapeE.Candidates.Count} kept, {v6Kept} IPv6, {fitDropped} dropped)");
        // two IPv6 on one /64 keep one, fit or not; one on another /64 stays
        var a64 = VaryIp(true).GetAddressBytes(); var b64 = (byte[])a64.Clone(); var c64 = (byte[])a64.Clone();
        b64[15] ^= 0x5A; b64[12] ^= 0x33; c64[7] ^= 0x01;
        var small = VaryRecord(Rendezvous.Kind.Reply);
        var first6 = VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Host, new System.Net.IPAddress(a64));
        small.Candidates = new List<Rendezvous.Candidate> { VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Host, lan), first6, VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Host, new System.Net.IPAddress(b64)),
                                                            VaryCandidate(Rendezvous.Via.Host, new System.Net.IPAddress(c64)), srflx };
        int smallDropped = Rendezvous.Fit(small, Rendezvous.Paste, new List<System.Net.IPAddress>(), lan);
        Check(smallDropped == 1 && small.Candidates.Count == 4 && small.Candidates.Contains(first6) && Rendezvous.Encode(small).Length <= 400,
              $"two IPv6 addresses on one /64 keep the first, though the code fits; one on another /64 stays ({smallDropped} dropped, {small.Candidates.Count} kept)");

        // THE SEAMS ARE MUTABLE, so they stay out of the build's fingerprint: swapped, it is the build's
        var clipField = typeof(Rendezvous).GetField(nameof(Rendezvous.Clipboard), BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static);
        var clipWas = Rendezvous.Clipboard;
        Rendezvous.Clipboard = () => "swapped";
        int swapped = FingerprintNow();
        Rendezvous.Clipboard = clipWas;
        Check(clipField != null && !clipField.IsInitOnly && !clipField.IsLiteral && swapped == Net.Protocol,
              $"Rendezvous.Clipboard is mutable and outside the build's fingerprint (swapped {swapped:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}{Moved(swapped)})");
    }

    // THE FINGERPRINT NOW (Net.Fingerprint, as Net.Protocol was taken at startup), and its PARTS: every
    // fixed value it reads, "Type.Field=value". The parts are taken at the top of the solo run, so a
    // check that finds the fingerprint moved can say what moved: R1's first solo run found it moved by
    // the pilot's purchases (Character.Bought, a readonly array until then).
    static int FingerprintNow() => (int)typeof(Net).GetMethod("Fingerprint", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static, Type.EmptyTypes).Invoke(null, null);
    static List<string> FingerprintParts()
    {
        var parts = new List<string>();
        var culture = System.Globalization.CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
        System.Globalization.CultureInfo.CurrentCulture = System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
        try { typeof(Net).GetMethod("Fingerprint", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static, null, new[] { typeof(List<string>) }, null).Invoke(null, new object[] { parts }); }
        finally { System.Globalization.CultureInfo.CurrentCulture = culture; }
        return parts;
    }
    static List<string> _partsAtStart;
    static int _hashAtStart;                // the fingerprint when the parts were taken: Net's, unless it moved before the run began
    // "" while the fingerprint is still the startup's; otherwise what moved: each changed part, cut to
    // 60 characters either side of where it first differs (a table's part is one long line).
    static string Moved(int now)
    {
        if (now == Net.Protocol) return "";
        if (_partsAtStart == null) return $"; the startup's was {Net.Protocol:x8}";
        string early = _hashAtStart == Net.Protocol ? "" : $" -- and it had moved before the run began ({_hashAtStart:x8} at the harness's start), which no part below can name";
        static string Key(string p) => p.Split('=')[0];
        static string Cut(string s, int at) { int from = Math.Max(0, at - 60); return s[from..Math.Min(s.Length, at + 60)]; }
        var was = _partsAtStart.GroupBy(Key).ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First());
        var moved = new List<string>();
        foreach (var p in FingerprintParts())
        {
            if (!was.TryGetValue(Key(p), out var old)) { moved.Add($"new \"{Cut(p, 0)}\""); continue; }
            if (old == p) continue;
            int at = 0;
            while (at < old.Length && at < p.Length && old[at] == p[at]) at++;
            moved.Add($"{Key(p)} at {at}: \"{Cut(old, at)}\" became \"{Cut(p, at)}\"");
        }
        return $"; the startup's was {Net.Protocol:x8}{early}; {moved.Count} parts moved since the run began: {string.Join(" | ", moved.Take(4))}";
    }

    // ── WEBRTC, R1: THE STUN ROWS AND THE WALK (§5, §10.4 R1) ───────────────────────────────────────
    // THE BOX (tools/smoketest/wan.py): the network between players, which run.ps1 starts for every run.
    const int BoxHttp = 19480;
    static System.Text.Json.JsonElement Box(string method, string path)
    {
        using var http = new System.Net.Http.HttpClient { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3) };
        using var ask = new System.Net.Http.HttpRequestMessage(new System.Net.Http.HttpMethod(method), $"http://127.0.0.1:{BoxHttp}{path}");
        string body;
        try { using var got = http.Send(ask); body = got.Content.ReadAsStringAsync().GetAwaiter().GetResult(); }
        catch (Exception e) { body = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { error = "the box did not answer: " + e.Message }); }
        using var doc = System.Text.Json.JsonDocument.Parse(body);
        return doc.RootElement.Clone();
    }
    static readonly Link.StunRow[] _servers = Link.Servers;          // the game's own rows, put back after
    // the box's rows: two ports that never answer, and its STUN responder
    static readonly Link.StunRow SilentA = new("stun:127.0.0.1:19482"), SilentB = new("stun:127.0.0.1:19483"), BoxStun = new("stun:127.0.41.1:3478");
    // LIBJUICE GATHERS A LOOPBACK-MAPPED SERVER-REFLEXIVE CANDIDATE: the box's responder maps every
    // request to 127.0.0.1. If a run shows libjuice drops it, this goes false and DESIGN.md says so; the
    // answering-row walk and the sealing rule then hold the timing and the host candidates only (§10.4 R1).
    static readonly bool LoopbackSrflx = true;

    // Steps walks once a frame, each after its peer's poll, until all are sealed or `ms` pass.
    async Task Walk(int ms, params Link.Gather[] gs)
    {
        ulong end = RtcPair.Now + (ulong)ms;
        while (gs.Any(g => !g.Done) && RtcPair.Now < end)
        {
            foreach (var mp in gs.Select(g => g.Mp).Distinct()) mp.Poll();
            foreach (var g in gs) g.Step();
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
    }
    static string Walked(Link.Gather g) =>
        $"row {g.Row}, done {g.Done} in {(g.Done ? (long)(g.DoneAt - g.Began) : -1)} ms, no STUN {g.NoStun}, re-added {g.Added}, seals "
        + string.Join(" ", g.Seals.Select(s => $"[{(s.complete ? "complete" : "clock")} at {(long)(s.at - g.Began)} ms, due {(long)(s.deadline - g.Began)}, step before {(long)(s.before - g.Began)}]"))
        + $", {g.Lines.Length} candidates: {string.Join(" | ", g.Lines)}";

    async Task Walks()
    {
        // THE TABLE, against the ruling's rows (§5.1) and the plan's timings (§3.3, §5.2)
        var row0 = Link.Config(0)["iceServers"].AsGodotArray();
        string url0 = row0.Count == 1 ? row0[0].AsGodotDictionary()["urls"].AsGodotArray()[0].AsString() : $"{row0.Count} rows";
        Check(_servers.Select(s => s.Url).SequenceEqual(new[] { "stun:stun.l.google.com:19302", "stun:stun.cloudflare.com:3478" })
              && url0 == "stun:stun.l.google.com:19302" && Link.Config(-1)["iceServers"].AsGodotArray().Count == 0 && Link.Config(2)["iceServers"].AsGodotArray().Count == 0
              && Link.GatherMs == 2000 && Link.LinkMs == 12000 && Link.InviteLifeS == 900,
              $"the STUN rows are Google's then Cloudflare's and nothing else, one to a connection (row 0 configures {url0}); a row is passed over after 2,000 ms, a reply has 12,000 ms, an invite 900 s");
        // THE SEAMS ARE MUTABLE, so they stay out of the build's fingerprint: swapped, it is the build's
        // (a readonly StunRow[] would be read: an array of records is Plain)
        var serversField = typeof(Link).GetField(nameof(Link.Servers), BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static);
        Link.Servers = new[] { SilentA, SilentB, BoxStun };
        int swapped = FingerprintNow();
        Link.Servers = _servers;
        Check(serversField != null && !serversField.IsInitOnly && !serversField.IsLiteral && swapped == Net.Protocol,
              $"Link.Servers is mutable and outside the build's fingerprint (with the box's three rows {swapped:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}{Moved(swapped)})");

        var box = Box("GET", "/box/stats");
        bool boxUp = box.TryGetProperty("stun", out var stunStats) && stunStats.GetProperty("bound").GetBoolean()
                     && box.GetProperty("silent_udp").EnumerateObject().Count() == 2 && box.GetProperty("silent_tcp").GetProperty("bound").GetBoolean();
        Check(boxUp, $"the box is up: its STUN responder on 127.0.41.1:3478, the silent UDP ports 19482 and 19483, the silent TCP port 19481 ({box})");
        if (!boxUp) return;

        var hostMp = new WebRtcMultiplayerPeer();
        hostMp.CreateServer(Link.Channels());
        var guestMps = new List<WebRtcMultiplayerPeer>();
        var walks = new List<Link.Gather>();
        Link.Gather HostWalk(bool stun) { var g = new Link.Gather(hostMp, Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue), stun, c => c.CreateOffer()); walks.Add(g); return g; }
        // A GUEST'S PEER 1, answering an invite: the spike's offer, with none of its candidates (a guest
        // adds the host's only after its walk, Link.Gather)
        Link.Gather GuestWalk()
        {
            var mp = new WebRtcMultiplayerPeer();
            mp.CreateClient(Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue), Link.Channels());
            guestMps.Add(mp);
            var g = new Link.Gather(mp, 1, true, c => c.SetRemoteDescription("offer", SpikeOffer));
            walks.Add(g);
            return g;
        }
        try
        {
            // TWO ROWS THAT NEVER ANSWER: each is given up at GatherMs and the connection made again on the
            // next with the same id; the bundle is sealed at 4,000 ms with this PC's own addresses and the
            // flag set, each seal on the first frame after its deadline
            Link.Servers = new[] { SilentA, SilentB }; Link.StunFirst = 0;
            var silent = new[] { HostWalk(true), GuestWalk() };
            await Walk(8000, silent);
            string Late(Link.Gather g)
            {
                // on time: the step before each seal came before its deadline, so the seal is on the first
                // frame after it (the rebuild between the two rows is not a frame, and is not counted)
                var timed = g.Seals.Where(s => !s.complete).ToList();
                long took = g.Done ? (long)(g.DoneAt - g.Began) : -1;
                bool ok = g.Done && g.NoStun && g.Row == 1 && g.Added == Error.Ok && g.Lines.Length > 0 && g.Lines.All(l => l.EndsWith(" typ host", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                          && g.Seals.Count == 2 && timed.Count == 2 && timed.All(s => s.before < s.deadline && s.deadline <= s.at) && took >= 4000;
                return ok ? "" : Walked(g);
            }
            string hostLate = Late(silent[0]), guestLate = Late(silent[1]);
            Check(hostLate == "" && guestLate == "" && Link.StunFirst == 2,
                  $"with two rows that never answer, a host's and a guest's bundle are sealed at 4,000 ms with host candidates only and the flag set, each row given up on the first frame after its 2,000 ms, the id taken back at once (host: {(hostLate == "" ? Walked(silent[0]) : hostLate)}; guest: {(guestLate == "" ? $"{(long)(silent[1].DoneAt - silent[1].Began)} ms" : guestLate)}; StunFirst {Link.StunFirst})");
            // THE MEMORY: the next gather in the process skips the rows that did not answer
            var memory = HostWalk(true);
            await Walk(3000, memory);
            long memoryMs = memory.Done ? (long)(memory.DoneAt - memory.Began) : -1;
            Check(memory.Done && memory.Row == -1 && memory.NoStun && memoryMs >= 0 && memoryMs < 100,
                  $"after no row answered, the next gather asks none and is sealed in under 100 ms ({memoryMs} ms; {Walked(memory)})");

            // ROW 2 ANSWERS: the walk passes row 1 at 2,000 ms, stops at row 2 with a server-reflexive
            // candidate, and remembers it
            Link.Servers = new[] { SilentA, BoxStun }; Link.StunFirst = 0;
            var second = new[] { HostWalk(true), GuestWalk() };
            await Walk(8000, second);
            string Answered(Link.Gather g)
            {
                bool srflx = g.Lines.Any(l => l.Contains(" typ srflx", StringComparison.Ordinal));
                bool ok = g.Done && g.Row == 1 && g.Added == Error.Ok && g.Seals.Count == 2 && !g.Seals[0].complete
                          && (LoopbackSrflx ? srflx && !g.NoStun && g.Seals[1].complete : g.NoStun);
                return ok ? "" : Walked(g);
            }
            string hostSecond = Answered(second[0]), guestSecond = Answered(second[1]);
            Check(hostSecond == "" && guestSecond == "" && Link.StunFirst == (LoopbackSrflx ? 1 : 2),
                  $"with row 1 silent and row 2 the box's STUN responder, a host's and a guest's walk stop at row 2 with a server-reflexive candidate, and StunFirst is 1 (host: {(hostSecond == "" ? Walked(second[0]) : hostSecond)}; guest: {(guestSecond == "" ? "row 2" : guestSecond)}; StunFirst {Link.StunFirst})");

            // THE SEALING RULE (v1 §2.6 S2): 20 gathers against the box's responder, each sealed by
            // Link.Sealed with its server-reflexive candidate in it...
            Link.Servers = new[] { BoxStun }; Link.StunFirst = 0;
            var answered = Enumerable.Range(0, 20).Select(_ => HostWalk(true)).ToArray();
            await Walk(5000, answered);
            var unsealed = answered.Where(g => !(g.Done && g.Seals.Count == 1 && g.Seals[0].complete
                                                 && g.Lines.Any(l => l.Contains(" typ srflx", StringComparison.Ordinal)) == LoopbackSrflx)).ToList();
            Check(unsealed.Count == 0, $"20 gathers against a STUN server that answers are each sealed by Link.Sealed, the server-reflexive candidate inside ({20 - unsealed.Count} of 20{(unsealed.Count == 0 ? "" : "; " + Walked(unsealed[0]))})");
            // ...and 20 with no STUN, each holding every host candidate that arrives in the second after
            var own = Enumerable.Range(0, 20).Select(_ => HostWalk(false)).ToArray();
            await Walk(3000, own);
            ulong settle = RtcPair.Now + 1000;
            while (RtcPair.Now < settle) { hostMp.Poll(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            var missed = own.Where(g => !(g.Done && g.Seals.Count == 1 && g.Seals[0].complete && g.Arrived.All(l => g.Lines.Contains(l)))).ToList();
            Check(missed.Count == 0,
                  $"20 gathers with no STUN, each sealed one poll after Complete, hold every host candidate a second later holds ({20 - missed.Count} of 20{(missed.Count == 0 ? "" : $"; {Walked(missed[0])}, then {string.Join(" | ", missed[0].Arrived)}")})");
        }
        finally
        {
            foreach (var g in walks) g.Close();
            hostMp.Close(); hostMp.Dispose();
            foreach (var mp in guestMps) { mp.Close(); mp.Dispose(); }
        }
    }

    // ── WEBRTC, R1: THE ROWS END TO END, THE COURIER, THE PAIR PROXY (§3.3, §10.2, §10.4 R1) ────────
    // A SESSION FOR THE ROWS: both desks over a real Rendezvous.Pending, answering with drawn invites and
    // replies, recording what each row asked of it. R2's Net is the real one.
    sealed class Desk : Rendezvous.IHostDesk, Rendezvous.IGuestDesk
    {
        public Rendezvous.Pending Pending { get; } = new();
        public bool Full { get; set; }
        public int Proto = Net.Protocol;                        // what this desk's knocks carry
        public uint Mark = Rendezvous.Guest;                    // ...and whose they are
        public TaskCompletionSource<Rendezvous.Record> Hold;    // set: the next invite waits on it (a knock in flight)
        public int Asked;                                       // invites the listener asked for
        public readonly List<Rendezvous.Record> Invites = new(), Replies = new(), Answered = new(), Made = new();
        public readonly List<int> Hung = new();
        public readonly List<Rendezvous.Why> Refusals = new();
        // an invite from this desk's host, its entry Waiting
        public Rendezvous.Record Invitation(string row, uint guest)
        {
            var inv = VaryRecord(Rendezvous.Kind.Invite);
            inv.Proto = Net.Protocol; inv.Build = Game.Build;
            Pending.Add(new Rendezvous.Entry { Id = inv.Id, Row = row, Guest = guest, Stage = Rendezvous.Stage.Waiting });
            Invites.Add(inv);
            return inv;
        }
        public Task<Rendezvous.Record> Invite(Rendezvous.Record knock)
        {
            Asked++;
            return Hold?.Task ?? Task.FromResult(Invitation(Rendezvous.Address.Id, knock.Guest));
        }
        public void Hang(int id) { Hung.Add(id); Pending.Remove(id); }
        public void Replied(Rendezvous.Record reply)
        {
            Replies.Add(reply);
            if (Pending.Find(reply.Id) is { } e) e.Stage = Rendezvous.Stage.Linking;
        }
        public void Refused(Rendezvous.Record knock, Rendezvous.Why why) => Refusals.Add(why);
        public Rendezvous.Record Knock() => new() { Kind = Rendezvous.Kind.Knock, Proto = Proto, Guest = Mark, Build = Game.Build, Name = "Harbour8" };
        public Task<Rendezvous.Record> Answer(Rendezvous.Record invite)
        {
            Answered.Add(invite);
            var reply = VaryRecord(Rendezvous.Kind.Reply);
            reply.Id = invite.Id;
            Made.Add(reply);
            return Task.FromResult(reply);
        }
    }
    static bool IsRefusal(Rendezvous.Record r, Rendezvous.Why why) =>
        r is { Kind: Rendezvous.Kind.Refuse } && r.Why == why && r.Proto == Net.Protocol && r.Build == Game.Build;

    // THE COURIER (§10.2): the harness carrying codes as Discord would. Its FILES sit beside the project
    // (res://, globalized: the scratch copy both roles of a run share), one per code, named for the host's
    // listener port and the code's number.
    static string CourierFile(string kind, int port, int n) => ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath($"res://{kind}-{port}-{n}.txt");
    // ITS REWRITE, for the box: the code unpacked with the game's own codec, one IPv4 host candidate kept
    // with the box's address and the given port in place of this PC's, packed again. `real` is the port it
    // replaced: libjuice binds one socket on every address, so 127.0.0.1 and that port reach it.
    const string BoxHost = "127.0.42.1";
    static string Rewrite(string code, Rendezvous.Kind kind, int boxPort, out int real)
    {
        var r = Rendezvous.Find(code, kind) ?? throw new FormatException($"the courier found no {kind} code");
        var keep = r.Candidates.FirstOrDefault(c => c.Type == Rendezvous.Via.Host && c.Address.AddressFamily == System.Net.Sockets.AddressFamily.InterNetwork)
                   ?? throw new FormatException($"the {kind} has no IPv4 host candidate for the courier to keep");
        real = keep.Port;
        r.Candidates = new List<Rendezvous.Candidate> { keep with { Address = System.Net.IPAddress.Parse(BoxHost), Port = boxPort } };
        return Rendezvous.Encode(r);
    }

    // Frames with every row polled (R2's Net._Process), until `done` or `ms` pass.
    async Task<bool> Polling(Func<bool> done, int ms)
    {
        ulong end = RtcPair.Now + (ulong)ms;
        while (!done() && RtcPair.Now < end)
        {
            foreach (var p in Rendezvous.Paths) p.Poll();
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        return done();
    }

    // THE ROWS END TO END with dummy codes (no plugin needed): the address row over a listener on a port
    // the OS picks (never 27015: the main checkout's runs use it), the paste row through the courier's files
    async Task Rows()
    {
        Check(Net.DefaultPort == 27015 && Rendezvous.ListenFrom == 27015
              && Rendezvous.ListenPorts(Net.DefaultPort).SequenceEqual(new[] { 27015, 27016, 27017, 27018, 27019, 27020, 27021, 27022, 27023, 27024, 0 })
              && Rendezvous.ListenPorts(0).SequenceEqual(new[] { 0 }) && Rendezvous.ReadMs == 3000 && Rendezvous.KnocksPerMinute == 20 && Rendezvous.PickupMs == 500,
              $"the listener takes 27015, then 27016 ... 27024, then a port the OS picks; a record has 3 s to arrive, an address may knock 20 times a minute, the clipboard is read at most twice a second (§3.3 B, §3.4; {string.Join(",", Rendezvous.ListenPorts(Net.DefaultPort))})");
        // THE GUEST'S MARK: drawn once per process, never 0, outside the build's fingerprint
        var markField = typeof(Rendezvous).GetField("_guest", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static);
        uint mark = Rendezvous.Guest;
        markField?.SetValue(null, (uint?)(mark ^ 0x80000000u));
        int swapped = FingerprintNow();
        markField?.SetValue(null, (uint?)mark);
        Check(mark != 0 && Rendezvous.Guest == mark && markField != null && !markField.IsInitOnly && !markField.IsLiteral && swapped == Net.Protocol
              && typeof(Rendezvous).GetField(nameof(Rendezvous.Guest), BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static) == null,
              $"the guest's mark is drawn once per process, never 0, a property over a mutable field outside the build's fingerprint ({mark:x8}; swapped {swapped:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}{Moved(swapped)})");

        var host = new Desk();
        int from = Rendezvous.ListenFrom;
        var clipWas = Rendezvous.Clipboard;
        var files = new List<string>();
        Rendezvous.ListenFrom = 0;
        try
        {
            Rendezvous.Address.Open(host);
            Rendezvous.Paste.Open(host);
            int port = Rendezvous.ListenPort;
            await PasteRows(host, port, files);
            await AddressRows(host, port);
        }
        finally
        {
            Rendezvous.Address.Close(); Rendezvous.Paste.Close();
            Rendezvous.ListenFrom = from; Rendezvous.Clipboard = clipWas;
            foreach (var f in files) System.IO.File.Delete(f);
        }
    }

    async Task AddressRows(Desk host, int port)
    {
        // KNOCK, INVITE, REPLY: three joins as a player types the host's address -- IPv4, IPv6, a name --
        // in a varied order, all from one game process (one mark)
        var forms = new List<string> { $"127.0.0.1:{port}", System.Net.Sockets.Socket.OSSupportsIPv6 ? $"[::1]:{port}" : $"127.0.0.1:{port}", $"localhost:{port}" }
                    .OrderBy(_ => Rng.Randi()).ToList();
        var guest = new Desk();
        var joins = new List<string>();
        // one session serves both rows: the paste rounds' entries are on this desk already
        int inv0 = host.Invites.Count, pending0 = host.Pending.Count, hung0 = host.Hung.Count;
        foreach (var at in forms)
        {
            int replies = host.Replies.Count, invites = host.Invites.Count;
            var row = Rendezvous.PathFor(at);
            Rendezvous.Record got;
            try { got = await row.Start(at, guest); }
            catch (Exception e) { joins.Add($"{at}: {e.GetType().Name}: {e.Message}"); continue; }
            await RtcUntil(() => host.Replies.Count > replies, Rendezvous.ReadMs);
            var inv = host.Invites.Count > invites ? host.Invites[^1] : null;
            var entry = inv == null ? null : host.Pending.Find(inv.Id);
            bool ok = row == Rendezvous.Address && inv != null && Said(got) == Said(inv) && guest.Answered.Count > 0 && Said(guest.Answered[^1]) == Said(inv)
                      && host.Replies.Count == replies + 1 && guest.Made.Count > 0 && Said(host.Replies[^1]) == Said(guest.Made[^1]) && host.Replies[^1].Id == inv.Id
                      && entry is { Stage: Rendezvous.Stage.Linking, Row: "address" } && entry.Guest == guest.Mark;
            joins.Add(ok ? $"{at} ok" : $"{at}: got {Said(got)}; {host.Replies.Count - replies} replies, entry {entry?.Stage.ToString() ?? "none"}");
        }
        Check(joins.Count == 3 && joins.All(j => j.EndsWith(" ok", StringComparison.Ordinal)),
              $"by the address row a knock brings an invite, and the reply reaches the host's desk with the invite's id, its entry Linking: over IPv4, IPv6 and a name ({string.Join("; ", joins)})");

        // ONE PENDING ENTRY PER GUEST: each newer knock hung the older entry up through the desk; a knock
        // from another game process keeps its own
        var second = new Desk { Mark = guest.Mark ^ 0x80000000u };
        var secondGot = await Rendezvous.Address.Start($"127.0.0.1:{port}", second);
        await RtcUntil(() => host.Replies.Any(r => r.Id == secondGot.Id), Rendezvous.ReadMs);
        var mine = host.Pending.OfGuest(guest.Mark);
        var joined = host.Invites.Skip(inv0).ToList();
        Check(joined.Count >= 3 && mine.Count == 1 && mine[0].Id == joined[2].Id && host.Hung.Skip(hung0).SequenceEqual(joined.Take(2).Select(i => i.Id))
              && secondGot.Kind == Rendezvous.Kind.Invite && host.Pending.OfGuest(second.Mark).Count == 1 && host.Pending.Count == pending0 + 2,
              $"a newer knock from the same guest leaves one pending entry, the older ones hung up through the desk; another guest's keeps its own ({host.Pending.Count - pending0} pending, {host.Hung.Count - hung0} hung, {mine.Count} for the first guest)");

        // REFUSED, and read by the dialer: no room, another build; each refusal carries the host's build
        host.Full = true;
        var third = new Desk { Mark = guest.Mark ^ 0x40000000u };
        var full = await Rendezvous.Address.Start($"127.0.0.1:{port}", third);
        host.Full = false;
        var stranger = new Desk { Mark = guest.Mark ^ 0x20000000u, Proto = Net.Protocol ^ 1 };
        var build = await Rendezvous.Address.Start($"localhost:{port}", stranger);
        Check(IsRefusal(full, Rendezvous.Why.Full) && IsRefusal(build, Rendezvous.Why.Build) && host.Refusals.SequenceEqual(new[] { Rendezvous.Why.Full, Rendezvous.Why.Build })
              && third.Answered.Count == 0 && stranger.Answered.Count == 0 && host.Pending.Count == pending0 + 2,
              $"a knock is refused `full` when the host has no room and `build` from another build, each read by the dialer with the host's build in it, nothing made for either ({Said(full)}; {Said(build)})");

        // THE RATE, on a fresh listener: 20 knocks a minute from one address, the 21st refused
        Rendezvous.Address.Close();
        Rendezvous.Address.Open(host);
        int fresh = Rendezvous.ListenPort;
        var said = new List<string>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 21; i++)
        {
            var r = await Rendezvous.Address.Start($"127.0.0.1:{fresh}", guest);
            said.Add(r.Kind == Rendezvous.Kind.Invite ? "i" : r.Why.ToString());
        }
        Check(said.Take(20).All(s => s == "i") && said[20] == "Rate" && host.Refusals[^1] == Rendezvous.Why.Rate && host.Pending.OfGuest(guest.Mark).Count == 1,
              $"an address that knocks 21 times in a minute is refused `rate` the 21st time, each of the 20 before invited ({string.Join(",", said)}; port {fresh})");

        // CLOSED: a knock waiting on the host's desk when the listener closes is refused `closed`, and the
        // invite the desk makes after that is hung up
        Rendezvous.Address.Close();
        Rendezvous.Address.Open(host);
        host.Hold = new TaskCompletionSource<Rendezvous.Record>();
        int asked = host.Asked;
        var fourth = new Desk { Mark = guest.Mark ^ 0x10000000u };
        var closing = Rendezvous.Address.Start($"127.0.0.1:{Rendezvous.ListenPort}", fourth);
        bool waiting = await RtcUntil(() => host.Asked > asked, Rendezvous.ReadMs);
        Rendezvous.Address.Close();
        var closed = await closing;
        var late = host.Invitation(Rendezvous.Address.Id, fourth.Mark);
        host.Hold.SetResult(late);
        host.Hold = null;
        await RtcUntil(() => host.Hung.Contains(late.Id), 1000);
        Check(waiting && IsRefusal(closed, Rendezvous.Why.Closed) && host.Hung.Contains(late.Id) && host.Pending.Find(late.Id) == null && fourth.Answered.Count == 0,
              $"a knock waiting on the host's desk when the listener closes is refused `closed`, and the invite made after is hung up ({Said(closed)}; waiting {waiting}, late invite hung {host.Hung.Contains(late.Id)})");
    }

    async Task PasteRows(Desk host, int port, List<string> files)
    {
        int reads = 0;
        string clip = "";
        Rendezvous.Clipboard = () => { reads++; return clip; };
        // THREE ROUNDS THROUGH THE COURIER'S FILES: the host posts its invite as a message (and it is on the
        // host's own clipboard); the friend copies the whole message into JOIN; the friend's whole reply
        // message comes back, and the host's game takes it off the clipboard once the host copies it
        var friend = new Desk();
        var rounds = new List<string>();
        for (int n = 0; n < 3; n++)
        {
            var inv = host.Invitation(Rendezvous.Paste.Id, 0);
            string invFile = CourierFile("invite", port, n), repFile = CourierFile("reply", port, n);
            files.Add(invFile); files.Add(repFile);
            clip = Rendezvous.Encode(inv);
            System.IO.File.WriteAllText(invFile, Message(clip));
            string text = System.IO.File.ReadAllText(invFile);
            var row = Rendezvous.PathFor(text);
            var got = row == null ? null : await row.Start(text, friend);
            var reply = friend.Made.Count > n ? friend.Made[^1] : null;
            if (reply == null) { rounds.Add($"round {n}: the {row?.Id ?? "no"} row made no reply"); continue; }
            System.IO.File.WriteAllText(repFile, Message(Rendezvous.Encode(reply)));
            int before = host.Replies.Count;
            await Polling(() => host.Replies.Count > before, 600);
            bool leftInvite = host.Replies.Count == before;
            clip = System.IO.File.ReadAllText(repFile);
            bool took = await Polling(() => host.Replies.Count > before, 2000);
            var entry = host.Pending.Find(inv.Id);
            bool ok = row == Rendezvous.Paste && got != null && Said(got) == Said(inv) && leftInvite && took && host.Replies.Count == before + 1
                      && Said(host.Replies[^1]) == Said(reply) && entry is { Stage: Rendezvous.Stage.Linking, Row: "paste", Guest: 0 };
            rounds.Add(ok ? "ok" : $"round {n}: row {row?.Id}, joined {got != null}, own invite left {leftInvite}, reply taken {took}, entry {entry?.Stage.ToString() ?? "none"}");
        }
        Check(rounds.Count == 3 && rounds.All(r => r == "ok"),
              $"by the paste row, through the courier's files: the friend joins by the whole invite message, and the host's game takes the friend's whole reply message off the clipboard -- never its own invite -- its entry Linking (three rounds: {string.Join("; ", rounds)})");

        // ONCE, AT MOST TWICE A SECOND, ONLY WHILE SOMETHING IS PENDING: the last reply is not taken again
        // (the same text, then re-armed with its entry Linking), nor a reply to no invite; with nothing
        // pending, or the row closed, the clipboard is not read at all
        int replies = host.Replies.Count;
        ulong t0 = RtcPair.Now;
        int r0 = reads;
        await Polling(() => false, 1200);
        long span = (long)(RtcPair.Now - t0);
        int paced = reads - r0;
        Rendezvous.Paste.Open(host);
        await Polling(() => false, 600);
        clip = Message(Rendezvous.Encode(VaryRecord(Rendezvous.Kind.Reply)));
        await Polling(() => false, 600);
        bool never = host.Replies.Count == replies;
        Rendezvous.Paste.Open(new Desk());
        int r1 = reads;
        await Polling(() => false, 600);
        int idle = reads - r1;
        Rendezvous.Paste.Close();
        await Polling(() => false, 600);
        int shut = reads - r1 - idle;
        Check(never && paced >= 2 && paced <= 1 + span / 500 && idle == 0 && shut == 0,
              $"the clipboard pickup hands a reply over once, and neither a reply to a Linking entry nor one to no invite; it reads at most every 500 ms, and not at all with nothing pending or the row closed ({host.Replies.Count - replies} more taken; {paced} reads in {span} ms; {idle} idle, {shut} closed)");
    }

    // THE PAIR PROXY (§10.2's unknown): an in-process pair whose bundles cross as codes the courier
    // rewrote -- one IPv4 host candidate kept, its address the box pair's -- so each side knows the other
    // only by a box address, and every datagram between them crosses the box
    async Task Proxied()
    {
        var made = Box("POST", "/box/pair");
        if (!made.TryGetProperty("n", out var nAt)) { Check(false, $"the box makes a pair for the pair-proxy check ({made})"); return; }
        int n = nAt.GetInt32(), boxA = made.GetProperty("a").GetInt32(), boxB = made.GetProperty("b").GetInt32();
        int id = Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue), hostPort = 0;
        var carried = new List<string>();
        (string, string[]) Carry(Rendezvous.Kind kind, string sdp, string[] lines)
        {
            bool invite = kind == Rendezvous.Kind.Invite;
            var r = Rendezvous.Sdp.Strip(kind, sdp, lines);
            r.Id = id; r.Name = "Harbour8";
            if (invite) { r.Proto = Net.Protocol; r.Build = Game.Build; }
            string code = Rewrite(Rendezvous.Encode(r), kind, invite ? boxA : boxB, out int real);
            if (invite) hostPort = real;
            else Box("POST", $"/box/pair/{n}?host=127.0.0.1:{hostPort}&guest=127.0.0.1:{real}&seed={Seed}");
            var arrived = Rendezvous.Find(Message(code), kind);
            var rebuilt = arrived.Candidates.Select(Rendezvous.Sdp.Line).ToArray();
            carried.Add($"{kind}: {string.Join(" | ", rebuilt)}");
            return (Rendezvous.Sdp.Build(arrived), rebuilt);
        }
        var p = new RtcPair(id, carry: Carry);
        try
        {
            ulong t0 = RtcPair.Now;
            bool up = await RtcUntil(() => p.Connected, 10000, p);
            var st = Box("GET", "/box/stats").GetProperty("pairs").GetProperty($"{n}");
            int toHost = st.GetProperty("to_host").GetInt32(), toGuest = st.GetProperty("to_guest").GetInt32();
            Check(up && toHost > 0 && toGuest > 0 && carried.Count == 2 && carried.All(c => c.Contains($" {BoxHost} ", StringComparison.Ordinal) && !c.Contains('|')),
                  $"an in-process pair whose codes the courier rewrote to the box's address connects through the box, datagrams both ways ({(up ? $"{RtcPair.Now - t0} ms" : p.Stage())}; box pair {n}: {toHost} to the host, {toGuest} to the guest, {st.GetProperty("unregistered")} before its endpoints were known; {string.Join("; ", carried)})");
            if (!up) return;
            // THE BLACKHOLE (R4's watchdog leans on it): a packet sent while the box drops every proxied
            // datagram for 1 s arrives after it lifts, and the box counts what it dropped
            int dropped = Box("GET", "/box/stats").GetProperty("blackholed").GetInt32();
            ulong t1 = RtcPair.Now;
            Box("POST", "/box/blackhole?s=1");
            p.Host.TransferChannel = StreamChannel; p.Host.TransferMode = MultiplayerPeer.TransferModeEnum.UnreliableOrdered; p.Host.SetTargetPeer(p.Id);
            p.Host.PutPacket(BitConverter.GetBytes(p.Id));
            bool through = await RtcUntil(() => p.Guest.GetAvailablePacketCount() > 0, 10000, p);
            long after = (long)(RtcPair.Now - t1);
            int blackholed = Box("GET", "/box/stats").GetProperty("blackholed").GetInt32() - dropped;
            bool whole = through && p.Guest.GetPacket() is { Length: 4 } body && BitConverter.ToInt32(body) == p.Id;
            Check(whole && after >= 1000 && blackholed > 0,
                  $"a packet sent into a 1 s blackhole arrives after it lifts, and the box counts what it dropped ({(through ? $"{after} ms" : "never")}; {blackholed} dropped)");
        }
        finally { p.Free(); }
    }

    async Task WebRtcPairs()
    {
        // the number every friend's countdown shows, proved against the measured literal here; the
        // pair below reads the row
        Check(Link.ReplyWindowS == 30, $"Link.ReplyWindowS is the 30 s R0 measured (DESIGN.md: every delay to 60 s connected; {Link.ReplyWindowS})");
        if (_replyWindow != null)
        {
            var w = await _replyWindow;
            Check(w.connected && w.appliedAfter >= Link.ReplyWindowS * 1000L && w.upAfter >= 0 && w.upAfter <= 2000,
                  $"a host that takes the reply {Link.ReplyWindowS} s after the friend made it still connects, within 2 s of taking it (taken after {w.appliedAfter} ms, connected {w.upAfter} ms later; {w.stage})");
        }
        // the codec needs no plugin: it runs on every machine
        try { Codes(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"the codec checks threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        // nor do the rows end to end: dummy codes over a real listener and the courier's files
        try { await Rows(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"the row checks threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        // THE PLUGIN LOADED, asked by the literal names the spike read (SPIKE Q1), so the row is
        // proved against them here and every other check reads the row.
        bool loaded = GDExtensionManager.GetLoadedExtensions().Contains("res://addons/webrtc_native/webrtc_native.gdextension");
        Check(loaded && ClassDB.ClassExists("WebRTCLibPeerConnection") && Link.Available && Link.Plugin.Extension == "res://addons/webrtc_native/webrtc_native.gdextension",
              $"the WebRTC plugin loaded: webrtc_native.gdextension is registered and WebRTCLibPeerConnection exists ({string.Join(", ", GDExtensionManager.GetLoadedExtensions())})");
        if (!loaded || !Link.Available) return;              // every check below would only wait out its time

        // THE CHANNEL TABLE: every [Rpc] in the build, the harness's stream included (§3.2). S1's 11
        // rows -- Cosmetic Reliable, ten ordered streams -- and the harness's stream on 12, the next
        // number after the last row.
        var modes = Link.Channels().Select(v => (MultiplayerPeer.TransferModeEnum)(int)v).ToList();
        Check(StreamChannel == 12 && modes.Count == 12 && modes[0] == MultiplayerPeer.TransferModeEnum.Reliable
              && modes.Skip(1).All(m => m == MultiplayerPeer.TransferModeEnum.UnreliableOrdered),
              $"a session negotiates 12 channels above 0: Cosmetic Reliable, then 11 ordered ones, the harness's stream the last ({string.Join(",", modes.Select(m => m.ToString()[0]))}; stream on {StreamChannel})");

        // A CONNECTED PAIR, the id drawn as the host will draw it (2 .. 2^31 - 1)
        var a = new RtcPair(Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue));
        Check(a.Served == Error.Ok && a.ConnectedAtOnce, $"CreateServer is Connected in the same call ({a.Served})");
        ulong t0 = RtcPair.Now;
        bool up = await RtcUntil(() => a.Connected, 5000, a);
        Check(up, $"an in-process pair connects, the host offering, bundles sealed and swapped whole ({RtcPair.Now - t0} ms, id {a.Id}{(up ? "" : "; " + a.Stage())})");
        if (!up) { a.Free(); return; }
        // THE CODEC ON THIS PC'S OWN BUNDLES (§4.2, §10.4 R1): the live pair's offer and answer,
        // stripped to a code and rebuilt, equal the plugin's own byte for byte but the o= id
        string liveOffer, liveAnswer;
