        try { liveOffer = ThroughCodec(Rendezvous.Kind.Invite, a.Offer, a.OfferLines); } catch (FormatException ex) { liveOffer = "refused: " + ex.Message; }
        try { liveAnswer = ThroughCodec(Rendezvous.Kind.Reply, a.Answer, a.AnswerLines); } catch (FormatException ex) { liveAnswer = "refused: " + ex.Message; }
        Check(liveOffer == "" && liveAnswer == "",
              $"the live pair's offer and answer, through the codec and back, equal the plugin's own byte for byte but the o= id ({(liveOffer + liveAnswer == "" ? $"{a.OfferLines.Length} and {a.AnswerLines.Length} candidates: {string.Join(" | ", a.OfferLines)}" : liveOffer + " " + liveAnswer)})");
        // every row's data channel open at both ends -- 15: channel 0's three and the 12 above -- and
        // every one of them reliable whatever the row asked for (Godot writes maxPacketLifetime, the
        // plugin reads maxPacketLifeTime: §2.1), which is why each row has its own
        var hostCh = RtcChannels(a.Host, a.Id); var guestCh = RtcChannels(a.Guest, 1);
        Check(hostCh.Count == 15 && guestCh.Count == 15 && hostCh.Concat(guestCh).All(c => c.state == WebRtcDataChannel.ChannelState.Open),
              $"every row's data channel opens at both ends: 15 each ({hostCh.Count} host, {guestCh.Count} guest; ids {string.Join(",", hostCh.Select(c => c.id))})");
        Check(hostCh.Concat(guestCh).All(c => c.life == -1),
              $"every data channel is reliable: no packet lifetime on any of them ({string.Join(",", hostCh.Select(c => c.life).Distinct())})");
        // a packet on the highest row arrives on that row, both ways
        var got = new List<string>();
        foreach (var (from, to, target) in new[] { (a.Host, a.Guest, a.Id), (a.Guest, a.Host, 1) })
        {
            from.TransferChannel = StreamChannel; from.TransferMode = MultiplayerPeer.TransferModeEnum.UnreliableOrdered; from.SetTargetPeer(target);
            from.PutPacket(BitConverter.GetBytes(a.Id ^ target));
            await RtcUntil(() => to.GetAvailablePacketCount() > 0, 2000, a);
            if (to.GetAvailablePacketCount() == 0) { got.Add("nothing"); continue; }
            int peer = to.GetPacketPeer(), ch = to.GetPacketChannel();
            byte[] body = to.GetPacket();
            got.Add($"{peer}@{ch}:{(body.Length == 4 && BitConverter.ToInt32(body) == (a.Id ^ target) ? "ok" : "garbled")}");
        }
        Check(got.SequenceEqual(new[] { "1@12:ok", $"{a.Id}@12:ok" }),
              $"a packet on the highest row (12) arrives on it, host to guest and guest to host ({string.Join("; ", got)})");
        // WHICH ROW AN RPC RIDES, by name (§3.2's rows: Ships 2, Raiders 5, Boss 10; the harness's stream 12)
        int[] rode = { Link.ChannelOf("NetShipState"), Link.ChannelOf("NetRaiders"), Link.ChannelOf("NetBoss"), Link.ChannelOf(nameof(NetStream)), Link.ChannelOf("NoSuchRpc") };
        Check(rode.SequenceEqual(new[] { 2, 5, 10, 12, -1 }), $"Link.ChannelOf names the row an RPC rides: ships 2, raiders 5, boss 10, the harness's stream 12, none -1 ({string.Join(", ", rode)})");
        // THE BACKLOG (§3.8): what waits on a row's data channel beyond the SCTP socket's own send buffer,
        // which Link.Backlog cannot see (R1's first run put 1 MB, all of it inside that buffer, and read
        // 0). So 200,000 B at a time, in one frame with no poll between, until the row backs up (8 MB at
        // most: past that the plugin reports nothing); then it drains to nothing once the far side has
        // taken it all
        a.Host.TransferChannel = StreamChannel; a.Host.TransferMode = MultiplayerPeer.TransferModeEnum.UnreliableOrdered; a.Host.SetTargetPeer(a.Id);
        var burst = new byte[200000];
        int put = 0, waiting = 0, taken = 0;
        while (waiting == 0 && put < 40) { a.Host.PutPacket(burst); put++; waiting = Link.Backlog(a.Host, a.Id, StreamChannel); }
        bool drained = await RtcUntil(() =>
        {
            for (; a.Guest.GetAvailablePacketCount() > 0; taken++) a.Guest.GetPacket();
            return taken == put && Link.Backlog(a.Host, a.Id, StreamChannel) == 0;
        }, 10000, a);
        Check(waiting > 0 && drained && Link.Backlog(a.Host, a.Id, NetChannels.Ships) == 0 && Link.Backlog(a.Host, Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue), StreamChannel) == 0,
              $"Link.Backlog reads a burst waiting on row 12 once it outgrows the SCTP send buffer ({waiting} B after {put} packets of 200,000 B), and nothing once the guest has taken all {put} ({(drained ? "drained" : $"{taken} taken, {Link.Backlog(a.Host, a.Id, StreamChannel)} B still waiting")}); an idle row and an unknown peer read 0");
        // a guest's Close() is seen by the host within 1 s
        a.Guest.Close(); t0 = RtcPair.Now;
        await RtcUntil(() => a.HostLostAt > 0, 3000, a);
        Check(a.HostLostAt > 0 && a.HostLostAt - t0 <= 1000, $"a guest's Close() is seen by the host within 1 s ({(a.HostLostAt > 0 ? $"{a.HostLostAt - t0} ms" : "never")})");
        // hanging up on an id that is gone, and one never known, prints nothing (an engine ERROR line
        // would turn this run red)
        Link.Hang(a.Host, a.Id); Link.Hang(a.Host, a.Id); Link.Hang(a.Host, Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue));
        Check(!a.Host.HasPeer(a.Id), "Link.Hang on a gone id, twice, and on one never known: no peer left, and nothing printed");

        // the host's DisconnectPeer is seen by the guest within 1 s
        var b = new RtcPair(Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue));
        bool bUp = await RtcUntil(() => b.Connected, 5000, b);
        b.Host.DisconnectPeer(b.Id); t0 = RtcPair.Now;
        await RtcUntil(() => b.GuestLostAt > 0, 3000, b);
        Check(bUp && b.GuestLostAt > 0 && b.GuestLostAt - t0 <= 1000,
              $"the host's DisconnectPeer is seen by the guest within 1 s ({(b.GuestLostAt > 0 ? $"{b.GuestLostAt - t0} ms" : "never")}, id {b.Id}{(bUp ? "" : "; " + b.Stage())})");

        // a pending entry -- an invite whose reply never came -- hung up through Link.Hang is gone.
        // THE INVITE NEVER REACHES THE GUEST: a guest holding it reaches the host before any reply is
        // applied (P2) and starts its DTLS handshake, and freeing it mid-handshake has the plugin
        // print two ERROR lines ("DTLS handshake failed") that turn the run red when the timing
        // lands (seen once in two runs). The host's side, the one Link.Hang acts on, is the same.
        var c = new RtcPair(Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue), delivered: false);
        await RtcUntil(() => c.OfferSealed, 3000, c);
        bool pending = c.Host.HasPeer(c.Id);
        Link.Hang(c.Host, c.Id);
        Check(c.OfferSealed && !c.Pasted && pending && !c.Host.HasPeer(c.Id), $"Link.Hang takes a pending entry off the host (pending {pending}, id {c.Id})");

        // every signal handler ran on the main thread, inside a Poll()
        var all = new[] { a, b, c };
        Check(all.Sum(p => p.Handlers) >= 12 && all.Sum(p => p.OffMain) == 0,
              $"every WebRTC signal handler runs on the main thread ({all.Sum(p => p.Handlers)} handlers, {all.Sum(p => p.OffMain)} off it)");
        foreach (var p in all) p.Free();

        try { await Walks(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"the STUN walk checks threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        finally { Link.Servers = _servers; Link.StunFirst = 0; }
        try { await Proxied(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"the pair-proxy checks threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }

        // NOTHING LEAKS: three connect-and-close cycles, the first to warm up, the count after it
        // against the count after the other two. A leaked pair is 34 objects (two peers, two
        // connections, 15 channels a side); the 20 allowed is the hub's own churn, as below.
        async Task<double> Objects()
        {
            for (int g = 0; g < 2; g++) { GC.Collect(); GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers(); }
            for (int f = 0; f < 3; f++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            return Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectCount);
        }
        double objA = 0;
        var cycled = new List<string>();
        for (int n = 0; n < 3; n++)
        {
            var p = new RtcPair(Rng.RandiRange(2, int.MaxValue));
            bool ok = await RtcUntil(() => p.Connected, 5000, p);
            cycled.Add(ok ? "up" : $"DOWN ({p.Stage()})");
            Link.Hang(p.Host, p.Id);
            p.Free();
            if (n == 0) objA = await Objects();
        }
        double objB = await Objects();
        Check(cycled.All(x => x == "up") && objB <= objA + 20,
              $"three WebRTC connect-and-close cycles leave the object count flat ({objA:0} -> {objB:0}; {string.Join(",", cycled)})");
    }

    // ── VARIED GEOMETRY, REPRODUCIBLE ──────────────────────────────────────
    // A check tied to one spot is a check that can only find the bug at that spot. Vary draws
    // from a per-run SEED, printed at the top of the log, so every run covers a different
    // geometry -- and any failure comes back with `run.ps1 -Seed <n>`, which puts the same
    // numbers back. It is never used for a figure the check asserts, only for where and which way.
    private static readonly RandomNumberGenerator Rng = new();
    public static ulong Seed { get; private set; }
    static void SeedFrom(string[] args)
    {
        foreach (var a in args)
            if (a.StartsWith("seed=") && ulong.TryParse(a[5..], out ulong n)) { Seed = n; break; }
        if (Seed == 0) Seed = (ulong)(Time.GetTicksUsec() ^ 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15);
        Rng.Seed = Seed;
        GD.Print($"SEED {Seed}");
    }
    static float Vary(float lo, float hi) => Rng.RandfRange(lo, hi);
    static float VaryAngle() => Rng.RandfRange(-Mathf.Pi, Mathf.Pi);
    // a spot `radius` u around `at`, in any direction
    static Vector2 VaryNear(Vector2 at, float radius) => at + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(radius * 0.4f, radius);

    public override async void _Ready()
    {
        var argv = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs();
        string role = argv.FirstOrDefault() ?? "solo";
        SeedFrom(argv);
        if (role == "solo") { _partsAtStart = FingerprintParts(); _hashAtStart = FingerprintNow(); _replyWindow = ReplyWindowHolds(); }
        Settings.TestVolume = 0.05f; Settings.ApplyVolume();   // six windows, nobody listening
        NoRouterNoInternet();
        await Wait(0.3);
        try { if (role == "solo") await QuitResetsSector(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"sector reset test threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        try { if (role == "solo") await Select(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"select test threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        try { if (role == "solo") await SaveCoverage(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"select screen test threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        // EVERY ROLE ITS OWN CHARACTER. All six processes share ONE user:// -- run.ps1 wipes it
        // once for the whole set -- and only the solo role ever made a character, so the host and
        // the guests all converged on the same file with last-writer-wins. A check that read "the
        // guest's file" was reading the host's: it held name="Arena Host" and that host's credits.
        // Solo is excluded on purpose: it asserts it starts with no saved characters, and it makes
        // and deletes its own along the way.
        // Ids get the role too: an id is a millisecond stamp, guest and guest2 start together, and the
        // host holds a dropped pilot's place by that id.
        if (role != "solo") { Character.NewBlank(); Character.Id += "-" + role; Character.Name = role; Character.Peak = Unlocks.Top; Character.Save(); }
        // A SAVED BASE HAS TO SURVIVE ENTERING THE HUB. Hub._Ready builds the Yard, and the Yard
        // reads Character.Base* in its _Ready -- so if the character is loaded AFTER that, the base
        // comes up zero and the Yard's first-frame autosave writes the zeros back over the file.
        // Set here, asserted below, for every role.
        Character.NewBlank(); if (role != "solo") Character.Id += "-" + role;
        // EVERY WALL OPEN (Unlocks): a pilot that has reached the top level once, at level 1, so every
        // check that reads the pilot's level keeps its meaning. The wall checks set lower peaks themselves.
        Character.Peak = Unlocks.Top;
        Character.Name = role + " Base"; Character.BaseCredits = 7777;
        Character.BaseStock["ore"] = 55; Character.Save();
        string baseId = Character.Id;
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn");
        await Wait(0.4);
        // Credits, not ore: ore is live stock and the hauler starts loading from it within the
        // first half second, so an exact ore figure here is a check on the clock. Credits only
        // move when something is bought.
        Check(H?.Yard != null && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - 7777) < 1e-6 && H.Yard.Stock("ore") > 0,
              $"a saved base survives entering the hub ({H?.Yard?.Credits:0} cr, {H?.Yard?.Stock("ore"):0} ore of 55)");
        var stillThere = new ConfigFile();
        stillThere.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{baseId}.cfg");
        Check(Math.Abs((double)stillThere.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - 7777) < 1e-6,
              $"...and the hub did not write zeros back over it ({(double)stillThere.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0})");
        // An exception must end the run as a failure. Without this the async test
        // simply stopped, nothing called Quit, and the run idled to its time limit.
        try { if (role == "solo") await Solo(); else if (role == "fly") await Fly(); else if (role is "ahost" or "aguest") await ArenaMp(role); else await Mp(role); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"test threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        GD.Print($"  elapsed {Time.GetTicksMsec() / 1000.0:0.0} s");     // for sizing the runners' time limits
        GD.Print($"DONE {role} fails={fails}");
        // THE GAME'S OWN WAY OUT, not a bare Quit: what it waits for (sounds, router jobs) is
        // exactly what the runner then checks for -- "resources still in use at exit" and a crash.
        Game.Quit(fails);
    }

    // Hub.Sector is static and only Hub.GoTo ever sets it, so quitting to the main menu FROM THE
    // ARENA used to leave it on Arena: the next session skipped BuildWorld and dropped the pilot
    // into an arena with no base, no economy and a boss. The menu now ends the trip, whichever
    // route reached it.
    async Task QuitResetsSector()
    {
        Hub.Sector = Hub.SectorKind.Arena;
        Yard.TripClock = 123; Yard.TripCredits = 456;      // as a quit from the arena would leave them
        Music.CombatZone = true;                            // and so would this
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://MainMenu.tscn");
        await Wait(0.4);
        Check(Hub.Sector == Hub.SectorKind.Home,
              $"reaching the main menu ends the trip: the next session starts at home, not in the arena ({Hub.Sector})");
        // Yard._Ready APPLIES the trip snapshot, so leaving one behind overwrote the next
        // session's economy with it -- a previous character's, even.
        Check(Yard.TripClock == 0 && Yard.TripCredits == 0,
              $"the trip snapshot is dropped too, so a fresh session is not paid an abandoned one ({Yard.TripClock:0}, {Yard.TripCredits:0})");
        // Hub sets CombatZone on the way in; nothing cleared it on the way out, so quitting from
        // the arena left the combat track playing over the menu.
        Check(!Music.CombatZone, "the combat zone flag is dropped too: the menu is not scored as a fight");
        // AND THE SESSION. Hosted first, so this is not a check that passes because nothing was
        // ever online -- it is the difference between a session that ends and one that does not.
        // The menu builds a real PlayerShip now; a session surviving into it means the title
        // screen sends state on an RPC path no peer has.
        Net.I.Host(27123);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 && !Net.IsOnline; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        bool wasOnline = Net.IsOnline;
        // EVERY ORDERED UNRELIABLE STREAM ON A CHANNEL OF ITS OWN. Such a packet is delivered only if
        // nothing sent after it on its channel arrived first, and they all shared channel 0: over the
        // simulated internet a base report was thrown away because a ship report sent a millisecond
        // later overtook it, and a guest saw the hauler's hold start seconds late. Read off every Rpc
        // attribute in the game: each UnreliableOrdered one names a row of NetChannels, and no other
        // RPC is on that channel; the rows are distinct and fit the channels a session opens.
        {
            const BindingFlags every = BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly;
            var rpcs = typeof(Net).Assembly.GetTypes().Where(t => !t.Name.StartsWith("_"))
                .SelectMany(t => t.GetMethods(every).Select(m => (who: $"{t.Name}.{m.Name}", a: m.GetCustomAttribute<RpcAttribute>())))
                .Where(x => x.a != null).ToList();
            var rows = typeof(NetChannels).GetFields(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static).Where(f => f.IsLiteral)
                .Select(f => (int)f.GetRawConstantValue()).ToList();
            var streams = rpcs.Where(x => x.a.TransferMode == MultiplayerPeer.TransferModeEnum.UnreliableOrdered).ToList();
            var offTable = streams.Where(x => x.a.TransferChannel == 0 || !rows.Contains(x.a.TransferChannel)).Select(x => x.who).ToList();
            // ...and on the Hub, the one node at the same path in every world: a late report for the base
            // landed on a guest already in the arena and was "Node not found: Hub/Yard"
            var offHub = streams.Where(x => !x.who.StartsWith("Hub.")).Select(x => x.who).ToList();
            var sharing = streams.Where(x => rpcs.Count(y => y.a.TransferChannel == x.a.TransferChannel) > 1).Select(x => $"{x.who}@{x.a.TransferChannel}").ToList();
            int opened = (Multiplayer.MultiplayerPeer as ENetMultiplayerPeer)?.Host?.GetMaxChannels() ?? 0;
            Check(streams.Count > 0 && offTable.Count == 0 && offHub.Count == 0 && sharing.Count == 0 && rows.Distinct().Count() == rows.Count
                  && rows.All(r => r >= 1 && r + 1 < opened) && !rows.Contains(StreamChannel),
                  $"every ordered unreliable stream is the Hub's, with a channel of its own from NetChannels, shared with no other RPC, inside the {opened} a session opens ({streams.Count} streams on {rows.Count} rows; off the table: {(offTable.Count == 0 ? "none" : string.Join(", ", offTable))}; off the Hub: {(offHub.Count == 0 ? "none" : string.Join(", ", offHub))}; sharing: {(sharing.Count == 0 ? "none" : string.Join(", ", sharing))})");
        }
        Session.Sectors[99] = Hub.SectorKind.Home;      // the book is Session's now, not a private static of Hub
        // The pilot's own battleship has a Barrage Broadside III fitted (six volleys): the title
        // screen must fly the kit regardless.
        var rack = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Battleship)[4];
        Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Battleship)[4] = "bs_barrage_3";
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://MainMenu.tscn");
        await Wait(0.4);
        Check(wasOnline && !Net.IsOnline, $"reaching the main menu ends the session too (was online: {wasOnline})");
        Check(Hub.PeerSector(99) == null, "...and forgets which world each guest was in");
        await MenuDiorama();
        Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Battleship)[4] = rack;
    }

    // The loudest sample in a wav, 0..1. Reads the asset itself: a check on a level that reads a
    // number in the code would pass whatever the file contains.
    static double Peak(string path)
    {
        // THE FILE, not GD.Load. An imported AudioStreamWav is what Godot made of the asset --
        // the importer can trim, loop or resample it -- so measuring that answers a question
        // about the importer. The claim here is about the asset as shipped, so read the bytes.
        var d = FileAccess.GetFileAsBytes(path);
        if (d == null || d.Length < 45) return 0;
        int max = 0;
        for (int i = 44; i + 1 < d.Length; i += 2)     // 44: past a canonical PCM WAV header
        {
            // Sign-extended and negated by hand: Math.Abs picks its short overload here, and
            // Math.Abs(short.MinValue) throws rather than returning 32768.
            int v = d[i] | (d[i + 1] << 8);
            if (v > 32767) v -= 65536;
            if (v < 0) v = -v;
            if (v > max) max = v;
        }
        return max / 32768.0;
    }

    // Everything Character holds, as one string, ENUMERATED BY REFLECTION. A list written out
    // here by hand would be a list that stops being true the first time someone adds a field.
    static string Describe(object v) => v switch
    {
        null => "null",
        string str => str,
        System.Collections.IDictionary d => "{" + string.Join(",", d.Keys.Cast<object>()
            .OrderBy(k => k.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal).Select(k => k + ":" + Describe(d[k]))) + "}",
        // An array's ORDER is part of its value (Bought is indexed by upgrade); a set's is not.
        Array arr => "[" + string.Join(",", arr.Cast<object>().Select(Describe)) + "]",
        System.Collections.IEnumerable e => "[" + string.Join(",", e.Cast<object>()
            .Select(Describe).OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.Ordinal)) + "]",
        _ => Convert.ToString(v, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
    };

    // NonPublic too: a private static field is state as much as a public one (the batched save's
    // countdown is), and a public-only sweep would never see it. Persisted state does not have to be
    // public.
    const BindingFlags CharFields = BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static;

    static string DumpCharacter() => string.Join(";", typeof(Character)
        .GetFields(CharFields)
        .OrderBy(f => f.Name, StringComparer.Ordinal)
        .Select(f => f.Name + "=" + Describe(f.GetValue(null))));

    // ── the save carries everything the character IS, and only this build can read it ────────
    // ── EVERY ITEM IN THE GAME, WRITTEN OUT FOR THE OWNER TO READ ───────────────────────────────
    // version/ITEMS.csv, from the LIVE table on every run, because a sheet kept by hand is a sheet
    // that disagrees with the game the first time a line changes. One row per part: what it is,
    // what it needs, every stat it moves and by how much, and the words the player is shown.
    // EVERY HULL, EVERY NUMBER, FROM THE LIVE SHEET. Written like WriteItemSheet and for the same
    // reason: the owner approves a balance change against what the GAME computes, not against what
    // the source appears to say. Ships.cs cannot be read for this -- the carrier's helm figures are
    // expressions over ShipStats.CarrierTop, and ShipStats fills in a default for every row a class
    // does not state, so a static read of the table is wrong in both directions.
    void WriteShipSheet()
    {
        string Q(string x) => "\"" + (x ?? "").Replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
        var flyable = Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).ToList();
        var sheets = flyable.ToDictionary(c => c.Id, c => new ShipStats(c.Id));
        // the union of every stat any hull has, in the order the first hull to carry it lists it
        var ids = new List<string>();
        foreach (var c in flyable) foreach (var st in sheets[c.Id].All) if (!ids.Contains(st.Id)) ids.Add(st.Id);

        var head = new List<string> { "class", "abilities", "targets", "sustained_dps", "weapons" };
        head.AddRange(ids);
        var rows = new List<string> { string.Join(",", head.Select(Q)) };
        foreach (var c in flyable)
        {
            var sh = sheets[c.Id];
            var cells = new List<string>
            {
                Classes.NameOf(c.Id),
                string.Join(" ", c.Abilities.Select(a2 => a2.Id)),
                c.Targets.ToString(),
                sh.SustainedDps.ToString("0.00"),
                string.Join("; ", sh.DamageLines.Select(l => $"{l.Label} {l.Dps:0.00}{(l.Sustained ? "" : " burst")}")),
            };
            cells.AddRange(ids.Select(id => sh.All.Any(st => st.Id == id) ? sh[id].ToString("0.###") : ""));
            rows.Add(string.Join(",", cells.Select(Q)));
        }
        using var f = Godot.FileAccess.Open("res://version/SHIPS.csv", Godot.FileAccess.ModeFlags.Write);
        if (f != null) f.StoreString(string.Join("\n", rows) + "\n");
        Check(rows.Count == flyable.Count + 1 && ids.Count > 20 && f != null,
              $"every flyable hull is written out for review: version/SHIPS.csv, {rows.Count - 1} hulls over {ids.Count} stats from the live sheets");
    }

    void WriteItemSheet()
    {
        var rows = new List<string> { "id,name,slot,rarity,kit,fits,needs,percent,flat,blurb" };
        string Q(string s) => "\"" + (s ?? "").Replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\"";
        string Moves(Dictionary<string, double> d, bool pct) =>
            string.Join("; ", d.OrderBy(kv => kv.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal)
                               .Select(kv => $"{kv.Key} {(kv.Value >= 0 ? "+" : "")}{(pct ? kv.Value * 100 : kv.Value):0.##}{(pct ? "%" : "")}"));
        foreach (var i in Equipment.All.OrderBy(x => x.Slot).ThenBy(x => x.Rarity).ThenBy(x => x.Id, StringComparer.Ordinal))
        {
            var fits = Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready && Equipment.Fits(i, i.Slot, c.Id)).Select(c => Classes.NameOf(c.Id)).ToList();
            rows.Add(string.Join(",", Q(i.Id), Q(i.Name), Q(i.Slot.ToString()), Q(i.Rarity.ToString()), Q(i.Kit ? "kit" : ""),
                                 Q(fits.Count == Classes.All.Count(c => c.Ready) ? "every hull" : string.Join(" / ", fits)),
                                 Q(string.Join(" or ", i.Needs)), Q(Moves(i.Pct, true)), Q(Moves(i.Add, false)), Q(i.Blurb)));
        }
        using var f = Godot.FileAccess.Open("res://version/ITEMS.csv", Godot.FileAccess.ModeFlags.Write);
        if (f != null) f.StoreString(string.Join("\n", rows) + "\n");
        Check(rows.Count == Equipment.All.Count() + 1 && f != null,
              $"every item in the game is written out for review: version/ITEMS.csv, {rows.Count - 1} parts from the live table");
    }

    async Task SaveCoverage()
    {
        WriteItemSheet();
        WriteShipSheet();
        // THE SHEETS ARE WRITTEN, NEVER IMPORTED. Godot imports a .csv as a string table -- one
        // .translation beside it per column -- and the export preset takes all_resources, so without
        // version/.gdignore both sheets would ride in every release as 45 translations nothing loads.
        // The runner's --import has run by now: anything it made in version/ is on the disk here.
        var madeByImport = System.IO.Directory.GetFiles(ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath("res://version"))
                                              .Select(p => System.IO.Path.GetFileName(p))
                                              .Where(n => n.EndsWith(".translation") || n.EndsWith(".import")).ToList();
        Check(madeByImport.Count == 0,
              $"version/ is never imported, so neither review sheet ships as a string table: {madeByImport.Count} import outputs there ({string.Join(", ", madeByImport.Take(3))})");
        // The refit's arithmetic runs here rather than in the world: it works on a BLANK pilot, and
        // this is the one place that already throws pilots away and loads the real one back at the
        // end. Run where the hub starts, its NewBlank wiped the tutorial's hint state.
        RefitChecks();
        WallChecks();
        ChipChecks();
        GearLevelChecks();
        BossScaleChecks();
        BuildChecks();
        await RecyclerChecks();

        // Every class is touched first: Load() fills the loadout for ALL of them, so a character that
        // had only ever used one would differ after a round trip for that reason alone and say
        // nothing about saving.
        Character.NewBlank();
        foreach (ShipClass sc in Enum.GetValues(typeof(ShipClass))) Character.LoadoutFor(sc);
        Character.Name = "Roundtrip"; Character.Class = ShipClass.Carrier;
        Character.Main = new Color(0.11f, 0.22f, 0.33f); Character.Accent = new Color(0.44f, 0.55f, 0.66f);
        Character.Exp = 777; Character.Level = 5; Character.Peak = 7; Character.Points = 3;
        for (int i = 0; i < Character.Bought.Length; i++) Character.Bought[i] = i % (Progression.MaxPerUpgrade + 1);
        Character.Spent.Clear(); Character.Spent.AddRange(new[] { 1, 3, 1 });   // the order they were bought in
        Character.Spent.Clear(); Character.Spent.AddRange(new[] { 1, 3, 1 });   // the order they were bought in
        Character.BossCleared["ironhold"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1, 3 };
        Character.BaseStock["ore"] = 123.5; Character.BaseStock["salvage"] = 67.25; Character.BaseCredits = 4321;
        Character.BaseLevels[Economy.All[0].Id] = 3;
        Character.BaseInvested[Economy.All[0].Tab] = 900;
        Character.GearHold["chip_combat_1"] = 2; Character.GearHold["std_drive"] = 1; Character.GearHold["cv_elite_3"] = 1;
        Character.GearLevel["bs_rapid_1"] = 7; Character.GearLevel["cv_elite_3"] = 40;
        Character.GearLocked.Add("cv_elite_3"); Character.TourDone = true;
        Character.Unclaimed.AddRange(new[] { "cv_elite_3", "sh_bulwark_2" });
        Character.PaidKills.AddRange(new[] { 638000000000000001L, 638000000000000002L });
        Character.HintsSeen.Add("flight"); Character.HintsSeen.Add("raid"); Character.HintsOff = true;
        Character.Bonuses["hull"] = 0.25;
        string id = Character.Id;
        Character.Save(); await Wait(0.05);
        string before = DumpCharacter();
        Character.NewBlank();                                  // wipe it out of memory entirely
        bool loaded = Character.Load(id);
        string after = DumpCharacter();
        // A FIELD ADDED TO Character AND NOT TO Save() FAILS HERE, on the day it is added. That is
        // what "the tests adapt to what changed" has to mean to be worth anything: not a promise
        // to remember, a check that notices.
        Check(loaded && before == after,
              loaded ? (before == after ? "everything a character holds survives a save and a load"
                                        : $"a character field is not being saved -- before {before}  |  after {after}")
                     : "everything a character holds survives a save and a load (the load failed)");

        // THE ROUND TRIP ONLY COVERS WHAT IT SETS, and reflection over the dump does not change
        // that: a field left at its default is identical before and after whether it is saved or
        // not. This is the part that actually notices -- every public static field of Character,
        // against the list this test exercises. Add a field and this goes red until it is either
        // set above or named here as deliberately not persisted. Found the hard way: the base
        // economy fields were added, not saved, and the round trip passed.
        // Bought is a property now (J9c, the fingerprint): reflection sees the compiler's backing
        // field, not the name a player reads, the same way LastSaveRenamed's already does below.
        var exercised = new[] { "Accent", "BaseCredits", "BaseInvested", "BaseLevels",
                                "BaseStock", "BossCleared", "Bonuses", "<Bought>k__BackingField", "Class", "Exp",
                                "GearHold", "GearLevel", "GearLocked", "HintsOff", "HintsSeen", "Id", "Level", "Loadout", "Main",
                                "Name", "PaidKills", "Peak", "Points", "Spent", "TourDone", "Unclaimed" };
        // Not state a character carries: a path, and the compiler's backing field for a property
        // that reports how the LAST save went. Named explicitly rather than filtered by a pattern,
        // so a real field that happens to look like one cannot slip through.
        var notPersisted = new[] { "Dir", "<LastSaveRenamed>k__BackingField", "PaidKept", "SaveDelay", "_saveIn",
                                   "MaxBonus", "MaxStock" };   // the bounds a figure off disk is held to, not state
        var present = typeof(Character).GetFields(CharFields)
                                       .Select(f => f.Name).OrderBy(n => n, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray();
        var accounted = exercised.Concat(notPersisted).OrderBy(n => n, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray();
        Check(present.SequenceEqual(accounted),
              $"every field of Character is accounted for by this test (new: {string.Join(",", present.Except(accounted))}; gone: {string.Join(",", accounted.Except(present))})");

        // THE DEFAULT COLOURS: a grey hull and a white accent. A pilot still in an earlier default --
        // the pale blue, the blue after it, the gold accent -- never chose it: it loads in today's,
        // and a colour a pilot did choose (the accent here, at first) stays.
        Check(Character.Defaults.Main.IsEqualApprox(new Color(0.60f, 0.60f, 0.60f)) && Character.Defaults.Accent.IsEqualApprox(new Color(1f, 1f, 1f)),
              "a new pilot flies a grey hull (0.60, 0.60, 0.60) with a white accent");
        Character.Main = new Color(0.55f, 0.72f, 1.00f); Character.Save(); await Wait(0.05);
        Character.NewBlank(); Character.Load(id);
        Check(Character.Main.IsEqualApprox(new Color(0.60f, 0.60f, 0.60f)) && Character.Accent.IsEqualApprox(new Color(0.44f, 0.55f, 0.66f)),
              $"a pilot saved in the first default's pale blue loads in grey; the accent it chose stays ({Character.Main})");
        Character.Main = new Color(0.30f, 0.50f, 0.95f); Character.Accent = new Color(1.00f, 0.78f, 0.35f); Character.Save(); await Wait(0.05);
        Character.NewBlank(); Character.Load(id);
        Check(Character.Main.IsEqualApprox(new Color(0.60f, 0.60f, 0.60f)) && Character.Accent.IsEqualApprox(new Color(1f, 1f, 1f)),
              $"...and one in the blue that followed, with the gold accent, loads in grey and white ({Character.Main}, {Character.Accent})");

        // ── the format-2 fields, read from a file on the player's disk: sanitised, never trusted ──
        {
            var bad = new ConfigFile();
            bad.SetValue("id", "version", Game.Version); bad.SetValue("id", "name", "Tampered");
            bad.SetValue("gear_hold", "std_drive", 2); bad.SetValue("gear_hold", "chip_combat_1", -3);
            bad.SetValue("gear_hold", "basic_deflector", 0); bad.SetValue("gear_hold", "no_such_part", 1);
            bad.SetValue("loot", "unclaimed", "std_drive,no_such_part,cv_elite_3");
            bad.SetValue("hints", "seen", "flight,no_such_hint,unlock_chipslot_1"); bad.SetValue("hints", "off", true);
            bad.SetValue("loot", "paid", string.Join(",", Enumerable.Range(1, 40)) + ",not_a_serial,40");
            bad.SetValue("base_levels", "miner_count", 99);
            bad.SetValue("gear_level", "bs_rapid_1", 99); bad.SetValue("gear_level", "no_such_part", 5);
            bad.SetValue("gear_level", "bs_rapid_2", 0); bad.SetValue("gear_level", "bs_salvo_1", 3);
            bad.Save($"{Character.Dir}/tampered.cfg");
            bool tl = Character.Load("tampered");
            Check(tl && Character.GearHold.Count == 1 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("std_drive") == 2,
                  $"the hold keeps known parts with counts above zero ({string.Join(",", Character.GearHold.Select(kv => kv.Key + "=" + kv.Value))})");
            Check(tl && Character.Unclaimed.SequenceEqual(new[] { "cv_elite_3" }),
                  $"unclaimed loot keeps only real drops -- no kit, no unknown ids ({string.Join(",", Character.Unclaimed)})");
            Check(tl && Character.PaidKills.SequenceEqual(Enumerable.Range(9, 32).Select(i => (long)i)),
                  $"the kills paid: numbers only, each once, the last 32 kept ({Character.PaidKills.Count}: {Character.PaidKills.FirstOrDefault()}..{Character.PaidKills.LastOrDefault()})");
            // an unlock card's id (Unlock.Hint) is no row of Hints.All (D15): it must survive the
            // same load that drops a truly unknown id, or a wall crossed once is a card shown again
            // every time this file is loaded.
            Check(tl && Character.HintsSeen.SetEquals(new[] { "flight", "unlock_chipslot_1" }) && Character.HintsOff,
                  $"hints: unknown ids dropped, an unlock card's id kept, the off switch kept ({string.Join(",", Character.HintsSeen)}, off {Character.HintsOff})");
            Check(tl && Character.BaseLevels.GetValueOrDefault("miner_count") == 4,
                  $"a base upgrade level is held to its cap: 99 miners' levels read as 4 (up to 5 miners) ({Character.BaseLevels.GetValueOrDefault("miner_count")})");
            // GEAR LEVELS OFF DISK, like a claim off the wire: parts this build knows, held to 40, a 0
            // dropped -- and a rack line that moved to the destroyer keeps the level paid for it.
            Check(tl && Character.GearLevel.Count == 2 && Character.GearLevel.GetValueOrDefault("bs_rapid_1") == 40
                  && Character.GearLevel.GetValueOrDefault("dd_salvo_1") == 3,
                  $"gear levels off disk: known parts only, held to 40, a renamed part's level follows it ({string.Join(",", Character.GearLevel.Select(kv => kv.Key + "=" + kv.Value))})");
            // a pilot with all of it in memory, then a file that has none of it
            var bare = new ConfigFile(); bare.SetValue("id", "version", Game.Version); bare.SetValue("id", "name", "Bare");
            bare.Save($"{Character.Dir}/bare.cfg");
            Character.Unclaimed.Add("cv_elite_3"); Character.HintsSeen.Add("raid"); Character.PaidKills.Add(5);
            bool bl = Character.Load("bare");
            Check(bl && Character.GearHold.Count == 0 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0 && Character.PaidKills.Count == 0 && Character.HintsSeen.Count == 0 && !Character.HintsOff,
                  "a file without the hold, loot, kills paid and hints loads them empty, not as the last pilot's");
            // NewBlank leaves nothing of the pilot before it
            Character.NewBlank(); string fresh = DumpCharacter(); string freshId = Character.Id;
            Character.GearHold["std_drive"] = 1; Character.Unclaimed.Add("cv_elite_3"); Character.HintsSeen.Add("flight"); Character.HintsOff = true;
            Character.PaidKills.Add(5);
            Character.NewBlank();
            Check(DumpCharacter().Replace(Character.Id, "") == fresh.Replace(freshId, ""),
                  "a new pilot starts with an empty hold, no loot, no hints seen and hints on");
            // A PILOT'S FILE FROM BEFORE THE RESOURCE TABLE, written key by key with the names
            // the old build used. The stock's keys ARE the resource ids and the level keys ARE
            // the upgrade ids, so there is nothing to migrate -- including "mine_rate", whose id
            // is not its gatherer's prefix and which a tidier generated id would have DROPPED,
            // losing a level the player paid for.
            var old = new ConfigFile();
            old.SetValue("id", "version", Game.Version); old.SetValue("id", "name", "OldKeys");
            old.SetValue("base", "ore", 1234.5); old.SetValue("base", "salvage", 678.25); old.SetValue("base", "credits", 4321.0);
            old.SetValue("base_levels", "miner_hold", 3); old.SetValue("base_levels", "mine_rate", 2);
            old.SetValue("base_levels", "salvage_rate", 1); old.SetValue("base_levels", "salvager_hull", 1);
            old.SetValue("base_invested", "MINERS", 900.0);
            old.Save($"{Character.Dir}/oldkeys.cfg");
            bool ol = Character.Load("oldkeys");
            Check(ol && Math.Abs(Character.BaseStock.GetValueOrDefault("ore") - 1234.5) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(Character.BaseStock.GetValueOrDefault("salvage") - 678.25) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(Character.BaseCredits - 4321) < 1e-6
                  && Character.BaseLevels.GetValueOrDefault("miner_hold") == 3
                  && Character.BaseLevels.GetValueOrDefault("mine_rate") == 2
                  && Character.BaseLevels.GetValueOrDefault("salvage_rate") == 1
                  && Character.BaseLevels.GetValueOrDefault("salvager_hull") == 1
                  && Math.Abs(Character.BaseInvested.GetValueOrDefault("MINERS") - 900) < 1e-6,
                  $"a base written with the old key names loads unchanged -- [base] ore/salvage/credits, and every upgrade id including mine_rate ({Character.BaseStock.GetValueOrDefault("ore"):0.0} ore, {Character.BaseStock.GetValueOrDefault("salvage"):0.00} salvage, {Character.BaseCredits:0} cr, mine_rate {Character.BaseLevels.GetValueOrDefault("mine_rate")}, salvage_rate {Character.BaseLevels.GetValueOrDefault("salvage_rate")})");
            Character.Save(); await Wait(0.05);
            var rewritten = new ConfigFile(); rewritten.Load($"{Character.Dir}/oldkeys.cfg");
            Check(Math.Abs((double)rewritten.GetValue("base", "ore", -1.0) - 1234.5) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs((double)rewritten.GetValue("base", "salvage", -1.0) - 678.25) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs((double)rewritten.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - 4321) < 1e-6
                  && rewritten.GetSectionKeys("base").Length == Gathering.Resources.Length + 1
                  && Gathering.Resources.All(r => rewritten.HasSectionKey("base", r)),
                  $"...and saving it writes those same keys back: one per resource id, then the credits ({string.Join(",", rewritten.GetSectionKeys("base"))})");
            Character.Delete("oldkeys");
            Character.Delete("tampered"); Character.Delete("bare");
            Check(Character.Load(id), "(back to the round-trip pilot)");
        }

        // ── A FILE FROM BEFORE THE DESTROYER: the battleship's missile racks are the destroyer's now ──
        // Its battleship had a Salvo Rack III fitted; its hold had a Seeker Rack I, the old kit rack and
        // (by some route) a destroyer Seeker Rack I too; a Buster Rack II waited unclaimed.
        {
            var old = new ConfigFile();
            old.SetValue("id", "version", Game.Version); old.SetValue("id", "name", "Veteran"); old.SetValue("id", "class", 0);
            old.SetValue("equipment", "Battleship", "bs_heavy_2,std_drive,basic_deflector,reinforced_frame,bs_salvo_3,,,,,,");
            old.SetValue("gear_hold", "bs_seeker_1", 1); old.SetValue("gear_hold", "bs_missile_rack", 1); old.SetValue("gear_hold", "dd_seeker_1", 1);
            old.SetValue("loot", "unclaimed", "bs_buster_2,cv_elite_3");
            old.Save($"{Character.Dir}/veteran.cfg");
            bool vl = Character.Load("veteran");
            var vb = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Battleship);
            Check(vl && vb[4] == "bs_broadside" && vb[0] == "bs_heavy_2" && vb.Length == 11 && vb.Skip(5).All(x => x == ""),
                  $"an old battleship: its rack's slot now holds the broadside kit, the rest of its gear untouched ({string.Join(",", vb)})");
            Check(vl && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("dd_salvo_3") == 1 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("dd_seeker_1") == 2
                  && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("dd_missile_rack") == 1 && !Character.GearHold.Keys.Any(k => k.StartsWith("bs_")),
                  $"...the Salvo Rack III it had fitted is in the hold, as the destroyer's; the hold's racks are the destroyer's too, counts merged ({string.Join(",", Character.GearHold.Select(kv => kv.Key + "=" + kv.Value))})");
            Check(vl && Character.Unclaimed.SequenceEqual(new[] { "dd_buster_2", "cv_elite_3" }),
                  $"...and unclaimed loot is renamed in place ({string.Join(",", Character.Unclaimed)})");
            Character.Delete("veteran");
            Check(Character.Load(id), "(back to the round-trip pilot)");
        }

        // ── the build stamp ──
        Check(Character.List().First(x => x.Id == id).Version == 3 && Game.Version == 3,
              $"a character records the build that wrote it (version {Character.List().First(x => x.Id == id).Version}, game {Game.Version})");

        // A character from ANOTHER build: written by hand on disk, because the only other way to
        // make one is to be another build.
        var cfg = new ConfigFile();
        Check(cfg.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{id}.cfg") == Error.Ok, "the saved character reads back as a config file");
        cfg.SetValue("id", "version", Game.Version + 1);
        cfg.SetValue("id", "name", "FromAnotherBuild");
        string alienId = "alien";
        cfg.Save($"{Character.Dir}/{alienId}.cfg");
        await Wait(0.05);
        var alien = Character.List().FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == alienId);
        Check(alien != null && !alien.Playable && alien.Name == "FromAnotherBuild",
              "a character from another build is listed, and marked unplayable");
        string keptId = Character.Id;
        Check(!Character.Load(alienId) && Character.Id == keptId,
              $"loading it is refused, and it does not become the character the next save overwrites ({Character.Id})");
        // THE LAST RELEASE'S CHARACTERS: save format 1, written the way that build wrote them (its
        // per-class spares and all). Format 2 changed what a file means -- the gear hold, loot, the
        // tutorial -- so, by the owner's rule, they are listed greyed out and refused, not repaired.
        var v1 = new ConfigFile();
        v1.SetValue("id", "version", 1); v1.SetValue("id", "name", "FromLastBuild"); v1.SetValue("id", "class", 1);
        v1.SetValue("spares", "Carrier", "chip_basic");
        v1.Save($"{Character.Dir}/v1old.cfg"); await Wait(0.05);
        var v1slot = Character.List().FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == "v1old");
        Check(v1slot != null && v1slot.Version == 1 && !v1slot.Playable && !Character.Load("v1old") && Character.Id == keptId,
              $"a character from the last release (save format 1) is listed, marked unplayable and refused (format {v1slot?.Version})");
        // FORMAT 2, the build before six chip slots and the level walls: refused the same way, with
        // nothing migrated and nothing refunded (the owner's ruling: existing saves are disregarded).
        var v2 = new ConfigFile();
        v2.SetValue("id", "version", 2); v2.SetValue("id", "name", "FiveChips"); v2.SetValue("id", "class", 0);
        v2.SetValue("progress", "level", 9);
        v2.Save($"{Character.Dir}/v2old.cfg"); await Wait(0.05);
        var v2slot = Character.List().FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == "v2old");
        Check(v2slot != null && v2slot.Version == 2 && !v2slot.Playable && !Character.Load("v2old") && Character.Id == keptId,
              $"a character from save format 2 (five chip slots, no walls) is listed, marked unplayable and refused (format {v2slot?.Version})");
        Character.Delete("v2old");

        // ...and the select screen greys it out and says why, rather than failing on PLAY.
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://CharacterSelect.tscn"); await Wait(0.3);
        var sel = GetTree().CurrentScene as CharacterSelect;
        var row = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).FirstOrDefault(p => p.Name == "Row_" + alienId);
        var play = row == null ? null : Nodes<Button>(row).FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Play");
        var note = row == null ? null : Nodes<Label>(row).FirstOrDefault(l => l.Name == "Incompatible");
        Check(row != null && play != null && play.Disabled && note != null && note.Text == Game.IncompatibleNote,
              $"the row is greyed out, PLAY is dead, and it says why ({note?.Text})");
        var v1row = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).FirstOrDefault(p => p.Name == "Row_v1old");
        var v1play = v1row == null ? null : Nodes<Button>(v1row).FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Play");
        var v1note = v1row == null ? null : Nodes<Label>(v1row).FirstOrDefault(l => l.Name == "Incompatible");
        Check(v1play != null && v1play.Disabled && v1note?.Text == Game.IncompatibleNote,
              "...and so is the last release's character");
        // The playable one beside it is still playable: this greys the character that cannot be
        // read, not the screen.
        var mineRow = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).FirstOrDefault(p => p.Name == "Row_" + id);
        var minePlay = mineRow == null ? null : Nodes<Button>(mineRow).FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Play");
        Check(minePlay != null && !minePlay.Disabled, "the character from THIS build is still playable");

        // A DIRECT ENTRY (the editor's F6, the smoke test) with the OLDEST file from another build:
        // it takes the first character that can load. Taking the oldest regardless made a new
        // "Commander" on every direct entry while a playable one sat further down the list.
        cfg.Save($"{Character.Dir}/0alien.cfg");                  // "0" sorts before any timestamp id
        var listed = Character.List();
        string oldest = listed[0].Id, firstPlayable = listed.First(x => x.Id != "0alien" && x.Id != alienId).Id;
        Character.Id = "";
        Character.EnsureLoaded();
        Check(oldest == "0alien" && Character.Id == firstPlayable && Character.List().Count == listed.Count,
              $"a direct entry skips the oldest file (another build's) for the first PLAYABLE one, and makes no new one ({Character.Id}, {Character.List().Count} of {listed.Count} files)");
        Character.Delete("0alien");

        // ── THE BATCHED SAVE: written 2-3 s after the LAST call (the owner's loot rule), each call
        //    restarting the wait; at once on leaving or switching pilot; never a file of its own ──
        {
            Character.Load(id);
            int Disk(string part, string who = null) => (int)OnDisk("gear_hold", part, 0, who);
            int r0 = Disk("reinforced_frame");
            Character.Stow("reinforced_frame"); Character.SaveSoon();
            await Wait(1.9); int at19 = Disk("reinforced_frame");
            await Wait(1.2); int at31 = Disk("reinforced_frame");
            Check(at19 == r0 && at31 == r0 + 1,
                  $"a batched save is written 2-3 s after the call, not at once ({r0}: {at19} at 1.9 s, {at31} at 3.1 s)");
            Character.Stow("reinforced_frame"); Character.SaveSoon();
            await Wait(1.9);
            Character.Stow("reinforced_frame"); Character.SaveSoon();
            await Wait(1.9); int at38 = Disk("reinforced_frame");
            await Wait(1.2); int at50 = Disk("reinforced_frame");
            Check(at38 == r0 + 1 && at50 == r0 + 3,
                  $"every call restarts the wait: two pickups 1.9 s apart are one write, after the second ({at38} at 3.8 s, {at50} at 5.0 s)");
            Character.Stow("reinforced_frame"); Character.SaveSoon();
            Net.I.GoOffline(); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Disk("reinforced_frame") == r0 + 4, $"leaving writes a waiting save at once, even with no session ({Disk("reinforced_frame")})");
            Character.NewBlank(); Character.Name = "SaveSoonB"; Character.Save(); string bId = Character.Id;
            Character.Load(id);
            int sd0 = Disk("std_drive");
            Character.Stow("std_drive"); Character.SaveSoon();
            Character.Load(bId);
            Check(Disk("std_drive", id) == sd0 + 1, $"loading another pilot writes the waiting save to the pilot it belongs to, at once ({sd0} -> {Disk("std_drive", id)})");
            Character.NewBlank(); Character.Id += "-ghost"; string ghost = Character.Id;
            Character.Stow("std_drive"); Character.SaveSoon(); await Wait(3.1);
            Check(!FileAccess.FileExists($"{Character.Dir}/{ghost}.cfg"), "a batched save never makes a file for a pilot not yet saved");
            Character.Delete(bId); Character.Load(id);
        }

        // ── THE HANDSHAKE SEES THE TABLES: a gear, price or stat number that differs between two
        //    builds must refuse the session like any other difference ──
        {
            var parts = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>();
            typeof(Net).GetMethod("Fingerprint", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static, null, new[] { typeof(System.Collections.Generic.List<string>) }, null)
                       .Invoke(null, new object[] { parts });
            bool sheet = parts.Contains("sheet Battleship.hull=300") && parts.Contains("sheet Carrier.fighter_speed=352");
            bool gear = parts.Any(p => p.StartsWith("Equipment.All=") && p.Contains("Id=cv_elite_3") && p.Contains("fighter_speed:2"));
            bool prices = parts.Any(p => p.StartsWith("Economy.All=") && p.Contains("Id=miner_count") && p.Contains("BaseCost=400"));
            // a wall level (ability 2 at level 3, level_walls.md) and a boss gate (Auto-sell after boss 3) --
            // both moved out of hashed fields and into Unlocks.All, so the table itself must be hashed now.
            bool walls = parts.Any(p => p.StartsWith("Unlocks.All=") && p.Contains("Pilot/3/Ability/2")
                                      && p.Contains("Boss/3/Economy/0/hauler_autosell"));
            Check(sheet && gear && prices && walls, $"the build fingerprint covers the stat sheets, the gear table, the prices and the unlock table (sheet {sheet}, gear {gear}, prices {prices}, walls {walls})");
        }

        Character.Delete(alienId); Character.Delete("v1old"); Character.Delete(id);
        await Wait(0.05);
    }

    // ── the title screen ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // The menu used to be sprites and structs, self-contained so no gameplay change could break
    // it -- and the cost was that it could show something the game does not do. It flies the REAL
    // battleship now, so these checks are also a check on the battleship.
    async Task MenuDiorama()
    {
        var menu = GetTree().CurrentScene as MainMenu;
        Check(menu?.DemoShip != null && menu.DemoShip.Demo && menu.DemoShip.Class == ShipClass.Battleship,
              "the title screen flies a REAL battleship, not a picture of one");
        if (menu?.DemoShip == null) return;
        var ship = menu.DemoShip;
        Check(ship.Peak == 14, $"...nobody's pilot, so it waits for no level: every wall open, peak 14 ({ship.Peak})");
        // The spec's numbers as literals, never the menu's own constants: a check that reads the
        // constant cannot catch the constant being wrong.
        Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["broadside_cooldown"] - 5) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["broadside_volleys"] - 3) < 1e-9
              && ship.Stats["missile_mag"] == 0 && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(ship.WarpHop, 500f),
              $"the kit's three-volley broadside every 5 s, no missile, and a 500 u hop ({ship.Stats["broadside_volleys"]:0} volleys / {ship.Stats["broadside_cooldown"]:0} s / {ship.WarpHop:0} u)");
        // The ship has to be the SIZE it is in game. The menu had its own zoom for a while and the
        // battleship was half as big again on the title screen as it is the moment you press PLAY.
        // Compared with 0.9 as a literal rather than with Hub.DefaultZoom, so this also fails if
        // the game's zoom is retuned and the menu is left behind.
        Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(menu.CameraZoom, 0.9f) && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(menu.CameraZoom, Hub.DefaultZoom),
              $"the title screen shows the ship at the game's own zoom ({menu.CameraZoom:0.00})");
        // ...and the ENEMIES are the size they are in the game too, for the same reason. Compared
        // against the raiders' own lengths: the claim is equality WITH THE GAME, not a magnitude,
        // so the game's number is exactly the right thing to compare with.
        Check(menu.Foes.Where(f => f.Kind == MenuFoeKind.Heavy).All(f => Mathf.IsEqualApprox(f.HitRadius, Raider.HeavyLength * 0.3f))
              && menu.Foes.Where(f => f.Kind != MenuFoeKind.Heavy).All(f => Mathf.IsEqualApprox(f.HitRadius, Raider.LightLength * 0.4f)),
              "the title screen's enemies are the size the game's raiders are");
        Check(menu.Foes.Count(f => f.Kind == MenuFoeKind.Heavy) == 2
              && menu.Foes.Count(f => f.Kind == MenuFoeKind.Web) == 1
              && menu.Foes.Count(f => f.Kind == MenuFoeKind.Light) == 3
              && menu.Foes.All(f => Combat.Hostiles.Contains(f)),
              "heavies, a webifier and light fighters, all of them real targets the ship can shoot");

        // THE HULL COMES ROUND AT THE SHEET'S RATE. turn_rate is RADIANS a second -- the sheet says
        // so ("Rudder limit", rad/s) and PlayerShip.Steer caps its yaw with the figure itself --
        // and the menu read it as degrees and multiplied by pi/180, so the title battleship turned
        // 1.08 degrees a second instead of 61.9. It never came onto the foe it was shooting at, and
        // the warp dodge below, which waits until the bow is within 0.25 rad of its heading, almost
        // never fired: the scene's set piece. 1.08 as a literal from the class row, never the
        // menu's own reading of it.
        Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["turn_rate"] - 1.08) < 1e-9,
              $"the title battleship's rudder limit is the battleship's own 1.08 rad/s ({ship.Stats["turn_rate"]:0.000} rad/s)");
        {
            // Where it sits and which way it faces are varied; the RATE is what is asserted.
            double turnRate = -1; string turnWhy = "";
            // MEASURED BETWEEN SHOTS, AND NOT IN A WARP. A pending area shot turns the bow AWAY from
            // the impact instead, and a dodge ends in a 500 u jump that outlives the shot it dodged
            // (Place does not cancel it), so a sample across either reads the scene's other business.
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && (menu.AreaShotIn >= 0 || ship.Warping); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var turnFoe = menu.Target is { Alive: true } fighting ? fighting
                        : Combat.Nearest(menu.Foes.Where(f => f.Alive), menu.Station, f => f.GlobalPosition);
            if (turnFoe != null)
            {
                // WELL OFF ITS FOE, never dead away from it (dead astern is a knife edge: the shortest
                // way round flips sign frame to frame), between the station and that foe.
                var turnAt = menu.Station + (turnFoe.GlobalPosition - menu.Station).Normalized() * Vary(15f, 35f);
                float turnOff = Vary(1.6f, 2.6f) * (Rng.Randf() < 0.5f ? -1f : 1f);
                ship.Place(turnAt, Aim.Face(turnAt, turnFoe.GlobalPosition) + turnOff);
                // THE FASTEST FRAME, NOT THE AVERAGE. What the scene turns toward is ITS business: the
                // foes circle, its gun kills them, and it may take another -- one already near the bow
                // turns the hull a little and then only tracks it. The rudder's RATE is what this
                // guards (the degrees-for-radians bug turned it at a sixtieth), and any frame spent
                // coming round onto anything shows it: the fastest frame is the rate, whoever the foe.
                float prevRot = ship.Rotation; int turnFrames = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < 45 && menu.AreaShotIn < 0 && !ship.Warping; i++)
                {
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double dt = GetProcessDeltaTime();
                    if (dt > 0) turnRate = Math.Max(turnRate, Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(prevRot, ship.Rotation)) / dt);
                    prevRot = ship.Rotation; turnFrames++;
                }
                turnWhy = $"; the fastest of {turnFrames} frames";
            }
            // The hull eases into its rudder rather than snapping to the limit, so three quarters of
            // a second off a standing start reads a fraction of the sheet's 1.08 -- but a FIFTEENTH
            // of it, not a sixtieth: the conversion left this at 0.019 rad/s, and no easing accounts
            // for that. The sheet's own figure is asserted on the line above.
            Check(turnRate > 0.9 * 1.08 && turnRate < 1.08 * 1.02,
                  $"and the diorama turns the hull at that figure, not a sixtieth of it: {turnRate:0.000} rad/s ({Mathf.RadToDeg((float)turnRate):0} deg/s) of 1.08, where the degrees-to-radians conversion left it at 0.019{turnWhy}");
        }
        // HOLDING STATION. Pushed well off its mark first: a ship that was never going to move
        // cannot demonstrate that it comes back, which is the same vacuity trap as the boss lock.
        // BETWEEN SHOTS, and measured as ground CLOSED rather than as arrival: a dodge turns the
        // bow away and ends in a 500 u jump, so a window that spans one says nothing about the
        // autopilot. It dodges every 15 s now that it can turn at all, where the scene used to
        // stand still and never jump.
        // AT REST WITH ITS BOW AWAY FROM HOME, the way every dodge leaves it: the jump goes along the
        // keel, away from where it stood, and ends dead in the water. Left to the autopilot, a
        // capital with its mark astern crawled round its turning circle for over 20 s, and the title
        // ship never got back on station between two area shots; the push used to keep whatever
        // heading the scene had, so this passed or failed on the draw. Well off home, never dead
        // astern (a knife edge: the short way round flips sign), in any direction off station.
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && (menu.AreaShotIn >= 0 || ship.Warping); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var pushTo = menu.Station + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * 250f;
        float pushOff = Vary(2.3f, 3.0f) * (Rng.Randf() < 0.5f ? -1f : 1f);
        ship.Place(pushTo, Aim.Face(pushTo, menu.Station) + pushOff);
        double drift0 = ship.Position.DistanceTo(menu.Station);
        bool sawBroadside = false, wasWarping = false; int dodges = 0;
        // THE NEAREST IT GETS over a window that spans whole cycles, rather than the distance at
        // one moment: the diorama dodges every 15 s and each dodge ends in a 500 u jump, so the
        // reading at any single instant is whatever the scene was doing just then.
        double drift1 = drift0;
        var eight = GetTree().CreateTimer(25.0);                     // GAME seconds, as Wait() counts them
        while (eight.TimeLeft > 0)
        {
            sawBroadside |= ship.BroadsideVolleysLeft > 0;
            if (ship.Warping && !wasWarping) dodges++;
            wasWarping = ship.Warping;
            drift1 = Math.Min(drift1, ship.Position.DistanceTo(menu.Station));
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(drift0 > 200 && drift1 < drift0 - 120,
              $"pushed {drift0:0} u off station, at rest with its bow {Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(pushOff)):0} deg off home as a dodge leaves it, it comes round and flies itself home between shots ({drift0 - drift1:0} u of it closed, nearest {drift1:0} u; {dodges} dodge(s) in the window)");
        // Its own window: the broadside fires whether or not a shot is pending (the guns are outside
        // the dodge), and the station measurement above stops the moment one is called.
        var broadsideWatch = GetTree().CreateTimer(16.0);
        while (broadsideWatch.TimeLeft > 0 && !sawBroadside)
        { sawBroadside |= ship.BroadsideVolleysLeft > 0; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(sawBroadside, "the title ship fires its broadside at the foes in reach");

        // THE DODGE. An area shot lands in 8 s; it starts the jump with 4 to go and the warp takes
        // 3, so it is clear ONE SECOND before impact. Each number is checked, not just the last.
        menu.ForceAreaShot();
        // Wait for the shot to actually be CALLED. Reading the countdown one frame after asking
        // for it read -1 (no shot yet) and every measurement below was taken from that -1.
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 && menu.AreaShotIn < 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        double called = menu.AreaShotIn;
        double startedAt = -1, landedAt = -1;
        var before = ship.Position;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && menu.AreaShotIn > 0; i++)
        {
            if (startedAt < 0 && ship.Warping) startedAt = menu.AreaShotIn;
            if (startedAt >= 0 && landedAt < 0 && !ship.Warping) landedAt = menu.AreaShotIn;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(Math.Abs(called - 8) < 0.2, $"the area shot is called 8 s before it lands ({called:0.0} s)");
        // It begins with its OWN warm-up plus its own half turn to go -- 3 s and 2.9 s on a
        // battleship's 1.08 rad/s -- because the jump goes along the keel, so the turn is the aim.
        // A flat 4 s was enough only while the diorama read the turn rate as degrees and the hull
        // barely moved: at the rate it really turns it cleared the blast with 0.2 s to spare.
        // The jump begins once the bow is round, which takes anything up to a half turn -- so what
        // is fixed is the WARNING it allows itself (warm-up + a half turn + a second), and what
        // varies is how much of that the turn ate. The margin is never nothing, which is the point.
        Check(startedAt > 2.5 && startedAt <= 7.0, $"it starts the jump once the bow is round, inside the warning it allows itself ({startedAt:0.0} s)");
        Check(landedAt > 0.9 && landedAt < 4.5, $"and is clear with a second in hand at worst ({landedAt:0.0} s)");
        Check(before.DistanceTo(ship.Position) > 300, $"the jump actually moved it ({before.DistanceTo(ship.Position):0} u)");
    }

    // ── character select ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async Task Select()
    {
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://CharacterSelect.tscn");
        await Wait(0.3);
        var sel = GetTree().CurrentScene as CharacterSelect;
        Check(sel != null, "select screen loads");
        // EVERY RUN STARTS WITH NOBODY. run.ps1 deletes the whole user:// folder first, so this
        // has always been true -- but nothing said so, and a run that quietly reused yesterday's
        // character would look like a passing run with an unexplained failure in it somewhere.
        // Asserted here, before anything creates one.
        Check(Character.List().Count == 0, $"this run starts with no saved characters ({Character.List().Count})");
        var slots = Character.List();
        // start with one saved character, made the way a player would
        Character.NewBlank(); Character.Name = "OldTimer"; Character.Class = ShipClass.Carrier; Character.Save();
        await Wait(0.05);
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://CharacterSelect.tscn"); await Wait(0.3);
        sel = GetTree().CurrentScene as CharacterSelect;
        slots = Character.List();
        Check(slots.Count == 1 && slots[0].Name == "OldTimer", "a saved character is listed");
        Check(Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).Count(p => p.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Row_")) == 1, "one row shown");

        // Character.Save writes a temp file and renames it over the real one, so a second
        // instance sharing this user:// can never read a half-written character. Saving
        // straight onto the live path caused "ConfigFile parse error ... Unterminated
        // string" on roughly 1 smoke run in 3. Two consequences are observable from here:
        // a stray .tmp is not a character, and the next save consumes it.
        var tmpPath = $"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg.tmp";
        using (var stray = FileAccess.Open(tmpPath, FileAccess.ModeFlags.Write))
            stray.StoreString("[id]\nname=\"half written");
        Check(FileAccess.FileExists(tmpPath) && Character.List().Count == 1,
              "a half-written .tmp is never listed as a character");
        Character.Save(); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(!FileAccess.FileExists(tmpPath),
              "Save() goes through a temp file and renames it away, leaving none behind");
        // ...and it really RENAMED rather than falling back to writing over the live file. Both
        // paths leave the same thing on disk, so the check above passes either way; only this one
        // can tell. Windows' rename() refuses an existing destination -- Godot works around that,
        // but if it ever stopped, every save would quietly take the unsafe path.
        Check(Character.LastSaveRenamed,
              "the save was committed by the rename, not by the fallback that writes over the live file");
        // user://settings.cfg is written the same way and for the same reason: two instances share
        // one user://, so a rebind saved by one used to leave the other reading a torn file and
        // silently falling back to defaults.
        var setTmp = Settings.Path + ".tmp";
        using (var strayCfg = FileAccess.Open(setTmp, FileAccess.ModeFlags.Write))
            strayCfg.StoreString("[audio]\nvolume_idx=");
        Settings.Save(); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(!FileAccess.FileExists(setTmp) && Settings.LastSaveRenamed,
              "Settings.Save() goes through a temp file too, and commits it by the rename");

        // new character: cancel writes nothing
        Nodes<Button>(sel).First(b => b.Text == "NEW CHARACTER").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.1);
        var cc = Nodes<CharacterCreator>().First();
        Check(cc.IsNew, "NEW CHARACTER opens a creator in new mode");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 0 && Character.List().Count == 1, "Esc cancels a new character without saving");

        // new character: create
        Nodes<Button>(sel).First(b => b.Text == "NEW CHARACTER").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.1);
        cc = Nodes<CharacterCreator>().First();
        var name = Nodes<LineEdit>(cc).First(); name.Text = "Ironside"; name.EmitSignal(LineEdit.SignalName.TextChanged, "Ironside");
        Nodes<Button>(cc).First(b => b.Text.StartsWith("BATTLESHIP")).EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.05);
        Nodes<Button>(cc).First(b => b.Text == "CREATE").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.2);
        slots = Character.List();
        Check(slots.Count == 2 && slots.Any(s => s.Name == "Ironside" && s.Class == ShipClass.Battleship), "CREATE saves a second character");
        Check(Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).Count(p => p.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Row_")) == 2, "list shows both");

        // delete: KEEP leaves it, DELETE removes the file
        var old = slots.First(s => s.Name == "OldTimer");
        var row = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).First(p => p.Name == "Row_" + old.Id);
        Nodes<Button>(row).First(b => b.Name == "Delete").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.1);
        var dlg = Nodes<ConfirmationDialog>(sel).First();
        Check(dlg.Visible && dlg.DialogText.Contains("OldTimer"), "DELETE asks first, naming the character");
        dlg.GetCancelButton().EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Character.List().Count == 2, "KEEP deletes nothing");
        row = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).First(p => p.Name == "Row_" + old.Id);
        Nodes<Button>(row).First(b => b.Name == "Delete").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.1);
        dlg.GetOkButton().EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
        Check(Character.List().Count == 1 && !FileAccess.FileExists($"{Character.Dir}/{old.Id}.cfg"), "confirmed DELETE removes the file");
        Check(Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).Count(p => p.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Row_")) == 1, "row gone from the list");

        // play
        var iron = Character.List().First();
        row = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).First(p => p.Name == "Row_" + iron.Id);
        Nodes<Button>(row).First(b => b.Name == "Play").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.4);
        Check(H != null && Character.Name == "Ironside", "PLAY enters the hub as that character");
    }

    // ── single player ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // MY ship on every peer: "Ship_1" is only right offline and on the host (id 1);
    // on a guest it is the HOST's ship.
    PlayerShip Me => Nodes<PlayerShip>().FirstOrDefault(x => x.Mine);
    // THE TUTORIAL, as a test reads it: a hint is MET once it is showing, waiting, or seen
    Queue<string> HintQueue => (Queue<string>)typeof(Hints).GetField("_queue", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H.Hints);
    string HintShowing => (string)typeof(Hints).GetField("_showing", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H.Hints);
    bool Met(string id) => Character.HintsSeen.Contains(id) || HintQueue.Contains(id) || HintShowing == id;
    // the hold as part ids, one per copy, sorted; and what the host rolled for a character at the Mp kill
    static string HoldIds() => string.Join(",", Character.GearHold.SelectMany(kv => Enumerable.Repeat(kv.Key, kv.Value)).OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.Ordinal));
    static string RolledFor(string id) { var f = new ConfigFile(); return f.Load("user://smoke_drops.cfg") == Error.Ok ? (string)f.GetValue("drops", id, "?") : "?"; }
    void Unmeet(params string[] ids)
    {
        foreach (var id in ids) Character.HintsSeen.Remove(id);
        var keep = HintQueue.Where(q => !ids.Contains(q)).ToList(); HintQueue.Clear(); foreach (var q in keep) HintQueue.Enqueue(q);
        if (ids.Contains(HintShowing))
        {   // the card on screen too, as if it had finished: while it shows, Met() is true whatever the trigger does
            typeof(Hints).GetField("_showing", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H.Hints, null);
            Nodes<PanelContainer>(H.Hints).First(p => p.Name == "HintCard").Visible = false;
        }
    }
    // Over a point: the ship -- or, in stasis, its escape pod
    void FlyOver(Vector2 at)
    {
        var s = Me; if (s == null) return;
        if (s.Alive) { s.Position = at; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; return; }
        if (typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_pod", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(s) is EscapePod p) { p.Position = at; p.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
    }
    // class changes go through the creator path now (there are no class hotkeys)
    // A refit, ANNOUNCED: PilotChanged applies it here and tells every peer, so a multiplayer
    // scenario can fly one of the nine and have the other side see which hull it is. Offline,
    // SendIdentity returns at once, so the solo callers are unchanged.
    async Task SetClass(ShipClass c) { Character.Class = c; H.PilotChanged(); await Wait(0.1); }
    System.Collections.Generic.List<(Vector2 p, float age)> Smoke(Shot t) =>
        (System.Collections.Generic.List<(Vector2 p, float age)>)typeof(Shot).GetField("_smoke", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(t);
    // the three projectile classes are one flyer with a row each now (Shots.All)
    System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<Shot> InFlight() => Nodes<Shot>().Where(x => x.IsMissile);
    System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<Shot> Shells() => Nodes<Shot>().Where(x => x.Kind == Shots.Shell);
    System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<Shot> Slugs() => Nodes<Shot>().Where(x => x.Kind == Shots.Slug || x.Kind == Shots.Scrap);
    Vector2 Scr(Vector2 w) => GetViewport().GetCanvasTransform() * w;
    async Task Park(Vector2 at, float rot = 0) { var s = Me; s.Position = at; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; s.Rotation = rot; await Wait(2.5); s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }

    // The base menu builds a tab's contents only while that tab is shown.
    void ShowTab(Node panel, string tab)
    {
        var tb = Nodes<Button>(panel).First(b => b.Name == "Tab_" + tab);
        if (!tb.ButtonPressed) tb.ButtonPressed = true;      // fires Toggled -> the tab is built
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // FLY — not a check: a PILOT. It takes every class out in clear space, flies it, shoots with
    // it, presses everything on its bar with a target selected, and stands it in front of a wave.
    // Nothing here asserts a known answer: it MEASURES, and says so. The checks prove what we
    // thought to ask; this is for what we did not.
    //
    //   FLY   a measurement
    //   ISSUE a measurement that does not match what the class's own sheet promises
    //
    // `tools\smoketest\run.ps1 -Fly`. The three flyable classes of the line go first as a control:
    // an ISSUE against one of them is the instrument, not the class.
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private int _issues;
    private readonly System.Collections.Generic.List<string> _table = new();
    void Note(string s) => GD.Print("FLY   " + s);
    void Issue(string s) { _issues++; GD.Print("ISSUE " + s); }

    // everything about a ship an ability could plausibly move, in one line
    string Snap(PlayerShip s, string id)
    {
        ref var sl = ref s.Sl(id);
        return $"L{sl.Left:0.00} C{sl.Cool:0.00} O{sl.Own:0.0} N{sl.N} st{s.Statuses.Bits} "
             + $"w{(s.WingTarget?.NetId ?? 0)} k{(s.StrikeTarget?.NetId ?? 0)} d{s.TurretsOut} "
             + $"m{(s.Staggered ? 1 : 0)} hp{s.Hp:0}";
    }

    async Task Fly()
    {
        var spot = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 2400);          // clear of the belt, the wreck, the dummies and the lane
        Note($"twelve classes, one at a time, in clear space at {spot}");
        foreach (var cd in Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).ToList())
        {
            try { await FlyOne(cd, spot); }
            catch (Exception ex) { Issue($"{cd.Name}: flying it threw {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        }
        GD.Print("FLY   ── the table ─────────────────────────────────────────────");
        GD.Print("FLY   class        top u/s (sheet)   180 deg   main DPS (sheet)   12 s under a wave");
        foreach (var row in _table) GD.Print("FLY   " + row);
        GD.Print($"FLY DONE issues={_issues}");
    }

    async Task FlyOne(ClassDef cd, Vector2 spot)
    {
        await SetClass(cd.Id);
        await Wait(0.3);
        var s = Me;
        if (s == null) { Issue($"{cd.Name}: no ship after the refit"); return; }
        var S = s.Stats;
        GD.Print($"FLY   ── {cd.Name} ({S["hull"]:0} hull) ─────────────────────────────");
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e12);
        await Wait(0.3);

        // ── THE HELM: does it reach its own top speed, and how long to come about? ──
        s.Position = spot; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; s.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
        double top = 0;
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W);
