        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6; i++) { top = Math.Max(top, s.SpeedAhead); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        double want = S["max_speed"];
        Note($"{cd.Name}: top speed {top:0} u/s, its sheet says {want:0}");
        if (top < want * 0.95) Issue($"{cd.Name}: never reaches its own top speed ({top:0} of {want:0} u/s)");

        // 180 degrees, at speed, on the rudder
        float last = s.Rotation; double acc = 0, turnT = -1, c0 = s.Clock;
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.A);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && turnT < 0; i++)
        {
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            acc += Math.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(last, s.Rotation)); last = s.Rotation;
            if (acc >= Math.PI) turnT = s.Clock - c0;
        }
        KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.A); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W);
        if (turnT < 0) Issue($"{cd.Name}: cannot turn 180 degrees in 10 s on full rudder at speed");
        else Note($"{cd.Name}: 180 degrees in {turnT:0.0} s at {s.SpeedAhead:0} u/s");

        // ── THE GUNS: a hull that cannot die, held at half their range, six seconds on the trigger ──
        double dps = 0;
        if (S["main_count"] >= 1)
        {
            s.Position = spot; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; s.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.4);
            var at = spot + new Vector2(0, -(float)S["main_range"] * 0.5f);
            var mark = H.SpawnRaider(at, Enemies.Gunship); mark.Hp = 1e9;
            AimWorld(at); await Wait(0.6);
            double hp0 = mark.Hp, g0 = s.Clock;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6; i++)
            {
                mark.Position = at; s.Position = spot; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; AimWorld(at);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) { mark.Position = at; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }   // the last shells land
            double secs = s.Clock - g0, dealt = hp0 - mark.Hp;
            dps = secs > 0 ? dealt / secs : 0;
            Note($"{cd.Name}: main guns landed {dealt:0} in {secs:0.0} s = {dps:0.0} DPS at {at.DistanceTo(spot):0} u, its sheet says {S.MainDps:0.0}");
            if (S.MainDps > 0 && dps < S.MainDps * 0.5)
                Issue($"{cd.Name}: its main guns land less than half what its sheet promises ({dps:0.0} of {S.MainDps:0.0} DPS) at half their range");
            mark.TakeDamage(1e12); await Wait(0.2);
        }

        // ── THE BAR: press everything on it, with a target selected and enemies about ──
        s.Position = spot; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; s.Rotation = 0f;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { var r = H.SpawnRaider(spot + Vector2.Right.Rotated(i * 2.1f) * 300f); r.Hp = 6000; }
        await Wait(0.6);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Tab); await Wait(0.3);
        if (H.Selected == null) Issue($"{cd.Name}: Tab picked nothing with three raiders 300 u out");
        foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(cd.Id).Where(a => !a.Open && a.Id != "guns").ToList())
        {
            string before = Snap(s, ab.Id);
            s.UseAbility(ab.Id, H.Selected?.NetId ?? 0);
            await Wait(0.4);
            string after = Snap(s, ab.Id);
            string why = s.FailNote(ab.Id);
            if (why != null) Note($"{cd.Name}: {ab.Short} refused -- {why}");
            else if (before == after) Issue($"{cd.Name}: {ab.Short} did nothing and gave no reason ({after})");
            else Note($"{cd.Name}: {ab.Short} -> {after}");
        }

        // ── TWELVE SECONDS UNDER A WAVE: three webifiers and a gunship, nothing pressed ──
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e12);
        await Wait(0.3);
        s.Position = spot; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; s.Rotation = 0f;
        s.Restore(s.MaxHp, true, 0);                                   // full hull, whatever the bar just did to it
        await Wait(0.3);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) H.SpawnRaider(spot + Vector2.Right.Rotated(i * 2.1f) * 320f);
        H.SpawnRaider(spot + new Vector2(0, 760), Enemies.Gunship);
        double hull0 = s.Hp;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        double lost = hull0 - s.Hp;
        Note($"{cd.Name}: 12 s under three webifiers and a gunship -- lost {lost:0} of {s.MaxHp:0} hull, {(s.Alive ? "alive" : "DEAD")}");
        if (lost <= 0) Issue($"{cd.Name}: a whole wave on it for 12 s took nothing off its hull -- nothing could reach it");
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e12);
        await Wait(0.3);
        if (!s.Alive) { s.Restore(s.MaxHp, true, 0); await Wait(0.4); }

        _table.Add($"{cd.Name,-12} {top,7:0} ({want:0})      {(turnT < 0 ? "  --  " : $"{turnT,5:0.0}s")}   {dps,7:0.0} ({S.MainDps:0.0})     {lost,5:0} of {s.MaxHp:0}{(s.Alive ? "" : "  DEAD")}");
    }

    // ── A REFIT COSTS A LEVEL AND UNDOES THE LAST POINT ──────────────────────────────────────
    // The file records the ORDER points were spent in now, because `Bought` only ever said how
    // many of each a pilot owned -- so "undo the most recent" had nothing to read and a refit
    // could only ever have taken the dearest. Both paths are proved: a pilot with an order, and a
    // file from before there was one.
    // ── THE RANGE TO THE TARGET, under it and on the HUD line ────────────────────────────────
    // The HUD named what was selected and nothing anywhere said how far off it was, which is the
    // figure that decides whether a weapon reaches at all.
    async Task RangeChecks()
    {
        var far = H.Raiders.FirstOrDefault();
        if (far == null) { Check(false, "range readout: no raider to select"); return; }
        H.SelectTarget(far);
        // TWO frames: the first may resume before the Hub's own _Process has run, and the line
        // would then still be the one written for the previous target.
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        // Whatever is selected when the line is WRITTEN, not what this asked for: a tutorial hint
        // and the ship's own acquisition both pick targets, and the check is about the readout.
        var shown = H.Selected ?? far;
        double want = Me.Position.DistanceTo(shown.Position);
        var line = Nodes<Label>().Select(l => l.Text).FirstOrDefault(t => t != null && t.Contains("target: "));
        // Read the figure back rather than matching the string: the raider is under way, so the
        // line was written a frame before this measured it and the two round a unit apart.
        var said = line == null ? null : System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Match(line, @"\((\d+) u\)");
        Check(said is { Success: true } && line.Contains(shown.Label) && Math.Abs(double.Parse(said.Groups[1].Value) - want) < 30,
              $"the HUD names the target AND says how far off it is: {shown.Label} at {said?.Groups[1].Value ?? "nothing"} u, measured {want:0} u");
        H.ClearSelection();
    }

    // ── THE BUILD A PLAYER READS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // One check, about a trap in THIS game's protocol hash that a build string can fall into.
    void BuildChecks()
    {
        // THE PILOT IS NOT THE BUILD: nothing a run changes about the pilot enters the fingerprint. The
        // bought upgrades were a readonly int[] -- Plain to the fingerprint -- so every purchase moved it,
        // and a peer whose Net was first touched after a pilot loaded would have hashed that pilot's
        // purchases. Character itself DOES contribute a few parts -- a save path, a kept-kills cap, the
        // bounds a stat off disk is clamped to -- every one `const`, the same for every peer on this
        // build whichever pilot is loaded; named here rather than asserted away. What must never show
        // up is anything a PLAYER decided -- Bought chief among them -- asserted by construction (no
        // such part) and by effect (purchases made, the fingerprint is still the build's).
        var buildConst = new[] { "Character.Dir=", "Character.MaxBonus=", "Character.MaxStock=",
                                  "Character.PaidKept=", "Character.SaveDelay=" };
        var pilotParts = FingerprintParts().Where(p => p.StartsWith("Character.", StringComparison.Ordinal)
                                                      && !buildConst.Any(c => p.StartsWith(c, StringComparison.Ordinal))).ToList();
        var boughtWas = Character.Bought.ToArray();
        for (int i = 0; i < Character.Bought.Length; i++) Character.Bought[i] = Rng.RandiRange(0, Progression.MaxPerUpgrade);
        int bought = FingerprintNow();
        boughtWas.CopyTo(Character.Bought, 0);
        Check(pilotParts.Count == 0 && bought == Net.Protocol && typeof(Character).GetField(nameof(Character.Bought), BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static) == null,
              $"nothing about the pilot is part of the build's fingerprint: its bought upgrades are a property over a mutable field, and buying moves nothing ({bought:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}; {(pilotParts.Count == 0 ? "no Character part" : string.Join(" | ", pilotParts))}{Moved(bought)})");
        // THE FINGERPRINT NEVER HASHES ITSELF, and it is whole when it is taken: Net.Protocol is a property
        // set by Net's static constructor, after every Net static is set. As a readonly field initializer
        // it hashed itself as 0 and every Net static below it as unset, so it differed from every
        // fingerprint taken later in the same process (R1's first runs: 3724c77b against 7991f5f3).
        var selfParts = FingerprintParts().Where(p => p.StartsWith("Net.Protocol", StringComparison.Ordinal)).ToList();
        int now = FingerprintNow();
        Check(typeof(Net).GetField(nameof(Net.Protocol), BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static) == null && selfParts.Count == 0 && _hashAtStart == Net.Protocol && now == Net.Protocol,
              $"the build's fingerprint never hashes itself and is whole when it is taken: at the harness's start and now it is the build's ({_hashAtStart:x8} at the start, {now:x8} now, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}{(selfParts.Count == 0 ? "" : "; " + string.Join(" | ", selfParts))})");
        // A STRUCT ROW IS HASHED: any game value type or System.ValueTuple`N whose public fields are
        // Plain or delegates, readonly or not (a site's post) is written out field by field -- an array
        // of them was not hashed at all -- so one field of one row, moved, moves the fingerprint, and
        // put back it is the build's again
        var posts = Emplacements.PirateBase;
        int at = Rng.RandiRange(0, posts.Length - 1);
        var post = posts[at];
        posts[at] = new Post(post.Row, post.At + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(1f, 50f));
        int shifted = FingerprintNow();
        posts[at] = post;
        string postPart = FingerprintParts().FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("Emplacements.PirateBase=", StringComparison.Ordinal)) ?? "none";
        Check(shifted != Net.Protocol && FingerprintNow() == Net.Protocol && postPart.Contains("Post{At=", StringComparison.Ordinal),
              $"a struct row is part of the build's fingerprint: one pirate-base post moved moves it, put back it is the build's again (post {at}: {shifted:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}; {postPart[..Math.Min(90, postPart.Length)]})");
        // A MUTABLE STRUCT ROW IS HASHED TOO: StatusGuard and TurretSpec carry no IsReadOnlyAttribute
        // (a mount's gun has settable fields) -- StructRow used to demand it, so a StatusGuard[] and
        // an EmplacementDef's TurretSpec? printed as their bare type name no matter their numbers, and
        // two builds that differed only there still shook hands.
        int gi = Rng.RandiRange(0, StatusSet.Guards.Length - 1);
        var guardWas = StatusSet.Guards[gi];
        StatusSet.Guards[gi] = new StatusGuard { Status = guardWas.Status, Stat = guardWas.Stat, Share = guardWas.Share + Vary(0.05f, 0.4f) };
        int guardShifted = FingerprintNow();
        StatusSet.Guards[gi] = guardWas;
        string guardPart = FingerprintParts().FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("StatusSet.Guards=", StringComparison.Ordinal)) ?? "none";
        Check(guardShifted != Net.Protocol && FingerprintNow() == Net.Protocol && guardPart.Contains("Share=", StringComparison.Ordinal),
              $"a mutable struct row is part of the build's fingerprint: one status guard's share moved moves it, put back it is the build's again (guard {gi}: {guardShifted:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}; {guardPart[..Math.Min(90, guardPart.Length)]})");
        var baseDef = Emplacements.Of(Emplacements.RowOf(Emplacements.Base));
        var gunWas = baseDef.Gun.GetValueOrDefault();
        var gun = gunWas; gun.Damage = gunWas.Damage + Vary(1f, 50f);
        baseDef.Gun = gun;
        int gunShifted = FingerprintNow();
        baseDef.Gun = gunWas;
        string gunPart = FingerprintParts().FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("Emplacements.All=", StringComparison.Ordinal)) ?? "none";
        Check(gunShifted != Net.Protocol && FingerprintNow() == Net.Protocol && gunPart.Contains("Damage=", StringComparison.Ordinal) && !gunPart.Contains("Gun=TurretSpec}", StringComparison.Ordinal),
              $"the pirate base's mutable gun is part of the build's fingerprint: its damage moved moves it, put back it is the build's again ({gunShifted:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8})");
        // A VALUE-TUPLE ROW IS HASHED TOO: Hub.PracticeTargets and Hub.Outposts are
        // System.ValueTuple`N tables, a different assembly than the game's own -- StructRow only ever
        // looked at the game's assembly, so an array of them was skipped whole, not merely printed
        // wrong. One practice target moved, moves the fingerprint; put back it is the build's again.
        int pi = Rng.RandiRange(0, Hub.PracticeTargets.Length - 1);
        var targetWas = Hub.PracticeTargets[pi];
        Hub.PracticeTargets[pi] = (targetWas.number, targetWas.at + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(1f, 50f), targetWas.armed, targetWas.fighter);
        int targetShifted = FingerprintNow();
        Hub.PracticeTargets[pi] = targetWas;
        string targetPart = FingerprintParts().FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("Hub.PracticeTargets=", StringComparison.Ordinal)) ?? "none";
        Check(targetShifted != Net.Protocol && FingerprintNow() == Net.Protocol && targetPart != "none",
              $"a value-tuple row is part of the build's fingerprint: one practice target moved moves it, put back it is the build's again (target {pi}: {targetShifted:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}; {targetPart[..Math.Min(90, targetPart.Length)]})");
        // A ROW WITH A DELEGATE FIELD IS HASHED TOO: WaveCrew.Count is a Func<WaveBrief,int> --
        // StructRow used to demand every field Plain, so a WaveCrew[] printed as its bare type name
        // ("WaveCrew"), and the private static readonly rows built from it (Waves.Patrol, Waves.HuntPin)
        // never entered the fingerprint at all. One crew's Step moved, moves it; put back it is the
        // build's again, and the printed row shows the struct written out, not a bare type name.
        var w = Waves.All.First(x => x.Id == "raid");
        int ci = Rng.RandiRange(0, w.Crew.Length - 1);
        var c = w.Crew[ci];
        w.Crew[ci] = new WaveCrew(c.Kind, c.Way, c.Nth, c.Count, c.At, c.Step + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(1f, 50f));
        int crewShifted = FingerprintNow();
        string crewPart = FingerprintParts().FirstOrDefault(p => p.StartsWith("Waves.All=", StringComparison.Ordinal)) ?? "none";
        try
        {
            Check(crewShifted != Net.Protocol && crewPart.Contains("WaveCrew{", StringComparison.Ordinal),
                  $"a row with a delegate field is part of the build's fingerprint: one wave crew's step moved moves it, and it is written out, not bare ({crewShifted:x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8}; {crewPart[..Math.Min(90, crewPart.Length)]})");
        }
        finally { w.Crew[ci] = c; }
        Check(FingerprintNow() == Net.Protocol,
              $"a wave crew row put back is the build's fingerprint again ({FingerprintNow():x8}, the build's {Net.Protocol:x8})");
        // Net.Fingerprint() folds in every static field of every null-namespace type that is
        // IsLiteral or IsInitOnly with a Plain field type -- and string is Plain. A
        // `public static readonly string Build` would therefore enter the hash two peers are
        // matched on, and two BYTE-IDENTICAL builds packed a minute apart would refuse each other
        // at the handshake. Asserted STRUCTURALLY, the way the trap is laid: the member must be a
        // property, and the static field behind it must be neither literal nor init-only.
        var buildProp = typeof(Game).GetProperty("Build", BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static);
        var buildFields = typeof(Game).GetFields(BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static)
                                      .Where(f => f.FieldType == typeof(string) && (f.IsLiteral || f.IsInitOnly)).ToList();
        Check(buildProp != null && buildProp.PropertyType == typeof(string)
              && typeof(Game).GetField("Build", BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static) == null
              && buildFields.All(f => f.Name != "_build")
              && Game.Build == "dev",
              $"the build a player reads is a property over a mutable static, so it cannot enter the protocol hash two peers are matched on -- and outside a release it says so ({Game.Build}; {buildFields.Count} frozen strings on Game, none of them the build)");
    }

    // ── A BOSS KEEPS UP WITH THE PILOT IT IS FIGHTING ───────────────────────────────────────────
    // Its hull and damage have always climbed 10% a level; its WIND-UPS and its REACH never moved,
    // so a pilot whose gear and points lengthen its own reach every level would fight each boss
    // from further out and dodge it more easily than the last. One percent a level, compounding:
    // quicker to wind up, faster in flight, longer-armed. The burn of a beam and how often that
    // burn is judged are left alone, because those are the damage and it has a scale of its own.
    void BossScaleChecks()
    {
        var row = Missions.ForLevel(1).Moves.First(m => m.Windup > 0 && m.Reach > 0);
        Check(Math.Abs(Missions.Quicken(1) - 1.0) < 1e-9
              && Math.Abs(Missions.Quicken(2) - 1.01) < 1e-9
              && Math.Abs(Missions.Quicken(11) - Math.Pow(1.01, 10)) < 1e-9
              && Math.Abs(Missions.Quicken(51) - Math.Pow(1.01, 50)) < 1e-9,
              $"a boss gains 1% a level on its wind-ups and its reach: x{Missions.Quicken(2):0.000} at level 2, x{Missions.Quicken(11):0.000} at 11, x{Missions.Quicken(51):0.000} at 51");

    }

    // ── THE RECYCLER: a hold turned back into salvage, and a lock so it cannot eat what you fly ──
    // A part could not be got rid of at all before this: a hold filled with commons a pilot would
    // never fit again, and salvage had nothing to buy but a refit and gear levels.
    async Task RecyclerChecks()
    {
        var yard = H.Yard;
        // THE RECYCLER BELONGS TO THE EQUIPMENT BASE, and serves only a pilot over it and calm
        // (Landmarks.Serves). The ship is set down on the pad at a seeded spot with its combat clock
        // run out, for everything below, and put back after.
        var me = Me; if (me == null) { Check(false, "the recycler checks need a ship to set down on the equipment base"); return; }
        var keepAt = me.Position; var pad = Landmarks.ById("equipment");
        me.Position = pad.At + new Vector2(Vary(-0.8f, 0.8f) * pad.Half.X, Vary(-0.8f, 0.8f) * pad.Half.Y); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_combatT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0);
        Character.GearHold.Clear(); Character.GearLocked.Clear();
        Character.Stow("bs_rapid_1"); Character.Stow("bs_rapid_1"); Character.Stow("cv_elite_3");
        yard.SetStock("salvage", 0);

        // A KIT PART IS NOT LOOT and is refused outright; a LOCKED part is refused too.
        Character.Stow("bs_main_battery");
        bool kitRefused = !yard.QueueScrap("bs_main_battery");
        Character.ToggleGearLock("cv_elite_3");
        bool lockedRefused = !yard.QueueScrap("cv_elite_3") && Character.GearLocked.Contains("cv_elite_3");

        // QUEUED: out of the hold at once, so it cannot be fitted or queued twice while it waits.
        bool queued = yard.QueueScrap("bs_rapid_1");
        bool goneFromHold = Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("bs_rapid_1") == 1 && yard.ScrapPending == 1;
        // ...and TAKE BACK puts it straight back.
        bool back = yard.UnqueueScrap("bs_rapid_1") && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("bs_rapid_1") == 2 && yard.ScrapPending == 0;
        Check(kitRefused && lockedRefused && queued && goneFromHold && back,
              $"the recycler takes loot and nothing else: a hull's own mount refused, a locked part refused, a queued part out of the hold at once and back again when taken back ({yard.ScrapPending} queued)");

        // IT PAYS, one part every Economy.ScrapEvery, in salvage, by rarity.
        // ONE EPIC, because the pile will not hold still: the salvagers fill it and the hauler
        // takes loads off it while this runs, so the payment has to be big enough to read through
        // that. 600 against a fleet that moves tens either way in five seconds.
        Character.ToggleGearLock("cv_elite_3");                      // unlocked, to be scrapped
        bool epicQueued = yard.QueueScrap("cv_elite_3");
        double paid0 = yard.Stock("salvage");
        await Wait(Economy.ScrapEvery + 0.6);
        double paid = yard.Stock("salvage") - paid0;
        Check(epicQueued && paid > 450 && paid < 750 && yard.ScrapPending == 0
              && Math.Abs(Economy.ScrapValue(Rarity.Common) - 100) < 1e-6
              && Math.Abs(Economy.ScrapValue(Rarity.Rare) - 250) < 1e-6
              && Math.Abs(Economy.ScrapValue(Rarity.Epic) - 600) < 1e-6,
              $"it chews through the queue one part every {Economy.ScrapEvery:0} s and pays by rarity -- an epic is 600 ({paid:0} into a pile the fleet is also working), a rare 250, a common 100");
        Character.ToggleGearLock("cv_elite_3");                      // ...and locked again for the round trip below
        Character.Stow("cv_elite_3");

        // A LOCK IS THE PART ID'S, like its level, and it survives a round trip to disk.
        string keepId = Character.Id;
        Character.Id = "recycler"; Character.Save();
        Character.GearLocked.Clear();
        bool loaded = Character.Load("recycler") && Character.GearLocked.Contains("cv_elite_3");
        Character.Delete("recycler"); Character.Id = keepId;
        Check(loaded, $"a lock belongs to the part id and comes back off disk ({string.Join(",", Character.GearLocked)})");
        Character.GearHold.Clear(); Character.GearLocked.Clear();
        me.Position = keepAt; me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
    }

    // ── THE LANES: four courier runs to the outposts, a quarter of the passive income each ──
    // Everything asserted here is a literal from the owner's words: four lanes, 25% each, both
    // directions, a quarter off for one blockade and a half for two, and the gunship's own 42
    // coming back the other way. Where things STAND is varied (VaryNear); what they are worth is
    // not.
    async Task LaneChecks()
    {
        var Y = H.Yard;
        var lv = (System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard)
                 .GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y);
        var cdF = typeof(LaneGun).GetField("_cd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
        double keepOre = Y.Stock("ore"), keepCredits = Y.Credits;
        var keepMe = Me.Position;
        // clear of every lane's stand by more than a raider's 2000 u notice, so nothing a check
        // puts on a lane comes after the pilot instead of holding it
        var park = VaryNear(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(-700, 700), 150f);
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
        Me.Position = park; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        await Wait(0.3);
        // 100 units delivered, and what the base is actually PAID for them. Yard.Deposit is the
        // one place the lanes are felt, and this is read in the SAME frame it is called, so the
        // fleet's own deliveries cannot move the figure.
        double Paid() { Y.SetStock("ore", 0); Y.Deposit("ore", 100); return Y.Stock("ore"); }

        // ── THE TABLE: four lanes, base to outpost, a quarter each ──────────────────────────
        Check(Lanes.All.Length == 4 && Lanes.All.All(l => Math.Abs(l.Share - 0.25) < 1e-9)
              && Math.Abs(Lanes.All.Sum(l => l.Share) - 1.0) < 1e-9
              && Enumerable.Range(0, Lanes.All.Length).All(i => Lanes.Ends(i).near == Hub.BasePos
                                                                && Lanes.Ends(i).far == Hub.Outposts[Lanes.All[i].Outpost].at)
              && Enumerable.Range(0, Lanes.All.Length).Select(Lanes.Name).SequenceEqual(new[] { "SE", "NE", "NW", "SW" }),
              $"four lanes, each from the base to one outpost, carrying a quarter of the passive income and a whole between them ({string.Join(", ", Enumerable.Range(0, Lanes.All.Length).Select(i => $"{Lanes.Name(i)} {Lanes.All[i].Share:0.00}"))})");

        // ── A COURIER DOCKS WHERE THE FLEET DOES ────────────────────────────────────────────
        // The owner: "let the trade ships from outposts share the landing zones with the miners and
        // salvagers. Not stopping early awkwardly." Every courier on every lane -- all four
        // bearings, every run -- is put the same VARIED time short of the end of its approach, one
        // of each pair to its base arm and the other to its outpost's clamp, seen to be flying, and
        // left to fly it in real frames. Where each ENDS is asserted: its centre within 50 u of the
        // pad's, its nose within 10 u of the open face, and pointing in through it within 5 degrees.
        var drones = Nodes<Courier>().ToList();
        var c0 = drones.FirstOrDefault(c => c.Lane == 0);
        Check(c0 != null && drones.Count == Lanes.All.Sum(l => l.Drones) && drones.Count(c => c.Lane == 0) == 2,
              $"every lane has its couriers ({drones.Count} of {Lanes.All.Sum(l => l.Drones)} built)");
        // THE DOCKS STAND WHERE THEY WERE MEASURED, proved here against literals so the checks below
        // may read the rows. The base's five arms did not move when they became rows of Docks.BaseArms:
        // each index on the spot it was measured at, in the order Yard.NetState sends an arm's index
        // down the wire. An outpost carries four clamps, one on each corner, opening outward. And
        // every lane docks at the arm on its own side of the base and the clamp on its outpost's
        // corner nearest the base. WHICH PLACE CARRIES WHICH DOCKS is its Landmark row, reached by
        // id: the base the five arms (the Yard's own, in the same order), each of the four outposts
        // the four clamps where it stands, and no other place any.
        var armWas = new[] { new Vector2(0f, -195.9f), new Vector2(-169.5f, -97.9f), new Vector2(169.5f, -97.9f),
                             new Vector2(-169.5f, 97.9f), new Vector2(169.5f, 97.9f) };
        var clampAt = new[] { new Vector2(-43.1f, -57.2f), new Vector2(43.1f, -57.2f), new Vector2(-43.1f, 55.5f), new Vector2(43.1f, 55.5f) };
        var clamps = Docks.On(Landmarks.Outposts[0]);
        bool carried = Docks.On(Landmarks.ById("base")).Select(d => d.Row.Id).SequenceEqual(Y.Arms.Select(a => a.Dock.Row.Id))
                       && Landmarks.Outposts.Length == 4
                       && Enumerable.Range(0, 4).All(i => Landmarks.Outposts[i].At == Hub.Outposts[i].at && Docks.On(Landmarks.Outposts[i]).Length == 4
                                                          && Docks.On(Landmarks.Outposts[i]).All(d => d.At == Hub.Outposts[i].at))
                       && Landmarks.All.Count(l => Docks.On(l).Length > 0) == 5;
        var ends = new[] { ("arm_se", "clamp_nw"), ("arm_ne", "clamp_sw"), ("arm_nw", "clamp_se"), ("arm_sw", "clamp_ne") };
        Check(carried && Y.Arms.Length == 5 && Enumerable.Range(0, 5).All(i => Y.Arms[i].Dock.Pad.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos + armWas[i]) < 0.05f)
              && clamps.Length == 4 && Enumerable.Range(0, 4).All(i => clamps[i].Pad.DistanceTo(Hub.Outposts[0].at + clampAt[i]) < 0.05f
                                                                     && Mathf.Abs(clamps[i].Row.Open.X) > 0.99f && clamps[i].Row.Open.X * clampAt[i].X > 0f)
              && drones.All(c => (c.Home.Row.Id, c.Away.Row.Id) == ends[c.Lane]),
              $"the docks stand where they were measured -- the base's five arms in the wire's order ({string.Join(" ", Y.Arms.Select(a => a.Dock.Row.Id))}), four clamps on an outpost's corners opening outward, carried by the base's place and each outpost's and by no other ({carried}) -- and every lane docks on its own side ({string.Join(", ", drones.GroupBy(c => c.Lane).Select(g => $"{Lanes.Name(g.Key)} {g.First().Home.Row.Id}/{g.First().Away.Row.Id}"))})");
        // TWO CRAFT MAY SHARE ONE PAD: a miner is put in the arm one courier (on a lane the seed
        // picks) is coming in to, holding it and unloading, before the courier arrives.
        foreach (var x in Y.Gatherers) { x.ResetToWork(); Y.Release(x); }   // every arm free, nobody in line
        int sl = Math.Min(Lanes.All.Length - 1, (int)Vary(0f, Lanes.All.Length));
        var sharer = drones.First(c => c.Lane == sl);
        int shared = Array.FindIndex(Y.Arms, a => a.Dock.Row == sharer.Home.Row);
        var sDock = Y.Arms[Math.Max(0, shared)].Dock;
        var holder = Y.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
        Y.SetArm(holder, Math.Max(0, shared)); holder.State = Gatherer.St.Unloading; holder.Cargo = 1e6;
        holder.Position = sDock.Berth(Gatherer.Length); holder.Rotation = sDock.Heading;
        var keepPhase = drones.Select(c => c.Phase).ToArray();
        var keepClock = drones.Select(c => c.Clock).ToArray();
        float before = Vary(0.3f, 1.5f);                 // how far out each starts: WHERE on its approach
        foreach (var c in drones)
        {
            bool toBase = drones.Where(d => d.Lane == c.Lane).ToList().IndexOf(c) % 2 == 0;   // the sharer is first of its lane
            c.Phase = 0f;
            c.Clock = (toBase ? c.Cycle : c.Cycle * 0.5) - before;
        }
        // timed from the effect: every one is seen in the air before its approach is counted
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        int flyingIn = drones.Count(c => !c.Docked);
        await Wait(before + 1.2);                        // in, and 1.2 s into its 2 s on the dock
        var landed = drones.Select(c =>
        {
            var dk = c.Outbound ? c.Home : c.Away;       // a half begins on a dock: home going out, away coming back
            var nose = Aim.Nose(c, c.Def.Length * 0.5f);
            return (c, dk, fromPad: c.Position.DistanceTo(dk.Pad),
                    toFace: (nose - dk.Pad).Dot(dk.Row.Open) - dk.Row.Reach,
                    skew: Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(c.Rotation, dk.Heading))));
        }).ToList();
        var farthest = landed.OrderByDescending(l => l.fromPad).First();
        Check(flyingIn == drones.Count
              && landed.All(l => l.c.Docked && !l.c.Held && l.fromPad < 50f && l.toFace > 0f && l.toFace < 10f && l.skew < 5f)
              && drones.All(c => Y.Arms.Any(a => a.Dock.Row == c.Home.Row)
                                 && Landmarks.Outposts[c.Def.Outpost].Docks.Contains(c.Away.Row) && c.Away.At == Hub.Outposts[c.Def.Outpost].at),
              $"every courier docks where the fleet does -- a base service arm at one end, its outpost's clamp at the other -- and not short of it: centre within 50 u of the pad's, nose within 10 u of the open face and pointing in, flown in from {before:0.00} s out on all four bearings ({flyingIn} of {drones.Count} flying at the start, {landed.Count(l => l.c.Docked)} docked; farthest {Lanes.Name(farthest.c.Lane)} {farthest.dk.Row.Id}: {farthest.fromPad:0.0} u off the pad's centre, nose {farthest.toFace:0.0} u off the face, {farthest.skew:0.0} deg off nose-in)");
        // ...and the one that came in to a held arm sits BESIDE the miner holding it: its centre past
        // the miner's own half-width along the face, its own outer edge still on the face. The miner
        // keeps its arm. Both read after the wait, where they are now.
        float along = (sharer.Position - sDock.Pad).Dot(sDock.Outboard);
        var sharerArt = Nodes<Sprite2D>(sharer).First();
        float sharerHalf = sharerArt.Texture.GetWidth() * sharerArt.Scale.X * 0.5f;
        Check(shared >= 0 && Y.Arms[shared].Occupant == holder && Y.QueueLength == 0
              && sharer.Docked && holder.Position.DistanceTo(sDock.Berth(Gatherer.Length)) < 1f
              && along > holder.Extent.halfWidth && along + sharerHalf <= sDock.Row.Face * 0.5f + 0.5f,
              $"two craft may share one pad: lane {Lanes.Name(sl)}'s courier docks at {sDock.Row.Id} while a miner holds it, beside it ({along:0.0} u outboard of the centre line, past the miner's {holder.Extent.halfWidth:0} u half-width; its outer edge {along + sharerHalf:0.0} u out on a {sDock.Row.Face * 0.5f:0.0} u half-face; still the miner's: {Y.Arms[Math.Max(0, shared)].Occupant == holder})");
        holder.ResetToWork(); Y.Release(holder);

        // ── A COURIER RUNS BOTH WAYS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        double cyc = c0.Cycle;
        c0.Phase = 0f;
        c0.Clock = 0;           await Wait(0.05); var atBase = c0.Position;      bool goingOut = c0.Outbound;
        c0.Clock = cyc * 0.25;  await Wait(0.05); var quarterOut = c0.Position;  bool stillOut = c0.Outbound;
        c0.Clock = cyc * 0.5;   await Wait(0.05); var atOutpost = c0.Position;
        c0.Clock = cyc * 0.75;  await Wait(0.05); var quarterBack = c0.Position; bool coming = !c0.Outbound;
        c0.Clock = cyc * 1.02;  await Wait(0.05); int runs = c0.Runs;
        double dBase = atBase.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos), dOut = atOutpost.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos);
        Check(goingOut && stillOut && coming && runs >= 2
              && atBase.DistanceTo(c0.HomeBerth) < 1f && atOutpost.DistanceTo(c0.AwayBerth) < 1f
              && quarterOut.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) > dBase + 400f
              && quarterBack.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) < dOut - 400f,
              $"a courier runs BOTH WAYS on its lane -- off its base arm out to its outpost's clamp, then home again, and round again ({dBase:0} u from the base -> {quarterOut.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0} -> {dOut:0} -> {quarterBack.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0}; {runs} one-way runs done)");
        for (int i = 0; i < drones.Count; i++) { drones[i].Phase = keepPhase[i]; drones[i].Clock = keepClock[i]; }

        // ── THE GUN AT THE FAR END ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        var guns = Nodes<LaneGun>().ToList();
        var gun = guns.FirstOrDefault(g => g.Lane == 0);
        Check(gun != null && guns.Count == Lanes.All.Length
              && gun.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.Outposts[0].at) < Hub.OutpostHeight
              && Nodes<Turret>(gun).Count() == 1
              && gun.Range > gun.Position.DistanceTo(Lanes.Stand(0)),
              $"every outpost carries a gun, on the one turret component, reaching the spot its own lane is cut on ({gun?.Range:0} u of the {gun?.Position.DistanceTo(Lanes.Stand(0)):0} u out to the stand)");
        // WHO IT MAY SHOOT is a filter, never a type test: raiding craft, and nothing of ours.
        var anyDummy = Nodes<TargetDummy>().FirstOrDefault();
        Check(!Targeting.Craft.Hits(Me) && !Targeting.Craft.Hits(Y.Hauler) && !Targeting.Craft.Hits(Y.Gatherers[0])
              && (H.Deployed.Count == 0 || !Targeting.Craft.Hits(H.Deployed[0]))
              && (anyDummy == null || !Targeting.Craft.Hits(anyDummy)),
              "the filter keeps the outposts off their own side: a pilot, the hauler, a miner, a dropped turret and a practice dummy are none of them raiding craft");
        Check(Math.Abs(gun.Range - 1200) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(gun.Interval - 10.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(gun.Speed - 300) < 1e-3,
              $"an unbought outpost gun: 1200 u, one missile every 10 s (6 a minute), 300 u/s ({gun.Range:0} u, {gun.Interval:0.00} s, {gun.Speed:0} u/s)");
        Y.Credits = 100000;
        bool three = Y.TryBuy(Lanes.RangeId) && Y.TryBuy(Lanes.RateId) && Y.TryBuy(Lanes.SpeedId);
        Check(three && Economy.All.Count(u => u.Tab == "OUTPOSTS") == 3 && Economy.Tabs.Contains("OUTPOSTS")
              && Math.Abs(gun.Range - 1320) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(gun.Interval - 9.0909) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(gun.Speed - 330) < 1e-3,
              $"the OUTPOSTS tab buys range, rate of fire and missile speed, +10% a level, for every outpost at once: {gun.Range:0} u, {gun.Interval:0.000} s a missile, {gun.Speed:0} u/s");
        lv.Remove(Lanes.RangeId); lv.Remove(Lanes.RateId); lv.Remove(Lanes.SpeedId);

        // NOTHING OF OURS DRAWS A SHOT: the pilot parked on the very spot a blockade stands on.
        Me.Position = Lanes.Stand(0); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        int blasts0 = H.BlastsPending, flying0 = Nodes<MissileVisual>().Count();
        await Wait(3.0);
        Check(H.BlastsPending <= blasts0 && Nodes<MissileVisual>().Count() <= flying0 && gun.Aimed == null,
              $"and it throws nothing at its own side: a pilot sitting on the stand for 3 s, well inside its 1200 u, is never even aimed at ({H.BlastsPending - blasts0} thrown)");
        Me.Position = park; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        await Wait(0.3);

        // ── A CUT LANE STOPS PAYING: A QUARTER FOR ONE, A HALF FOR TWO ──────────────────────
        double whole = Paid();
        var cutA = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(Lanes.Stand(0), 200f), Enemies.Webifier);
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        cutA.Hp = 5000;                                  // it is the thing being blockaded WITH
        double oneCut = Paid();
        bool threeQuarters = Math.Abs(Lanes.Flow(H) - 0.75) < 1e-9;
        var heldAt = c0.Position; double heldClock = c0.Clock;
        var cutB = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(Lanes.Stand(1), 200f), Enemies.Webifier);
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        cutB.Hp = 5000;
        double twoCut = Paid();
        Check(Math.Abs(whole - 100) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(oneCut - 75) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(twoCut - 50) < 1e-9
              && threeQuarters && Math.Abs(Lanes.Flow(H) - 0.5) < 1e-9
              && Lanes.Cut(H, 0) && Lanes.Cut(H, 1) && !Lanes.Cut(H, 2) && !Lanes.Cut(H, 3),
              $"one lane blockaded costs exactly a quarter of the passive income and two lanes a half: 100 units delivered pay {whole:0}, then {oneCut:0}, then {twoCut:0}");
        // ...and the courier on a cut lane holds where it is: the flow has stopped, visibly
        await Wait(0.6);
        Check(c0.Held && c0.Position.DistanceTo(heldAt) < 1f && Math.Abs(c0.Clock - heldClock) < 1e-6,
              $"the cut lane's couriers hold where they are, carrying nothing ({c0.Position.DistanceTo(heldAt):0.0} u in 0.6 s)");

        // ── THE OUTPOST ANSWERS: the heavy fighter's own missile, friendly-coloured ─────────
        cutB.TakeDamage(1e9);                            // one blockader, so one gun is firing
        await Wait(0.5);
        // held on the spot for a few frames first, so the LEAD is zero and this check is about the
        // blast: the prediction itself is proved against literals where Missiles.Predict is.
        var markAt = cutA.Position;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) { cutA.Position = markAt; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        cdF.SetValue(gun, 0.0);                          // its reload may be part-spent on the escort's hunters
        double hp0 = cutA.Hp;
        FxNode mark = null; MissileVisual flying = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && flying == null; i++)
        {
            cutA.Position = markAt;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            // THIS gun's, at THIS blockader: an outpost missile aimed where the blockader stands
            flying = Nodes<MissileVisual>().FirstOrDefault(mv => mv.Side == Missiles.Outpost && mv.To.DistanceTo(markAt) < 25f);
            mark = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(fn => fn.Id == Fx.AimZone && Math.Abs(fn.Radius - 90f) < 0.1f
                                                       && fn.Position.DistanceTo(markAt) < 25f);
        }
        bool marked = flying != null && mark != null && mark.Warn && mark.Anchor == Fx.World
                      && ReferenceEquals(gun.Aimed, cutA);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && cutA.Hp >= hp0; i++)
        { cutA.Position = markAt; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(marked && Math.Abs(hp0 - cutA.Hp - 42) < 1e-6,
              $"the outpost throws the heavy fighter's own predicted missile at the blockader, marks where it will land in friendly colours, and lands the gunship's 42 there ({hp0 - cutA.Hp:0} off the blockader)");

        // ── A BLOCKADE IS A ROW OF Waves.All, holding the lane's own stand ─────────────────
        var plainRow = Waves.For(WaveTrigger.Blockade, new WaveBrief { Index = 0 });
        var lateRow = Waves.For(WaveTrigger.Blockade, new WaveBrief { Index = 2 });
        H.Blockade(0);
        await Wait(0.3);
        var squad = H.Raiders.Where(r => r != cutA).ToList();
        Check(plainRow != null && plainRow.Id == "blockade" && Math.Abs(plainRow.Hold - 200f) < 1e-3 && plainRow.Crew.Length == 2
              && lateRow != null && lateRow.Id == "blockade_heavy" && lateRow.Crew.Length == 3
              && squad.Count == 4 && squad.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1
              && squad.All(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Lanes.Stand(0)) <= Lanes.Hold)
              && squad.All(r => r.Station.DistanceTo(Lanes.Stand(0)) < 1f && Math.Abs(r.Circuit - 200f) < 1e-3)
              && Lanes.Cut(H, 0) && Math.Abs(Lanes.Flow(H) - 0.75) < 1e-9,
              $"a blockade is a trigger and a row, not a spawner: three lights and a heavy holding a 200 u ring on the lane's own stand, and the base paid three quarters ({squad.Count} sent, {Lanes.Flow(H) * 100:0}% flow)");

        // ── CLEARING IT RESTORES THE INCOME ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
        await Wait(0.3);
        double again = Paid();
        Check(Enumerable.Range(0, Lanes.All.Length).All(i => !Lanes.Cut(H, i))
              && Math.Abs(Lanes.Flow(H) - 1.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(again - 100) < 1e-9
              && Lanes.Readout(H).Length == 0,
              $"clearing the blockade restores the income at once: 100 units delivered pay {again:0} again, and the HUD stops reporting a cut lane");

        Y.SetStock("ore", keepOre); Y.Credits = keepCredits;
        Me.Position = keepMe; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        await Wait(0.2);
    }

    // A sheet nobody has levelled: the kit's, and a part's own printed numbers.
    static readonly Dictionary<string, int> NoLevels = new();

    // ── LEVELLING A PART WITH SALVAGE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // 100 salvage for the first level and a quarter more each time, stopping at +200%. A level adds
    // 5% to what the part is FOR and nothing to what it takes in exchange, and it belongs to the
    // part ID, because the hold stores counts by id and no instance id exists anywhere.
    void GearLevelChecks()
    {
        var battery = Equipment.ById("bs_rapid_1");                  // faster guns, lighter shells
        if (battery == null) { Check(false, "gear levels: no bs_rapid_1 to level"); return; }

        double first = Equipment.NextLevelCost(battery.Id);
        Character.GearLevel[battery.Id] = 1;  double second = Equipment.NextLevelCost(battery.Id);
        Character.GearLevel[battery.Id] = 2;  double third  = Equipment.NextLevelCost(battery.Id);
        Character.GearLevel[battery.Id] = Equipment.MaxLevel;
        double ceiling = Equipment.NextLevelCost(battery.Id);
        double whole = 0; for (int k = 0; k < Equipment.MaxLevel; k++) whole += Math.Round(100 * Math.Pow(1.25, k));
        Check(Math.Abs(first - 100) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(second - 125) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(third - 156) < 1e-6
              && ceiling < 0 && Equipment.MaxLevel * Equipment.LevelStep == 2.0 && Math.Abs(whole - 3008864) < 2,
              $"a level costs 100 salvage and a quarter more each time, stopping at +200%: {first:0}, {second:0}, {third:0}, and {whole:0} for the whole ladder of {Equipment.MaxLevel}");

        // WHAT IT LIFTS: the part's own stats, and not what it takes in exchange -- by the levels the
        // sheet is GIVEN (its own pilot's), whatever the pilot at this keyboard holds. This one has the
        // same part at the ceiling, and none of that may show.
        Character.GearLevel.Clear();
        var worn = Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Battleship);
        worn[Array.IndexOf(Equipment.Core, battery.Slot)] = battery.Id;
        Character.GearLevel[battery.Id] = 40;
        var bare = Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Battleship, worn, 14, NoLevels);
        var lifted = Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Battleship, worn, 14, new Dictionary<string, int> { [battery.Id] = 10 });   // +50%
        string up = battery.Ups.FirstOrDefault(), down = battery.Pct.Keys.FirstOrDefault(k => Array.IndexOf(battery.Ups, k) < 0);
        Check(up != null && down != null
              && Math.Abs(lifted[up] - bare[up] * 1.5) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(lifted[down] - bare[down]) < 1e-9,
              $"ten levels lift what the part is FOR by half and leave what it costs alone: {up} {bare[up] * 100:0.#}% -> {lifted[up] * 100:0.#}%, {down} {bare[down] * 100:0.#}% either way");
        // A CLAIM MADE SAFE: levels announced over the wire are held to the ladder's 40, and a part the
        // game does not know, a level under one, or no id at all is dropped.
        var safe = Equipment.SanitizeLevels(new (string, int)[] { (battery.Id, 99), ("no_such_part", 5), ("bs_rapid_2", 0), ("bs_rapid_3", -3), (null, 1) });
        Check(safe.Count == 1 && safe.GetValueOrDefault(battery.Id) == 40,
              $"a pilot's claimed gear levels are held to 40, on parts that exist ({string.Join(", ", safe.Select(kv => $"{kv.Key}={kv.Value}"))})");
        Character.GearLevel[battery.Id] = 10;                        // what the disk round trip below expects back

        // A LEVEL IS THE ID'S, and it survives a round trip to disk. Under a scratch id of its own:
        // the pilot this run is flying has a base saved against it.
        string keepId = Character.Id;
        Character.Id = "gearlevel"; Character.Save();
        Character.GearLevel.Clear();
        bool loaded = Character.Load("gearlevel") && Equipment.LevelOf(battery.Id) == 10;
        Check(loaded && Equipment.LevelOf("bs_rapid_2") == 0,
              $"a level belongs to the part id, and comes back off disk ({Equipment.LevelOf(battery.Id)} on the battery, {Equipment.LevelOf("bs_rapid_2")} on the mark II)");
        Character.Delete("gearlevel");
        Character.GearLevel.Clear(); Character.Id = keepId;
    }

    // A wider view than before: a boss's reach grows with the level, and a fight whose edges are
    // off screen is a fight fought on the minimap.
    void ZoomChecks()
    {
        H.SetZoom(0.0001f);                                   // as far out as the wheel will go
        float out0 = H.ZoomLevel;
        H.SetZoom(1000f);                                     // ...and as far in
        float in0 = H.ZoomLevel;
        H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom);
        Check(Math.Abs(out0 - Hub.DefaultZoom / 1.53f) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(in0 - Hub.DefaultZoom * 1.5f) < 1e-6
              && Math.Abs(Hub.ZoomOutMax / 1.33f - 1.15f) < 0.005f,
              $"the wheel reaches 15% further out than it did -- 1.33 of the default was the limit, 1.53 is ({out0:0.000} out, {in0:0.000} in)");
    }

    // ── THE SKY: layered, and every layer moving at its own speed ───────────────────────────────
    // What this replaces could not move AT ALL: one TextureRect anchored FullRect to the screen,
    // so a ship at 190 u/s crossed a starfield that never shifted a pixel. The rows are proved
    // against literals here; that they actually MOVE, and move by DIFFERENT amounts, is proved by
    // shifting the camera and reading them back -- which is the only check that would have caught
    // the thing being fixed.
    async Task SkyChecks()
    {
        var sky = H.GetNodeOrNull<CanvasLayer>("Stars");
        var layers = sky == null ? new List<SkyPlane>() : Nodes<SkyPlane>(sky).ToList();
        Check(sky != null && sky.Layer == -100 && layers.Count == Sky.All.Length && Sky.All.Length == 3
              && layers.Select(l => l.Name.ToString()).SequenceEqual(Sky.All.Select(r => "Sky_" + r.Id)),
              $"the sky is a layer per row, all of it behind the world ({layers.Count} of {Sky.All.Length} rows on layer {sky?.Layer})");

        // FAR TO NEAR, strictly. The illusion IS the ordering: equal drifts are one flat sheet,
        // and a row out of order reads as the distance running backwards. Every row must drift
        // MORE than nothing -- a 0 is the static sky this replaced -- and less than 0.6, past
        // which the stars stop reading as distance and start reading as debris going by.
        bool climbs = true;
        for (int i = 1; i < Sky.All.Length; i++) if (Sky.All[i].Drift <= Sky.All[i - 1].Drift) climbs = false;
        Check(climbs && Sky.All.All(r => r.Drift > 0f && r.Drift < 0.6f)
              && Math.Abs(Sky.All[0].Drift - 0.08f) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(Sky.All[Sky.All.Length - 1].Drift - 0.45f) < 1e-6,
              $"every layer moves and none keeps up: {string.Join(" < ", Sky.All.Select(r => r.Drift.ToString("0.00")))} of the world's motion, ordered far to near");

        // A ROW'S LOOK REACHES ITS PLANE: tiled, at its own scale and its own angle. A plane that is
        // not tiled shows one copy of stars.png and then nothing; one at the wrong scale or angle
        // lines up with its neighbour and reads as one grid sliding over itself.
        bool tiles = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < layers.Count; i++)
        {
            var row = Sky.All[i];
            if (layers[i].Art == null) { tiles = false; break; }
            if (layers[i].TextureRepeat != CanvasItem.TextureRepeatEnum.Enabled) tiles = false;
            if (Math.Abs(layers[i].Scale.X - row.Scale) > 1e-6) tiles = false;
            if (Math.Abs(layers[i].RotationDegrees - row.Spin) > 1e-3) tiles = false;
        }
        Check(tiles && layers.Count > 0,
              $"each layer draws its own art, tiled, at its own scale and angle ({layers.Count} layers)");

        // AND IT MOVES THE WAY THE WORLD DOES. The ship hops 200 u in a direction drawn from the
        // seed; every layer must shift on screen, a NEARER one further than the one behind it, each
        // by its own row's share -- and every one of them the SAME WAY as a point fixed in the
        // world. Measuring only how FAR each layer moved was the hole the reversed sky went
        // through: a sky sliding with the ship reads as the stars swinging round it, and an
        // absolute distance passes that happily. Measured where it is DRAWN, as a screen vector.
        // A plane's origin is continuous (the region re-centres by whole tiles; the plane never
        // jumps), so any hop reads true.
        var hop = Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * 200f;
        var fixedPoint = Me.Position + hop.Normalized() * 600f;
        Vector2 OnScreen(Vector2 w) => H.GetViewport().GetCanvasTransform() * w;
        var markBefore = OnScreen(fixedPoint);
        var wasAt = Me.Position;
        var before = layers.Select(l => l.GetGlobalTransformWithCanvas().Origin).ToList();
        Me.Position = wasAt + hop;
        for (int f = 0; f < 6; f++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var shift = layers.Select((l, i) => l.GetGlobalTransformWithCanvas().Origin - before[i]).ToList();
        var moved = shift.Select(d => (double)d.Length()).ToList();
        var worldWent = OnScreen(fixedPoint) - markBefore;
        // within 3 degrees of the world's own direction on screen, every layer
        var off = shift.Select(d => d.Length() < 0.01f ? 0f : Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(d.AngleTo(worldWent)))).ToList();
        bool sameWay = worldWent.Length() > 1f && off.All(a2 => a2 < 3f);
        bool anyMoved = moved.Any(d => d > 1f);
        bool deeper = true;
        for (int i = 1; i < moved.Count; i++) if (moved[i] <= moved[i - 1]) deeper = false;
        // ...AND BY THE SHARE ITS ROW ASKED FOR. Each layer's travel over the same hop, divided by
        // its own Drift, must come to the same camera distance -- which is what makes this a check
        // on the NUMBERS in the table and not merely on the ordering of them.
        bool byShare = true;
        double unit = moved.Count > 0 ? moved[0] / Sky.All[0].Drift : 0;
        for (int i = 1; i < moved.Count; i++)
            if (Math.Abs(moved[i] / Sky.All[i].Drift - unit) > unit * 0.05) byShare = false;
        Me.Position = wasAt;
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(anyMoved && deeper && byShare && sameWay,
              $"200 u of ship at {Mathf.RadToDeg(hop.Angle()):0} deg moves every layer THE WAY THE WORLD GOES (off by {string.Join(", ", off.Select(a2 => a2.ToString("0.0")))} deg), each nearer one further than the one behind it, and each by its own row's share ({string.Join(", ", moved.Select(d => d.ToString("0.0")))} u over {string.Join(", ", Sky.All.Select(r => r.Drift.ToString("0.00")))})");

        // NO EDGE, HOWEVER FAR OUT. Far from the origin the old plane had moved off its own drawn
        // region, and the corners of the screen showed through as large dark wedges. Tens of
        // thousands of units out, in a direction drawn from the seed, every corner of the screen
        // must land inside what each layer actually draws.
        var farAt = VaryNear(wasAt, 80000f);
        Me.Position = farAt; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        for (int f = 0; f < 4; f++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var screen = layers.Count > 0 ? layers[0].GetViewportRect() : new Rect2();
        var corners = new[] { screen.Position, screen.Position + new Vector2(screen.Size.X, 0),
                              screen.Position + new Vector2(0, screen.Size.Y), screen.End };
        var bare = layers.Where(l => corners.Any(c => !l.Drawn.HasPoint(l.Transform.AffineInverse() * c)))
                         .Select(l => l.Name.ToString()).ToList();
        Me.Position = wasAt;
        for (int f = 0; f < 4; f++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(layers.Count > 0 && screen.Size.X > 0 && bare.Count == 0,
              $"{farAt.DistanceTo(wasAt):0} u out, the whole screen is inside every layer's drawn stars ({(bare.Count == 0 ? "none bare" : "bare: " + string.Join(", ", bare))})");
    }

    // ── THE SOFT TUTORIAL: a card at a time, CONTINUE, and SKIP in the top right ────────────────
    // On a FIRST character only. It is an order of hint ids rather than a second copy of the
    // words, so a card here and a card met in play are the same card, and a card dismissed here is
    // never shown again.
    async Task TourChecks()
    {
        var tour = Nodes<Tour>().FirstOrDefault();
        Check(tour != null && Hints.Tour.Length >= 6 && Hints.Tour.All(id => Hints.All.ContainsKey(id))
              && Hints.Tour.Distinct().Count() == Hints.Tour.Length,
              $"the tour is an ORDER of hints, not a second copy of them: {Hints.Tour.Length} steps, every one a card the game already has, none of them twice");
        if (tour == null) return;

        // A pilot that has met anything is not new, and is never offered it.
        bool offeredToVeteran = false;
        var was = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<string>(Character.HintsSeen);
        Character.HintsSeen.Add("flight");
        var late = new Tour { Hub = H }; AddChild(late); late.StartIfNew();
        offeredToVeteran = late.Running;
        late.QueueFree();
        Character.HintsSeen.Clear(); foreach (var id in was) Character.HintsSeen.Add(id);
        Check(!offeredToVeteran, "a pilot that has met anything at all is not walked through it");

        // CONTINUE steps forward and marks the card seen; the last one reads FLY.
        Character.HintsSeen.Clear(); Character.TourDone = false;
        var fresh = new Tour { Hub = H }; AddChild(fresh); fresh.StartIfNew();
        bool started = fresh.Running;
        var cont = Nodes<Button>(fresh).First(b => b.Name == "Continue");
        var skipBtn = Nodes<Button>(fresh).First(b => b.Name == "Skip");
        cont.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        bool stepped = Character.HintsSeen.Contains(Hints.Tour[0]) && fresh.Running;
        // SKIP ends it and marks the WHOLE tour seen, so the same pilot is not asked again.
        skipBtn.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        bool skipped = Hints.Tour.All(Character.HintsSeen.Contains) && Character.TourDone;
        Check(started && stepped && skipped,
              $"CONTINUE marks a card seen and moves on; SKIP ends it and marks every card on it, so nothing is ever said twice ({Character.HintsSeen.Count} seen, done {Character.TourDone})");
        Character.HintsSeen.Clear(); Character.TourDone = false;
        foreach (var id in was) Character.HintsSeen.Add(id);
    }

    void RefitChecks()
    {
        Character.NewBlank();
        Character.Level = 40; Character.Points = 39; Character.Exp = 560;     // 560 of 1000 toward 41
        bool bought = Progression.TryBuy("hull") && Progression.TryBuy("hull") && Progression.TryBuy("turn");
        int pointsBefore = Character.Points, hullBefore = Character.Bought[1], turnBefore = Character.Bought[0];
        var (undone, back) = Progression.Refit();
        Check(bought && undone == "Rudder" && back == 1 && Character.Bought[0] == turnBefore - 1
              && Character.Bought[1] == hullBefore && Character.Level == 39 && Character.Exp == 560
              && Character.Points == pointsBefore + 1 - 1,
              $"a refit takes a level and undoes the LAST point bought -- rudder, not the two hull levels that cost more -- refunds what it cost, and leaves the progress toward the next level alone ({undone} came off for {back} back, level {Character.Level}, {Character.Exp} of 1000, {Character.Points} points)");

        // ...and a pilot from before the order was written down loses its DEAREST point instead,
        // which is the most a file with nothing to read can honestly say.
        Character.NewBlank();
        Character.Level = 40; Character.Points = 39;
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) Progression.TryBuy("hull");
        Progression.TryBuy("speed");
        Character.Spent.Clear();                                              // as an old file loads: no order at all
        var (old, oldBack) = Progression.Refit();
        Check(old == "Hull" && oldBack == 4 && Character.Bought[1] == 3,
              $"a pilot saved before the order was recorded loses its dearest point instead: the fourth hull level, {oldBack} points back ({old})");
        Character.NewBlank();
    }

    // ── THE LEVEL WALLS (Unlocks): what a pilot's highest level reached opens ─────────────────────
    // docs/plans/level_walls.md with the owner's rulings (6 chip slots on every hull, walls read the
    // highest level ever reached). The levels are the plan's literals, never the table's own numbers;
    // levels are never varied. Runs beside the refit's checks, on pilots it throws away.
    void WallChecks()
    {
        // THE TABLE, in one place (level_walls check 2)
        var abil = new[] { 1, 2, 3 }.Select(n => Unlocks.At(Opens.Ability, n)).ToArray();
        var chip = Enumerable.Range(1, 6).Select(n => Unlocks.At(Opens.ChipSlot, n)).ToArray();
        Check(abil.SequenceEqual(new[] { 1, 3, 6 }) && chip.SequenceEqual(new[] { 2, 4, 8, 10, 12, 14 }) && Unlocks.Top == 14
              && Unlocks.At(Opens.Ability, 4) == int.MaxValue && Unlocks.At(Opens.ChipSlot, 7) == int.MaxValue,
              $"the walls: abilities at levels 1, 3, 6; chip slots at 2, 4, 8, 10, 12, 14; every wall open at 14; never a 4th ability or a 7th slot (abilities {string.Join(",", abil)}; chips {string.Join(",", chip)}; top {Unlocks.Top})");
        var chipsOpen = new[] { 1, 2, 4, 8, 14 }.Select(l => Unlocks.Count(Opens.ChipSlot, l)).ToArray();
        var abOpen = new[] { 1, 2, 3, 5, 6 }.Select(l => Unlocks.Count(Opens.Ability, l)).ToArray();
        Check(chipsOpen.SequenceEqual(new[] { 0, 1, 2, 3, 6 }) && abOpen.SequenceEqual(new[] { 1, 1, 2, 2, 3 }),
              $"...so every hull has 0, 1, 2, 3 and 6 chip slots open at levels 1, 2, 4, 8 and 14, and 1, 1, 2, 2 and 3 abilities at 1, 2, 3, 5 and 6 ({string.Join(",", chipsOpen)}; {string.Join(",", abOpen)})");
        // THE BASE'S ROWS, measured by the base owner's bosses beaten: Auto-sell at boss 3, the lanes'
        // blockades at boss 1, and an Economy row no wall names open from the start
        Check(Unlocks.Boss(Opens.Economy, "hauler_autosell") == 3 && Unlocks.Boss(Opens.Raids) == 1 && Unlocks.Boss(Opens.Economy, "miner_hold") == 0,
              $"the base's walls are rows of the same table: Auto-sell once boss 3 is beaten, blockades once boss 1 is, the miners' hold never walled ({Unlocks.Boss(Opens.Economy, "hauler_autosell")}, {Unlocks.Boss(Opens.Raids)}, {Unlocks.Boss(Opens.Economy, "miner_hold")})");
        // ...and the lanes' clock reads it: with no bounty boss beaten it never runs; from boss 1 it does
        var laneIn = typeof(Raids).GetField("_laneIn", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
        foreach (var (beaten, runs) in new[] { (0, false), (1, true), (4, true) })
        {
            Character.NewBlank();
            if (beaten > 0) Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"] = Enumerable.Range(1, beaten).ToHashSet();
            var lanes = new Raids(null); laneIn.SetValue(lanes, 5.0); lanes.Tick(0.5);
            double left = (double)laneIn.GetValue(lanes);
            Check(Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Bounty) == beaten && Math.Abs(left - (runs ? 4.5 : 5.0)) < 1e-9,
                  $"with bounty boss {beaten} beaten the lanes' blockade clock {(runs ? "runs" : "waits for boss 1")}: 5 s -> {left:0.0} s in half a second");
        }

        // THE ABILITY WALLS BY POSITION (D4/D5): a class's abilities 1, 2 and 3 are its rows in list order,
        // skipping a weapon's own actions and the open hotkeys. The weapon rows are exactly these eight.
        var weaponRows = typeof(Ab).GetFields(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static)
                                   .Select(f => f.GetValue(null)).OfType<AbilityDef>().Where(a => a.Weapon).Select(a => a.Id).OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray();
        Check(weaponRows.SequenceEqual(new[] { "attack", "collect", "deploy", "firemode", "guns", "pd", "recall", "reload" }),
              $"never walled: the guns, fire mode, point defence, the missile's reload, the wing's attack and recall, the sentries' deploy and collect ({string.Join(",", weaponRows)})");
        // ...and the rule, on real rows in three orders: weapon rows first, walled rows first, the two interleaved
        foreach (var (kit, want) in new[] {
            (new[] { Ab.Guns, Ab.FireMode, Ab.Broadside, Ab.Pd, Ab.Missile, Ab.Reload, Ab.Railgun }, new[] { 1, 1, 1, 1, 3, 1, 6 }),
            (new[] { Ab.Attack, Ab.Recall, Ab.Bombers, Ab.Pd, Ab.Hunters, Ab.Bubble, Ab.Echo }, new[] { 1, 1, 1, 1, 3, 6, int.MaxValue }),
            (new[] { Ab.Echo, Ab.Guns, Ab.Stealth, Ab.Deploy, Ab.Collect, Ab.Rush, Ab.FireMode }, new[] { 1, 1, 3, 1, 1, 6, 1 }) })
        {
            var got = kit.Select(a => Unlocks.AbilityAt(kit, a)).ToArray();
            Check(got.SequenceEqual(want) && Unlocks.AbilityAt(kit, Ab.Reboard) == 1,
                  $"walls by place in the list: {string.Join(", ", kit.Select(a => a.Id))} open at levels {string.Join(", ", want.Select(w => w == int.MaxValue ? "never" : w.ToString()))}; reboard, on no list, at 1 ({string.Join(", ", got.Select(g => g == int.MaxValue ? "never" : g.ToString()))})");
        }
        // EVERY FLYABLE CLASS at level 1: every weapon row and ability 1 open, and a walled row is always
        // a press (a held row is polled, not pressed: nothing on that path reads a wall)
        var flyable = Classes.All.Where(cd => cd.Ready).ToList();
        var shutAt1 = flyable.SelectMany(cd => cd.Abilities.Where(a => Unlocks.LockedAt(cd.Id, 1, a) != null && a != Unlocks.Nth(cd.Id, 2) && a != Unlocks.Nth(cd.Id, 3))
                                                           .Select(a => $"{cd.Id}.{a.Id}")).ToList();
        var heldWalled = flyable.SelectMany(cd => cd.Abilities.Where(a => Unlocks.Walled(a) && a.Kind != AbilityKind.Press).Select(a => $"{cd.Id}.{a.Id}")).ToList();
        Check(flyable.Count == 12 && shutAt1.Count == 0 && heldWalled.Count == 0 && flyable.All(cd => Unlocks.Nth(cd.Id, 1) != null),
              $"all 12 classes at level 1: ability 1 and every weapon row open, and every walled row a press ({string.Join(",", shutAt1)}; held {string.Join(",", heldWalled)})");
        // level_walls check 3: exactly three walled abilities a class, the Warrior learning lunge, whirlwind,
        // prism and the Carrier bombers, gunships, supercarrier. PEND while the kits are one ability each.
        var walledCount = flyable.ToDictionary(cd => cd.Id, cd => cd.Abilities.Count(Unlocks.Walled));
        bool Learns(ShipClass c, params string[] names) => names.Select((nm, i) => Unlocks.Nth(c, i + 1)?.Name.Contains(nm, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true).All(x => x);
        if (walledCount.Values.Any(n => n < 1 || n > 3))
            Check(false, $"every class carries one to three walled abilities: {string.Join(", ", walledCount.Where(kv => kv.Value < 1 || kv.Value > 3).Select(kv => $"{kv.Key} {kv.Value}"))}");
        else if (walledCount.Values.All(n => n == 3))
            Check(Learns(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, "lunge", "whirlwind", "prism") && Learns(ShipClass.Carrier, "bomber", "gunship", "supercarrier"),
                  $"every class learns three abilities in its list's order: the Warrior lunge, whirlwind, prism; the Carrier bombers, gunships, supercarrier ({string.Join(", ", Enumerable.Range(1, 3).Select(n => Unlocks.Nth(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, n)?.Name))}; {string.Join(", ", Enumerable.Range(1, 3).Select(n => Unlocks.Nth(ShipClass.Carrier, n)?.Name))})");
        else
            GD.Print($"PEND walls: three walled abilities a class (level_walls check 3) -- {string.Join(", ", walledCount.Where(kv => kv.Value < 3).Select(kv => $"{kv.Key} {kv.Value}"))}");
        // THE CARDS AND THE NEXT LINE, from the table: a pilot row's card id, a chip slot's card on three
        // hulls, and what the PILOT window names next at levels 3, 12 and 14
        var hintIds = Unlocks.All.Select(u => u.Hint).ToArray();
        Check(hintIds.SequenceEqual(new[] { "unlock_ability_1", "unlock_chipslot_1", "unlock_ability_2", "unlock_chipslot_2", "unlock_ability_3",
                                            "unlock_chipslot_3", "unlock_chipslot_4", "unlock_chipslot_5", "unlock_chipslot_6", null, null }),
              $"every pilot row has a card of its own, the base's rows none ({string.Join(",", hintIds.Select(h => h ?? "-"))})");
        foreach (var c in new[] { ShipClass.Battleship, ShipClass.HeavyWarden, ShipClass.LightEcho })
        {
            var card = Unlocks.Card("unlock_chipslot_2", c);
            var crossed = Unlocks.Crossed(1, 2, c).Select(u => u.Hint).ToArray();
            var next = new[] { 3, 12, 14 }.Select(p => Unlocks.Next(c, p)).ToArray();
            Check(card?.Title == "LEVEL 4 · CHIP SLOT 2" && crossed.SequenceEqual(new[] { "unlock_chipslot_1" }) && Unlocks.Card("unlock_ability_1", c) != null
                  && Unlocks.Card("no_such_row", c) == null && next.SequenceEqual(new[] { "LEVEL 4 · CHIP SLOT 2", "LEVEL 14 · CHIP SLOT 6", null }),
                  $"{c}: the card for chip slot 2 reads LEVEL 4 · CHIP SLOT 2; level 1 to 2 crosses chip slot 1 alone; next at levels 3, 12, 14: chip slot 2, chip slot 6, nothing ({card?.Title}; {string.Join(",", crossed)}; {string.Join(" / ", next.Select(n => n ?? "-"))})");
        }

        // THE PEAK (level_walls check 8, the pilot's half): a level up raises it, a refit never lowers
        // it, and it survives a save and a load. Three pilots: into ability 3, into chip slot 6, into ability 2.
        foreach (int from in new[] { 5, 13, 2 })
        {
            Character.NewBlank(); Character.Level = from; Character.Peak = from;
            Progression.AddExp(1000);                                   // saves
            int grew = Character.Peak;
            Progression.Refit();                                        // saves
            string pid = Character.Id; int refitTo = Character.Level;
            Character.NewBlank(); bool back = Character.Load(pid);
            Check(grew == from + 1 && refitTo == from && back && Character.Level == from && Character.Peak == from + 1,
                  $"a pilot at level {from} levels up to {from + 1}, refits back to {from}, and its highest level reached stays {from + 1} through a save and a load (peak {grew} after the level; level {Character.Level}, peak {Character.Peak} loaded)");
            Character.Delete(pid);
        }
        // A FILE WITHOUT THE KEY reads its level, and so does one whose peak is under its level
        foreach (var (level, peak, want) in new[] { (4, -1, 4), (9, 2, 9), (3, 12, 12) })
        {
            var f = new ConfigFile(); f.SetValue("id", "version", Game.Version); f.SetValue("id", "name", "PeakFile");
            f.SetValue("progress", "level", level); if (peak >= 0) f.SetValue("progress", "peak", peak);
            f.Save($"{Character.Dir}/peakfile.cfg");
            bool ok = Character.Load("peakfile");
            Check(ok && Character.Peak == want && Character.Level == level,
                  $"a file at level {level} {(peak < 0 ? "with no peak" : $"with peak {peak}")} loads with peak {want} ({Character.Peak})");
            Character.Delete("peakfile");
        }
        Character.NewBlank();
    }

    // ── THE ABILITY WALLS IN THE WORLD (level_walls checks 3, 4, 5, 9 and the PILOT window's NEXT line) ──
    // Run from the pilot's progression block, in the hub. Leaves the pilot as it found it: its class,
    // level, EXP, points and peak, and its ship where it was. Checks 4 and 5 need a class's ability 2 or 3,
    // so they print PEND walls until the kits carry them (lane A, slice 6).
    string Sn(PlayerShip s, string id) { var t = Snap(s, id); return t[..t.LastIndexOf(" hp", StringComparison.Ordinal)]; }   // the hull is the gunship's business
    async Task WallChecksLive()
    {
        var cls0 = Character.Class; int lv0 = Character.Level, exp0 = Character.Exp, pts0 = Character.Points, peak0 = Character.Peak;
        var here0 = Me.Position; float rot0 = Me.Rotation;
        var ids = Unlocks.All.Select(u => u.Hint).Where(h => h != null).ToArray();
        var doAb = typeof(PlayerShip).GetMethod("DoAbility", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
        var spot = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 2400);                  // clear of the belt, the wreck, the dummies and the lane
        async Task At(ShipClass c, int peak)
        {
            Character.Class = c; Character.Peak = peak; H.PilotChanged(); await Wait(0.1);
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e12);
            var s = Me; s.Position = VaryNear(spot, 200f); s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; s.Restore(s.MaxHp, true, 0);
        }
        Raider Mark(Vector2 at) { var m = H.SpawnRaider(at, Enemies.Gunship); m.Hp = 1e9; return m; }

        // CHECK 9, THE CARDS: 2600 EXP from level 1 queues chip slot 1's card, then ability 2's naming this
        // class's own key and ability (the Destroyer's Q, the Warrior's Q, the Carrier's E)
        foreach (var (c, key) in new[] { (ShipClass.Destroyer, "Q"), (ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, "Q"), (ShipClass.Carrier, "E") })
        {
            await At(c, 1);
            Character.Level = 1; Character.Exp = 0; Unmeet(ids);
            Progression.AddExp(2600);
            var q = HintQueue.Where(h => h.StartsWith("unlock_", StringComparison.Ordinal)).ToList();
            var a2 = Unlocks.Nth(c, 2);
            Check(Character.Level == 3 && Character.Peak == 3 && q.FirstOrDefault() == "unlock_chipslot_1" && Hints.Card(q[0])?.Title == "LEVEL 2 · CHIP SLOT 1"
                  && q.Count == (a2 == null ? 1 : 2),
                  $"{c}: 2600 EXP from level 1 reaches level 3 and queues the chip slot 1 card first: LEVEL 2 · CHIP SLOT 1 ({Character.Level}, peak {Character.Peak}; {string.Join(",", q)})");
            if (a2 == null) GD.Print($"PEND walls: {c} has no ability 2 for its LEVEL 3 · {key} card (level_walls check 9)");
            else Check(q.Count == 2 && q[1] == "unlock_ability_2" && Hints.Card(q[1])?.Title == $"LEVEL 3 · {key} · {a2.Name.ToUpperInvariant()}" && Hints.Card(q[1])?.Body == a2.Blurb,
                       $"{c}: ...then ability 2's: LEVEL 3 · {key} · {a2.Name.ToUpperInvariant()}, with its blurb ({(q.Count > 1 ? Hints.Card(q[1])?.Title : "none")})");
            Unmeet(ids);
        }

        // THE PILOT WINDOW'S NEXT LINE at levels 3, 12 and 14, drawn
        Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
        var said = new List<string>();
        foreach (int p in new[] { 3, 12, 14 })
        {
            Character.Peak = p; await Wait(0.05);
            said.Add(Nodes<PilotWindow>().SelectMany(w => Nodes<Label>(w)).FirstOrDefault(l => l.Name == "Next")?.Text);
        }
        Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(said.SequenceEqual(new[] { "NEXT  LEVEL 4 · CHIP SLOT 2", "NEXT  LEVEL 14 · CHIP SLOT 6", "every wall open" }),
              $"the PILOT window names the next wall: at level 3 chip slot 2, at 12 chip slot 6, at 14 nothing ({string.Join(" / ", said)})");

        // CHECK 3, LIVE: at level 1, a capital, a freighter and a light press ability 1 through the host's gate
        // (DoAbility) and it acts; the bar's locked set is exactly ability 2 and 3 (the spec's own walls,
        // level_walls s.1, kits_v31 s.3.6 -- the static twin at WallChecks excludes the same two rows);
        // and Space on a gunship 300-600 u off, inside the gun's reach, at a varied bearing, lands
        foreach (var c in new[] { ShipClass.Battleship, ShipClass.FreightHauler, ShipClass.LightEcho })
        {
            await At(c, 1);
            var s = Me; var a1 = Unlocks.Nth(c, 1);
            var expectShut = new[] { Unlocks.Nth(c, 2), Unlocks.Nth(c, 3) }.Where(a => a != null).Select(a => a.Id).OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray();
            var shut = Abilities.For(c).Where(a => !a.Open && AbilityBar.StateOf(s, a.Id, null).Locked).Select(a => a.Id).OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToList();
            // the guns first: ability 1 after (a broadside would land on the gunship too)
            float reach = Math.Min(600f, (float)s.Stats["main_range"] * 0.9f), bearing = VaryAngle(), dist = Vary(300f, reach);
            var from = s.Position; var at = from + Vector2.Right.Rotated(bearing) * dist;
            var mark = Mark(at); s.Rotation = bearing + Mathf.Pi / 2;
            AimWorld(at); await Wait(0.5);
            double hp0 = mark.Hp;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && mark.Hp >= hp0; i++)
            {
                mark.Position = at; s.Position = from; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; AimWorld(at);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space);
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && mark.Hp >= hp0; i++) { mark.Position = at; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            double dealt = hp0 - mark.Hp;
            mark.TakeDamage(1e12); await Wait(0.1);
            s.Sl(a1.Id).Cool = 0;
            string b1 = Sn(s, a1.Id);
            doAb.Invoke(s, new object[] { a1.Id, 0 });
            await Wait(0.1);
            string a1After = Sn(s, a1.Id);
            Check(s.Peak == 1 && shut.SequenceEqual(expectShut) && a1After != b1 && dealt > 0,
                  $"{c} at level 1: the bar's locked set is exactly ability 2 and 3 when this class has them ({string.Join(",", shut)} vs {string.Join(",", expectShut)}), its guns land on a gunship {dist:0} u off ({dealt:0.0} dealt), and its {a1.Name} (ability 1) acts through the host's gate ({b1} -> {a1After})");
        }

        // CHECK 4, THE OWNER'S REFUSAL: ability 2 at level 2 says LOCKED · L3 and nothing moves; at 3 it acts
        foreach (var c in new[] { ShipClass.Destroyer, ShipClass.HeavyWarden, ShipClass.LightEcho })
        {
            var a2 = Unlocks.Nth(c, 2);
            if (a2 == null) { GD.Print($"PEND walls: {c} has no ability 2 to refuse at level 2 (level_walls check 4)"); continue; }
            foreach (int p in new[] { 2, 3 })
            {
                await At(c, p);
                var s = Me; var mark = Mark(VaryNear(s.Position, 300f)); H.SelectTarget(mark); await Wait(0.1);
                s.Sl(a2.Id).Cool = 0;
                string before = Sn(s, a2.Id);
                s.UseAbility(a2.Id, mark.NetId); await Wait(0.3);
                string after = Sn(s, a2.Id), note = s.FailNote(a2.Id);
                if (p == 2)
                    Check(note == "LOCKED · L3" && before == after && AbilityBar.StateOf(s, a2.Id, mark).Fail,
                          $"{c} at level 2: {a2.Name} (ability 2) is refused with LOCKED · L3 and nothing moves ({note}; {before} -> {after})");
                else
                    Check(note == null && before != after, $"{c} at level 3: {a2.Name} acts ({note}; {before} -> {after})");
                mark.TakeDamage(1e12);
            }
        }

        // CHECK 5, THE HOST'S GATE: ability 3 delivered straight to DoAbility (a guest's request, past its own
        // refusal) does nothing at level 5 and acts at 6
        foreach (var c in new[] { ShipClass.Battleship, ShipClass.Destroyer, ShipClass.HeavyWarrior })
        {
            var a3 = Unlocks.Nth(c, 3);
            if (a3 == null) { GD.Print($"PEND walls: {c} has no ability 3 for the host's gate at level 5 (level_walls check 5)"); continue; }
            foreach (int p in new[] { 5, 6 })
            {
                await At(c, p);
