                var s = Me; var mark = Mark(VaryNear(s.Position, 300f)); H.SelectTarget(mark); await Wait(0.1);
                s.Sl(a3.Id).Cool = 0;
                string before = Sn(s, a3.Id);
                doAb.Invoke(s, new object[] { a3.Id, mark.NetId }); await Wait(0.3);
                string after = Sn(s, a3.Id);
                Check(p == 5 ? before == after : before != after,
                      $"{c} at level {p}: {a3.Name} (ability 3) sent straight to the host {(p == 5 ? "does nothing" : "acts")} ({before} -> {after})");
                mark.TakeDamage(1e12);
            }
        }

        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e12);
        Character.Class = cls0; Character.Level = lv0; Character.Exp = exp0; Character.Points = pts0; Character.Peak = peak0;
        H.PilotChanged(); await Wait(0.1);
        var me = Me; me.Position = here0; me.Rotation = rot0; me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.Restore(me.MaxHp, true, 0);
        Character.Save();
    }

    // ── CHIP SLOTS AND KINDS (F15; level_walls checks 6, 6b and 7) ────────────────────────────────
    // Six chip slots on every hull, opened by the pilot's highest level; at most 3 Combat and 3
    // Utility chips; a file never loses a chip it cannot fly. A capital, a heavy and a light, with
    // their class rows' hulls as literals: battleship 300, warden 140, echo 90.
    void ChipChecks()
    {
        var three = new[] { (c: ShipClass.Battleship, hull: 300.0), (c: ShipClass.HeavyWarden, hull: 140.0), (c: ShipClass.LightEcho, hull: 90.0) };
        string[] With(ShipClass c, params string[] chips)
        {
            var l = Equipment.Default(c);
            for (int k = 0; k < chips.Length; k++) l[5 + k] = chips[k];
            return l;
        }
        double Hull(ShipClass c, string[] l, int peak) =>
            new ShipStats(c, Equipment.Bonuses(c, l, peak, NoLevels), Equipment.Adds(c, l, peak, NoLevels))["hull"];
        const string A = "chip_armour_1", C = "chip_combat_1";     // Armour Chip I: +8% hull (Utility); Combat Chip I: -5% hull (Combat)
        foreach (var (c, hull) in three)
        {
            // CHECK 6: open slots at levels 1, 2, 4, 8 and 14 are 0, 1, 2, 3 and 6 -- through Sanitize and the sheet
            var alt = With(c, A, C, A, C, A, C);
            var peaks = new[] { 1, 2, 4, 8, 14 };
            var open = peaks.Select(p => Equipment.Sanitize(c, alt, p).Skip(5).Count(x => x != "")).ToArray();
            var hulls = peaks.Select(p => Hull(c, alt, p)).ToArray();
            var want = new[] { 1.0, 1.08, 1.03, 1.11, 1.09 }.Select(m => hull * m).ToArray();
            Check(open.SequenceEqual(new[] { 0, 1, 2, 3, 6 }) && hulls.Zip(want).All(p => Math.Abs(p.First - p.Second) < 1e-6),
                  $"{c}: chip slots open at levels 1, 2, 4, 8, 14 are 0, 1, 2, 3, 6 -- Armour, Combat, Armour, Combat... fitted, the sheet flies only the open ones: {string.Join(" / ", want.Select(w => w.ToString("0.##")))} hull ({string.Join(",", open)}; {string.Join(" / ", hulls.Select(h => h.ToString("0.##")))})");
            // THE KIND CAP: four Armour Chips and two Combat Chips at level 14 fly three Armour: the fourth is emptied
            var four = Equipment.Sanitize(c, With(c, A, A, A, A, C, C), 14);
            Check(four.Skip(5).SequenceEqual(new[] { A, A, A, "", C, C }) && Math.Abs(Hull(c, With(c, A, A, A, A, C, C), 14) - hull * 1.14) < 1e-6,
                  $"{c}: at most 3 Utility chips -- a fourth Armour Chip is emptied, so the sheet reads {hull * 1.14:0.##} hull, not {hull * 1.22:0.##} ({string.Join(",", four.Skip(5))})");
            // CHECK 6b, what EQUIP says (Equipment.ChipFit): the first open empty slot, or why not
            var f1 = Equipment.ChipFit(Equipment.Default(c), 1, Equipment.ById(A));
            var f4 = Equipment.ChipFit(With(c, C), 4, Equipment.ById(A));
            var f8 = Equipment.ChipFit(With(c, C, A, A), 8, Equipment.ById("chip_engine_1"));
            var f14 = Equipment.ChipFit(With(c, C, C, C), 14, Equipment.ById("chip_combat_2"));
            var f14u = Equipment.ChipFit(With(c, C, C, C), 14, Equipment.ById(A));
            Check(f1 == (-1, "next chip slot opens at level 2") && f4.slot == 6 && f4.why == null && f8 == (-1, "next chip slot opens at level 10")
                  && f14 == (-1, "at most 3 Combat chips") && f14u.slot == 8 && f14u.why == null,
                  $"{c}: EQUIP at level 1 waits for level 2; at level 4 the second slot takes it; at level 8 with three on it waits for level 10; a fourth Combat chip is refused, a Utility one takes slot 4 ({f1}, {f4}, {f8}, {f14}, {f14u})");
            // CHECK 7, SAVES NEVER DELETE: five Combat Chips fitted in a file, one more in its hold. Each
            // pilot keeps all six: as many fitted as its level opens (3 at most, the kind cap), the rest held.
            foreach (var (level, fitted) in new[] { (1, 0), (3, 1), (14, 3) })
            {
                var f = new ConfigFile(); f.SetValue("id", "version", Game.Version); f.SetValue("id", "name", "ChipFile"); f.SetValue("id", "class", (int)c);
                f.SetValue("progress", "level", level); f.SetValue("progress", "peak", level);
                f.SetValue("equipment", c.ToString(), string.Join(",", With(c, C, C, C, C, C)));
                f.SetValue("gear_hold", C, 1);
                f.Save($"{Character.Dir}/chipfile.cfg");
                bool ok = Character.Load("chipfile");
                int on = Character.LoadoutFor(c).Skip(5).Count(x => x == C), held = Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault(C);
                Check(ok && on == fitted && held == 6 - fitted,
                      $"{c}: a file at level {level} with five Combat Chips fitted and one held loads {fitted} fitted and {6 - fitted} held -- six owned before and after ({on} + {held})");
                Character.Delete("chipfile");
            }
        }
        // ...and a file with no `peak` reads its level: level 3, one slot open, one of the five fitted
        {
            var f = new ConfigFile(); f.SetValue("id", "version", Game.Version); f.SetValue("id", "name", "ChipFile");
            f.SetValue("progress", "level", 3);
            f.SetValue("equipment", nameof(ShipClass.Destroyer), string.Join(",", With(ShipClass.Destroyer, C, C, C, C, C)));
            f.Save($"{Character.Dir}/chipfile.cfg");
            bool ok = Character.Load("chipfile");
            int on = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Destroyer).Skip(5).Count(x => x == C), held = Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault(C);
            Check(ok && on == 1 && held == 4, $"a file at level 3 with no peak and five Combat Chips fitted loads 1 fitted and 4 held ({on} + {held})");
            Character.Delete("chipfile");
        }
        Character.NewBlank();
    }

    async Task Solo()
    {
        Check(H != null, "hub loaded");
        Check(Count<PlayerShip>() == 1 && Me != null, "one ship named Ship_1");
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 0, "no creator on entry");

        // ── THE TUTORIAL: a new pilot's first hub entry shows FLYING in the corner; THE BASE waits ──
        {
            var hn = H.Hints;
            var card = Nodes<PanelContainer>(hn).FirstOrDefault(p => p.Name == "HintCard");
            Label CardTitle() => Nodes<Label>(card).First();
            Check(card != null && card.Visible && CardTitle().Text == "FLYING" && Character.HintsSeen.Contains("flight") && HintQueue.Contains("base"),
                  $"a new pilot's first hub entry: FLYING in the corner, THE BASE waiting its turn ({CardTitle()?.Text}; queue {string.Join(",", HintQueue)})");
            var ctrls = Nodes<Control>(hn).ToList();
            Check(ctrls.Count >= 4 && ctrls.All(c => c.MouseFilter == Control.MouseFilterEnum.Ignore && c.FocusMode == Control.FocusModeEnum.None),
                  $"the hint card never takes input: every part of it lets clicks through and never holds the keyboard ({ctrls.Count} controls)");
            // the Esc menu covers the game: the card's clock holds
            var ageF = typeof(Hints).GetField("_age", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
            H.ToggleEscMenu(); await Wait(0.1);
            double age0 = (double)ageF.GetValue(hn); await Wait(2.0); double age1 = (double)ageF.GetValue(hn);
            H.ToggleEscMenu(); await Wait(1.0); double age2 = (double)ageF.GetValue(hn);
            Check(Math.Abs(age1 - age0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(age2 - age1 - 1.0) < 0.05, $"the card's clock holds while the Esc menu is open ({age0:0.00} -> {age1:0.00} in 2 s; +{age2 - age1:0.00} in 1 s after)");
            // ...and under the ship menu, and the pilot's stats (K)
            Priv(H, "OpenCreator"); await Wait(0.1);
            double ageC0 = (double)ageF.GetValue(hn); await Wait(0.5); double ageC1 = (double)ageF.GetValue(hn);
            bool creatorUp = H.CreatorOpen;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
            double ageK0 = (double)ageF.GetValue(hn); await Wait(0.5); double ageK1 = (double)ageF.GetValue(hn);
            bool statsUp = H.StatsOpen;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(creatorUp && statsUp && !H.CreatorOpen && !H.StatsOpen && Math.Abs(ageC1 - ageC0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(ageK1 - ageK0) < 1e-9,
                  $"...and under the ship menu and the pilot's stats (K) ({ageC0:0.00} -> {ageC1:0.00}; {ageK0:0.00} -> {ageK1:0.00})");
            // 8 s in full, a 1 s fade, then the next
            double fadeAt = -1, halfAlpha = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && CardTitle().Text == "FLYING"; i++)
            {
                double age = (double)ageF.GetValue(hn);
                if (fadeAt < 0 && card.Modulate.A < 1f) fadeAt = age;
                if (halfAlpha < 0 && age >= 8.5) halfAlpha = card.Modulate.A;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            Check(Math.Abs(fadeAt - 8.0) < 0.05 && Math.Abs(halfAlpha - 0.5) < 0.1 && CardTitle().Text == "THE BASE" && card.Visible,
                  $"a card stays 8 s in full, fades over 1 s, and the next takes its place (fading at {fadeAt:0.00} s, half gone at 8.5 s: {halfAlpha:0.00})");
            // several at once queue in order; one already waiting is not queued twice
            H.Hints.Meet("tio"); H.Hints.Meet("raid"); H.Hints.Meet("tio");
            Check(CardTitle().Text == "THE BASE" && HintQueue.SequenceEqual(new[] { "tio", "raid" }),
                  $"hints met at once wait their turn, in order, each once ({string.Join(",", HintQueue)})");
            // remembered per character: on its file within 3 s -- and never shown again
            typeof(Yard).GetField("_saveCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H.Yard, 999.0);
            await Wait(0.4); bool early = ((string)OnDisk("hints", "seen", "")).Split(',').Contains("base");
            await Wait(2.6); bool late = ((string)OnDisk("hints", "seen", "")).Split(',').Contains("base");
            H.Hints.Meet("flight");                               // shown, and long finished
            Check(!early && late && !HintQueue.Contains("flight") && HintShowing != "flight",
                  $"a hint shown is on the pilot's file within 3 s, and never shown again (at 0.4 s {early}, at 3.0 s {late}; queue {string.Join(",", HintQueue)})");
            typeof(Yard).GetField("_saveCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H.Yard, 30.0);
            // the triggers: each system the first time a pilot meets it
            var me = Me; var hd1 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"); var here = me.Position;
            Unmeet("target", "abilities", "tio", "warp", "stasis", "base");
            // west of dummy 1: every other target is more than 700 u from here
            me.Position = hd1.Position + new Vector2(-520, 0); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.1);
            bool farT = Met("target");
            me.Position = hd1.Position + new Vector2(-480, 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!farT && Met("target"), "TARGETING: met within 500 u of something to target, not at 520");
            bool noSel = Met("abilities");
            H.SelectTarget(hd1); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!noSel && Met("abilities"), "ABILITIES: met with the first target selected");
            H.ClearSelection();
            me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(610, 0); await Wait(0.1);
            bool farBase = Met("base");
            me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(590, 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!farBase && Met("base"), "THE BASE: met within 600 u of it, not at 610");
            me.Position = Hub.TioPos + new Vector2(450, 0); await Wait(0.1);
            bool farTio = Met("tio");
            me.Position = Hub.TioPos + new Vector2(350, 0); await Wait(0.1);
            bool nearTio = Met("tio");
            Unmeet("tio"); me.Position = Hub.TioPos + new Vector2(700, 0); await Wait(0.1);
            H.OpenTio(); await Wait(0.1); bool byOpen = Met("tio"); Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!farTio && nearTio && byOpen, $"THE TIO: met within 400 u of it, not at 450 -- or on opening it from anywhere ({farTio}, {nearTio}, {byOpen})");
            me.Position = here;
            H.SelectWaypoint("NEAR", here + new Vector2(0, 1450), 200f); await Wait(0.1);
            bool nearWp = Met("warp");
            H.SelectWaypoint("FAR", here + new Vector2(0, 1550), 200f); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!nearWp && Met("warp"), "WARP: met with a target or waypoint more than 1500 u away: not at 1450, at 1550");
            H.ClearSelection();
            me.TakeDamage(me.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Met("stasis"), "STASIS: met when the ship is shot down");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_stasis", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0);
            me.UseAbility("reboard", 0); await Wait(0.1); me.Hp = me.MaxHp; me.Position = here; me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            // THE OFF SWITCH, in the Esc menu: the pilot's, saved at once; the card goes with it
            H.ToggleEscMenu(); await Wait(0.1);
            var sw = Nodes<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "HintsToggle");
            bool onByDefault = sw != null && sw.Text == "HINTS: ON";
            sw?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool offSaved = Character.HintsOff && (bool)OnDisk("hints", "off", false) && sw.Text == "HINTS: OFF";
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            H.Hints.Meet("loot");
            bool gone = !card.Visible && HintQueue.Count == 0 && !Met("loot");
            sw?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(onByDefault && offSaved && gone && !Character.HintsOff && !(bool)OnDisk("hints", "off", true) && sw.Text == "HINTS: ON",
                  $"the Esc menu turns hints off -- for this pilot, saved at once -- and the card and its queue go with them ({onByDefault}, {offSaved}, {gone})");
            H.ToggleEscMenu(); await Wait(0.1);
        }

        // the ship menu is behind REFIT in the BASE menu now, and it costs 10%
        Key(global::Godot.Key.C); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 0, "C no longer opens the ship menu");
        H.Yard.SetStock("ore", 1000); H.Yard.SetStock("salvage", 500); H.Yard.Credits = 200;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        var bp = Nodes<BasePanel>().FirstOrDefault();
        Check(bp != null, "B opens the base menu");
        // THE THEME HAS TO ACTUALLY REACH THE BUTTONS, and for the project's whole history it did
        // not: a theme set on the root window does not cross a CanvasLayer, every piece of UI here
        // hangs off one, and so every button in the game drew Godot's stock theme. It went unnoticed
        // because the stock theme is ALSO a dark rounded rectangle -- it took a deliberately red
        // stylebox and a pixel count to expose it. Compared with the palette as literals rather than
        // with Ui.Card, so this fails both when a subtree stops inheriting and when the look changes
        // by accident. Counting the buttons first: an empty list must not pass as "all themed".
        var btns = Nodes<Button>().ToList();
        var unthemed = btns.Where(b => (b.GetThemeStylebox("normal") as StyleBoxFlat) is not StyleBoxFlat sb
                                       || sb.BgColor != new Color(0.102f, 0.129f, 0.173f)
                                       || sb.BorderColor != new Color(0.184f, 0.227f, 0.302f)).ToList();
        Check(btns.Count >= 8 && unthemed.Count == 0,
              $"all {btns.Count} buttons take the game's theme, not Godot's" +
              (unthemed.Count > 0 ? $" -- {unthemed.Count} do not: {string.Join(", ", unthemed.Take(5).Select(b => b.GetParent()?.Name + "/" + b.Name))}" : ""));
        ShowTab(bp, "REFIT");
        var reset = Nodes<Button>(bp).First(b => b.Name == "Reset");
        // The docked hauler keeps loading from stock every frame, so amounts are read
        // immediately around each click (a button press runs synchronously).
        double o0 = H.Yard.Stock("ore");
        reset.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 0 && H.Yard.Stock("ore") == o0, "first RESET click charges nothing and opens nothing");
        await Wait(0.05);
        Check(reset.Text.StartsWith("CLICK AGAIN"), "it asks for a second click");
        double o1 = H.Yard.Stock("ore"), s1 = H.Yard.Stock("salvage"), c1 = H.Yard.Credits;
        reset.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 1 && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Stock("ore") - o1 * 0.9) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Stock("salvage") - s1 * 0.9) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - c1 * 0.9) < 1e-9,
              "second click: exactly 10% of ore, salvage and credits taken; the ship menu opens");
        await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0, "the base menu steps aside for it");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0, "B does nothing while the ship menu owns the screen");
        // THE CARD SAYS WHAT THE SHIP WILL HAVE: the same sheet the ship fights with, this pilot's
        // parts in it, rather than a hull hand-written into each class's blurb. A stock battleship
        // carries no chips, so its card reads the class's 300.
        {
            var cards = Nodes<Button>(Nodes<CharacterCreator>().First()).Where(b2 => b2.Text.Contains(" hull")).ToList();
            double bsHull = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship,
                                          Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Battleship, Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Battleship), Character.Peak, Character.GearLevel),
                                          Equipment.Adds(ShipClass.Battleship, Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Battleship), Character.Peak, Character.GearLevel))["hull"];
            var bsCard = cards.FirstOrDefault(b2 => b2.Text.StartsWith("BATTLESHIP"));
            Check(cards.Count == 3 && bsCard != null && Math.Abs(bsHull - 300) < 1e-9
                  && bsCard.Text.Contains($"{bsHull:0} hull") && !Classes.All.Any(c2 => c2.Blurb.Contains(" hull.")),
                  $"a class card prints the hull the ship will actually have -- the sheet's, with the starting kit in it ({bsHull:0} on the battleship's card, and no class blurb writes one any more)");
        }
        foreach (var c in Nodes<CharacterCreator>()) c.Close();
        await Wait(0.1);

        // the network options are folded behind one button
        var netToggle = Nodes<Button>().First(b => b.Name == "NetToggle");
        var netOpts = Nodes<VBoxContainer>().First(b => b.Name == "NetOptions");
        Check(!netOpts.Visible && netToggle.Text.Contains("MULTIPLAYER") && netToggle.Text.Contains("offline"),
              $"network options start folded behind '{netToggle.Text}'");
        netToggle.ButtonPressed = true; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(netOpts.Visible, "the button unfolds them");
        Check(H.GetNodeOrNull("Stars") is CanvasLayer sky && sky.Layer <= -100, "background stars on a deep layer");
        Check(Nodes<PanelContainer>().Any(p => p.Name == "HudPanel"), "stats line sits on a panel");

        var le = Nodes<LineEdit>().First();
        le.GrabFocus(); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Hub.ControlsLocked, "controls locked while typing");
        Click(new Vector2(1200, 700)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(GetViewport().GuiGetFocusOwner() == null && !Hub.ControlsLocked, "click off releases focus");
        netToggle.ButtonPressed = false; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(!netOpts.Visible, "and folds them away again");

        await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
        var ship = Me;

        // ── no class hotkeys; class-specific controls line ──
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Key2); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Me.Class == ShipClass.Battleship, "2 no longer changes class");
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier); Key(global::Godot.Key.Key1); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Me.Class == ShipClass.Carrier, "1 no longer changes class");
        var help = (Label)H.GetType().GetField("_help", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H);
        Check(help.Text.StartsWith("CARRIER") && !help.Text.Contains("mouse aims"), "controls line is carrier-specific");
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(help.Text.StartsWith("BATTLESHIP") && help.Text.Contains("mouse aims the main guns"), "controls line is battleship-specific");
        Check(Abilities.ControlsHint((ShipClass)99) == "placeholder", "an undefined class shows 'placeholder'");
        ship = Me;

        // the owner's line-art hull, its painted turrets painted over: the moving turrets stand where
        // they stood, on the spine, and point defence on the stern
        var hullSpr = ship.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First();
        Check(hullSpr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("battleship_hull.png") && Math.Abs(hullSpr.Texture.GetHeight() * hullSpr.Scale.Y - 378f) < 0.5f
              && Math.Abs(ship.MyArt.HalfWidth - 43.875f) < 1e-4,
              "battleship is the owner's line-art hull made twice as wide, then 35% and 25% larger: 378 u (224 x 1.35 x 1.25), a 43.875 u half-beam");
        var tsprites = Nodes<Turret>(ship).Select(x => x.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().FirstOrDefault()).ToList();
        Check(tsprites.Count == 6 && tsprites.All(x => x != null)
              && tsprites.Count(x => x.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("turret_main.png")) == 4
              && tsprites.Count(x => x.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("turret_pd.png")) == 2
              && Nodes<Turret>(ship).All(x => x.PointDefense ? x.Offset.Y > 150f : Mathf.Abs(x.Offset.X) < 1e-6f),
              "four main turrets on the spine and two point-defence turrets on the stern, in the owner's turret art");
        // DAMAGE NUMBERS: small figures where damage lands -- gold for damage dealt, red for damage
        // taken -- floating up and gone after a second or so; hits in quick succession add into one
        {
            var dn = H.GetNodeOrNull<Popups>("Popups");
            var dum = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            dum.TakeDamage(5); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            dum.TakeDamage(2.5); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            ship.Hit(4, ship.Position + new Vector2(60, 0), "test");
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var dealt = dn?.Figures.Where(f => f.Who == dum.GetInstanceId()).ToList();
            var taken = dn?.Figures.Where(f => f.Who == ship.GetInstanceId()).ToList();
            Check(dealt?.Count == 1 && Math.Abs(dealt[0].Amount - 7.5) < 1e-6 && dealt[0].Kind == Pop.Dealt && dealt[0].At.DistanceTo(dum.Position) < 15f,
                  $"damage dealt shows as one gold figure where it landed: 5 and 2.5 a frame apart read 7.5 ({dealt?.Count} figure(s), {dealt?.FirstOrDefault()?.Amount:0.##})");
            Check(taken?.Count == 1 && Math.Abs(taken[0].Amount - 4) < 0.1 && taken[0].Kind == Pop.Taken,
                  $"damage taken shows red on the ship that took it ({taken?.FirstOrDefault()?.Amount:0.##})");
            // ── EXP IS ITS OWN READOUT, over the pilot's own hull ──
            // Asked for as a copy of the damage numbers; built as a row of the one mechanism, which
            // is why the file is named for what it does rather than for the first thing that used
            // it. Bolder, whiter, larger, higher and longer-lived than a hit -- and it NEVER merges,
            // because two kills adding into one bigger figure would read as one bigger kill.
            int popsBefore = dn.Figures.Count;
            Popups.Exp(ship, 650); Popups.Exp(ship, 400);
            var expPops = dn.Figures.Where(f => f.Kind == Pop.Exp).ToList();
            var expRow = Pop.Of(Pop.Exp); var dealtRow = Pop.Of(Pop.Dealt);
            Check(expPops.Count == 2 && dn.Figures.Count == popsBefore + 2
                  && Math.Abs(expPops[0].Amount - 650) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(expPops[1].Amount - 400) < 1e-6
                  && expPops.All(f => f.At.Y < ship.Position.Y && Math.Abs(f.At.X - ship.Position.X) < 1e-3)
                  && expRow.Tint == new Color(1f, 1f, 1f) && expRow.Merge == 0 && expRow.Prefix == "+"
                  && expRow.Size > dealtRow.Size && expRow.Rise > dealtRow.Rise && expRow.Life > dealtRow.Life,
                  $"EXP rises off the pilot's own ship as its own kind of readout -- white, larger, higher, longer, and two kills are TWO figures where two hits would have merged into one ({expPops.Count} figures of {expPops.Sum(f => f.Amount):0} over the hull)");
            foreach (var f in expPops) f.Age = 99;                      // gone before the next block counts figures
            await Wait(1.3);
            Check(dn != null && dn.Figures.All(f => f.Who != dum.GetInstanceId() && f.Who != ship.GetInstanceId()), "the figures fade out after a second or so");
            ship.Hp = ship.MaxHp;
        }
        Check(tsprites.All(x => x.Modulate == ship.Accent) && hullSpr.Modulate == ship.Main && ship.Accent != ship.Main,
              "the turrets wear the pilot's accent colour, the hull its hull colour");
        // the fields: each one's nearest edge 1500 u from the base -- room for a blockade
        {
            var rocks = H.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().Where(x => x.Texture != null && x.Texture.ResourcePath.Contains("asteroid_")).ToList();
            float beltEdge = rocks.Min(x => x.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - x.Texture.GetWidth() * x.Scale.X * 0.34f);
            var wreck = H.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First(x => x.Texture != null && x.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("behemoth_wreck.png"));
            float wreckEdge = wreck.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - 340f;                 // its visible edge toward the base
            GD.Print($"  field edges from the base: belt {beltEdge:0} u, wreck {wreckEdge:0} u");
            Check(rocks.Count == 9 && Math.Abs(beltEdge - 1500) < 8 && Math.Abs(wreckEdge - 1500) < 8,
                  "the mining belt's and the wreck's nearest edges are both 1500 u from the base");
        }
        // A PACKET FOR A WORLD ON ITS WAY OUT: a peer's ship report (20 Hz) or identity can land on a Hub
        // that has left the tree (a scene change, the game quitting), where Multiplayer is null. It must
        // be dropped, not thrown: it threw on the arena host after every check had passed.
        {
            var loose = new Hub();
            bool dropped = true;
            try
            {
                // (the report names its ship's owner: one that is not the sender, which must not move it either)
                typeof(Hub).GetMethod("NetShipState", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)
                           .Invoke(loose, new object[] { 5, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, false, false, 0f, 0f, 0f, false });
                typeof(Hub).GetMethod("NetIdentity", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)
                           .Invoke(loose, new object[] { 5, "X", Colors.White, Colors.White, 0, new int[0], 1, 1, new string[0], new string[0], new int[0], "x" });
                dropped &= !Net.FromPlayer(loose, out int who) && who == 0;
            }
            catch (Exception) { dropped = false; }
            loose.Free();
            Check(dropped, "a ship report or identity landing on a world already out of the tree is dropped, not thrown");
        }
        // THE OUTPOSTS: 3000 u out on the diagonals, named for their corners, each with its label
        {
            double c = 3000 / Math.Sqrt(2);      // 2121.3 u east or west, north or south
            var want = new[] { ("SE", new Vector2((float)c, (float)c)), ("NE", new Vector2((float)c, (float)-c)), ("NW", new Vector2((float)-c, (float)-c)), ("SW", new Vector2((float)-c, (float)c)) };
            var opLabels = H.GetChildren().OfType<WorldLabel>().ToList();
            bool built = want.All(w => H.GetNodeOrNull<Sprite2D>("Outpost" + w.Item1) is { } o && o.Position.DistanceTo(w.Item2) < 0.5f
                                       && o.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("outpost.png") && Math.Abs(o.Texture.GetHeight() * o.Scale.Y - 170f) < 0.5f
                                       && opLabels.Any(l => l.Text == "OUTPOST " + w.Item1 && l.Position.DistanceTo(w.Item2) < 120f));
            Check(built && Hub.Outposts.Select(o => o.name).SequenceEqual(new[] { "SE", "NE", "NW", "SW" }),
                  "four outposts 3000 u out on the diagonals (±2121, ±2121), labelled OUTPOST SE / NE / NW / SW");
        }
        var tioS = H.GetNodeOrNull<Sprite2D>("TIO");
        Check(tioS != null && tioS.Position == Hub.TioPos && Math.Abs(tioS.Texture.GetHeight() * tioS.Scale.Y - Hub.TioHeight) < 0.5f,
              "the Threat Intelligence Operations building stands south-west of the base");
        Check(Math.Abs(Wings.Of(WingKind.Bomber).Length - 37.5f * 0.75f) < 1e-4, "bombers are 25% smaller (28.1 u)");
        var baseSpr = H.GetNode<Sprite2D>("Base");
        Check(baseSpr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("base_station.png") && Math.Abs(baseSpr.Scale.X - Hub.BaseScale) < 0.001f, "the base is the new station sprite");
        Check(ship.Position.Y - Classes.Art(ship.Class).Length / 2 > Hub.BaseBottom, "a new ship spawns clear of the base");


        // ── the idle economy: layout ──
        var Y = H.Yard;
        var portal = H.GetNode<Node2D>("Portal");
        var hauler = Y.Hauler;
        Check(portal.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY && Hub.HaulerPad.Y == Hub.LaneY, "portal and the hauler's pad share one horizontal lane");
        var baseImg = H.GetNode<Sprite2D>("Base").Texture.GetImage();
        // sample the art across the pad's centre row, wherever the image puts it
        int padRow = (int)(Hub.HaulerPad.Y / Hub.BaseScale + baseImg.GetHeight() / 2f);
        int padW = 0; for (int x = 0; x < baseImg.GetWidth(); x++) if (baseImg.GetPixel(x, padRow).A > 0.2f) padW++;
        Check(Math.Abs(padW * Hub.BaseScale - 140f) < 3f, $"the bottom pad is 140 u wide ({padW * Hub.BaseScale:0} u)");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && hauler.Position == Hub.HaulerPad && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, Hauler.East)) < 0.01f
              && Math.Abs(hauler.VisualScale - Economy.HaulerLandedScale) < 1e-4, "hauler sits landed (65%) on the pad, facing the portal");
        // ── THE PRACTICE RANGE: centred south of the base, and out of the hauler's way ──
        // The hauler's own point defence takes any LIGHT craft inside 400 u -- a practice fighter
        // included -- and a hunter's 90 u blast lands where the hauler will be 12 s on, on whoever is
        // practising there, so every target keeps 500 u from every leg the hauler flies: the lone run
        // out and back, and the escort pad -> SE -> NE -> NW -> SW -> portal, each taken PAST ITS END
        // (the 12 s guess carries a blast beyond the stop). And 500 u from every lane to an outpost,
        // and from the line its couriers fly -- berth to berth, the base's arm to the outpost's
        // clamp, which is off the lane's own line. Read off what the hub BUILT, which must be its
        // table row for row.
        {
            var rangeTargets = Nodes<TargetDummy>().ToList();
            var escortPts = new[] { Hub.HaulerPad }.Concat(Hub.EscortRoute).ToArray();
            var haulLegs = new List<(Vector2 a, Vector2 b)> { (Hub.HaulerPad, Hub.PortalPos), (Hub.PortalPos, Hub.HaulerPad) };
            for (int i = 0; i + 1 < escortPts.Length; i++) haulLegs.Add((escortPts[i], escortPts[i + 1]));
            float PastEnd(Vector2 p, (Vector2 a, Vector2 b) leg)
            {
                var dir = (leg.b - leg.a).Normalized();
                return p.DistanceTo(leg.a + dir * Mathf.Max(0f, (p - leg.a).Dot(dir)));
            }
            float OnSegment(Vector2 p, Vector2 a, Vector2 b)
            {
                var d = b - a;
                return p.DistanceTo(a + d * Mathf.Clamp((p - a).Dot(d) / d.LengthSquared(), 0f, 1f));
            }
            float legClear = rangeTargets.Min(t => haulLegs.Min(l => PastEnd(t.Position, l)));
            var flights = Nodes<Courier>().Select(c => (a: c.HomeBerth, b: c.AwayBerth)).ToList();
            float laneClear = rangeTargets.Min(t => Enumerable.Range(0, Lanes.All.Length).Select(i => (a: Lanes.Ends(i).near, b: Lanes.Ends(i).far))
                                                             .Concat(flights).Min(l => OnSegment(t.Position, l.a, l.b)));
            bool asTable = rangeTargets.Count == Hub.PracticeTargets.Length
                && Hub.PracticeTargets.All(r => rangeTargets.Any(t => t.Number == r.number && t.Position == r.at && t.Armed == r.armed && t.Fighter == r.fighter));
            Check(asTable && rangeTargets.Count == 4 && legClear >= 500f && laneClear >= 500f && flights.Count == Lanes.All.Sum(l => l.Drones),
                  $"the practice range is the hub's table, four targets, out of the hauler's way: every one 500 u or more from every leg it flies, past each leg's end -- beyond its 400 u point defence and a hunter's 90 u blast -- and from every lane to an outpost and every courier's flight on it (nearest leg {legClear:0} u, nearest lane or flight {laneClear:0} u, {flights.Count} flights)");
            float rangeWest = rangeTargets.Min(t => t.Position.X), rangeEast = rangeTargets.Max(t => t.Position.X);
            float rangeNear = rangeTargets.Min(t => t.Position.Y - Hub.BasePos.Y);
            float rangeMid = (rangeWest + rangeEast) / 2f - Hub.BasePos.X;
            float fromSpawn = rangeTargets.Min(t => t.Position.DistanceTo(ship.Position));
            Check(rangeNear >= 1200f && Mathf.Abs(rangeMid) <= 50f && fromSpawn >= 800f,
                  $"...and centred south of the base: every target 1200 u or more below it (the nearest {rangeNear:0} u), the range's middle {rangeMid:0} u off its line, {fromSpawn:0} u from where a new ship spawns");
        }

        // ── miners and salvagers ──
        var miner = Y.Gatherers.First(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
        var salv = Y.Gatherers.First(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager);
        Check(Y.Gatherers.Count == 2 && miner.Hold == 100 && salv.Hold == 100 && miner.Rate == 2 && salv.Rate == 2, "one of each: 100 hold, 2 per second");
        for (int i = 0; i < 6000 && !(miner.Beaming && salv.Beaming); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(miner.Beaming && salv.Beaming, "both reach their work and start beaming");
        await Wait(0.2);
        var bolts = (System.Collections.IList)typeof(Gatherer).GetField("_bolts", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(salv);
        var nose = salv.ToGlobal(new Vector2(0, -Gatherer.Length * 0.45f)); var aim = (salv.BeamTo - nose).Angle();
        float cone = 0; foreach (Vector2[] bl in bolts) cone = Mathf.Max(cone, Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(aim, (bl[^1] - nose).Angle())));
        Check(bolts.Count >= 2 && cone < 0.33f, $"salvager's beam is a narrow scan: {bolts.Count} arcs within {cone:0.00} rad of centre");
        Check(miner.BeamTo.DistanceTo(Y.WorkSpot(miner).target) < 0.01f, "miner's single beam holds on its asteroid");
        double mined0 = Y.Delivered("ore"), salv0 = Y.Delivered("salvage");
        for (int i = 0; i < 12000 && (Y.Delivered("ore") - mined0 < 99.9 || Y.Delivered("salvage") - salv0 < 99.9); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(Y.Delivered("ore") - mined0 >= 99.9 && Y.Delivered("salvage") - salv0 >= 99.9, "a full hold (100) of each is unloaded through an arm");

        // ── upgrades, by tab ──
        Y.Credits = 1000;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        var panel = Nodes<BasePanel>().First();
        Button Buy(string id)
        {
            ShowTab(panel, Economy.ById(id).Tab);                  // an upgrade lives on its own tab
            return Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(panel).First(r => r.Name == "Up_" + id)).First(b => b.Name == "Buy");
        }
        Check(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(panel).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Up_")) == 5, "MINERS tab: five upgrades (count, hold, engines, beam, hull)");
        // EVERY GATHERER'S FIVE ROWS ARE ONE SHAPE, GENERATED FROM ONE PLACE. They were written
        // out twice -- same kind, same price, same growth, same cap -- differing only in an id
        // prefix, a label and a unit, so a third gatherer was five more copies to keep in step.
        // The numbers are the request's literals, never Economy's own constants: a check that
        // reads the constant cannot catch the constant being wrong.
        bool oneShape = Gathering.All.Length > 0;
        foreach (var d in Gathering.All)
        {
            var rows = new[] { Economy.ById(d.CountId), Economy.ById(d.HoldId), Economy.ById(d.SpeedId), Economy.ById(d.RateId), Economy.ById(d.HullId) };
            if (rows.Any(u => u == null)) { oneShape = false; break; }
            oneShape &= rows.All(u => u.Tab == d.Tab) && Economy.All.Count(u => u.Tab == d.Tab) == 5;
            oneShape &= rows[0].Kind == Economy.Kind.Count && rows[0].BaseCost == 400 && rows[0].Max == 4 && rows[0].BaseValue == 1 && rows[0].Step == 1;
            oneShape &= rows.Skip(1).All(u => u.Kind == Economy.Kind.Percent && u.Max == int.MaxValue && Math.Abs(u.Per - 0.10) < 1e-12);
            oneShape &= rows[1].BaseCost == 100 && rows[2].BaseCost == 100 && rows[3].BaseCost == 100 && rows[4].BaseCost == 125;
            oneShape &= rows[1].BaseValue == 100 && rows[2].BaseValue == 110 && rows[3].BaseValue == 2 && rows[4].BaseValue == 120;
            oneShape &= rows[1].Unit == d.Unit && rows[2].Unit == "u/s" && rows[3].Unit == d.Unit + "/s" && rows[4].Unit == "hull";
            oneShape &= rows[0].Name == d.Name + "s" && rows[1].Name == d.Name + " hold" && rows[2].Name == d.Name + " engines"
                        && rows[3].Name == d.RateName && rows[4].Name == d.Name + " hull";
            oneShape &= rows.Select(u => u.Id).SequenceEqual(Economy.All.Where(u => u.Tab == d.Tab).Select(u => u.Id));
        }
        Check(oneShape && Gathering.All.Length == 2 && Gathering.Resources.SequenceEqual(new[] { "ore", "salvage" })
              && Economy.All.Take(10).Select(u => u.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "miner_count", "miner_hold", "miner_speed", "mine_rate", "miner_hull",
                                                                             "salvager_count", "salvager_hold", "salvager_speed", "salvage_rate", "salvager_hull" }),
              $"each gatherer's five rows are one generated shape -- 400/100/100/100/125 cr, 1 ship, 100 hold, 110 u/s, 2 a second, 120 hull, the count capped at 4 purchases -- in the order the wire reads them, for each of {Gathering.All.Length} gatherers ({string.Join(", ", Gathering.Resources)})");
        // The footnote called every Percent row "+10%", which two HAULER rows are not, and stated
        // both cost curves as text; the fleet line wrote "(10%)" beside a figure computed from
        // Economy.RebuildShare.
        Check(Nodes<Label>(panel).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("Percent upgrades cost 1.25× per level"))
              && Nodes<Label>(panel).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("Step upgrades cost 2× per level"))
              && !Nodes<Label>(panel).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("+10% upgrades"))
              && Economy.ById("hauler_speed").Blurb.Contains("+2%") && Economy.ById("hauler_pd_damage").Blurb.Contains("+5%"),
              "the footnote reads its two cost curves off Economy.Cost (1.25x a level, 2x for a step) and no longer calls every percent row +10%: the hauler's engines are +2% a level and its point defence +5%");
        Check(Nodes<Label>(panel).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("a rebuild costs") && l.Text.Contains(" cr (10%)"))
              && Math.Abs(Economy.RebuildShare - 0.10) < 1e-12,
              "and the fleet line's share is Economy.RebuildShare printed, not the word 10% typed beside it");
        Buy("miner_hold").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("miner_hold").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Y.Level("miner_hold") == 2 && Y.Credits == 775 && Math.Abs(miner.Hold - 120) < 1e-9, "+10%: 100 then 125 (x1.25); hold 120");
        Buy("mine_rate").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Math.Abs(miner.Rate - 2.2) < 1e-9, "mining beam +10%: 2.2/s");
        panel.ShowTab("SALVAGERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("salvager_speed").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Math.Abs(salv.Speed - 121) < 1e-9, "salvager engines +10%: 121 u/s");
        Y.Credits = 10; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Buy("salvage_rate").Disabled && !Y.TryBuy("salvage_rate"), "short of credits: BUY greyed out and refused");
        Y.Credits = 30000; panel.ShowTab("MINERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        double c0 = Y.Credits;
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) { Buy("miner_count").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.02); }   // one press too many
        Check(Y.Level("miner_count") == 4 && Math.Abs(c0 - Y.Credits - 6000) < 1e-6, "+1 miner x4: 400, 800, 1600, 3200 (the fifth press refused)");
        Check(Buy("miner_count").Disabled && Buy("miner_count").Text == "MAX" && !Y.TryBuy("miner_count"), "capped at 5 miners: MAX");
        // THE BASE IS PART OF THE CHARACTER NOW. Read back off DISK, not out of memory: the
        // claim is that a purchase survives the session, and memory would say yes either way.
        // A purchase saves at once -- the autosave is for income ticking over, not for this.
        var onDisk = new ConfigFile();
        Check(onDisk.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg") == Error.Ok, "the character file is on disk after a purchase");
        Check((int)onDisk.GetValue("base_levels", "miner_count", 0) == 4
              && Math.Abs((double)onDisk.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - Y.Credits) < 1e-6
              && Math.Abs((double)onDisk.GetValue("base_invested", "MINERS", -1.0) - Y.Invested("MINERS")) < 1e-6,
              $"a base purchase reaches the character file at once (4 miners, {Y.Credits:0} cr, {Y.Invested("MINERS"):0} invested)");

        panel.ShowTab("SALVAGERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { Buy("salvager_count").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.02); }
        await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Y.Gatherers.Count(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Miner) == 5 && Y.Gatherers.Count(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager) == 5, "five miners and five salvagers");
        panel.ShowTab("HAULER"); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("hauler_pods").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Y.Pods == 2 && Y.Capacity == 600, "HAULER tab: a second pod (500 cr): capacity 600");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);

        // ── the arms and the queue ──
        foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers) { g.State = Gatherer.St.Working; g.Cargo = g.Hold; }   // everyone home at once
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var occupants = Y.Arms.Select(a => a.Occupant).Where(o => o != null).ToList();
        Check(occupants.Count == 5 && occupants.Distinct().Count() == 5 && Y.QueueLength == 5, $"five arms taken, one ship each; five wait in line ({Y.QueueLength})");
        var head = Y.Gatherers.First(g => Y.QueueIndex(g) == 0);
        bool firstIsHead = false, northOk = true, oneEach = true; int unloadedSeen = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 9000 && (Y.QueueLength > 0 || Y.Gatherers.Any(g => g.State is Gatherer.St.Docking or Gatherer.St.Unloading or Gatherer.St.Queued)); i++)
        {
            if (!firstIsHead && Y.ArmOf(head) >= 0) firstIsHead = Y.QueueLength == 4;
            foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers.Where(g => g.State == Gatherer.St.Unloading))
            {
                var dock = Y.Arms[Y.ArmOf(g)].Dock;
                northOk &= (g.Position - dock.Pad).Dot(dock.Row.Open) > dock.Row.Reach;       // on the bay's open side
                unloadedSeen++;
            }
            oneEach &= Y.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null) <= 5 && Y.Arms.Where(a => a.Occupant != null).Select(a => a.Occupant).Distinct().Count() == Y.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(firstIsHead, "the first in line takes the first arm that frees");
        Check(northOk && unloadedSeen > 0, "ships unload through each bay's open north face");
        Check(oneEach && Y.QueueLength == 0 && Y.Gatherers.All(g => g.Cargo == 0 || g.State is Gatherer.St.Working or Gatherer.St.Outbound), "the line clears; never two of the fleet holding one arm");

        // ── the hauler: dispatch rules, landing, the run ──
        // Ten ships are working by now. Send them all back out empty, so no delivery
        // lands in the stock while the hauler's rules are checked (a hold takes 50+ s).
        foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers) g.ResetToWork();
        hauler.ResetToPad();                // it may be mid-run from the earlier sections
        hauler.Cargo = 0; Y.SetStock("ore", 150); Y.SetStock("salvage", 0);
        await Wait(8.0);
        GD.Print($"  hauler: {hauler.State} cargo {hauler.Cargo:0} / pod {Y.PodSize:0} x {Y.Pods}; stock {Y.Stock("ore"):0}+{Y.Stock("salvage"):0}");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && !hauler.CanDispatch, "150 of 300 in the first pod: cannot dispatch");
        Unmeet("hauler"); await Wait(0.1);
        bool haulerEarly = Met("hauler");
        hauler.Dispatch(escorted: false); await Wait(0.05);
        bool plainRefused = hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading;
        hauler.Dispatch(escorted: true); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(plainRefused && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading, "dispatch refused until a pod is full, alone or escorted");
        Hauler.PretendRoll = 0f;                    // the dice with this run: the lost run has checks of its own
        Y.SetStock("ore", Y.Stock("ore") + 200); await Wait(10.0);
        var hud = Nodes<HaulerHud>().First(); var go = Nodes<Button>(hud).First(b => b.Name == "Dispatch");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && hauler.CanDispatch && hauler.FullPods == 1 && !go.Disabled,
              $"one pod full (350 of 600): the floating DISPATCH button lights ({go.Text})");
        Check(!haulerEarly && Met("hauler"), "THE HAULER: met when its DISPATCH goes live, not before");
        go.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting && !hud.Visible, "DISPATCH: it lifts off (and the button goes)");
        float s0 = hauler.VisualScale; await Wait(Economy.HaulerLift + 0.1);
        Check(s0 < 0.7f && Math.Abs(hauler.VisualScale - 1f) < 1e-3 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing, "lifting grows it from landed to flight size");
        bool flat = true, facing = true; int frames = 0;
        while (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing && frames++ < 6000)
        {
            flat &= hauler.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY; facing &= Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, Hauler.East)) < 0.001f;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(flat && facing, $"it moves perfectly flat along the lane, facing the portal ({frames} frames checked)");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging && hauler.AuraOn && Math.Abs(hauler.Position.X - Hub.PortalPos.X) < 0.6f, "at the portal: blue aura, shaking");
        double credits0 = Y.Credits;
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerCharge + 0.5);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Away && hauler.WarpedOut, "warped out through the portal");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerAway + 0.6);
        Check(Math.Abs(Y.Credits - credits0 - 350) < 1e-6 && !hauler.WarpedOut && hauler.LastSale == 350, "back after 30 s: its 350 sold for 350 credits");
        for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && hauler.State != Hauler.St.Returning; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        flat = true; frames = 0;
        while (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Returning && frames++ < 6000) { flat &= hauler.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(flat && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Landing && hauler.Position == Hub.HaulerPad, "back over its pad along the same flat lane");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerLand / 2);
        float midScale = hauler.VisualScale, midRot = hauler.Rotation;
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Landing && midScale > Economy.HaulerLandedScale + 0.05f && midScale < 0.98f
              && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(midRot, Hauler.West)) > 0.3f && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(midRot, Hauler.East)) > 0.3f,
              $"it turns WHILE it descends (half way: scale {midScale:0.00}, heading between west and east)");
        // half way round it points north: its nose, at that size, must clear the station (lowest point y ~ 110)
        Check(Hub.HaulerPad.Y - Hauler.Length * midScale / 2 > 110, "turning on the way down, it clears the station");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerLand / 2 + 0.2);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Math.Abs(hauler.VisualScale - Economy.HaulerLandedScale) < 1e-4
              && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, Hauler.East)) < 0.01f, "lands facing the portal, and loads");
        // AUTO-SELL: completely full, it waits for DISPATCH -- until the level-3 boss is beaten and
        // the 4462 cr upgrade bought; then it goes by itself
        Y.SetStock("ore", 700); Y.SetStock("salvage", 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && hauler.Cargo < Y.Capacity - 1e-6; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        await Wait(2.0);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Math.Abs(hauler.Cargo - 600) < 1e-6, $"completely full (600), it waits while AUTO-SELL is locked ({hauler.State}, {hauler.Cargo:0})");
        Check(Math.Abs(Economy.Cost(Economy.ById("hauler_autosell"), 0) - 4462) < 1e-9,
              $"AUTO-SELL costs 4462 cr: 3x the average level-5 salvager upgrade ({Economy.Cost(Economy.ById("hauler_autosell"), 0):0})");
        var ev = Economy.ById("hauler_evasion");
        Check(ev != null && Economy.Value(ev, 0) == 60 && Economy.Value(ev, 1) == 67 && Economy.Value(ev, 5) == 95 && Economy.Maxed(ev, 5) && !Economy.Maxed(ev, 4)
              && new[] { 0, 1, 2, 3, 4 }.Select(l => Economy.Cost(ev, l)).SequenceEqual(new double[] { 400, 800, 1600, 3200, 6400 }),
              "EVASION: a lone run is 60% safe, +7% a level to 95% at its 5-level cap (400, 800, 1600, 3200, 6400 cr)");
        Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1, 2 };
        Character.BossCleared["ironhold"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 9 };     // a boss this build does not know counts for nothing
        Check(Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Bounty) == 2, $"the highest boss beaten is of the bosses this build knows ({Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Bounty)})");
        Y.Credits = 100000;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        Nodes<Button>(Nodes<BasePanel>().First()).First(b => b.Name == "Tab_HAULER").ButtonPressed = true; await Wait(0.1);
        var asRow = Nodes<HBoxContainer>(Nodes<BasePanel>().First()).FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == "Up_hauler_autosell");
        var asBuy = asRow == null ? null : Nodes<Button>(asRow).First(b => b.Name == "Buy");
        bool lockedUi = asBuy != null && asBuy.Disabled && asBuy.Text.Contains("LOCKED");
        bool asRefused = !Y.TryBuy("hauler_autosell");
        Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"].Add(3);
        await Wait(0.1);
        bool open = asBuy != null && !asBuy.Disabled;
        bool bought = Y.TryBuy("hauler_autosell");
        Check(lockedUi && asRefused && open && bought && Y.Level("hauler_autosell") == 1 && Math.Abs(Y.Credits - (100000 - 4462)) < 1e-6,
              $"AUTO-SELL reads LOCKED and is refused until the level-3 boss is beaten; then it is bought for 4462 cr (ui {lockedUi}, refused {asRefused}, open {open}, {Y.Credits:0} cr)");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting && Math.Abs(hauler.Cargo - 600) < 1e-6 && !hauler.Escorted, "AUTO-SELL bought: completely full, it leaves by itself, alone");
        ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y)).Remove("hauler_autosell");
        Character.BossCleared.Clear();

        // ── a LONE RUN against the dice: EVASION's chance, or the cargo is lost past the portal ──
        async Task FastRun()
        {   // lift, cruise, charge and sell in a moment: the states are what is being checked
            for (int i = 0; i < 900 && hauler.State is not (Hauler.St.Arriving or Hauler.St.Destroyed); i++)
            {
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerLift;
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing && hauler.Position.X < Hub.PortalPos.X - 5) hauler.Position = new Vector2(Hub.PortalPos.X - 1, Hub.LaneY);
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerCharge;
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Away) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerAway;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
        }
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 350; Hauler.PretendRoll = 0.65f;          // Evasion 0: 60% safe, and the dice read 0.65
        double lc0 = Y.Credits;
        hauler.Dispatch(escorted: false); await FastRun();
        Check(hauler.RunLost && hauler.LastSale == 0 && Y.Credits == lc0 && hauler.Cargo == 0,
              $"a lone run the dice go against (0.65 against 60% safe): the cargo is lost past the portal, nothing paid ({hauler.LastSale:0} cr)");
        ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y))["hauler_evasion"] = 1;
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 350;
        double lc1 = Y.Credits;
        hauler.Dispatch(escorted: false); await FastRun();
        Check(!hauler.RunLost && Math.Abs(Y.Credits - lc1 - 350) < 1e-6,
              $"one level of EVASION (67%) gets the same dice through: 350 sold for 350 ({Y.Credits - lc1:0})");
        ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y)).Remove("hauler_evasion");
        Hauler.PretendRoll = null;

        // ── the hauler's OWN point defence: one mount, always on, bought up 5% a level ──
        {
            hauler.ResetToPad();
            var mounts = Nodes<Turret>(hauler).ToList();
            Check(mounts.Count == 1 && mounts[0].PointDefense && hauler.PdOnline && hauler.PdRing == 0f,
                  $"the hauler carries one point-defence mount, always online, with no window to run down ({mounts.Count} mounts)");
            Check(Math.Abs(hauler.Spec(true).Damage - 1.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(hauler.Spec(true).Interval - 0.5) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(hauler.Spec(true).Range - 400f) < 1e-3,
                  $"its gun: 1 damage a shot every 0.5 s inside 400 u ({hauler.Spec(true).Damage:0.00} / {hauler.Spec(true).Interval:0.00} s / {hauler.Spec(true).Range:0} u)");
            ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y))["hauler_pd_damage"] = 3;
            Check(Math.Abs(hauler.Spec(true).Damage - 1.15) < 1e-9,
                  $"three levels of hauler point defence: +5% each, 1.15 a shot ({hauler.Spec(true).Damage:0.000})");
            ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y)).Remove("hauler_pd_damage");
            // A HUNTER CALLED OFF WHILE THIS MOUNT HOLDS IT. It is freed with its hull left, so it
            // still reads "alive"; the mount must let go of it, not ask the freed node where it is
            // (Turret.StillThere). Nothing tells this mount anything -- it is on no pilot's ship --
            // and the missile below is the proof it is still standing its watch afterwards.
            var pdMount = mounts[0];
            var pdHunter = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(pdMount.GlobalPosition, 300f));   // inside its 400 u, any bearing
            pdHunter.Hp = 9000; pdHunter.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 20); pdHunter.Quarry = hauler;
            for (int pdI = 0; pdI < 60 && !ReferenceEquals(pdMount.Target, pdHunter); pdI++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool pdHeld = ReferenceEquals(pdMount.Target, pdHunter);
            float pdGap = pdMount.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(pdHunter.Position);
            H.CallOff(hauler); await Wait(0.3);
            Check(pdHeld && !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(pdHunter) && !ReferenceEquals(pdMount.Target, pdHunter),
                  $"a hunter called off while the hauler's own mount holds it ({pdHeld}, {pdGap:0} u off) is freed with {pdHunter.Hp:0} hull left, and the mount lets go of it");
            // a hostile missile crossing the pad is shot down without anyone opening a window
            int down0 = Shot.Intercepted;
            var mp = hauler.Position + new Vector2(0, -250);
            Combat.LaunchTorpedo(mp, Vector2.Down, 40f, 600f, 5, 0, 0f, heavy: false, hostile: true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Shot.Intercepted == down0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Shot.Intercepted > down0, "and it shoots down a missile that comes at it, with nobody flying it");
            // ...and a CRUISE missile, whose body carries a hull: point defence takes that too, a shot at
            // a time. 0.75 s in, at most two of its 1-damage shots (0.5 s apart) can have landed on 3.
            // Three seeded bearings, all from the north: the pilot's ship waits south of the pad, and
            // a round that struck it would be a hit on the pilot, not a fall.
            int padPass = 0; string padLast = "";
            for (int pk = 0; pk < 3; pk++)
            {
                int down1 = Shot.Intercepted;
                var padFrom = hauler.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(Vary(-1.2f, 1.2f)) * Vary(220f, 320f);
                var padCruise = Combat.Fire(Shots.Cruise, padFrom, hauler.Position - padFrom, 40f, 600f, 5, size: 3f, hull: 3);
                await Wait(0.75);
                bool padStood = padCruise != null && padCruise.Alive && Shot.Intercepted == down1;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && padCruise != null && padCruise.Alive; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                if (padStood && padCruise != null && !padCruise.Alive && Shot.Intercepted == down1 + 1) padPass++;
                else padLast = $"bearing {pk + 1}: {(padStood ? "stood" : "fell early")}, {Shot.Intercepted - down1} down";
            }
            Check(padPass == 3,
                  $"...and a cruise missile with a hull of 3 crossing the pad: still up 0.75 s in, then down, a shot at a time ({padPass} of 3 seeded bearings{(padLast.Length > 0 ? "; " + padLast : "")})");
        }

        // bought for real: five levels to 95%, and no sixth
        Y.Credits = 100000;
        bool fiveBought = Enumerable.Range(0, 5).All(_ => Y.TryBuy("hauler_evasion"));
        Check(fiveBought && Math.Abs(Y.RunSafe - 0.95) < 1e-9 && !Y.TryBuy("hauler_evasion") && Math.Abs(Y.Credits - (100000 - 12400)) < 1e-6,
              $"EVASION bought five times (12400 cr): 95% safe, and a sixth is refused ({Y.RunSafe:0.00})");
        ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y)).Remove("hauler_evasion");

        // ── THE ESCORT: the long way round, waves of hunters after the hauler, x5 at the portal ──
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 300; Y.SetStock("ore", 0); Y.SetStock("salvage", 0);
        await Wait(0.2);
        var hh = Nodes<HaulerHud>().FirstOrDefault(); var escBtn = hh == null ? null : Nodes<Button>(hh).FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Escort");
        Check(escBtn != null && escBtn.Visible, "the base owner's hauler offers ESCORT beside DISPATCH");
        Y.RequestDispatch(escorted: true);
        // THE ROUTE, in the spec's terms: the four outposts counter-clockwise from the south-east --
        // held beside, on the base's side -- then the portal
        var corners = new[] { new Vector2(2121.3f, 2121.3f), new Vector2(2121.3f, -2121.3f), new Vector2(-2121.3f, -2121.3f), new Vector2(-2121.3f, 2121.3f) };
        bool routeOk = Hub.EscortRoute.Length == 5 && Hub.EscortRoute[4] == new Vector2(1500, 219)
                       && Enumerable.Range(0, 4).All(i => Hub.EscortRoute[i].DistanceTo(corners[i]) < 250f && Hub.EscortRoute[i].Length() < corners[i].Length()
                                                          && Hub.EscortRoute[i].DistanceTo(corners[i]) > 100f + 85f);
        Check(routeOk, $"the escort's route: SE, NE, NW, SW -- beside each outpost -- then the portal ({string.Join(" ", Hub.EscortRoute.Select(p => $"({p.X:0},{p.Y:0})"))})");
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && hauler.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.HaulerPad) < 150; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var toFirst = Hub.EscortRoute[0] - hauler.Position;
        float heading = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, toFirst.Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f)));
        var went = (hauler.Position - Hub.HaulerPad).Normalized();
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Escorting && hauler.Escorted && hauler.Leg == 0 && went.X > 0.6f && went.Y > 0.5f && heading < 10,
              $"ESCORT leaves the lane south-east, for the first outpost, nose on its heading ({hauler.State}, going ({went.X:0.00}, {went.Y:0.00}), {heading:0.0} deg off)");
        // the first wave: 1 patrol for a pilot alone -- and it goes for the hauler, not the nearer ship
        int huntR0 = H.Raiders.Count;
        var hunterEdge = Raider.EdgeSpot(hauler.Position);
        Me.Position = hauler.Position + (hunterEdge - hauler.Position) * 0.35f; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        for (int i = 0; i < 600 && H.Raiders.Count == huntR0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        double firstWaveAt = hauler.T;
        await Wait(0.5);
        var hunters = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Quarry == hauler).ToList();
        bool meNearer = hunters.Count > 0 && hunters.All(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Me.Position) < r.Position.DistanceTo(hauler.Position));
        Check(H.Raiders.Count - huntR0 == 3 && hunters.Count == 3 && hunters.All(r => r.Target == hauler) && meNearer && Math.Abs(firstWaveAt - 5.0) < 0.05,
              $"5 s in, a wave of 3 hunts the hauler -- not the ship that is nearer to them ({H.Raiders.Count - huntR0} spawned at {firstWaveAt:0.00} s, {hunters.Count(r => r.Target == hauler)} on it)");
        Check(hunters.All(r => !r.Heavy && Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - 0.5 * 25 * r.Strength) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.Hp - r.MaxHull) < 1e-9),
              $"the first wave is light fighters only, at half hull ({string.Join(", ", hunters.Select(r => $"{(r.Heavy ? "heavy" : "light")} {r.Hp:0.0}"))})");
        // THE ESCORT'S THREAT sets each wave: a quarter each from the load (a level per 1000 cr it will
        // fetch escorted), the party (half its mean level, half its ships' toughness -- a level per 10%
        // of hull over its class's own), the highest boss, and the route (half a level a wave)
        Check(Math.Abs(Waves.EscortThreat(0, 1, 1, 0, 0) - 1.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Waves.EscortThreat(4000, 5, 3, 5, 8) - 4.75) < 1e-9,
              "an escort's threat: 1 for a fresh base's first wave; 4.75 for 4000 cr aboard, a level-5 party of toughness 3, the level-5 boss beaten, the ninth wave");
        Check(Math.Abs(Waves.ThreatStrength(4.75) - Math.Pow(Missions.LevelStep, 3.75)) < 1e-9 && Waves.ThreatLights(1.0) == 3 && Waves.ThreatLights(4.75) == 4 && Waves.ThreatLights(40) == 6
              && Math.Abs(Waves.ThreatAgility(4.75) - 1.0375) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Waves.ThreatAgility(40) - 1.1) < 1e-9,
              "...which sets the hunters' hull and damage (the level scale), their numbers (a light more every 3 levels, 3 more at most) and, very slightly, their speed and turning (1% a level, 10% at most)");
        {
            double ownHull = new ShipStats(Me.Class)["hull"];
            double tough = 1 + Math.Log(Math.Max(1, Me.MaxHp / ownHull)) / Math.Log(Missions.LevelStep);
            double threat = 0.25 * ((1 + 300 * 5 / 1000.0) + (0.5 * Character.Level + 0.5 * tough) + Math.Max(1, Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Bounty)) + 1);
            Check(hunters.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Strength - Math.Pow(Missions.LevelStep, threat - 1)) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.Agility - (1 + Math.Min(0.1, 0.01 * (threat - 1)))) < 1e-9),
                  $"the first wave carries THIS escort's threat: 300 units at x5 aboard, this pilot (level {Character.Level}), the highest boss, the first wave ({threat:0.000}; x{hunters.FirstOrDefault()?.Strength:0.000})");
        }
        Me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(-2600, 1800); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        hauler.T = 24.9;
        await Wait(0.05); int before25 = H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler);
        await Wait(0.2);
        var wave2 = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Quarry == hauler).ToList();
        var heavy2 = wave2.FirstOrDefault(r => r.Heavy);
        Check(before25 == 3 && wave2.Count == 7 && wave2.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1 && heavy2 != null && Math.Abs(heavy2.MaxHull - 0.5 * Enemies.Of(heavy2.Kind).Hull * heavy2.Strength) < 1e-9,
              $"the next wave 20 s later, at 25 s, brings a heavy (at half hull): 3 hunters just before, 7 after ({before25}, {wave2.Count}, {wave2.Count(r => r.Heavy)} heavy)");
        hauler.T = 44.9; await Wait(0.2);
        Check(H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) == 10 && H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler && r.Heavy) == 1,
              $"...and the third, at 45 s, is light fighters only again ({H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler)} hunters)");

        // AT THE FIRST OUTPOST: it eases in beside it and holds 4 s, offloading a quarter of the load --
        // the pods drain, the cargo (and so the pay) untouched, the waves still coming -- then flies on
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);                     // a latched hunter pins it: clear them
        await Wait(0.4);
        hauler.Position = Hub.EscortRoute[0] + new Vector2(-30, -30);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && hauler.StopLeft <= 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var docked = hauler.Position; double stop0 = hauler.StopLeft, shown0 = hauler.ShownCargo, tStop0 = hauler.T, cargo0 = hauler.Cargo;
        await Wait(2.0);
        double shownMid = hauler.ShownCargo, tMid = hauler.T;
        bool held = hauler.Position.DistanceTo(docked) < 0.01f && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Escorting && hauler.Leg == 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && hauler.Leg == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        double shownAfter = hauler.ShownCargo;
        await Wait(1.0);
        Check(stop0 > 3.9 && stop0 <= 4.0 && docked.DistanceTo(Hub.EscortRoute[0]) < 0.6f && held && tMid - tStop0 > 1.9,
              $"at the south-east outpost it holds beside it for 4 s -- its clock, and the waves, running on ({stop0:0.00} s, {docked.DistanceTo(Hub.EscortRoute[0]):0.0} u off)");
        Check(Math.Abs(shown0 - 300) < 8 && Math.Abs(shownMid - 262.5) < 8 && Math.Abs(shownAfter - 225) < 1 && hauler.Cargo == cargo0,
              $"offloading: the pods drain a quarter of the load, 300 -> 262 -> 225 shown, the cargo aboard untouched ({shown0:0}, {shownMid:0}, {shownAfter:0}; {hauler.Cargo:0})");
        Check(hauler.Leg == 1 && hauler.Position.DistanceTo(docked) > 5f && (hauler.Position - docked).Dot(Hub.EscortRoute[1] - docked) > 0,
              $"then on to the north-east outpost (leg {hauler.Leg}, {hauler.Position.DistanceTo(docked):0} u on)");
        // THE PRACTICE RANGE IS OUT OF ITS REACH. The hauler's point defence takes any light craft -- a
        // practice fighter included (Targeting.PointDefence) -- so the range stands 500 u off every leg it
        // flies (Hub.PracticeTargets). On the escort's LAST leg, the one that crosses south of the base,
        // at about its nearest point to the range, nothing of the range is taken and no meter moves.
        // Then the control, so this is not a gun that simply never fires: a practice fighter brought
        // inside its 400 u IS taken -- held beside the hauler as it flies, so the flight never carries
        // it out of reach. No hunters about (a nearer light would win), and the wave clock held between
        // waves (the fourth is due at 65 s).
        {
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
            await Wait(0.2);
            var legField = typeof(Hauler).GetField("_leg", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
            int legWas = hauler.Leg; var posWas = hauler.Position; hauler.T = 50.0;
            var rangeTargets = Nodes<TargetDummy>().ToList();
            Vector2 lastFrom = Hub.EscortRoute[^2], lastTo = Hub.EscortRoute[^1];
            var lastDir = (lastTo - lastFrom).Normalized(); float lastLen = lastFrom.DistanceTo(lastTo);
            Vector2 OnLast(Vector2 p) => lastFrom + lastDir * Mathf.Clamp((p - lastFrom).Dot(lastDir), 0f, lastLen);
            var nearRange = rangeTargets.Select(t => OnLast(t.Position)).OrderBy(p => rangeTargets.Min(t => t.Position.DistanceTo(p))).First();
            foreach (var rangeTarget in rangeTargets) rangeTarget.ResetMeter();
            legField.SetValue(hauler, Hub.EscortRoute.Length - 1);
            hauler.Position = nearRange + lastDir * Vary(-150f, 150f);           // anywhere about the nearest point
            bool tookPractice = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < 90; i++)
            {
                tookPractice |= Nodes<Turret>(hauler).Any(tu => tu.Target != null && TagExt.Is(tu.Target, Tag.Dummy));
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            double practiceHit = rangeTargets.Sum(t => t.Total);
            float practiceGap = rangeTargets.Min(t => t.Position.DistanceTo(hauler.Position));
            var bait = rangeTargets.First(t => t.Fighter); var baitHome = bait.Position;
            var baitOff = Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(200f, 350f);
            bool baitTaken = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < 120 && !baitTaken; i++)
            {
                bait.Position = hauler.Position + baitOff;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                baitTaken = Nodes<Turret>(hauler).Any(tu => ReferenceEquals(tu.Target, bait));
            }
            bait.Position = baitHome; bait.ResetMeter();
            legField.SetValue(hauler, legWas); hauler.Position = posWas;
            Check(!tookPractice && practiceHit == 0 && baitTaken,
                  $"the hauler on its escort's last leg, about the point nearest the practice range ({practiceGap:0} u from its nearest target), takes none of it with its point defence and moves no meter ({practiceHit:0.0} damage) -- and a practice fighter brought inside its 400 u ({baitOff.Length():0} u off) is taken at once ({baitTaken})");
        }
        // FOR THE WHOLE ROUTE: the twelfth wave, far past the four there used to be, still comes on time
        typeof(Hauler).GetField("_waves", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hauler, 11);
        int preLate = H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler);
        hauler.T = 224.9; await Wait(0.2);
        // FIVE, not four: from the tenth wave the rotation fields TWO standoffs 120 u apart -- one
        // drawn from the rotation, the gunship named outright -- beside its line of pinners. No
        // builder could field two of one way before the wave rows; one kind of each way per wave
        // was its whole vocabulary. The late escort is harder by exactly that one standoff.
        Check(H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) - preLate == 5, $"waves keep coming for the whole route: the twelfth, at 225 s ({H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) - preLate} more)");
        // near the end of the route: it charges at the portal, and the hunters withdraw at the jump
        typeof(Hauler).GetField("_leg", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hauler, Hub.EscortRoute.Length - 1);
        hauler.Position = Hub.PortalPos + new Vector2(0, 20);
        for (int i = 0; i < 120 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Escorting; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging && hauler.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.PortalPos) < 0.6f, $"the route ends in the portal: it charges there ({hauler.State})");
        int booms0 = Nodes<FxNode>().Count(f => !f.Warn);
        double ec0 = Y.Credits;
        hauler.T = Economy.HaulerCharge; await Wait(0.1);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Away && H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) == 0 && Nodes<FxNode>().Count(f => !f.Warn) <= booms0,
              $"through the portal: the hunters withdraw, none shot down ({H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler)} left)");
        double atJump = hauler.Cargo;                  // 300, and whatever a delivery added before it lifted
        hauler.T = Economy.HaulerAway; await Wait(0.1);
        Check(atJump >= 300 && Math.Abs(Y.Credits - ec0 - 5 * atJump) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(hauler.LastSale - 5 * atJump) < 1e-6,
              $"an escort pays 5x: {atJump:0} sold for {Y.Credits - ec0:0}");
        // lost on an escort: the cargo goes, the hunters leave, and the usual rebuild starts
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 300;
        Y.RequestDispatch(escorted: true); hauler.T = Economy.HaulerLift; await Wait(0.1);
        hauler.T = Economy.EscortFirstWave; await Wait(0.3);
        int huntersOut = H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler);
        double dc0 = Y.Credits;
        hauler.TakeDamage(1000); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(huntersOut == 3 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Destroyed && hauler.Cargo == 0 && Y.Credits == dc0 && !hauler.Escorted
              && Math.Abs(hauler.RebuildIn - 30) < 0.2 && H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) == 0,
              $"destroyed on the escort: the cargo is gone, the hunters leave, a rebuild in 30 s ({huntersOut} hunters, {hauler.RebuildIn:0.0} s)");
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
        hauler.ResetToPad();
        // ...and one through the portal counts what it will be paid: x5 on an escort, nothing for a lost run
        async Task ToAway()
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < 900 && hauler.State != Hauler.St.Away; i++)
            {
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerLift;
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing && hauler.Position.X < Hub.PortalPos.X - 5) hauler.Position = new Vector2(Hub.PortalPos.X - 1, Hub.LaneY);
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Escorting && hauler.Leg < Hub.EscortRoute.Length - 1)
                { typeof(Hauler).GetField("_leg", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hauler, Hub.EscortRoute.Length - 1); hauler.Position = Hub.PortalPos + new Vector2(0, 20); }
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerCharge;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
        }
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 300; Y.RequestDispatch(escorted: true); await ToAway();
        Y.SaveBase(); double escortOnFile = (double)OnDisk("base", "credits", -1.0) - Y.Credits;
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 300; Hauler.PretendRoll = 0.99f; Y.RequestDispatch(escorted: false); await ToAway();
        Y.SaveBase(); double lostOnFile = (double)OnDisk("base", "credits", -1.0) - Y.Credits;
        double lostOre = (double)OnDisk("base", "ore", -1.0) - Y.Stock("ore") - Y.Gatherers.Where(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner).Sum(g => g.Cargo);
        double lostSalvage = (double)OnDisk("base", "salvage", -1.0) - Y.Stock("salvage") - Y.Gatherers.Where(g => g.Kind != GatherKind.Miner).Sum(g => g.Cargo);
        Hauler.PretendRoll = null;
        Check(Math.Abs(escortOnFile - 1500) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(lostOnFile) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(lostOre) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(lostSalvage) < 1e-6,
              $"a save while the hauler is through the portal counts what it will be paid: an escort's 300 as 1500, a lost run's as nothing -- no credits, no stock back ({escortOnFile:0}, {lostOnFile:0}, ore {lostOre:0}, salvage {lostSalvage:0})");
        hauler.ResetToPad();
        // A SAVE COUNTS THE LOADS: with AUTO-SELL locked a full hauler waiting on its pad is the
        // usual state, and a quit used to throw its load away
        hauler.Cargo = 300;
        double loaded = Y.Stock("ore") + Y.Stock("salvage") + hauler.Cargo + Y.Gatherers.Sum(g => g.Cargo);
        Y.SaveBase();
        double onFile = (double)OnDisk("base", "ore", -1.0) + (double)OnDisk("base", "salvage", -1.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(onFile - loaded) < 1e-6 && hauler.Cargo == 300, $"a save counts what the hauler and the fleet are carrying, as if home ({onFile:0} of {loaded:0})");
        hauler.ResetToPad();

        await LaneChecks();

        var t = Nodes<Turret>(ship).First();
        ship.Rotation = 1.3f; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(t.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.ToGlobal(t.Offset)) < 0.5f, "turret on its mount after turning");
