                // identity for the 0.52 s gap, so all three land; the tally is keyed by the WEAPON
                // that fired them, so it stays one line per gun instead of gaining one per round
                // ever fired at this hull.
                double Volley() => me.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("test:volley", out var v) ? v : 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                    Combat.FireSlug(volFrom, volAim, 900f, volStand * 2f, 6f, 7.0, scrap: false, 0, "test:volley");
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && Volley() < 21.0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                int volLines = me.DamageBySource.Keys.Count(k => k.StartsWith("test:volley"));
                Check(Math.Abs(Volley() - 21.0) < 1e-9 && volLines == 1,
                      $"a volley of three from one weapon lands three times on one hull -- 3 x 7 = 21, where one shared name left a hull feeling 7 -- and the tally it lands in is still ONE line for that weapon ({Volley():0.000} over {volLines} line, fired from {volStand:0} u off at {Mathf.RadToDeg(volBear):0} deg)");
            }
            Check(PlayerShip.CombatHold == 12, "out of combat means 12 s without dealing or taking damage");
            me.TakeDamage(me.Hp - me.MaxHp * 0.5); me.NoteCombat(); double h0 = me.Hp;
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Hp - h0) / me.MaxHp - 0.005) < 0.0015, $"in combat it regenerates 0.5% of max hull a second ({(me.Hp - h0) / me.MaxHp * 100:0.00}%)");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_combatT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0);
            double h1 = me.Hp; await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Hp - h1) / me.MaxHp - 0.03) < 0.004, $"out of combat, 3% a second ({(me.Hp - h1) / me.MaxHp * 100:0.00}%)");
            me.TakeDamage(-(me.MaxHp - me.Hp));
        }

        // ── raiders (6a): the light fighter -- cruise, boost, post, pin, laser ──
        {
            var me = Me; var keepPos = me.Position;
            me.Position = new Vector2(4000, 2500); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            var r1 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(0, 2300));
            await Wait(0.5); var rp0 = r1.Position; await Wait(0.5);
            float cruise = (r1.Position - rp0).Length() / 0.5f;
            Check(r1.Target == me && Math.Abs(cruise - 100f) < 8f && !r1.Boosting, $"a light raider goes for the nearest ship at an unupgraded capital ship's pace ({cruise:0} u/s)");
            Check(Math.Abs(Raider.BoostAt - 1600f) < 1e-3, "it boosts from 1600 u (3 s at 500%, plus its 100 u reach)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !r1.Boosting; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float atBoost = r1.Position.DistanceTo(me.Position); var b0 = r1.Position; await Wait(0.4);
            float rboost = (r1.Position - b0).Length() / 0.4f;
            Check(Math.Abs(atBoost - 1600) < 30 && Math.Abs(rboost - 500) < 30, $"at 1600 u ({atBoost:0}) it boosts to 500% ({rboost:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && !r1.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float rheld = Raider.Gap(r1.Position, me);                 // from the HULL: beside the ship, never on it
            Check(r1.Latched && rheld > 60 && rheld <= Raider.PinRange, $"it lands near its target, not on top: posted {rheld:0} u off the hull");
            var r2 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(300, 0)); var r3 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(-300, 0));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && !(r2.Latched && r3.Latched); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var bow = Vector2.Up.Rotated(me.Rotation);
            var angs = new[] { r1, r2, r3 }.Select(r => Mathf.RadToDeg(bow.AngleTo(r.Position - me.Position))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
            Check(r2.Latched && r3.Latched && Math.Abs(angs[0] + 90) < 20 && Math.Abs(angs[1]) < 20 && Math.Abs(angs[2] - 90) < 20,
                  $"three hold posts ahead, left and right ({string.Join(", ", angs.Select(a => a.ToString("0")))} deg off the bow)");
            Check(me.Pinned, "rheld there, they pin it");
            float rot0 = me.Rotation;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.A, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.A, Pressed = true });
            await Wait(1.0);
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.A, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.A, Pressed = false });
            float rspd = me.Velocity.Length(), rtop = (float)me.Stats["max_speed"];
            Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(rot0, me.Rotation)) < 0.01f && rspd <= rtop * 0.2f + 1f && me.SpeedAhead > 5f,
                  $"pinned: A does not turn it, and forced thrust holds it to 20% of top speed ({rspd:0} of {rtop:0} u/s)");
            double Raided() => me.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key.StartsWith("raider:")).Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            double dm0 = Raided(); await Wait(5.0);
            double dps = (Raided() - dm0) / 5.0;
            Check(Math.Abs(dps - 3.0) < 0.7, $"three lights, 1 DPS each ({dps:0.00} DPS)");
            foreach (var r in new[] { r1, r2, r3 }) r.TakeDamage(999);
            await Wait(0.5);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0 && !Combat.Hostiles.OfType<Raider>().Any() && !me.Pinned, "shot down, they are gone -- and the pin lets go");
            // A miner OUT IN THE BELT, beyond the base's own guns (600 u): one caught docking had its
            // raider shot down by them before it could latch. Where the fleet is at this point depends
            // on how long the router scenarios above took in real time, so it is waited for.
            Gatherer mnr = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 40 && mnr == null; i++)
            {
                mnr = H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner && g.State == Gatherer.St.Working && g.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) > 900);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            mnr ??= H.Yard.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
            // an EMPTY hold, so it goes on working (still) through the whole check: one picked nearly
            // full left for home at its full 110 u/s as the raider arrived, and a raider cruising at
            // 100 with its boost spent never caught it to latch
            mnr.Cargo = 0;
            var r4 = H.SpawnRaider(mnr.Position + new Vector2(120, 0));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !r4.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float mr0 = mnr.Rotation; var mp0 = mnr.Position; await Wait(0.5);
            float rmspd = (mnr.Position - mp0).Length() / 0.5f;
            Check(r4.Target == mnr && mnr.Pinned && Math.Abs(rmspd - (float)mnr.Speed * 0.2f) < 3f && Math.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(mr0, mnr.Rotation)) < 0.01f,
                  $"a miner is pinned the same way: forward at 20%, no turning ({rmspd:0} u/s of {mnr.Speed:0}; target {(r4.Target as Node)?.Name}, pinned {mnr.Pinned}, turned {Mathf.AngleDifference(mr0, mnr.Rotation):0.000})");
            r4.TakeDamage(999); await Wait(0.3);
            me.Position = keepPos; me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.TakeDamage(-(me.MaxHp - me.Hp));   // hand over at full hull
        }

        // ── raiders (6b): the heavy -- waits at the map's edge; a predicted missile; boosts at 700% on a pinned target ──
        {
            var hm = Me; var keepH = hm.Position;
            hm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2000, 0); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            var hv = H.SpawnRaider(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 500), Enemies.Gunship);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hv.Heavy && Math.Abs(hv.Length - 4 * Raider.LightLength) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(Raider.HeavyDps - 2 * Raider.RaiderDps) < 1e-9 && Raider.RaiderDps == 1,
                  "a heavy raider: four times a light's length, twice the raider damage (2 DPS)");
            double HvLaser() => hm.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == $"raider:{hv.NetId}").Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            double HvMissile() => hm.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == $"heavy:{hv.NetId}:missile").Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            var edge = Raider.EdgeSpot(hm.Position);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 22 && hv.Position.DistanceTo(edge) > 8f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(0.5);
            float hvFacing = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hv.Rotation, (hm.Position - hv.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f)));
            Check(hv.Position.DistanceTo(edge) < 10f && Math.Abs(hv.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - 4200f) < 10f && hvFacing < 15f && HvLaser() == 0,
                  $"its target unpinned, it waits at the map's edge nearest it ({hv.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0} u from the base), facing it, laser silent");
            // the missile: within 500 u of the heavy, at where the target will be in TWELVE s
            hm.Position = edge + (Hub.BasePos - edge).Normalized() * 420f; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && H.BlastsPending == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var blastRing = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone && Math.Abs(t.Radius - Raider.BlastRadius) < 0.1f);
            Check(H.BlastsPending == 1 && blastRing != null && blastRing.Position.DistanceTo(hm.Position) < 6f
                  && Math.Abs(blastRing.Time - 12) < 1e-6 && blastRing.Warn && blastRing.Anchor == Fx.World,
                  "within 500 u it fires at where the target will be; a red circle -- a warning row of Fx.All, pinned to the ground -- marks it for 12 s, long enough to be somewhere else by then");
            double mm0 = HvMissile();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 15 && H.BlastsPending > 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(HvMissile() - mm0 - 42) < 1e-6, $"staying on the circle, the gunship row's 42-damage blast lands -- 40% harder than the 30 it was, for being 12 s in the air rather than 7 ({HvMissile() - mm0:0})");
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 0.0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && H.BlastsPending == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            hm.Position += new Vector2(0, 300); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;                         // off the circle
            // ...and no SECOND missile while this one flies: the flight is 12 s and so is the
            // cadence, so a 15 s wait would otherwise end with a fresh one still in the air.
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 99.0);
            double mm1 = HvMissile();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 15 && H.BlastsPending > 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(H.BlastsPending == 0 && HvMissile() == mm1, "moving off the circle dodges it");
            // anything out of place -- a wild speed, a broken number -- and it aims right at the target
            var pz = new Vector2(100, 200);
            Check(Missiles.Predict(pz, new Vector2(10, 0), Raider.MissileFlight) == pz + new Vector2(120, 0)
                  && Missiles.Predict(pz, new Vector2(5000, 0), Raider.MissileFlight) == pz
                  && Missiles.Predict(pz, new Vector2(float.NaN, 0), Raider.MissileFlight) == pz
                  && Missiles.Predict(pz, new Vector2(float.PositiveInfinity, 1), Raider.MissileFlight) == pz,
                  "the missile leads a sane speed the whole 12 s of its flight -- 10 u/s carried 120 u -- and a wild speed, NaN or infinity aims at where the target is");
            // live: 2700 u/s in 45 u steps (wild, yet under the 50 u jump filter) -- the circle must sit on the ship
            hm.Position = edge + (Hub.BasePos - edge).Normalized() * 400f; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 0.12);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            int rings0 = Nodes<FxNode>().Count(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone);
            Vector2 atFire = hm.Position;
            for (int i = 0; i < 20 && Nodes<FxNode>().Count(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone) == rings0; i++)
            {
                atFire = hm.Position; hm.Position += new Vector2(0, 45);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var wildRing = Nodes<FxNode>().LastOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone && Math.Abs(t.Radius - Raider.BlastRadius) < 0.1f);
            Check(wildRing != null && wildRing.Position.DistanceTo(atFire) < 100f,
                  $"a target moving at a wild 2700 u/s: the circle sits on it, not 7 s ahead ({(wildRing == null ? -1 : wildRing.Position.DistanceTo(atFire)):0} u off)");
            // pinned: it boosts at 700% until 300 u away, then closes at cruise and fires 2 DPS
            hm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(1900, 0); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.Rotation = 0f;
            var lights = new[] { H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(0, -250)), H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(250, 0)), H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(-250, 0)) };
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !hm.Pinned; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(0.1); var hp1 = hv.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            float burn = (hv.Position - hp1).Length() / 0.2f;
            // the spec's numbers as literals: a check that reads the code's own constant cannot catch it being wrong
            Check(hm.Pinned && Math.Abs(burn - 700f) < 60f, $"its target pinned, it boosts at 700% ({burn:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && hv.Position.DistanceTo(hm.Position) > 300f - 20f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var hp2 = hv.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            float slow = (hv.Position - hp2).Length() / 0.2f;
            // (over the ground: cruise plus the pinned target's own forced thrust, ~20 u/s)
            Check(slow <= 100f + 0.2f * (float)hm.Stats["max_speed"] + 5f, $"inside 300 u it closes at cruise ({slow:0} u/s over the ground)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !hv.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            double hl0 = HvLaser(); await Wait(3.0);
            double hdps = (HvLaser() - hl0) / 3.0;
            Check(hv.Latched && Raider.Gap(hv.Position, hm) <= Raider.HeavyReach && Math.Abs(hdps - 2) < 0.4,
                  $"from 135 u off the hull its laser does 2 DPS ({hdps:0.00})");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(9999);
            await Wait(0.3);
            hm.Position = keepH; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.TakeDamage(-(hm.MaxHp - hm.Hp));
        }

        // ── raiders (6c): patrols -- 3 lights and 1 heavy; nothing within 2000 u, no target ──
        {
            int pid = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 6500));
            var pat = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == pid).ToList();
            Check(pat.Count == 4 && pat.Count(r => !r.Heavy) == 3 && pat.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1, "a patrol: three lights and one heavy");
            await Wait(1.0);
            float pd0 = pat[0].Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos);
            Check(pat.All(r => r.Target == null) && pd0 < 6500 - 60 && pd0 > 6500 - 200,
                  $"nothing within 2000 u: they take no target, and head for the perimeter at a patrol's pace ({6500 - pd0:0} u in 1 s)");
            var pm = Me; var keepP = pm.Position;
            pm.Position = pat[0].Position + new Vector2(0, -1500); pm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            await Wait(0.5);
            var heavyP = pat.First(r => r.Heavy);
            Check(pat.Where(r => !r.Heavy).All(r => r.Target == pm) && heavyP.Target == pm,
                  "a ship within 2000 u: the lights go for it, and their heavy takes the same target");
            await RangeChecks();                // something real to select, at a real distance
            ZoomChecks();
            await SkyChecks();
            await TourChecks();
            // ── THREE AT ONCE, ON THE HULLS BIG ENOUGH TO HOLD THEM ──
            // How many a hull may hold is its class's row, so "capital ships only" is a number and
            // not a list of class names in an `if`. A light still holds one: picking a second
            // REPLACES the first rather than being refused, because a pilot who clicks a new
            // target means to take it.
            {
                var trio = H.Raiders.Take(4).Cast<IHittable>().ToList();
                if (trio.Count < 4) { Check(false, "multi-target: fewer than four raiders to pick"); }
                else
                {
                    H.ClearSelection();
                    foreach (var pick in trio) H.AddTarget(pick);
                    int capHeld = H.Targets.Count;
                    bool orderKept = H.Targets.SequenceEqual(trio.Take(3));
                    H.AddTarget(trio[1]);                          // picking one already held drops it
                    bool dropped = H.Targets.Count == 2 && !H.Targets.Contains(trio[1]);
                    var firstOf = H.Selected;
                    H.SelectTarget(trio[3]);                        // a plain click starts over
                    bool restart = H.Targets.Count == 1 && H.Selected == trio[3];
                    Check(H.TargetCap == 3 && capHeld == 3 && orderKept && dropped && restart
                          && ReferenceEquals(firstOf, trio[0]),
                          $"a capital holds trio targets at once and no more, in the order it picked them; picking one it holds drops that one, and a plain click starts over ({capHeld} of {H.TargetCap} held)");

                    // ...and a light fighter holds exactly one, from the same row.
                    var keepClass = Character.Class;
                    await SetClass(ShipClass.LightDart);
                    H.ClearSelection();
                    foreach (var pick in trio) H.AddTarget(pick);
                    Check(H.TargetCap == 1 && H.Targets.Count == 1 && H.Selected == trio[3]
                          && Classes.Of(ShipClass.Battleship).Targets == 3 && Classes.Of(ShipClass.LightDart).Targets == 1,
                          $"a light holds one, and taking another replaces it -- the number is the class's row, not a list of names ({H.Targets.Count} of {H.TargetCap})");
                    await SetClass(keepClass);
                    H.ClearSelection();
                }
            }
            foreach (var r in pat) if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(r)) r.TakeDamage(9999);
            await Wait(0.3);
            pm.Position = keepP; pm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; pm.TakeDamage(-(pm.MaxHp - pm.Hp));
        }

        // ── the base's own weapons: a 5 DPS laser at 300 u, a tracking 25-damage missile at 600 u -- raiders only ──
        {
            var bd = Nodes<BaseDefense>().First();
            Check(BaseDefense.LaserRange == 300 && BaseDefense.MissileRange == 600 && BaseDefense.LaserDps == 5 && BaseDefense.MissileDamage == 25
                  && Math.Abs(BaseDefense.MissileSpeed - 1.2f * 100f) < 1e-3, "the base: a 5 DPS laser at 300 u; a 25-damage missile at 600 u, 120 u/s");
            var dd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"); var dHome = dd.Position;
            dd.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 250); dd.ResetMeter();
            double l0 = bd.LaserDealt; int m0 = bd.MissilesFired; await Wait(2.0);
            Check(bd.LaserDealt == l0 && bd.MissilesFired == m0 && dd.Total == 0, "it never fires at the practice dummies");
            dd.Position = dHome;
            var spot1 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 280);
            var rb = H.SpawnRaider(spot1); rb.Hp = 1000;
            double lz = bd.LaserDealt;
            for (int i = 0; i < 120; i++) { rb.Position = spot1; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            double laser2 = bd.LaserDealt - lz;
            Check(Math.Abs(laser2 - 10) < 2.6, $"a raider inside 300 u: the laser burns it at 5 DPS ({laser2:0.0} in 2 s)");
            rb.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            var spot2 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -500);        // north of the base: clear of the hauler pad's own gun
            var rc = H.SpawnRaider(spot2); rc.Hp = 1000;
            int mz = bd.MissilesFired; double lz2 = bd.LaserDealt;
            Shot bm = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && bm == null; i++)
            {
                rc.Position = spot2;
                bm = InFlight().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.HostileFire && !t.Heavy && t.TargetId == rc.NetId);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var bp1 = bm?.GlobalPosition ?? Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) { rc.Position = spot2; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            float bspd = bm != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(bm) ? (bm.GlobalPosition - bp1).Length() / 0.25f : 0f;
            double hpc = rc.Hp;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rc.Hp == hpc; i++) { rc.Position = spot2; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(bm != null && bd.MissilesFired > mz && bd.LaserDealt == lz2 && Math.Abs(bspd - 120f) < 6f && Math.Abs(hpc - rc.Hp - 25) < 1e-6,
                  $"a raider at 500 u: no laser, but a tracking missile at {bspd:0} u/s lands for {hpc - rc.Hp:0}");
            rc.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            var spot3 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -700);
            var rd = H.SpawnRaider(spot3); rd.Hp = 1000;
            int mw = bd.MissilesFired; double lw = bd.LaserDealt;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6; i++) { rd.Position = spot3; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(bd.MissilesFired == mw && bd.LaserDealt == lw, "a raider at 700 u: out of reach, nothing fires");
            rd.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
        }

        // ── enemy fire, shields, stasis, the escape pod, re-boarding ──
        {
            var d3x = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
            Check(d3x.Armed && !H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1").Armed, "only the bottom-right dummy is armed");
            var sh = Me;
            double Dummy() => sh.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("?", out var v) ? v : 0;    // (hull regenerates: count the hit itself)
            double hit0 = Dummy();
            sh.Position = d3x.Position + new Vector2(0, -120); sh.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; sh.Rotation = 0f;
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && Dummy() == hit0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(Dummy() - hit0 - 50) < 0.01, $"the armed dummy's missile hits a ship within 150 u for 50 ({Dummy() - hit0:0})");
            var shield = Nodes<ShieldFlash>(sh).First();
            Check(shield.Glow(2) > 0 && shield.Glow(0) == 0, "the shield lights the side facing the dummy (astern), not the bow");
            sh.Position = d3x.Position + new Vector2(0, -1200); await Wait(6.0);
            double taken1 = sh.DamageBySource.Values.Sum(); await Wait(6.0);        // (hull regenerates: count damage instead)
            Check(sh.DamageBySource.Values.Sum() == taken1, "outside 150 u it holds fire");
            Check(sh.Covers(sh.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * (Classes.Art(sh.Class).Length * 0.45f), 0f)
                  && !sh.Covers(sh.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * (Classes.Art(sh.Class).HalfWidth + 12f), 0f),
                  "the collider is a capsule: the bow is inside, a point just off the beam is not");
            // death
            sh.TakeDamage(sh.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            var pod = Nodes<EscapePod>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(!sh.Alive && sh.StasisLeft > 23 && sh.StasisLeft <= 24 && pod != null && !Combat.Hostiles.Contains(pod as IHittable) && !Combat.Players.Any(p => (object)p == pod),
                  $"destroyed: stasis (0:24; {sh.StasisLeft:0.0} s left) and an escape pod that nothing can target");
            Check(pod != null && Math.Abs(pod.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First().Texture.GetHeight() * pod.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First().Scale.Y - 24f) < 0.5f,
                  "the pod is 24 u long (60% of a salvager's 40)");
            var podStart = pod.Position;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W);
            Check(pod.Position.DistanceTo(podStart) > 20f && sh.Velocity == Vector2.Zero, "W flies the pod; the ship stays put in stasis");
            Check(H.GetType().GetField("_cam", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance) != null && sh.ViewPosition == pod.Position, "the camera follows the pod");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!sh.Alive, "F does nothing before stasis ends");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_stasis", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(sh, 0.5);
            await Wait(0.7);
            Check(sh.CanReboard, "after the timer the ship is ready");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(sh.Alive && sh.Hp >= sh.MaxHp * 0.33 - 1e-6 && sh.Hp < sh.MaxHp * 0.34 && Nodes<EscapePod>().Count() == 0, "F re-boards at 33% hull (regenerating from there); the pod is gone");
            sh.TakeDamage(-(sh.MaxHp - sh.Hp));  // (test only) back to full for what follows
        }

        // a double-click on empty space clears the target; a single click there does not
        {
            var dd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Click(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            var empty = Me.Position + new Vector2(-260, -260);
            Click(Scr(empty)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dd, "a single click on empty space keeps the target");
            DoubleClick(Scr(empty)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == null, "a double-click on empty space clears it");
            Click(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            DoubleClick(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dd, "a double-click on a target keeps it selected");
        }

        // selection rules still hold
        await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(-300, 300));
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Tab); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d1, "Tab selects the enemy nearest the ship");
        Click(Scr(d2.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d2, "left-click selects another");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == null && H != null, "Esc clears the target before leaving");

        // placement hook
        Vector2? placedAt = null;
        H.BeginPlacement("TEST", p => placedAt = p);
        var spot = d1.Position + new Vector2(-150, -150);
        Click(Scr(spot)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(placedAt.HasValue && placedAt.Value.DistanceTo(spot) < 3f && !H.Placing, "left-click confirms a placement at the cursor");

        // wings: class swap and rebuild leave no orphans
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
        Check(Count<Wing>() == 0, "to battleship: wings gone");
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier);
        Priv(H, "RebuildShips"); await Wait(0.1);
        int craft = (int)(Me.Stats["fighter_count"] + Me.Stats["bomber_count"]);
        Check(Count<Wing>() == craft && Count<PlayerShip>() == 1, $"rebuild leaves no orphan wings ({Count<Wing>()} of {craft})");

        // ── camera: wheel zoom, the free camera (Y), its tether ──
        {
            var sh = Me;
            void Wheel(MouseButton b) { Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex = b, Pressed = true, Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) }); }
            for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) Wheel(MouseButton.WheelDown); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, Hub.DefaultZoom / Hub.ZoomOutMax), $"wheel out stops at 33% further out ({H.ZoomLevel:0.000})");
            for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) Wheel(MouseButton.WheelUp); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, Hub.DefaultZoom * Hub.ZoomInMax), $"wheel in stops at 1.5x ({H.ZoomLevel:0.000})");
            H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom * 0.8f); float kept = H.ZoomLevel;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) });
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Y); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.FreeCamera, "Y frees the camera");
            var cam0 = H.CameraPosition;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Right); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Right);
            Check(H.CameraPosition.X - cam0.X > 800, $"arrow keys move it ({H.CameraPosition.X - cam0.X:0} u in 1 s)");
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Right); await Wait(6.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Right);
            float tether = H.CameraPosition.DistanceTo(sh.ViewPosition);
            Check(Mathf.Abs(tether - sh.MyArt.CameraRange) < 1f && sh.MyArt.CameraRange == 5000f, $"it stops 5000 u from the ship ({tether:0})");
            var cam1 = H.CameraPosition;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(2, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(2, 540)) });
            await Wait(0.5);
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) });
            Check(H.CameraPosition.X < cam1.X - 100f, "the mouse against the screen edge pans it too");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Y); await Wait(2.0);
            Check(!H.FreeCamera && H.CameraPosition.DistanceTo(sh.ViewPosition) < 30f && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, kept),
                  "Y again returns to the ship, keeping the zoom");
            H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom);
        }

        // ── THE ENEMY TABLE: six rows, and every figure in them ──────────────────
        // (Behaviour checks elsewhere read Enemies.Of(kind); these are the literals behind them.)
        {
            EnemyDef E(int k) => Enemies.Of(k);
            Check(Enemies.All.Length == 6
                  && E(Enemies.Webifier).Hull == 25 && E(Enemies.Webifier).Dps == 1.0 && E(Enemies.Webifier).Reach == 100f
                  && E(Enemies.Gunship).Hull == 100 && E(Enemies.Gunship).Dps == 2.0 && E(Enemies.Gunship).Reach == 150f,
                  "the two the game is balanced on: a webifier of 25 hull and 1 x damage inside 100 u, a gunship of 100 and 2 x inside 150");
            Check(E(Enemies.Talon).Hull == 18 && E(Enemies.Talon).Dps == 1.4 && E(Enemies.Talon).Cruise == 130f
                  && E(Enemies.Pod).Hull == 60 && E(Enemies.Pod).Dps == 0.7 && E(Enemies.Pod).Cruise == 78f
                  && E(Enemies.Cross).Hull == 130 && E(Enemies.Cross).Dps == 2.6 && !E(Enemies.Cross).Missiles
                  && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Hull == 150 && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Reach == 260f && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Missiles,
                  "and the owner's four: a talon (18 hull, 1.4 x, 130 u/s), a pod (60, 0.7, 78), a cross (130, 2.6, no missiles), a lancerkin (150, standing 260 u off, with them)");
            Check(Enemies.OfWay(EnemyWay.Pin).Length == 3 && Enemies.OfWay(EnemyWay.Standoff).Length == 3
                  && Enemies.All.All(e => e.Texture != null && e.Texture.StartsWith("res://")),
                  "three that pin and three that stand off, each with its own hull drawn");
            // ── AN ENEMY'S MISSILE IS ITS OWN ROW ──
            // The figures were Raider consts, so both rows that throw a missile threw the SAME
            // missile: the Lancerkin, which stands 260 u off against the gunship's 150, fired the
            // gunship's 500 u missile and could not be given one of its own. Given one here, the
            // gunship's must not move -- which is the thing a shared const could never manage. The
            // row is put back before the Check, and no frame runs in between.
            {
                var kin = E(Enemies.Lancerkin); var gun = E(Enemies.Gunship);
                float keepRange = kin.MissileRange; double keepEvery = kin.MissileEvery, keepDamage = kin.MissileDamage;
                kin.MissileRange = 900f; kin.MissileEvery = 5.0; kin.MissileDamage = 44;
                bool apart = kin.MissileRange == 900f && kin.MissileEvery == 5.0 && kin.MissileDamage == 44
                             && gun.MissileRange == 500f && gun.MissileEvery == 12.0 && gun.MissileDamage == 42;
                kin.MissileRange = keepRange; kin.MissileEvery = keepEvery; kin.MissileDamage = keepDamage;
                Check(apart && Enemies.All.Count(x => x.Missiles) == 2
                      && kin.MissileRange == 500f && kin.MissileEvery == 12.0 && kin.MissileDamage == 42 && kin.BlastRadius == 90f
                      && gun.BlastRadius == 90f && Math.Abs(Raider.MissileFlight - 12) < 1e-9,
                      $"the missile is each row's own: 42 damage inside 500 u every 12 s in a 90 u blast, 12 s out -- and a lancerkin given 900 u / 5 s / 44 leaves the gunship's 500 u / 12 s / 42 exactly where they were (back at {kin.MissileRange:0} u)");
            }
            // a wave draws from the table, so the kinds rotate rather than being written in
            var p1 = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -3400));
            var p2 = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -3600));
            await Wait(0.1);
            var w1 = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p1).ToList();
            var w2 = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p2).ToList();
            Check(w1.Count > 0 && w2.Count > 0 && w1.Select(r => r.Kind).Distinct().Count() == 2
                  && w1.Select(r => r.Kind).First() != w2.Select(r => r.Kind).First(),
                  $"one patrol of each way, and the next wave draws different kinds ({string.Join(",", w1.Select(r => Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Name).Distinct())} then {string.Join(",", w2.Select(r => Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Name).Distinct())})");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p1 || r.Patrol == p2).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(99999);
            await Wait(0.3);

            // ── A WAVE IS A ROW (Waves.All) ─────────────────────────────────
            // Both of these are sent by the SAME builder that sent the two patrols above -- the
            // only builder there is (Raids.Send) -- and both were impossible before it: the old
            // Hub.SpawnPatrol drew ONE pin kind and ONE standoff kind a wave, and took `heavy` as
            // a bool. The escort's late waves are the rows that ask for them.
            {
                var was = H.Raiders.Select(r => r.NetId).ToHashSet();
                H.HuntWave(Me, 6);                                    // an even wave from the seventh on: hunt_mixed
                await Wait(0.05);
                var mixed = H.Raiders.Where(r => !was.Contains(r.NetId)).ToList();
                var pins = mixed.Where(r => !r.Heavy).ToList();
                Check(mixed.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 0 && pins.Count >= 5
                      && pins.Select(r => r.Kind).Distinct().Count() == 2
                      && pins.GroupBy(r => r.Kind).Min(g => g.Count()) == 2
                      && pins.All(r => Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - 0.5 * Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Hull * r.Strength) < 1e-9),
                      $"a wave MIXES TWO PINNER KINDS -- the rotation's next row, two of them, at half hull ({string.Join(" + ", pins.GroupBy(r => r.Kind).Select(g => $"{g.Count()} {Enemies.Of(g.Key).Name}"))})");
                foreach (var r in mixed) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
                await Wait(0.2);

                was = H.Raiders.Select(r => r.NetId).ToHashSet();
                H.HuntWave(Me, 9);                                    // an odd wave from the tenth on: hunt_twin
                await Wait(0.05);
                var twin = H.Raiders.Where(r => !was.Contains(r.NetId)).ToList();
                var stand = twin.Where(r => r.Heavy).ToList();
                Check(stand.Count == 2 && twin.Count(r => !r.Heavy) >= 3
                      && stand.Any(r => r.Kind == Enemies.Gunship),
                      $"...and a wave fields TWO STANDOFFS -- one drawn from the rotation and the gunship named outright ({string.Join(" + ", stand.Select(r => Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Name))})");
                foreach (var r in twin) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
                await Wait(0.2);
            }

            // ── A THIRD HOST-SPAWNED REPLICATED KIND IS A ROW, NOT A THIRD COPY ──
            // The raiders and the deployed turrets were the same six methods written twice: mint
            // an id from a NetIds space, add, tell the world, guard for idempotence, remove with
            // or without a burst. Registering a kind here adds NOTHING to Hub -- the row says its
            // space, what bag holds it and how one is built, and Hub.Spawn / Hub.Down take it.
            {
                var nfm = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
                int raiders0 = H.Raiders.Count, turrets0 = H.Deployed.Count;
                int extra = Spawns.All.Count;
                Spawns.All.Add(new SpawnKind {
                    Id = "Probe", Space = NetIds.Menu, Burst = 0f,
                    Bag = k => new SpawnSet<SpawnProbe>(k),
                    Make = (h, s) => new SpawnProbe { NetId = s.NetId, Position = s.At },
                    Seed = n => new SpawnSeed(((SpawnProbe)n).NetId, n.Position, 0, 0, 0),
                });
                var probeAt = VaryNear(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -5400), 400f);
                var probe = H.Spawn(extra, probeAt, 0, 0, 0) as SpawnProbe;
                bool born = probe != null && probe.GetParent() == H && probe.Position == probeAt
                            && probe.NetId > NetIds.Menu && probe.NetId < NetIds.Menu + 10000
                            && probe.Name.ToString() == $"Probe_{probe.NetId}";
                H.Down(extra, probe, burst: false, 0f);
                bool buried = probe != null && probe.IsQueuedForDeletion();
                // ...and the six each kind used to need are GONE, with exactly two spawn RPCs left
                // on Hub for every kind there will ever be. NetDeployHulls is in the list too: the
                // 10 Hz HULL packet was the turrets' own, and is now one packet for every kind that
                // fills in SpawnKind.Hull (Hub.NetHulls), so a further kind adds no RPC either.
                int spawnRpcs = typeof(Hub).GetMethods(nfm).Count(mi => mi.Name.StartsWith("NetSpawn"));
                bool noCopies = new[] { "NetRaiderSpawn", "AddRaider", "DropRaider", "NetRaiderGone",
                                        "NetDeploy", "AddDeployed", "DropDeployed", "RemoveDeployed", "NetDeployGone",
                                        "NetDeployHulls" }
                                .All(nm => typeof(Hub).GetMethod(nm, nfm) == null);
                Spawns.All.RemoveAt(extra);                           // the table back as the game ships it
                Check(born && buried && spawnRpcs == 2 && noCopies
                      && H.Raiders.Count == raiders0 && H.Deployed.Count == turrets0,
                      $"a FURTHER host-spawned replicated kind took the generic path with no new method: its own id space ({probe?.NetId}), "
                      + $"built, named and dropped by Hub.Spawn/Hub.Down, {spawnRpcs} spawn RPCs on Hub and none of the per-kind methods left ({noCopies})");
            }
        }

        // ── THE NINE NEW CLASSES ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // Every class in the table flies, with the hull, mounts and bar its row says; then the
        // one thing each of the nine is FOR.
        {
            foreach (var cd in Classes.All.Where(x => x.Ready).ToList())
            {
                await SetClass(cd.Id);
                var nc = Me; var sheet = nc.Stats;
                int nMain = Nodes<Turret>(nc).Count(t => !t.PointDefense), nPd = Nodes<Turret>(nc).Count(t => t.PointDefense);
                Check(nc.Class == cd.Id && Math.Abs(nc.MaxHp - sheet["hull"]) < 1e-6
                      && nMain == (int)sheet["main_count"] && nPd == (int)sheet["pd_count"]
                      && Abilities.For(cd.Id).Length == cd.Abilities.Length + 6
                      && Classes.Art(cd.Id).Texture != null,
                      $"{cd.Name} flies: {sheet["hull"]:0} hull, {nMain} main and {nPd} PD mounts, {cd.Abilities.Length} abilities and six open slots");
            }

            // ── FREIGHTER: three turrets out, one collected, and a bubble that soaks 400 ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightHauler);
            // EMPTY SPACE to work in: the practice fighters south of the base are small craft, so a
            // deployed turret would lock onto one and never kill it, and the belt, the wreck and the
            // portal all have traffic. South-east and far out is clear of every one of them.
            // CLEAR OF THE OUTPOST GUNS. Every outpost stands 3000 u out on a diagonal and
            // answers out to Lanes.Range, so a mark placed where this arena used to sit took an
            // outpost's 42 before the ability under test ever reached it. Out here nothing but the
            // check is shooting.
            var fr = Me; var yonder = VaryNear(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(4200, 3900), 500f);
            fr.Position = yonder; fr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
                fr.UseAbility("deploy", 0);
                fr.Position += Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(150f, 240f);
                await Wait(0.1);
            }
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 3 && H.Deployed.Count(t => t.OwnerId == fr.OwnerId) == 3,
                  $"T drops a turret where you are, and a fourth is refused: three out ({fr.TurretsOut})");
            {   // ONE TICK A FRAME, not two. The deploy cooldown used to be run down by a line of
                // its own outside the loop that runs the slots down; the loop reaches every row
                // now, so that line is gone. Two ticks would spend the 6 s between drops in 3.
                fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 6.0;
                double cool0 = fr.Sl("deploy").Cool, clock0 = fr.Clock;
                await Wait(1.0);
                double offCool = cool0 - fr.Sl("deploy").Cool, offClock = fr.Clock - clock0;
                Check(Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.FreightHauler)["deploy_cooldown"] - 6) < 1e-9
                      && Math.Abs(offCool - offClock) < 0.05,
                      $"the 6 s between drops counts down a second a second, not two ({offCool:0.00} s off it in {offClock:0.00} s of clock)");
                fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
            }
            var one = H.Deployed.First();
            // its gun is its OWNER's sheet, read live -- so the pilot's points and its gear reach
            // every turret it has out. (A bare freighter's 6 a shot is a literal where the table is proved.)
            Check(Math.Abs(one.Hp - fr.Stats["deploy_hull"]) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Damage - fr.Stats["deploy_damage"]) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Interval - fr.Stats["deploy_interval"]) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Range - (float)fr.Stats["deploy_range"]) < 1e-3
                  && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.FreightHauler)["deploy_damage"] - 6) < 1e-9,
                  $"a dropped turret fires its owner's sheet: {one.Spec(true).Damage:0.0} a shot every {one.Spec(true).Interval:0.00} s inside {one.Spec(true).Range:0} u, on {one.Hp:0} hull");
            fr.Position = one.Position + new Vector2(90, 0); await Wait(0.15);
            fr.UseAbility("collect", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 2, $"C over one picks it up again ({fr.TurretsOut} left out)");
            fr.Position = yonder + new Vector2(0, -900); await Wait(0.15);       // clear of all three
            fr.UseAbility("collect", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 2 && fr.FailNote("collect") == "NOT OVER ONE", "and from 400 u away it refuses, saying why");
            // a raider walks into one: the turret shoots it, and the raider shoots back
            var tgt = H.Deployed.First();
            var standAt = VaryNear(tgt.Position, 420f);                          // inside its 500 u, any direction
            var rr = H.SpawnRaider(standAt); rr.Hp = 400;
            double rh0 = rr.Hp;
            // held where it appeared: this is a check on the GUN, not on how long a raider with
            // business elsewhere happens to stay in reach of it
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rr.Hp == rh0; i++)
            { rr.Position = standAt; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(rr.Hp < rh0,
                  $"a turret left standing shoots what comes near it ({rh0 - rr.Hp:0.0} damage at {standAt.DistanceTo(tgt.Position):0} u, aiming at {(tgt.Aimed == null ? "nothing" : "it")})");
            rr.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── WHAT A TURRET LEFT STANDING TAKES (Targeting.Sentry) ──
            // Point defence's filter would keep it to missiles and small craft, and a heavy, a boss
            // and a station are most of what a freighter's turrets are for. The table, in the
            // request's words and in ONE place; the live turret below reads the row.
            {
                var snKinds = new[] { Tag.Missile, Tag.Light, Tag.Fighter, Tag.Heavy, Tag.Boss, Tag.Structure };
                var snFallbacks = new[] { Tag.Dummy, Tag.Dummy | Tag.Light };
                bool SnTakes(TargetFilter snF, Tag snTag) => snF.Chooses(new StandIn { Tags = snTag });
                bool SnSpares(TargetFilter snF, Tag snTag) => snF.IsFallback(new StandIn { Tags = snTag });
                Check(snKinds.All(snT => SnTakes(Targeting.Sentry, snT) && !SnSpares(Targeting.Sentry, snT))
                      && snFallbacks.All(snT => SnTakes(Targeting.Sentry, snT) && SnSpares(Targeting.Sentry, snT))
                      && new[] { Tag.Heavy, Tag.Boss, Tag.Structure, Tag.Dummy }.All(snT => !SnTakes(Targeting.PointDefence, snT))
                      && snKinds.Concat(snFallbacks).All(snT => !SnSpares(Targeting.PointDefence, snT))
                      && H.Deployed.All(snD => snD.Spec(true).Prey.Equals(Targeting.Sentry)),
                      $"a turret left standing takes {string.Join(", ", snKinds.Where(snT => SnTakes(Targeting.Sentry, snT)))}, and a practice dummy only as a fallback ({string.Join(" / ", snFallbacks.Where(snT => SnSpares(Targeting.Sentry, snT)))}); point defence takes no heavy, boss, station or dummy hulk, and has no fallback");
            }
            // ...AND ON A LIVE TURRET. One left standing, and in ONE frame a light, a heavy and a
            // boss put in its reach -- the heavy and the boss NEARER than the light, so distance
            // alone would choose wrong. The small craft first; with it gone, the nearer of the two
            // big ones; that heavy called off with its hull left (still "alive", and freed at the
            // frame's end: the gun must let go of it, not read it); then the boss. The boss is
            // built from a row that stands still and does nothing, as the arena's row check builds
            // one, with a hull no check here can empty.
            {
                var snGun = H.Deployed.First();
                foreach (var snOther in H.Deployed.Where(snD => snD != snGun).ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(snOther);
                await Wait(0.1);
                float snReach = snGun.Spec(true).Range, snWay = VaryAngle();
                var snAt = snGun.Position;
                Vector2 SnAround(float snShare, float snTurn) => snAt + Vector2.Right.Rotated(snWay + snTurn) * snReach * snShare;
                var snLight = H.SpawnRaider(SnAround(Vary(0.75f, 0.9f), 0f));
                var snHeavy = H.SpawnRaider(SnAround(Vary(0.3f, 0.4f), Vary(1.6f, 2.4f)), Enemies.Gunship);
                var snType = Missions.Bosses[0];
                var snPost = new Boss { Hub = H, Position = SnAround(Vary(0.5f, 0.65f), Vary(-2.4f, -1.6f)),
                                        Type = new Missions.BossType { Id = "post", Name = "POST", Hull = 100000, Sprite = snType.Sprite,
                                                                       Length = snType.Length, HalfWidth = snType.HalfWidth,
                                                                       CloseSpeed = 0f, TurnRate = 0f, Moves = System.Array.Empty<BossMove>() } };
                AddChild(snPost);                                        // its _Ready puts it among the hostiles
                foreach (var snR in new[] { snLight, snHeavy }) { snR.Hp = 9000; snR.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 30); }
                for (int snI = 0; snI < 30 && snGun.Aimed == null; snI++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(ReferenceEquals(snGun.Aimed, snLight),
                      $"a turret left standing takes the small craft first: a light {snAt.DistanceTo(snLight.Position):0} u off, over a heavy at {snAt.DistanceTo(snHeavy.Position):0} u and a boss at {snAt.DistanceTo(snPost.Position):0} u (aiming at {snGun.Aimed?.Label ?? "nothing"})");
                snLight.TakeDamage(1e9);
                for (int snI = 0; snI < 30 && !ReferenceEquals(snGun.Aimed, snHeavy); snI++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                for (int snI = 0; snI < 60 * 3 && snHeavy.Hp >= 9000; snI++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(ReferenceEquals(snGun.Aimed, snHeavy) && snHeavy.Hp < 9000,
                      $"...then the HEAVY, the nearer of the two big ones: {9000 - snHeavy.Hp:0.0} into a gunship {snAt.DistanceTo(snHeavy.Position):0} u off (aiming at {snGun.Aimed?.Label ?? "nothing"})");
                float snHeavyGap = snAt.DistanceTo(snHeavy.Position), snPostGap = snAt.DistanceTo(snPost.Position);
                snHeavy.Quarry = fr;
                H.CallOff(fr);                                           // withdrawn with its hull left: still "alive"
                for (int snI = 0; snI < 30 && !ReferenceEquals(snGun.Aimed, snPost); snI++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                bool snLetGo = !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(snHeavy) && ReferenceEquals(snGun.Aimed, snPost);
                for (int snI = 0; snI < 60 * 3 && snPost.Hp >= snPost.MaxHp; snI++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(snLetGo && snPost.Hp < snPost.MaxHp,
                      $"...a heavy called off with {snHeavy.Hp:0} hull left is let go, not read after it is freed ({snLetGo}, {snHeavyGap:0} u off); then the BOSS: {snPost.MaxHp - snPost.Hp:0.0} into it at {snPostGap:0} u");
                // A PRACTICE DUMMY IS A FALLBACK. With the boss gone and a dummy nearer than anything
                // was, the dummy is taken -- a pilot drops turrets beside one to read them -- and a
                // light that comes into reach FURTHER off than the dummy takes the gun off it at once.
                var snDummy = new TargetDummy { Number = 9, Position = SnAround(Vary(0.15f, 0.25f), Vary(-0.6f, 0.6f)) };
                AddChild(snDummy); Combat.Hostiles.Add(snDummy);         // as Hub adds its own
                float snDummyGap = snAt.DistanceTo(snDummy.Position);
                snPost.QueueFree();                                       // out of the hostiles as it leaves the tree
                for (int snI = 0; snI < 60 * 3 && snDummy.Total <= 0; snI++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                bool snTested = ReferenceEquals(snGun.Aimed, snDummy) && snDummy.Total > 0;
                var snLate = H.SpawnRaider(SnAround(Vary(0.75f, 0.9f), Vary(2.6f, 3.6f)));
                snLate.Hp = 9000; snLate.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 30);
                float snLateGap = snAt.DistanceTo(snLate.Position);
                for (int snI = 0; snI < 10 && !ReferenceEquals(snGun.Aimed, snLate); snI++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(snTested && ReferenceEquals(snGun.Aimed, snLate),
                      $"a practice dummy is a fallback: alone in reach at {snDummyGap:0} u it is taken ({snDummy.Total:0.0} on its meter), and a light at {snLateGap:0} u takes the gun off it (aiming at {snGun.Aimed?.Label ?? "nothing"})");
                snLate.TakeDamage(1e9);
                Combat.Hostiles.Remove(snDummy); snDummy.QueueFree();
                await Wait(0.1);
            }
            foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);
            await Wait(0.1);

            fr.UseAbility("bubble", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(fr.BubbleUp && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleLeft - 400) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleRadius - 260f) < 1e-3,
                  $"F raises a bubble of 400 over 260 u ({fr.BubbleLeft:0})");
            double hull0 = fr.Hp;
            fr.Hit(150, fr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:bubble1");
            Check(Math.Abs(fr.Hp - hull0) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleLeft - 250) < 1e-6,
                  $"a 150 blow comes out of the bubble, not the hull ({fr.BubbleLeft:0} left, hull {fr.Hp:0})");
            fr.Hit(300, fr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:bubble2");
            Check(fr.BubbleLeft == 0 && Math.Abs(fr.Hp - (hull0 - 50)) < 1e-6,
                  $"and when the pool is spent the rest lands on the hull ({fr.Hp:0} of {hull0:0})");

            // ── A WRECK FIRES NOTHING, AND PRESSES NOTHING BUT REBOARD ──
            // Both halves are the HOST's rule, so both are poked the way a guest reaches them: the
            // trigger comes off the wire (a guest's 20 Hz report is built before the host's 10 Hz
            // word of its death arrives, so it re-armed a cleared trigger for a round trip, and a
            // client that never cleared it fired out of stasis for ever), and a press arrives at
            // DoAbility, where Refuse -- the owner's courtesy -- has no say.
            var doAbility = typeof(PlayerShip).GetMethod("DoAbility", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
            foreach (var dpl0 in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl0);
            await Wait(0.1);
            fr.Restore(0, false, 0);                       // a wreck, its stasis already run out
            fr.Trigger = true;                             // ...as the wire would set it
            bool wreckTrigger = fr.Trigger;
            fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
            doAbility.Invoke(fr, new object[] { "deploy", 0 });
            await Wait(0.15);
            int outWhileDead = fr.TurretsOut;
            doAbility.Invoke(fr, new object[] { "reboard", 0 });
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!wreckTrigger && outWhileDead == 0 && fr.Alive && Math.Abs(fr.Hp - fr.MaxHp * 0.33) < 0.5,
                  $"a wreck fires nothing and drops nothing: the wire's trigger reads {wreckTrigger} on it and a T from its own peer makes {outWhileDead} turrets -- and reboard, the one ability declared for a wreck, still climbs aboard at a third of the hull ({fr.Hp:0} of {fr.MaxHp:0})");
            fr.Restore(fr.MaxHp, true, 0); fr.Trigger = false;
            await Wait(0.1);

            // ── A WAVE LEAVES A MISSILE IN FLIGHT ALONE ──
            // The wave, the EMP and the echo read the raw hostile list, where an interceptable
            // seeker also lives. So they deleted missiles (counting each as a point-defence
            // intercept), and the wave THREW one its full push -- on the host only, while every
            // guest flew its own copy along the old path: two peers, one damaging missile, a
            // thousand units apart.
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightBastion);
            var bn = Me; bn.Position = yonder; bn.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            int took0 = Shot.Intercepted;
            Combat.LaunchTorpedo(bn.Position + new Vector2(0, -140), Vector2.Down, 40f, 900f, 5, bn.NetId, 0.4f,
                                 heavy: false, hostile: true);
            await Wait(0.1);
            var seeker = Nodes<Shot>().FirstOrDefault(s => s.HostileFire && s.Kind == Shots.Seeker);
            var seekerWas = seeker?.Position ?? Vector2.Zero;
            // ...and a body in flight with a hull of its own, which is no Missile but is not thrown either
            // (140 u off the other side of the keel from the seeker: its capsule is symmetric, and the
            // seeker there has not touched it)
            var cruiseNear = Combat.Fire(Shots.Cruise, bn.Position + new Vector2(0, 140), Vector2.Up, 40f, 900f, 5,
                                         targetId: bn.NetId, turnRate: 0.4f, size: 3f, hull: 30);
            var cruiseWas = cruiseNear?.Position ?? Vector2.Zero;
            bn.Sl("shockwave").Cool = 0; bn.UseAbility("shockwave", 0);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(seeker != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(seeker) && seeker.Position.DistanceTo(seekerWas) < 60f
                  && cruiseNear != null && cruiseNear.Alive && cruiseNear.Position.DistanceTo(cruiseWas) < 60f
                  && Shot.Intercepted == took0 && Math.Abs(bn.Stats["wave_push"] - 1000) < 1e-6,
                  $"a 1000 u shockwave throws nothing in flight -- a seeker or a cruise missile -- or counts it as an intercept: they moved {seeker?.Position.DistanceTo(seekerWas):0} and {cruiseNear?.Position.DistanceTo(cruiseWas):0} u of their own and {Shot.Intercepted - took0} were taken");
            if (seeker != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(seeker)) seeker.QueueFree();
            if (cruiseNear != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(cruiseNear)) cruiseNear.QueueFree();
            // The ring this raised must be gone before the effects block below counts what is up:
            // the checks between here and there are table reads, and pass no game time at all.
            await Wait(0.9);

            // ── TENDER: everything it owns fires twice as fast ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightTender);
            var td = Me; td.Position = VaryNear(yonder, 200f); td.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; td.Rotation = VaryAngle(); await Wait(0.2);
            td.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0; td.UseAbility("deploy", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            var tt = H.Deployed.First();
            double gun0 = td.Spec(false).Interval, drop0 = tt.Spec(true).Interval;
            // THE SHELLS THEMSELVES, counted as they leave the barrel: the mean gap from the first to
            // the last over four seconds on the trigger. Spec().Interval is a figure the point
            // defence reads, and this check read it too, while the main gun fired off whatever
            // FireControl read -- the raw reload. A check on the spec alone passed while the gun fired
            // one shell a second whatever was pressed.
            var shellKey = Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.FreightTender, "guns");
            async Task<(double gap, int n)> GunCadence(double seconds)
            {
                var gunSeen = new HashSet<Shot>(Nodes<Shot>().Where(bolt => bolt.Source == td));
                var gunAt = new List<double>();
                double gunC0 = td.Clock;
                KeyDown(shellKey);
                while (td.Clock - gunC0 < seconds)
                {
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    foreach (var bolt in Nodes<Shot>()) if (bolt.Source == td && gunSeen.Add(bolt)) gunAt.Add(td.Clock);
                }
                KeyUp(shellKey);
                return (gunAt.Count > 1 ? (gunAt[^1] - gunAt[0]) / (gunAt.Count - 1) : 99, gunAt.Count);
            }
            var gunSlow = await GunCadence(4.0);
            await Wait(1.0);                  // off the trigger the reload runs out, so the next run starts loaded
            td.UseAbility("overdrive", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Math.Abs(td.Spec(false).Interval - gun0 / 2) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(tt.Spec(true).Interval - drop0 / 2) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(td.FireRate - 2f) < 1e-6,
                  $"overdrive halves the reload of its gun AND of every turret it has out ({gun0:0.00} -> {td.Spec(false).Interval:0.00} s)");
            var gunFast = await GunCadence(4.0);
            Check(gunSlow.n >= 4 && gunFast.n >= 8 && Math.Abs(gunSlow.gap / gunFast.gap - 2) < 0.1,
                  $"...and its gun FIRES twice as fast: a shell every {gunSlow.gap:0.000} s, then every {gunFast.gap:0.000} s under the overdrive ({gunSlow.n} then {gunFast.n} shells in 4 s)");
            foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);

            // ── EVERY EFFECT IS A ROW, AND EVERY PEER GETS IT ──
            // The shockwave, the EMP and the echo used to be drawn as spokes of LASER FLASHES,
            // because that was the only drawing that reached a guest, and the echo's burst was
            // added straight to the host's own tree where nobody else ever saw it.
            {
                // ── A CLAIMED LEVEL BUYS ONLY WHAT IT COULD HAVE PAID FOR ──
                // The host has no way to check a guest's level, so what a claim may SPEND is
                // capped. The affordability gate used to weigh the RAW wire level and the clamp
                // landed after it, on the stored field: a peer claiming two billion bought every
                // row maxed on the host's own copy of its ship -- +300 hull and four times the
                // damage on a level-1 pilot. And a claim it could not pay for was refused WHOLE,
                // so an honest pilot past the cap arrived anywhere as a stock hull.
                int Spent(int[] b) { int s = 0; for (int i = 0; i < b.Length; i++) for (int k = 0; k < b[i]; k++) s += Progression.Cost(k); return s; }
                var greedy = Progression.Afford(new[] { 60, 60, 60, 60 }, 2000000000);
                var honest = Progression.Afford(new[] { 0, 13, 0, 0 }, 92);          // 13 hull levels cost 1+2+...+13 = 91
                var past   = Progression.Afford(new[] { 0, 60, 0, 0 }, 100000);
                double greedyHull = Progression.Flats(greedy, ShipClass.Battleship).GetValueOrDefault("hull");
                Check(Progression.MaxSpendLevel == 100 && Spent(greedy) <= 99 && greedyHull <= 65
                      && Spent(honest) == 91 && honest[1] == 13
                      && Spent(past) <= 99 && past[1] == 13,
                      $"a claim buys only what its level could have paid for, and no more than 99 points on anyone's host: every row maxed at level two billion comes to {Spent(greedy)} points and +{greedyHull:0} hull, not +300; an honest 13 hull levels at level 92 survive whole; and a pilot past the cap keeps {past[1]} of its 60");

                // ── A PILOT'S WEAPONS POINTS REACH WHAT THE CLASS CALLS ITS WEAPONS ──
                // The damage upgrade used to name ONE stat for every class in the game -- torpedoes
                // for a carrier, main guns for everything else -- so a sniper's railgun, a warden's
                // hunters, a warrior's EMP and a freighter's deployed turrets took nothing from it.
                {
                    var keepBought = (int[])Character.Bought.Clone();
                    int[] two = new int[Progression.All.Length];
                    two[System.Array.FindIndex(Progression.All, u => u.Id == "damage")] = 2;   // two Weapons levels
                    // A SHARE, so two levels is 6% of whatever that weapon is on that hull -- the
                    // railgun's 150, the hunters' 45, the EMP's 60. The class still says WHICH
                    // stats; what changed is that the figure compounds instead of being a step.
                    double Rise(ShipClass c, string stat)
                        => new ShipStats(c, Progression.Shares(two, c))[stat] - new ShipStats(c)[stat];
                    double Six(ShipClass c, string stat) => new ShipStats(c)[stat] * 0.06;
                    Check(Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rail_damage") - Six(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rail_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.HeavyWarden, "hunter_damage") - Six(ShipClass.HeavyWarden, "hunter_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, "emp_damage") - Six(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, "emp_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.FreightHauler, "deploy_damage") - Six(ShipClass.FreightHauler, "deploy_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.LightEcho, "main_damage") - Six(ShipClass.LightEcho, "main_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rail_damage") - 9) < 1e-9,
                          $"two Weapons levels are 6% of each weapon the class declares: the sniper's railgun +{Rise(ShipClass.HeavySniper, "rail_damage"):0.0} of its 150, the warden's hunters +{Rise(ShipClass.HeavyWarden, "hunter_damage"):0.0}, the warrior's EMP +{Rise(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior, "emp_damage"):0.0}, a dropped turret +{Rise(ShipClass.FreightHauler, "deploy_damage"):0.00}");
                    // ...and change NOTHING a saved pilot already had on the three it could reach
                    Check(Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.Battleship, "main_damage") - Six(ShipClass.Battleship, "main_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Rise(ShipClass.Battleship, "pd_damage") == 0
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.Carrier, "torpedo_damage") - Six(ShipClass.Carrier, "torpedo_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Rise(ShipClass.Carrier, "fighter_damage") == 0
                          && Math.Abs(Rise(ShipClass.Destroyer, "main_damage") - Six(ShipClass.Destroyer, "main_damage")) < 1e-9
                          && Rise(ShipClass.Destroyer, "missile_damage") == 0,
                          $"and the three original hulls take it where they always did -- 6% of the gun or the torpedoes (+{Rise(ShipClass.Battleship, "main_damage"):0.0} on the battleship) -- and nothing at all on the fighters, the missiles or the point defence, whose rows are declared at zero and stay there");
                    // the gear that lifts "every weapon" reaches the same list, and nothing else
                    Check(string.Join(",", Classes.EveryDamageStat())
                          == "deploy_damage,emp_damage,fighter_damage,hunter_damage,main_damage,missile_damage,rail_damage,torpedo_damage"
                          && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => c.Damage.Count > 0)
                          && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => c.Damage.Keys.All(k => new ShipStats(c.Id)[k] > 0)),
                          $"eight weapon stats in the whole game, every flyable class names at least one, and every one it names is a row on its own sheet");

                    // ── AND THE K WINDOW'S DAMAGE BLOCK IS A ROW PER SYSTEM (ClassDef.Weapons) ──
                    // Its total added four named figures -- main, missile, broadside, point defence
                    // -- so the sniper read 7.50 DPS beside a railgun worth 37.50, and the two
                    // point-defence lines were drawn on the five classes that mount none.
                    var snL = new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavySniper);
                    Check(Math.Abs(snL.SustainedDps - 45.0) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(snL.DamageLines.First(l => l.Label.StartsWith("Railgun")).Dps - 37.5) < 1e-6
                          && snL.DamageLines.Count() == 2 && !snL.DamageLines.Any(l => l.Label.Contains("Point defence")),
                          $"the sniper's total counts its railgun: the gun 6/0.8 = 7.5 and the rail 150/(3+1) = 37.5 -- 45 DPS, not 7.50, and no point-defence line on a hull that mounts none ({snL.SustainedDps:0.00})");
                    Check(Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavyWarden).SustainedDps - (12 / 0.6 + 6 * 45 / 14.0 + 5 / 0.5)) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.FreightHauler).SustainedDps - 49.0) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior).SustainedDps - (2 * 9 / 0.7 + 60 / 18.0)) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.LightEcho).SustainedDps - (5 / 0.35 + (5 / 0.35) * 5 / 15.0)) < 1e-6,
                          $"...and every other signature weapon counts too: the warden 20 + 19.29 + 10, the freighter's gun 12 + three dropped turrets 36 + 1 = 49, the warrior's EMP 60 every 18 s, the echo repeating five seconds of its own damage every 15");
                    Check(Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier).SustainedDps - (3 * (2.5 / 0.35) * (15.0 / 18.0) + 4 * 2 * 137.5 / 27.5 + 3 * 0.5 / 0.5 * 0.5)) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier).DamageLines.First(l => l.Label.StartsWith("Bomber")).Dps - 40.0) < 1e-6,
                          $"the carrier's torpedoes are in its total at last: 8 x 137.5 = 1100 over a 27.5 s run (20 s out and back, 1.5 s launching, 6 s rearming) = 40 DPS, beside 17.86 from the fighters and 1.5 from the point defence ({new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier).SustainedDps:0.00})");
                    Check(Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => c.Weapons.Length > 0)
                          && Classes.All.Where(c => c.Ready).All(c => new ShipStats(c.Id).DamageLines.All(l => l.Dps > 0 && l.Sustained)),
                          "every flyable class declares what it kills with, and every line it declares is a rate above zero");

                    // ── AND NO BLURB WRITES A FIGURE ITS OWN ROWS CARRY ──
                    // "three volleys", "400 damage", "six hunter-seekers", "1.2 s": all of them are
                    // live rows that gear and pilot points move, so the first gear family written
                    // for one of those systems made the words beside it lie.
                    Check(!Classes.All.Any(c => c.Blurb.Any(char.IsDigit))
                          && !Classes.All.SelectMany(c => Abilities.For(c.Id)).Any(a => a.Blurb.Any(char.IsDigit)),
                          "no class or ability blurb writes a number: every figure is on the STATS tab, where gear and points move it");
                    // ── EVERY UPGRADE ROW SAYS WHAT IT MOVES ──
                    // What a row moved lived in a SECOND private table plus `if (id == "damage")`,
                    // so a row missing from that table was fully purchasable and moved nothing at
                    // all. One level of every row, on every flyable class, has to land on the
                    // sheet -- and the three that name their own stats move them by the figures
                    // the design asks for: a degree of rudder, 5 hull, 1 u/s.
                    {
                        (string stat, double per)[] Moved(string id)
                            => Progression.StatsFor(Progression.All.First(u => u.Id == id), ShipClass.Battleship).ToArray();
                        var silent = new List<string>();
                        int[] oneLevel = new int[Progression.All.Length];
                        for (int i = 0; i < Progression.All.Length; i++)
                        {
                            System.Array.Clear(oneLevel); oneLevel[i] = 1;
                            foreach (var cd in Classes.All.Where(x => x.Ready))
                            {
                                // BOTH SIDES: a row's figures are amounts (hull, rudder, engines)
                                // or shares (weapons, reach, cooling, gunnery), and a row that
                                // moves nothing on either side is a row that cannot be bought.
                                var moved = ShipStats.Sum(Progression.Flats(oneLevel, cd.Id),
                                                          Progression.Shares(oneLevel, cd.Id));
                                if (!Progression.StatsFor(Progression.All[i], cd.Id).Any() || moved.Values.All(v => v == 0))
                                    silent.Add($"{Progression.All[i].Id}/{cd.Id}");
                            }
                        }
                        // SEVEN ROWS: the four it opened with, and the owner's three -- reach,
                        // cooling and gunnery. Every one of them must move something on every
                        // flyable class, which is what makes them worth a point on any hull.
                        var reachOf = Moved("reach"); var coolOf = Moved("cool"); var rofOf = Moved("rof");
                        Check(Progression.All.Length == 7 && silent.Count == 0
                              && Moved("turn").Length == 1 && Moved("turn")[0].stat == "turn_rate"
                              && Math.Abs(Moved("turn")[0].per - Mathf.DegToRad(1f)) < 1e-9
                              && Moved("hull")[0] == ("hull", 5.0) && Moved("speed")[0] == ("max_speed", 1.0)
                              && reachOf.Any(t => t.stat == "main_range" && Math.Abs(t.per - 0.01) < 1e-9)
                              && Progression.All.Count(u => u.Share) == 4 && Progression.All.First(u => u.Id == "damage").Share
                              && coolOf.Length == 1 && coolOf[0].stat == "cooldown_share" && Math.Abs(coolOf[0].per - 0.005) < 1e-9
                              && rofOf.Any(t => t.stat == "main_interval" && Math.Abs(t.per - 0.005) < 1e-9),
                              $"all 7 upgrade rows move at least one stat on each of the {Classes.All.Count(x => x.Ready)} flyable classes -- a degree of rudder, 5 hull, 1 u/s, the weapons the class names, 1% of its reach, half a percent off its cooldowns and half a percent off its gun's cycle ({(silent.Count == 0 ? "none silent" : string.Join(" ", silent))})");
                        // ...AND THEY MOVE THE RIGHT WAY. An interval and the cooldown share are INVERSE
                        // stats, which a share divides: twenty Gunnery points are a 2 s battleship reload
                        // of 2 / 1.1 s, and twenty Cooling points cooldowns x1/1.1. Written negative, both
                        // went UP, and the table check above passed.
                        {
                            int[] twenty = new int[Progression.All.Length];
                            twenty[System.Array.FindIndex(Progression.All, u => u.Id == "rof")] = 20;
                            twenty[System.Array.FindIndex(Progression.All, u => u.Id == "cool")] = 20;
                            var drilled = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship, Progression.Shares(twenty, ShipClass.Battleship));
                            Check(Math.Abs(drilled["main_interval"] - 2.0 / 1.1) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(drilled["cooldown_share"] - 1 / 1.1) < 1e-9,
                                  $"twenty Gunnery points shorten a battleship's 2 s reload to {drilled["main_interval"]:0.000} s and twenty Cooling points take its cooldowns to x{drilled["cooldown_share"]:0.000} -- down, both of them");
                        }
                    }
                    // ── WHAT A TARGET IS CALLED, AND WHAT COMING FOR IT IS WORTH ──
                    // Both were type tests in Hub: `Selected is TargetDummy td` for the HUD's
                    // target line and `quarry is Hauler h ? h.Payout : 0` to size an escort, so a
                    // second kind with a name or a load of its own meant a second arm on each.
                    // They are contracts on IHittable and IRaidTarget now, with the default that
                    // everything nameless and empty-handed takes.
                    Check(((IHittable)Me).Label == $"#{Me.NetId}"
                          && H.Yard.Gatherers.All(g => ((IRaidTarget)g).Payout == 0),
                          $"a target names itself and says what it is worth: this ship is {((IHittable)Me).Label}, and a miner is worth 0 credits to the raid that comes for it");
                    System.Array.Copy(keepBought, Character.Bought, keepBought.Length);
                }

                // ── EVERYTHING THAT FLIES IS ONE FLYER WITH A ROW ──
                // Three classes (Shell, Slug, Torpedo) with one copy each of the same sweep,
                // lifetime, cosmetic-copy rule and hit. Seven rows and one node now, and ONE launch
                // on the wire where there were three.
                Check(Shots.All.Length == 7
                      && !Shots.Of(Shots.Shell).AtPlayers && Shots.Of(Shots.Shell).Sweep == 6f && Shots.Of(Shots.Shell).Burst == 0
                      && Shots.Of(Shots.Slug).AtPlayers && Shots.Of(Shots.Scrap).AtPlayers
                      && Shots.Of(Shots.Slug).Burst == 0.25 && Shots.Of(Shots.Scrap).Look == ShotLook.Shard
                      && Shots.Of(Shots.Missile).Heavy && !Shots.Of(Shots.Torpedo).Heavy
                      && Shots.Of(Shots.Seeker).Interceptable && !Shots.Of(Shots.Missile).Interceptable
                      && Shots.All.Count(x => x.Smoke) == 4
                      && Shots.All.Count(x => x.Tags == Tag.Hulled) == 1 && Shots.Of(Shots.Cruise).Tags == Tag.Hulled,
                      "seven kinds of shot, each a row: a shell, a boss's slug and its scrap, a torpedo, a heavy missile, the seeker point defence exists for, and the cruise missile anything may shoot down");
                // ── A BODY IN FLIGHT WITH A HULL OF ITS OWN (Tag.Hulled) ──
                // A missile is point defence's alone: a shell passes through it, a blow skips it, Tab
                // and a wing cannot have it, and one hit deletes it. A row that gives its body a hull
                // (the cruise missile) is a target like any hull instead -- worn down, not deleted --
                // and keeps one rule from a missile: nothing throws it. (30, 10, 19 and 1 are this
                // check's own inputs, not a row's.) Three seeded spots, the shells coming from a seeded
                // side each time, the seeker 400 u off their line so the one that passes through it
                // never reaches the cruise missile.
                {
                    int hPass = 0; string hLast = "";
                    for (int hk = 0; hk < 3; hk++)
                    {
                        var hulledAt = fr.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(1500f, 2000f);
                        var hFrom = Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle());          // the side the shells come from
                        var hcm = Combat.Fire(Shots.Cruise, hulledAt, Vector2.Down, 0.001f, 4000f, 1, size: 3f, hull: 30);
                        var hsk = Combat.Fire(Shots.Seeker, hulledAt + hFrom.Orthogonal() * 400f, Vector2.Down, 0.001f, 4000f, 1);
                        await Wait(0.1);
                        bool hTagged = TagExt.Is(hcm, Tag.Hulled) && !TagExt.Is(hcm, Tag.Missile) && TagExt.Is(hsk, Tag.Missile) && !TagExt.Is(hsk, Tag.Hulled);
                        bool hFilters = Targeting.PointDefence.Chooses(hcm) && Turret.Rank(hcm) == 0
