                                        && Targeting.Attackable.Chooses(hcm) && Targeting.WingPrey.Chooses(hcm) && !Targeting.Throwable.Hits(hcm)
                                        && !Targeting.Attackable.Chooses(hsk) && !Targeting.Throwable.Hits(hsk)
                                        && Combat.Pickable(hcm) && !Combat.Pickable(hsk) && ((IHittable)hcm).Label == "CRUISE MISSILE"
                                        && Combat.Hostiles.Contains(hcm) && Combat.Hostiles.Contains(hsk);
                        // a shell strikes the one and passes through the other (both taken where they
                        // stand after the wait, not where they were fired)
                        Combat.Fire(Shots.Shell, hcm.Position + hFrom * 300f, -hFrom, 650f, 1000f, 10, source: fr);
                        Combat.Fire(Shots.Shell, hsk.Position + hFrom * 300f, -hFrom, 650f, 1000f, 10, source: fr);
                        await Wait(0.8);
                        bool hShelled = hcm.Alive && Math.Abs(hcm.Hull - 20) < 1e-9 && hsk.Alive;
                        int hDown0 = Shot.Intercepted;
                        hcm.TakeDamage(19); bool hStood = hcm.Alive && Shot.Intercepted == hDown0;
                        hcm.TakeDamage(1); bool hFell = !hcm.Alive && Shot.Intercepted == hDown0 + 1 && !Combat.Hostiles.Contains(hcm);
                        hsk.TakeDamage(0.5); bool hSeeker = !hsk.Alive && Shot.Intercepted == hDown0 + 2;
                        if (hTagged && hFilters && hShelled && hStood && hFell && hSeeker) hPass++;
                        else hLast = $"spot {hk + 1}: tags {hTagged}, filters {hFilters}, shelled {hShelled} ({hcm.Hull:0.#} hull left), stood {hStood}, fell {hFell}, seeker {hSeeker}";
                        await Wait(0.8);          // the shell that passed the seeker spent (1000 u at 650 u/s) before the next spot
                    }
                    Check(hPass == 3,
                          $"a cruise missile's body is HULLED, not a missile: point defence first, a blow, a wing and a seeker may have it, a throw may not, Tab picks it by name; a shell takes 10 of its 30 and passes the seeker beside it; 19 more and it stands, 1 more and it falls -- where the seeker falls to its first 0.5 ({hPass} of 3 seeded spots{(hLast.Length > 0 ? "; " + hLast : "")})");
                }
                // AND EVERY EFFECT AND EVERY WARNING IS ONE NODE WITH A ROW. Telegraph was a
                // second node for the same sentence -- a shape, at a place, for a time, with a
                // sound -- with a `bool Line` where this has a shape and an RPC of its own beside
                // the one that already carried effects. Two warning rows, a lane and a zone; how
                // long, how wide and what it sounds like are the MOVE's and come with the raise,
                // because one boss move plays its cue with no warning at all.
                var warnLane = Fx.Of(Fx.WarnLane); var warnZone = Fx.Of(Fx.WarnZone); var aimZone = Fx.Of(Fx.AimZone);
                Check(Fx.All.Length == 10 && warnLane.Warn && warnZone.Warn && aimZone.Warn
                      && warnLane.Shape == FxShape.Lane && warnZone.Shape == FxShape.Zone && aimZone.Shape == FxShape.Zone
                      && warnLane.Sound == null && warnZone.Sound == null && aimZone.Sound == null
                      && new[] { Fx.Burst, Fx.Lost, Fx.Rebuilt, Fx.Wave, Fx.Emp, Fx.Echo, Fx.Rail }.All(i => !Fx.Of(i).Warn)
                      && Fx.All.Count(x => x.Warn) == 3 && Fx.World == 0 && Fx.Flash == 0.35
                      // ...and the friendly one is the SAME telegraph in another colour: where one
                      // of YOUR shots will land reads nothing like where one of THEIRS will.
                      && aimZone.Tint != warnZone.Tint && warnLane.Tint == warnZone.Tint,
                      $"ten rows of Fx.All, seven effects and three telegraphs: a red lane and a red zone whose clock and sounds are the raise's, and the same zone in your own colours for where your own shot lands ({Fx.All.Length} rows, {Fx.All.Count(x => x.Warn)} telegraphs)");
                // the sweep the shell and the rock share: a step longer than a hull is how a shot
                // passes through it between frames
                {
                    int seen = 0;
                    bool struck = Shots.Sweep(Vector2.Zero, new Vector2(60, 0), 6f, _ => { seen++; return false; });
                    bool stops = Shots.Sweep(Vector2.Zero, new Vector2(60, 0), 6f, _ => true);
                    int once = 0;
                    Shots.Sweep(Vector2.Zero, new Vector2(600, 0), 0f, _ => { once++; return false; });
                    Check(seen == 10 && !struck && stops && once == 1,
                          $"Shots.Sweep walks a 60 u step in ten 6 u tests, stops at the first touch, and tests only the end point when a row asks for no sweep ({seen}, {once})");
                }

                Check(Fx.All.Length == 10 && Fx.Of(Fx.Wave).Shape == FxShape.Ring
                      && Fx.Of(Fx.Emp).Shape == FxShape.Spokes && Fx.Of(Fx.Echo).Shape == FxShape.Spokes
                      && Fx.Of(Fx.Rail).Shape == FxShape.Bar && Fx.Of(Fx.Burst).Shape == FxShape.Burst
                      && Fx.Of(Fx.Rebuilt).Tint != Fx.Of(Fx.Lost).Tint,
                      "the effects, each a row: a burst, a fleet craft lost and rebuilt, a wave, an EMP, an echo and a railgun's bar -- and beside them the three telegraphs, which were their own node until they became rows too");
                int fxBefore = Nodes<FxNode>().Count();
                Fx.Raise(Fx.Wave, fr.Position + new Vector2(0, -600), 400f);
                await Wait(0.1);
                var raised = Nodes<FxNode>().Where(f => f.Id == Fx.Wave).ToList();
                Check(raised.Count == 1 && Nodes<FxNode>().Count() == fxBefore + 1 && Math.Abs(raised[0].Radius - 400f) < 1e-3,
                      $"Fx.Raise puts one up in the live world, at the reach it was given ({raised.Count} up)");
                await Wait(1.0);
                Check(!Nodes<FxNode>().Any(f => f.Id == Fx.Wave), "and it takes itself away when its life is up");
            }

            // ── BASTION: everything within 1000 u thrown a thousand units clear ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightBastion);
            var bs = Me; bs.Position = yonder; bs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var near = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(300, 0)); near.Hp = 2000;
            var far = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(1600, 0)); far.Hp = 2000;
            await Wait(0.1);
            float nd0 = near.Position.DistanceTo(yonder), fd0 = far.Position.DistanceTo(yonder);
            bs.UseAbility("shockwave", 0); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float nd1 = near.Position.DistanceTo(yonder), fd1 = far.Position.DistanceTo(yonder);
            Check(Nodes<FxNode>().Any(f => f.Id == Fx.Wave), "the shockwave puts its ring up where it went off");
            Check(nd1 - nd0 > 950 && nd1 - nd0 < 1050 && Math.Abs(fd1 - fd0) < 60,
                  $"the shockwave throws what is within 1000 u a thousand units clear, and nothing beyond it ({nd1 - nd0:0} u / {fd1 - fd0:0} u)");
            near.TakeDamage(99999); far.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── SNIPER: 3 s locked in place, then 150 down a 2500 u line ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavySniper);
            var sn = Me; var dTgt = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            // One Weapons point on this hull is +1 on the gun AND +7.5 on the railgun. The L window
            // printed a single "next +1" for the pair, because it read the row's Per and not what
            // the purchase actually does (Progression.StatsFor).
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.15);
            var spw = Nodes<PilotWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            var sRow = spw == null ? null : Nodes<Label>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(spw).First(r => r.Name == "Pilot_damage")).First();
            // A SHARE READS AS A PERCENTAGE: +3% of each weapon the class names, where the window
            // used to advertise a step of +1 the purchase never actually gave.
            Check(sRow != null && sRow.Text.Contains("Damage per shot") && sRow.Text.Contains("+3% a level")
                  && sRow.Text.Contains("Railgun · Damage") && !sRow.Text.Contains("+0.03"),
                  $"the sniper's Weapons row prints what a point really buys -- 3% of its gun AND 3% of its railgun, as a percentage rather than three hundredths of a damage ({sRow?.Text.Replace('\n', '|')})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
            float railAt = Vary(900f, 2200f);                     // anywhere inside its 2500 u reach
            sn.Position = dTgt.Position + new Vector2(0, railAt); sn.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; sn.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            double dmg0 = dTgt.Total;
            sn.UseAbility("railgun", 0); await Wait(0.2);
            var wasAt = sn.Position;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.A); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.A);
            Check(sn.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled) && sn.Position.DistanceTo(wasAt) < 1f,
                  $"the railgun charges with the hull locked: W and A move it nowhere ({sn.Position.DistanceTo(wasAt):0.00} u)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && dTgt.Total == dmg0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(dTgt.Total - dmg0 - sn.Stats["rail_damage"]) < 0.01 && !sn.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled)
                  && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavySniper)["rail_damage"] - 150) < 1e-9,
                  $"then its railgun's whole {sn.Stats["rail_damage"]:0.0} lands {railAt:0} u away, on the line, and the hull is free again ({dTgt.Total - dmg0:0.0})");

            // ── WARRIOR: two and a half times its speed, half the damage, then the EMP ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior);
            var wr = Me; wr.Position = yonder; wr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var e1 = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(yonder, 200f)); e1.Hp = 500;        // inside the EMP's 260 u
            var e2 = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(800f, 1200f)); e2.Hp = 500;   // well outside its 260 u
            var e2Home = e2.Position;
            await Wait(0.1);
            double top0 = wr.Stats["max_speed"];
            wr.UseAbility("rush", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            double wh0 = wr.Hp;
            wr.Hit(100, wr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:rush");
            Check(wr.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(wh0 - wr.Hp - 50) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(wr.SpeedMult - 2.5f) < 1e-6,
                  $"the rush: 2.5x top speed ({top0 * wr.SpeedMult:0} u/s) and a 100 blow lands for {wh0 - wr.Hp:0}");
            double e10 = e1.Hp, e20 = e2.Hp;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && e1.Hp == e10; i++) { e2.Position = e2Home; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(Math.Abs(e10 - e1.Hp - wr.Stats["emp_damage"]) < 0.01 && e1.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled) && e2.Hp == e20
                  && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior)["emp_damage"] - 60) < 1e-9,
                  $"and it ends in an EMP: 60 to what is within 260 u ({e1.Position.DistanceTo(wr.Position):0} u) and it is held; nothing at {e2.Position.DistanceTo(wr.Position):0} u ({e10 - e1.Hp:0})");
            e1.TakeDamage(99999); e2.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            // THE RUSH LIFTS THE PUSH AS WELL AS THE TOP SPEED. The water holds a hull to thrust /
            // drag, so a lift on the cap alone stopped there: 130 against 0.35 is 371 u/s, and from
            // rest the 2.5 s rush gathered about 214 of the 475 its 2.5x promises. Measured, not
            // read: from rest, the same short spell on the throttle without the rush and with it,
            // and then the whole rush on it.
            {
                var runAt = VaryNear(yonder, 300f); float runHeading = VaryAngle();
                double spell = Vary(0.4f, 0.8f);                       // short of either top speed
                async Task<(double gathered, double top)> Gather(bool rushing)
                {
                    wr.Sl("rush").Left = 0; wr.Sl("rush").Cool = 0; wr.Statuses.Clear(Status.Hardened);
                    wr.Position = runAt; wr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; wr.Rotation = runHeading;
                    if (rushing) wr.UseAbility("rush", 0);
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    wr.Position = runAt; wr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; wr.Rotation = runHeading;
                    // TIMED FROM THE FIRST FRAME THE HULL MOVES, not from the key: the press lands on
                    // the next frame or the one after, and a frame of thrust the clock did not count
                    // is 3% of a 0.6 s spell -- enough to read 2.42 for an exact 2.5.
                    double rushTop = 0;
                    KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 30 && wr.SpeedAhead <= 0.01f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double heldFrom = wr.Clock, v0 = wr.SpeedAhead;
                    while (wr.Clock - heldFrom < spell) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double gathered = (wr.SpeedAhead - v0) / (wr.Clock - heldFrom);        // u/s gathered a second
                    while (rushing && wr.Sl("rush").Left > 0)
                    { rushTop = Math.Max(rushTop, wr.SpeedAhead); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W); wr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    return (gathered, rushTop);
                }
                var unrushed = await Gather(false);
                var rushRun = await Gather(true);
                Check(Math.Abs(wr.Stats["max_speed"] - 190) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(rushRun.gathered / unrushed.gathered - 2.5) < 0.05
                      && rushRun.top >= 0.98 * 2.5 * 190,
                      $"the rush lifts the push with the top speed: from rest it gathers way 2.5x as fast ({unrushed.gathered:0} -> {rushRun.gathered:0} u/s a second), and before it ends it reaches its whole 2.5x of 190 ({rushRun.top:0} of 475 u/s)");
                wr.Statuses.Clear(Status.Hardened);
            }

            // ── WARDEN: point defence with no window, and six hunters on six targets ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarden);
            var wd = Me; wd.Position = yonder; wd.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            Check(wd.PdOnline && !wd.PdActive && wd.PdRing == 0f && Math.Abs(wd.Spec(true).Damage - 5.0) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(wd.Stats["pd_interval"] - 0.5) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(wd.Stats.PdDuty - 1) < 1e-9,
                  $"the warden's point defence is on with nothing pressed -- 5 a shot every 0.5 s, on all of the time ({wd.Stats.PdSustainedDps:0.0} DPS sustained)");
            // and it KILLS: one light raider inside its 420 u, nothing else firing, dead in seconds.
            // (At the 0.25 a shot it was first written with, this took 50 s and the class's whole
            // reason for existing did nothing a pilot could see.)
            {
                var pdMark = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(wd.Position, 380f));       // inside 420 u, any bearing
                double pdStart = wd.Clock;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && pdMark.Alive; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(!pdMark.Alive && wd.Clock - pdStart < 6,
                      $"...and it kills a light raider on its own in {wd.Clock - pdStart:0.0} s, with nothing pressed");
            }
            var marks = new List<Raider>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { var m = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(300 + 60 * i, -200 + 90 * i)); m.Hp = 3000; marks.Add(m); }
            await Wait(0.2);
            int torp0 = InFlight().Count();
            wd.UseAbility("hunters", 0); await Wait(0.2);
            var fired = InFlight().Where(t => !t.HostileFire && t.TargetId != 0).ToList();
            Check(fired.Count >= 6 - torp0 && fired.Select(t => t.TargetId).Distinct().Count() >= 4,
                  $"six hunter-seekers away, each on a target of its own ({fired.Count} out, {fired.Select(t => t.TargetId).Distinct().Count()} targets)");
            foreach (var m in marks) m.TakeDamage(99999);
            await Wait(0.3);

            // ── DART: untouchable through the roll, then faster and firing quicker ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightDart);
            var dt2 = Me; dt2.Position = yonder; dt2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            double dh0 = dt2.Hp, rof0 = dt2.Spec(false).Interval;
            dt2.UseAbility("roll", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            dt2.Hit(80, dt2.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:roll");
            Check(dt2.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && Math.Abs(dt2.Hp - dh0) < 1e-6,
                  "through the barrel roll nothing touches it at all");
            await Wait(1.4);
            Check(!dt2.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && Math.Abs(dt2.SpeedMult - 1.6f) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(dt2.Spec(false).Interval - rof0 / 1.25) < 1e-9,
                  $"and it comes out of it 60% faster, firing a quarter quicker ({rof0:0.000} -> {dt2.Spec(false).Interval:0.000} s)");
            {   // ...and BOTH multipliers are the rows', not two `if`s naming slot ids by string:
                // with EVERY one of the dart's slots running at once, the only two stats that
                // move them are the roll's own -- 1.25x the rate of fire and 1.6x the speed.
                foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(ShipClass.LightDart)) dt2.Sl(ab.Id).Left = 5.0;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(!dt2.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && Math.Abs(dt2.FireRate - 1.25f) < 1e-6
                      && Math.Abs(dt2.SpeedMult - 1.6f) < 1e-6,
                      $"with every one of its slots running, only the roll's own two stats move them ({dt2.FireRate:0.00}x rate, {dt2.SpeedMult:0.00}x speed)");
                foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(ShipClass.LightDart)) dt2.Sl(ab.Id).Left = 0;
            }
            // RATE BUFFS ADD, THEY DO NOT MULTIPLY (the owner's ruling, and the rule a sheet's bonuses
            // already follow): two x2 lifts at once are x3, not x4; x1.25 with x1.6 is x1.85, not
            // x2.0; a cut to x0.85 is x0.85; a row the sheet has not got lifts nothing; and a stack of
            // cuts stops at the sheet's floor of a tenth, never at a standstill. And a lift ADDS TO A
            // PART: a +100% rate part under a x2 overdrive fires the tender's 1 s gun every third of a
            // second, not every quarter. No class carries two lifts of one kind and no check wears a
            // rate part, so the rule is proved here, on literals.
            {
                double Lifted(params double[] mults) => Stat.Scale(PlayerShip.LiftShares(mults));
                var partWorn = new ShipStats(ShipClass.FreightTender, new Dictionary<string, double> { ["main_interval"] = 1.0 });
                double partAndLift = partWorn.With("main_interval", PlayerShip.LiftShares(new[] { 2.0 }));
                Check(Math.Abs(Lifted(2.0, 2.0) - 3) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Lifted(1.25, 1.6) - 1.85) < 1e-9
                      && Math.Abs(Lifted(0.85) - 0.85) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Lifted(2.0, 0.0) - 2) < 1e-9
                      && Math.Abs(Lifted(0.3, 0.3) - 0.1) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Lifted() - 1) < 1e-9
                      && Math.Abs(new ShipStats(ShipClass.FreightTender)["main_interval"] - 1.0) < 1e-9
                      && Math.Abs(partWorn["main_interval"] - 0.5) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(partAndLift - 1.0 / 3) < 1e-9,
                      $"rate buffs add: x2 with x2 is x{Lifted(2.0, 2.0):0.00} (not x4), x1.25 with x1.6 is x{Lifted(1.25, 1.6):0.00}, two cuts to x0.3 stop at x{Lifted(0.3, 0.3):0.00}, nothing running is x{Lifted():0.00} "
                      + $"-- and a +100% part under a x2 overdrive fires the tender's 1 s gun every {partAndLift:0.000} s (x3), not every 0.250 s");
            }

            // ── ECHO: what it deals for five seconds, all at once at the end ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightEcho);
            var ec = Me; ec.Position = yonder; ec.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var mark = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(150, 0)); mark.Hp = 4000;
            var near2 = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(260, 0)); near2.Hp = 4000;
            await Wait(0.1);
            ec.UseAbility("echo", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            ec.NoteDealt(90, mark.Position);                       // as a shell of its own would
            Check(Math.Abs(ec.Sl("echo").Own - 90) < 1e-6, $"the echo remembers what it deals ({ec.Sl("echo").Own:0})");
            double m0 = mark.Hp, n0 = near2.Hp;
            var markHome = mark.Position; var nearHome = near2.Position;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && ec.Sl("echo").Left > 0; i++)
            { mark.Position = markHome; near2.Position = nearHome; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Math.Abs(m0 - mark.Hp - 90) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(n0 - near2.Hp - 90) < 0.01,
                  $"and puts all of it down where the last of it landed, on everything within 220 u ({m0 - mark.Hp:0})");
            mark.TakeDamage(99999); near2.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── WRAITH: five seconds nothing hostile can pick it ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightWraith);
            var wt = Me; wt.Position = yonder; wt.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var hunter = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(yonder, 500f)); hunter.Hp = 4000;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && hunter.Target != wt; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hunter.Target == wt, "a raider comes for the wraith");
            wt.UseAbility("stealth", 0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && hunter.Target == wt; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(wt.Statuses.Has(Status.Untargetable) && hunter.Target != wt && Targeting.Hidden(wt),
                  "and the moment it goes dark the raider lets go of it");
            // ...AND WHAT A RAID THROWS STILL LANDS. Stealth stops a raider CHOOSING the ship; it
            // never stopped a burst falling where the ship is. The raid's blast was resolved over
            // Hub.RaiderTargets, which left every hidden ship out, so a dark ship sat in a burst
            // untouched. One thrown by hand, as a raid's own row, onto a spot inside its 90 u.
            {
                string burstKey = $"heavy:{hunter.NetId}:missile";
                double burst0 = wt.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault(burstKey);
                var burstAt = VaryNear(wt.Position, 40f);
                H.ThrowMissile(new MissileSpec { Side = Missiles.Raid, Damage = 42, Blast = 90f, Flight = 0.5 },
                               hunter.Position, burstAt, hunter.NetId);
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && wt.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault(burstKey) == burst0; i++)
                { wt.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                double burstTook = wt.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault(burstKey) - burst0;
                Check(Targeting.Hidden(wt) && hunter.Target != wt && Math.Abs(burstTook - 42) < 1e-6,
                      $"...and a raid's 42-damage burst {burstAt.DistanceTo(wt.Position):0} u from the dark wraith lands on it in full, while the raider still has not picked it ({burstTook:0} taken)");
            }
            await Wait(5.2);
            Check(!wt.Statuses.Has(Status.Untargetable), "five seconds later it is visible again");
            // AND THE HUNT COMES BACK. A raider re-decides only when its target stops being valid,
            // so the miner it took while the wraith was dark stayed valid for ever: one press of
            // stealth used to hand the whole wave to the base's fleet permanently.
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && hunter.Target != wt; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hunter.Target == wt,
                  $"...and the raider takes the wraith back the moment it can see it again (now after {(hunter.Target == wt ? "it" : Raider.Up(hunter.Target) ? "something else" : "nothing")})");
            hunter.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── EVERY WEAPON A PILOT CARRIES, AGAINST THE CRUISE MISSILE ─────────────
            // The filters say a rail, an EMP, an echo, a seeker, a wing, a dropped turret and point
            // defence may each have a body with a hull of its own (Tag.Hulled); these fire the real
            // thing at one. Each at a seeded spot out here, at a cruise missile of 30 hull standing
            // still (0.001 u/s, homing on nothing), and each reads the first blow the frame it lands:
            // the hull comes off by THAT weapon's own damage, off the ship's live sheet (the class
            // checks above pin each sheet to its literal).
            {
                Shot XCruise(Vector2 at) => Combat.Fire(Shots.Cruise, at, Vector2.Down, 0.001f, 4000f, 1, size: 3f, hull: 30);
                async Task<double> XFirstBlow(Shot cm, double seconds, System.Action hold)
                {
                    for (int i = 0; i < (int)(60 * seconds) && cm.Alive && cm.Hull >= 30; i++)
                    { hold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    return 30 - cm.Hull;
                }

                // THE RAILGUN: on its line, anywhere inside its 2500 u
                await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavySniper);
                {
                    var xrShip = Me; var xrAt = VaryNear(yonder, 300f); float xrHead = VaryAngle();
                    void XrHold() { xrShip.Position = xrAt; xrShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; xrShip.Rotation = xrHead; }
                    XrHold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); XrHold();
                    var xrCm = XCruise(xrAt + Vector2.Up.Rotated(xrHead) * Vary(600f, 2200f));
                    xrShip.UseAbility("railgun", 0);
                    double xrDrop = await XFirstBlow(xrCm, 6, XrHold);
                    Check(Math.Abs(xrDrop - xrShip.Stats["rail_damage"]) < 1e-6 && !xrCm.Alive,
                          $"the railgun brings a cruise missile down: {xrDrop:0.0} off its 30 hull, the rail's own {xrShip.Stats["rail_damage"]:0}, {xrCm.Position.DistanceTo(xrAt):0} u down its line");
                    xrCm.Intercept();
                }
                // THE EMP at the end of the warrior's rush: inside its 260 u, clear of the 120 u hull
                await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior);
                {
                    var xeShip = Me; var xeAt = VaryNear(yonder, 300f);
                    void XeHold() { xeShip.Position = xeAt; xeShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                    XeHold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); XeHold();
                    var xeCm = XCruise(xeAt + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(110f, 220f));
                    xeShip.UseAbility("rush", 0);
                    double xeDrop = await XFirstBlow(xeCm, 5, XeHold);
                    Check(Math.Abs(xeDrop - xeShip.Stats["emp_damage"]) < 1e-6 && !xeCm.Alive && !typeof(IStatused).IsAssignableFrom(typeof(Shot)),
                          $"the warrior's EMP lands on a cruise missile {xeCm.Position.DistanceTo(xeAt):0} u off for its own {xeShip.Stats["emp_damage"]:0}: {xeDrop:0.0} off its 30 hull, and down -- its hold has nothing to hold, a body in flight carrying no statuses");
                    xeCm.Intercept();
                }
                // THE ECHO: what it remembers, put down where the last of it landed -- on the missile
                await SetClass(ShipClass.LightEcho);
                {
                    var xcShip = Me; var xcAt = VaryNear(yonder, 300f);
                    void XcHold() { xcShip.Position = xcAt; xcShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                    XcHold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); XcHold();
                    var xcCm = XCruise(xcAt + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(150f, 400f));
                    xcShip.UseAbility("echo", 0); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    xcShip.NoteDealt(24, xcCm.Position);                 // a hit of its own landing there, as a shell's would
                    double xcDrop = await XFirstBlow(xcCm, 7, XcHold);
                    Check(Math.Abs(xcDrop - 24 * xcShip.Stats["echo_share"]) < 1e-6,
                          $"the echo puts down what it remembered on a cruise missile: 24 dealt where it stands, {xcDrop:0.0} off its 30 hull at the echo's x{xcShip.Stats["echo_share"]:0.00}");
                    xcCm.Intercept();
                }
                // A SEEKER HOMING ON IT: the warden's cell, past its own point defence's 420 u
                await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarden);
                {
                    var xhShip = Me; var xhAt = VaryNear(yonder, 300f);
                    void XhHold() { xhShip.Position = xhAt; xhShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                    XhHold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); XhHold();
                    var xhCm = XCruise(xhAt + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(600f, 1000f));
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    xhShip.UseAbility("hunters", 0); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var xhAway = InFlight().Where(t => !t.HostileFire && t.Source == xhShip).ToList();
                    double xhDrop = await XFirstBlow(xhCm, 8, XhHold);
                    Check(xhAway.Count == (int)xhShip.Stats["hunter_count"] && xhAway.All(t => t.TargetId == xhCm.NetId)
                          && Math.Abs(xhDrop - xhShip.Stats["hunter_damage"]) < 1e-6 && !xhCm.Alive,
                          $"the warden's seekers home on a cruise missile {xhCm.Position.DistanceTo(xhAt):0} u off: {xhAway.Count(t => t.TargetId == xhCm.NetId)} of {xhAway.Count} on it, and the first to arrive takes {xhDrop:0.0} off its 30 hull, one seeker's {xhShip.Stats["hunter_damage"]:0}, and brings it down");
                    xhCm.Intercept();
                }
                // A CARRIER WING RETARGETING TO IT: sent at a light raider that then dies, the wing
                // takes the nearest other hostile for itself -- the missile
                await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier);
                {
                    var xwShip = Me; var xwAt = VaryNear(yonder, 300f); float xwWay = VaryAngle();
                    void XwHold() { xwShip.Position = xwAt; xwShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                    XwHold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); XwHold();
                    var xwFirst = H.SpawnRaider(xwAt + Vector2.Right.Rotated(xwWay) * Vary(350f, 500f));
                    xwFirst.Hp = 9000; xwFirst.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 30);
                    var xwCm = XCruise(xwAt + Vector2.Right.Rotated(xwWay + Vary(-0.8f, 0.8f)) * Vary(550f, 800f));
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    xwShip.UseAbility("attack", xwFirst.NetId);
                    bool xwSent = ReferenceEquals(xwShip.WingTarget, xwFirst);
                    xwFirst.TakeDamage(1e9);
                    double xwDrop = await XFirstBlow(xwCm, 15, XwHold);
                    Check(xwSent && ReferenceEquals(xwShip.WingTarget, xwCm) && Math.Abs(xwDrop - xwShip.Stats["fighter_damage"]) < 1e-6,
                          $"a carrier's wing sent at a raider that dies takes the cruise missile {xwCm.Position.DistanceTo(xwAt):0} u off for itself (sent {xwSent}; now on {(ReferenceEquals(xwShip.WingTarget, xwCm) ? "the missile" : xwShip.WingTarget?.Label ?? "nothing")}), and its first pass takes {xwDrop:0.0} off the 30 hull, one fighter's shot of {xwShip.Stats["fighter_damage"]:0.0}");
                    xwShip.RecallWing(); xwCm.Intercept();
                }
                // A DROPPED TURRET PICKS IT BEFORE A LIGHT RAIDER: the light nearer, the missile further
                // round, both in its reach, and nothing else here
                await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightHauler);
                {
                    var xsShip = Me; var xsAt = VaryNear(yonder, 300f);
                    xsShip.Position = xsAt; xsShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    xsShip.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0; xsShip.UseAbility("deploy", 0); await Wait(0.1);
                    var xsGun = H.Deployed.FirstOrDefault(t => t.OwnerId == xsShip.OwnerId);
                    var xsOff = xsAt + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * 1500f;   // the freighter well clear of it
                    void XsHold() { xsShip.Position = xsOff; xsShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                    XsHold();
                    float xsReach = xsGun?.Spec(true).Range ?? 500f, xsWay = VaryAngle();
                    var xsCentre = xsGun?.Position ?? xsAt;
                    var xsLight = H.SpawnRaider(xsCentre + Vector2.Right.Rotated(xsWay) * xsReach * Vary(0.3f, 0.45f));
                    xsLight.Hp = 9000; xsLight.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 30);
                    var xsCm = XCruise(xsCentre + Vector2.Right.Rotated(xsWay + Vary(1.6f, 2.4f)) * xsReach * Vary(0.7f, 0.85f));
                    for (int i = 0; i < 30 && xsGun != null && xsGun.Aimed == null; i++) { XsHold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    bool xsTook = xsGun != null && ReferenceEquals(xsGun.Aimed, xsCm);
                    double xsDrop = await XFirstBlow(xsCm, 4, XsHold);
                    Check(xsTook && Math.Abs(xsDrop - xsShip.Stats["deploy_damage"]) < 1e-6 && xsLight.Hp == 9000,
                          $"a dropped turret picks a cruise missile {xsCentre.DistanceTo(xsCm.Position):0} u off before a light raider {xsCentre.DistanceTo(xsLight.Position):0} u off (aiming at {xsGun?.Aimed?.Label ?? "nothing"} first), and its first shot takes {xsDrop:0.0} off the 30 hull -- the turret's own {xsShip.Stats["deploy_damage"]:0.0} -- with the raider untouched ({xsLight.Hp:0})");
                    xsLight.TakeDamage(1e9); xsCm.Intercept();
                    foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);
                }
                // A WARSHIP'S POINT DEFENCE PICKS IT BEFORE A LIGHT RAIDER: off its first mount, both
                // astern of it (so the missile stands clear of the hull it would otherwise strike), the
                // light nearer and the missile further, both in reach of either mount. The mount that
                // chooses first takes the missile; the other, finding it held, takes the light.
                await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
                {
                    var xpShip = Me; var xpAt = VaryNear(yonder, 300f); float xpHead = VaryAngle();
                    void XpHold() { xpShip.Position = xpAt; xpShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; xpShip.Rotation = xpHead; }
                    XpHold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); XpHold();
                    var xpFirst = xpShip.PdTurrets[0]; var xpFrom = xpFirst.GlobalPosition; var xpAft = Vector2.Down.Rotated(xpHead);
                    var xpLight = H.SpawnRaider(xpFrom + xpAft.Rotated(Vary(-1.0f, -0.3f)) * Vary(140f, 200f));
                    xpLight.Hp = 9000; xpLight.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 30);
                    var xpCm = XCruise(xpFrom + xpAft.Rotated(Vary(0.3f, 1.0f)) * Vary(300f, 380f));
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    xpShip.UseAbility("pd", 0);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 30 && xpShip.PdTurrets.All(t => t.Target == null); i++) { XpHold(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    bool xpTook = ReferenceEquals(xpFirst.Target, xpCm);
                    double xpDrop = await XFirstBlow(xpCm, 5, XpHold);
                    Check(xpTook && Math.Abs(xpDrop - xpShip.Spec(true).Damage) < 1e-6,
                          $"a battleship's point defence picks a cruise missile {xpFrom.DistanceTo(xpCm.Position):0} u off before a light raider {xpFrom.DistanceTo(xpLight.Position):0} u off: the first mount to choose takes the missile (on {xpFirst.Target?.Label ?? "nothing"}; the other on {xpShip.PdTurrets[^1].Target?.Label ?? "nothing"}), and its first shot takes {xpDrop:0.00} off the 30 hull, one point-defence shot of {xpShip.Spec(true).Damage:0.00}");
                    xpShip.Sl("pd").Left = 0; xpLight.TakeDamage(1e9); xpCm.Intercept();
                    await Wait(0.2);
                }
            }

            // ── EVERY TIMED ROW TICKS, WHATEVER CLASS CARRIES IT ────────────────────
            // A timed ability was a row everywhere except the tick: PlayerShip kept a string[]
            // of the nine ids that had timers, so a tenth added as a row compiled, bound, drew
            // on the bar and its Left never moved, with nothing to say so. Put 5 s of Left and
            // 5 s of Cool on EVERY slot of every flyable class and watch all of them fall. This
            // FAILS the moment one row is dropped from the tick again.
            {
                var stuck = new List<string>();
                int rows = 0;
                foreach (var cd in Classes.All.Where(x => x.Ready).ToList())
                {
                    await SetClass(cd.Id);
                    var tk = Me; tk.Position = yonder; tk.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(cd.Id)) { tk.Sl(ab.Id).Left = 5.0; tk.Sl(ab.Id).Cool = 5.0; rows++; }
                    await Wait(0.5);
                    foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(cd.Id))
                        if (tk.Sl(ab.Id).Left > 4.7 || tk.Sl(ab.Id).Cool > 4.7) stuck.Add($"{cd.Name}.{ab.Id}");
                    foreach (var ab in Abilities.For(cd.Id)) { tk.Sl(ab.Id).Left = 0; tk.Sl(ab.Id).Cool = 0; }
                }
                Check(stuck.Count == 0 && rows >= 12 * 6,
                      $"every ability row of every class runs its own timers down: 5 s put on all {rows} of them, none above 4.7 s half a second later ({(stuck.Count == 0 ? "none stuck" : string.Join(", ", stuck))})");
            }

            // ── EVERY ABILITY ON EVERY BAR, AND HOW FAR EVERY CLASS REACHES ──────────
            // The blocks above take ONE class at a time and prove the one thing it is for. These
            // two take all twelve and sweep them: every ability each of them carries, pressed --
            // and every reach each of them declares, measured from just inside it and from just
            // outside it. Both are driven from the TABLES (Classes.All, ClassDef.Abilities,
            // ClassDef.Reach), so a thirteenth class, a new ability or a new reach id is swept
            // the moment it is a row -- and one with nothing here to witness it FAILS rather
            // than passing silently, which is the whole point of a sweep.
            {
                // Somewhere with nothing in it: past the outposts (3000 u) and past the edge a
                // raid comes in from (4200 u), so nothing can wander into a measurement. Every
                // placement below is drawn from the run's own SEED around this one spot.
                var afield = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(4200, 3900);
                // A HOSTILE HELD EXACTLY WHERE IT IS PUT. Disabled is the shockwave's own hold on
                // a boss, and a raider under it sits still -- so no marker can fly off on an
                // errand mid-measurement and no check below is a check on the clock.
                async Task<Raider> Marker(Vector2 at)
                {
                    var born = H.SpawnRaider(at);
                    born.Hp = 9000; born.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 20);
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    return born;
                }
                void Sink(Raider mk1) { if (mk1 != null && mk1.Alive) mk1.TakeDamage(99999); }

                // ── A. EVERY ABILITY, PRESSED -- AND THE SECOND PRESS REFUSED ─────────────
                // WHAT COUNTS AS DONE, one row per ability id: a slot lit, a cooldown set, a
                // status applied, bodies launched, a turret dropped. Reached BY ID, never by
                // class, so two classes carrying the same ability share the one witness -- and an
                // ability added to the catalogue with nothing here to see it fails the coverage
                // check below. (The trigger's own path from key to shell is proved by the
                // battleship and destroyer DPS checks earlier in this run; what is asked of a
                // HOLD row here is that its own key reaches the ship at all.)
                int sweepShots = 0, sweepTurrets = 0, sweepMag = 0;
                bool sweepMode = false;
                Raider sweepMark = null;
                var witness = new Dictionary<string, Func<PlayerShip, bool>>
                {
                    ["guns"]      = sp => sp.Trigger && Abilities.Find(sp.Class, "guns").State(sp, null).Lit,
                    // LOCAL: the owner's own intent, which never leaves this machine. It flips,
                    // and it engages no slot at all.
                    ["firemode"]  = sp => sp.Staggered != sweepMode && sp.Sl("firemode").Left == 0 && sp.Sl("firemode").Cool == 0,
                    ["broadside"] = sp => sp.BroadsideTracking && sp.Sl("broadside").Left > sp.Stats["broadside_windup"] - 0.1,
                    ["pd"]        = sp => sp.PdActive && sp.Sl("pd").Left > sp.Stats["pd_active"] - 0.1,
                    ["missile"]   = sp => sp.MissilesLoaded == sweepMag - 1 && InFlight().Count() >= sweepShots + PlayerShip.BurstSides.Length,
                    ["reload"]    = sp => sp.Reloading && sp.Sl("reload").Left > sp.Stats["missile_reload"] - 0.1,
                    ["attack"]    = sp => ReferenceEquals(sp.WingTarget, sweepMark),
                    ["recall"]    = sp => sp.WingTarget == null,
                    ["bombers"]   = sp => ReferenceEquals(sp.StrikeTarget, sweepMark),
                    ["deploy"]    = sp => sp.TurretsOut == sweepTurrets + 1 && sp.Sl("deploy").Cool > 0,
                    ["collect"]   = sp => sp.TurretsOut == sweepTurrets - 1,
                    ["bubble"]    = sp => sp.BubbleUp && Math.Abs(sp.BubbleLeft - sp.Stats["bubble_pool"]) < 0.5,
                    ["overdrive"] = sp => Math.Abs(sp.FireRate - (float)sp.Stats["overdrive_mult"]) < 1e-3f,
                    ["shockwave"] = sp => sp.Sl("shockwave").Cool > 0 && Nodes<FxNode>().Any(fx3 => fx3.Id == Fx.Wave),
                    ["railgun"]   = sp => sp.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled) && sp.Sl("railgun").Left > sp.Stats["rail_charge"] - 0.1,
                    ["rush"]      = sp => sp.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(sp.SpeedMult - (float)sp.Stats["rush_mult"]) < 1e-3f,
                    ["hunters"]   = sp => sp.Sl("hunters").Cool > 0 && InFlight().Count() >= sweepShots + (int)sp.Stats["hunter_count"],
                    ["roll"]      = sp => sp.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && sp.Sl("roll").Left > sp.Stats["roll_time"] + sp.Stats["boost_time"] - 0.1,
                    ["echo"]      = sp => sp.Sl("echo").Left > sp.Stats["echo_time"] - 0.1,
                    ["stealth"]   = sp => sp.Statuses.Has(Status.Untargetable) && sp.Sl("stealth").Left > sp.Stats["stealth_time"] - 0.1,
                };
                var onBars = Classes.All.Where(cd2 => cd2.Ready).SelectMany(cd2 => cd2.Abilities)
                                        .Where(ab3 => !ab3.Open).Select(ab3 => ab3.Id).Distinct().ToList();
                var unwitnessed = onBars.Where(id2 => !witness.ContainsKey(id2)).ToList();
                Check(unwitnessed.Count == 0 && witness.Count == onBars.Count,
                      $"every ability on every bar has a witness in this sweep: {onBars.Count} ids across the twelve flyable classes against {witness.Count} rows "
                      + $"({(unwitnessed.Count == 0 ? "none missing" : "MISSING " + string.Join(", ", unwitnessed))})");

                // WHAT A FITTING BRINGS. A class carries an ability because of a fitting; where it
                // has NOT got the fitting, that ability must not be on its bar at all. One row per
                // fitting, so a new ability of an existing kind is covered by adding its id here.
                var fitRows = new (Fit fit, string[] ids)[] {
                    (Fit.Guns,      new[] { "guns", "firemode" }),
                    (Fit.Broadside, new[] { "broadside" }),
                    (Fit.Missiles,  new[] { "missile", "reload" }),
                    (Fit.Wing,      new[] { "attack", "recall", "bombers" }),
                    (Fit.Deploy,    new[] { "deploy", "collect" }),
                };

                foreach (var cls in Classes.All.Where(cd2 => cd2.Ready).ToList())
                {
                    await SetClass(cls.Id);
                    var sweepShip = Me;
                    sweepShip.Position = VaryNear(afield, 700f); sweepShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    sweepShip.Rotation = VaryAngle();
                    await Wait(0.15);

                    // ...and the ABSENCE where that is the fact: a light has no point defence, so
                    // it has no pd slot and nothing of its own is online. Point defence is the one
                    // fitting that comes apart from its ability -- a hull whose PD never switches
                    // off (the warden) HAS the fitting and carries no key for it.
                    var wrongFit = fitRows.Where(fr2 => fr2.ids.Any(id3 => (Abilities.Find(cls.Id, id3) != null) != cls.Has(fr2.fit)))
                                          .Select(fr2 => fr2.fit.ToString()).ToList();
                    bool pdRight = (Abilities.Find(cls.Id, "pd") != null) == (cls.Has(Fit.Pd) && !cls.Has(Fit.AlwaysPd))
                                   && sweepShip.PdOnline == cls.Has(Fit.AlwaysPd);
                    Check(wrongFit.Count == 0 && pdRight,
                          $"{cls.Name}: every ability on its bar is one of its fittings, and every fitting it has NOT got takes its abilities with it -- "
                          + $"point defence {(cls.Has(Fit.AlwaysPd) ? "always on, no key" : Abilities.Find(cls.Id, "pd") != null ? "on a key, switched off" : "not carried at all")}"
                          + $"{(wrongFit.Count == 0 ? "" : ", WRONG: " + string.Join(", ", wrongFit))}");

                    // THE SIX OPEN HOTKEYS after its own abilities: bound, shown, and they do
                    // nothing. Pressing every one of them must engage no slot -- they are where
                    // the next ability goes, and until then a press is a press on nothing.
                    var opens = Abilities.For(cls.Id).Where(ab4 => ab4.Open).ToList();
                    foreach (var openAb in opens) doAbility.Invoke(sweepShip, new object[] { openAb.Id, 0 });
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    Check(opens.Count == 6 && opens.All(openAb => openAb.Press == null
                                                        && sweepShip.Sl(openAb.Id).Left == 0 && sweepShip.Sl(openAb.Id).Cool == 0),
                          $"{cls.Name}: {opens.Count} open hotkeys after its {cls.Abilities.Length} own, and pressing every one of them engages no slot");

                    // the one target every press that wants one is given, put back before each
                    sweepMark = await Marker(VaryNear(sweepShip.Position, 320f));
                    var markAt = sweepMark.Position;

                    foreach (var abil in cls.Abilities)
                    {
                        sweepMark.Position = markAt;                  // back where it was, whatever moved it
                        sweepShots = InFlight().Count();
                        sweepTurrets = sweepShip.TurretsOut;
                        sweepMode = sweepShip.Staggered;
                        sweepMag = sweepShip.MissilesLoaded;
                        var abKey = Abilities.KeyFor(cls.Id, abil.Id);
                        bool isHold = abil.Kind == AbilityKind.Hold;
                        if (isHold)
                        {   // a HOLD row has no Press at all: its own key goes down and stays down
                            KeyDown(abKey);
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                        }
                        else if (abil.Local) sweepShip.UseAbility(abil.Id, 0);
                        else doAbility.Invoke(sweepShip, new object[] { abil.Id, sweepMark.NetId });
                        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                        bool didIt = witness.TryGetValue(abil.Id, out var sawIt) && sawIt(sweepShip);
                        if (isHold) { KeyUp(abKey); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }

                        // THE SECOND PRESS. A row that engaged its slot must refuse it: neither
                        // the time it has left nor its cooldown may go UP. (A row that engages no
                        // slot -- the carrier's attack and recall, a freighter's collect -- has
                        // nothing to refuse, and its witness is the whole of it; the bombers'
                        // refusal of a second strike is measured with strike_range below.)
                        var slotWas = sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id);
                        bool engaged = slotWas.Left > 0 || slotWas.Cool > 0;
                        bool refusedAgain = true;
                        if (engaged)
                        {
                            doAbility.Invoke(sweepShip, new object[] { abil.Id, sweepMark.NetId });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            var slotNow = sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id);
                            refusedAgain = slotNow.Left <= slotWas.Left + 1e-9 && slotNow.Cool <= slotWas.Cool + 1e-9;
                        }
                        string told = engaged
                            ? refusedAgain ? $"a second press while it is engaged is refused ({slotWas.Left:0.0} s up, {slotWas.Cool:0.0} s cooling)"
                                           : $"a second press RESTARTED it ({slotWas.Left:0.0} s up, {slotWas.Cool:0.0} s cooling)"
                            : $"it engages no slot, so there is nothing to refuse (controls locked: {Hub.ControlsLocked})";
                        Check(didIt && refusedAgain,
                              $"{cls.Name} {abil.Short}: the press {(didIt ? "did" : "did NOT do")} what its row says, and {told}");

                        // nothing this press started may run on into the next one
                        sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id).Left = 0; sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id).Cool = 0; sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id).Own = 0;
                    }

                    // THE WORLD PUT BACK, so the class after this one starts where this one did:
                    // every slot at rest, the magazine full, every status this class's abilities
                    // put on the hull cleared, the wing called home, and whatever it dropped or
                    // was aiming at gone.
                    foreach (var abil in Abilities.For(cls.Id))
                    {
                        sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id).Left = 0; sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id).Cool = 0;
                        sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id).Own = 0; sweepShip.Sl(abil.Id).N = 0;
                    }
                    sweepShip.Sl("missile").N = (int)sweepShip.Stats["missile_mag"];
                    foreach (var flag in new[] { Status.Disabled, Status.Evading, Status.Hardened, Status.Untargetable })
                        sweepShip.Statuses.Clear(flag);
                    sweepShip.RecallWing();
                    sweepShip.Forget(sweepMark);
                    foreach (var standing in H.Deployed.ToList())
                        if (standing.OwnerId == sweepShip.OwnerId) H.DeployedTaken(standing);
                    Sink(sweepMark);
                    await Wait(0.1);
                }

                // ── B. MINIMUM AND MAXIMUM RANGE, CLASS BY CLASS AND REACH BY REACH ──────
                // ClassDef.Reach is what a class says its weapons REACH, by stat id: the list a
                // pilot's REACH points and the Targeting Chip both lift. Nothing proved that any
                // of those figures was the distance the weapon actually stops at -- so a reach
                // could be raised by a purchase and change nothing a pilot could see. For every
                // flyable class and EVERY id it declares, a target stands just inside and a second
                // just outside, on bearings this run drew, and the weapon must engage the one and
                // not the other. The `default` arm FAILS: a reach nothing measures cannot be added
                // quietly.
                //
                // THE THREE THAT ARE FLOWN, NOT FIRED, and so are not here: a fighter opens up at
                // fighter_range only after it has launched and flown its approach; a bomber turns
                // to launch after a run out to launch_range at 190 u/s; and the torpedo's own run
                // is that flight again. Each is about ten seconds in the air for one figure, and
                // the carrier's block above already flies a whole strike.
                var flownNotFired = new[] { "fighter_range", "launch_range", "torpedo_range" };
                var skipped = new List<string>();

                async Task ProveReach(PlayerShip pilot, ClassDef cdef, string stat)
                {
                    double reach = pilot.Stats[stat];
                    float bearing = VaryAngle();
                    var way = Vector2.Right.Rotated(bearing);
                    var wide = way.Rotated(Vary(1.2f, 2.4f));      // a second bearing, well clear of the first
                    bool proved = false; string told = "";
                    switch (stat)
                    {
                        case "main_range":
                        {
                            // The gun's OWN round, out of the ship's own spec, through Combat.Fire
                            // -- the one door everything that flies comes through, and the exact
                            // call Turret.Shoot makes. Only the SPEED is the harness's (four
                            // reaches a second, so the flight is a quarter of a second rather than
                            // a second and a half); the reach, the damage and the row of Shots.All
                            // are the ship's. The far mark stands a quarter past the reach because
                            // a shell always flies one frame's step past it before it ends.
                            var gun = pilot.Spec(false);
                            var nearAt = pilot.Position + way * (float)(reach * Vary(0.80f, 0.92f));
                            var farAt = pilot.Position + wide * (float)(reach * Vary(1.25f, 1.45f));
                            var mkIn = await Marker(nearAt); var mkOut = await Marker(farAt);
                            double inWas = mkIn.Hp, outWas = mkOut.Hp;
                            Combat.Fire(gun.Kind, pilot.Position, way, (float)reach * 4f, gun.Range, gun.Damage, source: pilot);
                            Combat.Fire(gun.Kind, pilot.Position, wide, (float)reach * 4f, gun.Range, gun.Damage, source: pilot);
                            await Wait(0.35);
                            proved = mkIn.Hp < inWas && mkOut.Hp == outWas;
                            told = $"a {gun.Damage:0.0} shell lands {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(nearAt):0} u out ({inWas - mkIn.Hp:0.0} taken) and dies short of {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(farAt):0} u ({outWas - mkOut.Hp:0.0} taken)";
                            Sink(mkIn); Sink(mkOut);
                            break;
                        }
                        case "pd_range":
                        {
                            // Point defence picks its OWN target, so it is the ACQUISITION that
                            // stops at the reach and no shot has to be waited for. A hull whose PD
                            // never switches off has no window to open first.
                            var mount = Nodes<Turret>(pilot).FirstOrDefault(tu2 => tu2.PointDefense);
                            if (mount == null) { told = "it declares a point-defence reach and mounts no point-defence turret"; break; }
                            if (!pilot.PdOnline) { pilot.Sl("pd").Cool = 0; doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "pd", 0 }); }
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            var mountAt = mount.GlobalPosition;
                            var mkIn = await Marker(mountAt + way * (float)(reach * Vary(0.60f, 0.90f)));
                            for (int frame = 0; frame < 8 && mount.Target == null; frame++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            bool gotIt = ReferenceEquals(mount.Target, mkIn);
                            float gotAt = mountAt.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position);
                            mkIn.Position = mountAt + way * (float)(reach * Vary(1.30f, 1.70f));
                            for (int frame = 0; frame < 8 && mount.Target != null; frame++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            proved = gotIt && mount.Target == null;
                            told = $"a turret takes a light at {gotAt:0} u and lets it go at {mountAt.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u";
                            Sink(mkIn);
                            pilot.Sl("pd").Left = 0; pilot.Sl("pd").Cool = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        case "missile_range":
                        {
                            var mkIn = await Marker(pilot.Position + way * (float)(reach * Vary(0.55f, 0.90f)));
                            var mkOut = await Marker(pilot.Position + wide * (float)(reach * Vary(1.15f, 1.45f)));
                            int flying0 = InFlight().Count();
                            pilot.Sl("missile").N = (int)pilot.Stats["missile_mag"]; pilot.Sl("missile").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "missile", mkIn.NetId });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            int flew = InFlight().Count() - flying0;
                            pilot.Sl("missile").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "missile", mkOut.NetId });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            int flewMore = InFlight().Count() - flying0 - flew;
                            proved = flew >= PlayerShip.BurstSides.Length && flewMore == 0;
                            told = $"a burst of {flew} away at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u and {flewMore} away at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkOut.Position):0} u";
                            Sink(mkIn); Sink(mkOut);
                            pilot.Sl("missile").N = (int)pilot.Stats["missile_mag"]; pilot.Sl("missile").Cool = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        case "control_range":
                        {
                            // The fighters are ordered onto a target only while it is inside the
                            // control range, and the slot itself says so -- which is what a pilot
                            // has to go on before pressing it.
                            var atkRow = Abilities.Find(pilot.Class, "attack");
                            var mkIn = await Marker(pilot.Position + way * (float)(reach * Vary(0.55f, 0.90f)));
                            var mkOut = await Marker(pilot.Position + wide * (float)(reach * Vary(1.15f, 1.45f)));
                            string nearLine = atkRow.State(pilot, mkIn).Line, farLine = atkRow.State(pilot, mkOut).Line;
                            proved = nearLine == "READY" && farLine == "OUT OF RANGE";
                            told = $"the attack slot reads {nearLine} at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u and {farLine} at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkOut.Position):0} u";
                            Sink(mkIn); Sink(mkOut);
                            break;
                        }
                        case "strike_range":
                        {
                            var mkOut = await Marker(pilot.Position + wide * (float)(reach * Vary(1.15f, 1.45f)));
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "bombers", mkOut.NetId });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            bool refusedFar = pilot.StrikeTarget == null;
                            var mkIn = await Marker(pilot.Position + way * (float)(reach * Vary(0.45f, 0.85f)));
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "bombers", mkIn.NetId });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            bool tookNear = ReferenceEquals(pilot.StrikeTarget, mkIn);
                            // ...and a second strike ordered while one is out is refused: the
                            // bombers already called keep the target they were called for.
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "bombers", mkOut.NetId });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            bool stillOne = ReferenceEquals(pilot.StrikeTarget, mkIn);
                            proved = refusedFar && tookNear && stillOne;
                            told = $"a strike at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkOut.Position):0} u is refused, one at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u is taken, and a second order while it is out is refused ({stillOne})";
                            Sink(mkIn); Sink(mkOut);
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            break;
                        }
                        case "rail_range":
                        {
                            // The line goes off the NOSE, so the hull is turned to whatever
                            // heading this run drew and the two marks stand on it. The charge is
                            // cut to one frame: what is measured here is the shot, not the wait.
                            pilot.Rotation = bearing;
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            var railWay = Vector2.Up.Rotated(pilot.Rotation);
                            var railFrom = Aim.Nose(pilot, pilot.MyArt.Length * 0.5f);
                            var mkIn = await Marker(railFrom + railWay * (float)(reach * Vary(0.55f, 0.90f)));
                            var mkOut = await Marker(railFrom + railWay * (float)(reach * Vary(1.10f, 1.30f)));
                            double inWas = mkIn.Hp, outWas = mkOut.Hp;
                            pilot.Sl("railgun").Left = 0; pilot.Sl("railgun").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "railgun", 0 });
                            pilot.Sl("railgun").Left = 0.001;
                            await Wait(0.12);
                            proved = Math.Abs(inWas - mkIn.Hp - pilot.Stats["rail_damage"]) < 0.01 && mkOut.Hp == outWas;
                            told = $"the slug takes {inWas - mkIn.Hp:0} off what stands {railFrom.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u down the line and {outWas - mkOut.Hp:0} off what stands {railFrom.DistanceTo(mkOut.Position):0} u down it";
                            Sink(mkIn); Sink(mkOut);
                            pilot.Statuses.Clear(Status.Disabled);
                            pilot.Sl("railgun").Left = 0; pilot.Sl("railgun").Cool = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        case "hunter_range":
                        {
                            // THREE SITUATIONS, because two of them were wrong in the shipped game
                            // and the old check called one of them correct.
                            //   1. NOTHING IN REACH: nothing launches, the cooldown is NOT spent,
                            //      and the slot says so. It used to take the cooldown on the line
                            //      above the search, so a press at an empty sky burned all 14 s and
                            //      fired nothing -- which from the cockpit is the ability being
                            //      broken, and is what the owner reported.
                            //   2. A PRACTICE DUMMY IN REACH: it launches. The cell used
                            //      Targeting.WingPrey, which forbids a dummy because a WING would
                            //      strafe something immortal for ever -- so the one thing a pilot
                            //      tests a hull on was the one thing its missiles refused to fly
                            //      at. A seeker hits once and dies; it uses Attackable now.
                            //   3. SOMETHING REAL IN REACH: it launches and the cooldown IS spent.
                            var mkOut = await Marker(pilot.Position + way * (float)(reach * Vary(1.15f, 1.45f)));
                            int flying0 = InFlight().Count();
                            pilot.Sl("hunters").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "hunters", 0 });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            int flewNone = InFlight().Count() - flying0;
                            bool keptCool = pilot.Sl("hunters").Cool == 0;
                            string emptySays = Abilities.Find(pilot.Class, "hunters").Refuse?.Invoke(pilot, null);
                            float noneAt = pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkOut.Position);
                            Sink(mkOut);
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);

                            // 2 -- a DUMMY, the thing a pilot actually practises on
                            var dummy = Nodes<TargetDummy>().FirstOrDefault();
                            int flewDummy = -1;
                            if (dummy != null)
                            {
                                var wasAt = pilot.Position;
                                pilot.Position = dummy.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * (float)(reach * Vary(0.35f, 0.7f));
                                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                                flying0 = InFlight().Count();
                                pilot.Sl("hunters").Cool = 0;
                                doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "hunters", 0 });
                                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                                flewDummy = InFlight().Count() - flying0;
                                pilot.Position = wasAt;
                                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            }

                            // 3 -- something real, and the cooldown goes this time
                            var mkIn = await Marker(pilot.Position + wide * (float)(reach * Vary(0.40f, 0.85f)));
                            flying0 = InFlight().Count();
                            pilot.Sl("hunters").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "hunters", 0 });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            int flew = InFlight().Count() - flying0;
                            bool spentCool = pilot.Sl("hunters").Cool > 0;
                            int want = (int)pilot.Stats["hunter_count"];
                            proved = flewNone == 0 && keptCool && emptySays == "NOTHING IN REACH"
                                     && flewDummy >= want && flew >= want && spentCool;
                            told = $"empty sky: {flewNone} away, cooldown kept ({keptCool}), slot says {emptySays ?? "nothing"}; "
                                 + $"a dummy at hand: {flewDummy} away; a hostile at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u: {flew} away and the cooldown spent ({spentCool})";
                            Sink(mkIn);
                            // ITS OWN SEEKERS DOWN before the next case: one whose mark is gone flies on
                            // down its heading and strikes whatever it meets, and these twelve crossed the
                            // Echo's blast marks three cases later (136 where 80 was stored, twice).
                            foreach (var m in InFlight().Where(x => x.Source == pilot).ToList()) m.Intercept();
                            pilot.Sl("hunters").Cool = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        case "emp_range":
                        {
                            var mkIn = await Marker(pilot.Position + way * (float)(reach * Vary(0.45f, 0.90f)));
                            var mkOut = await Marker(pilot.Position + wide * (float)(reach * Vary(1.20f, 1.60f)));
                            double inWas = mkIn.Hp, outWas = mkOut.Hp;
                            pilot.Sl("rush").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "rush", 0 });
                            pilot.Sl("rush").Left = 0.001;             // the rush cut short: the EMP is what reaches
                            await Wait(0.12);
                            proved = Math.Abs(inWas - mkIn.Hp - pilot.Stats["emp_damage"]) < 0.01 && mkOut.Hp == outWas;
                            told = $"{inWas - mkIn.Hp:0} at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u and {outWas - mkOut.Hp:0} at {pilot.Position.DistanceTo(mkOut.Position):0} u";
                            Sink(mkIn); Sink(mkOut);
                            pilot.Statuses.Clear(Status.Hardened);
                            pilot.Sl("rush").Left = 0; pilot.Sl("rush").Cool = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        case "echo_radius":
                        {
                            // The blast goes off where the LAST of the damage landed, not where
                            // the ship is, so both marks are measured from that spot.
                            var landedAt = pilot.Position + way * Vary(60f, 140f);
                            var mkIn = await Marker(landedAt + way.Rotated(1f) * (float)(reach * Vary(0.40f, 0.85f)));
                            var mkOut = await Marker(landedAt + way.Rotated(-1f) * (float)(reach * Vary(1.20f, 1.60f)));
                            // NOTHING ELSE LANDS ON THE MARKS: a hit while the echo is up is stored and
                            // blasted too, and one before it would read as blast. Twice this read 136
                            // where 80 was stored -- the Warden's seekers, flying on after their mark was
                            // sunk (the hunters case downs its own now). Guns off, a beat for this ship's
                            // own shells, and the hulls read AFTER it, never before the wait.
                            pilot.Trigger = false;
                            await Wait(0.8);
                            double inWas = mkIn.Hp, outWas = mkOut.Hp;
                            int strays = InFlight().Count();
                            pilot.Sl("echo").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "echo", 0 });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            pilot.NoteDealt(80, landedAt);             // as a shell of its own would
                            pilot.Sl("echo").Left = 0.001;
                            await Wait(0.12);
                            double blastWas = 80 * pilot.Stats["echo_share"];
                            proved = Math.Abs(inWas - mkIn.Hp - blastWas) < 0.01 && mkOut.Hp == outWas;
                            told = $"{blastWas:0} stored puts {inWas - mkIn.Hp:0} on what stands {landedAt.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u from where it landed and {outWas - mkOut.Hp:0} on what stands {landedAt.DistanceTo(mkOut.Position):0} u from it ({strays} missiles in flight at the press)";
                            Sink(mkIn); Sink(mkOut);
                            pilot.Sl("echo").Left = 0; pilot.Sl("echo").Cool = 0; pilot.Sl("echo").Own = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        case "wave_range":
                        {
                            var mkIn = await Marker(pilot.Position + way * (float)(reach * Vary(0.40f, 0.85f)));
                            var mkOut = await Marker(pilot.Position + wide * (float)(reach * Vary(1.15f, 1.50f)));
                            float inWas = mkIn.Position.DistanceTo(pilot.Position), outWas = mkOut.Position.DistanceTo(pilot.Position);
                            pilot.Sl("shockwave").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "shockwave", 0 });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            float inNow = mkIn.Position.DistanceTo(pilot.Position), outNow = mkOut.Position.DistanceTo(pilot.Position);
                            proved = Math.Abs(inNow - inWas - pilot.Stats["wave_push"]) < 2 && Math.Abs(outNow - outWas) < 2;
                            told = $"what stood {inWas:0} u off is thrown {inNow - inWas:0} u and what stood {outWas:0} u off moves {outNow - outWas:0} u";
                            Sink(mkIn); Sink(mkOut);
                            pilot.Sl("shockwave").Cool = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        case "deploy_range":
                        {
                            // A turret left standing picks its own target with the same component
                            // point defence uses, so again it is the acquisition that stops.
                            pilot.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
                            doAbility.Invoke(pilot, new object[] { "deploy", 0 });
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            var standing = H.Deployed.LastOrDefault(tu3 => tu3.OwnerId == pilot.OwnerId);
                            if (standing == null) { told = "T dropped nothing to measure the reach of"; break; }
                            var mountAt = standing.Position;
                            var mkIn = await Marker(mountAt + way * (float)(reach * Vary(0.60f, 0.90f)));
                            for (int frame = 0; frame < 8 && standing.Aimed == null; frame++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            bool gotIt = ReferenceEquals(standing.Aimed, mkIn);
                            float gotAt = mountAt.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position);
                            mkIn.Position = mountAt + way * (float)(reach * Vary(1.30f, 1.70f));
                            for (int frame = 0; frame < 8 && standing.Aimed != null; frame++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            proved = gotIt && standing.Aimed == null;
                            told = $"the turret it left takes a light at {gotAt:0} u and lets it go at {mountAt.DistanceTo(mkIn.Position):0} u";
                            Sink(mkIn);
                            foreach (var taken2 in H.Deployed.ToList())
                                if (taken2.OwnerId == pilot.OwnerId) H.DeployedTaken(taken2);
                            pilot.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        default:
                            told = "nothing in this sweep knows how to measure it, and a reach nothing measures is a reach an upgrade can raise for free";
                            break;
                    }
                    Check(proved, $"{cdef.Name} {AllStats.Said(stat)} is {reach:0} u: {told}");
                }

                foreach (var cls in Classes.All.Where(cd2 => cd2.Ready).ToList())
                {
                    await SetClass(cls.Id);
                    var reachShip = Me;
                    reachShip.Position = VaryNear(afield, 700f); reachShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    reachShip.Rotation = VaryAngle();
                    await Wait(0.15);
                    // ...and the STRIKE RANGE with them, LAST. It is the one reach no row
                    // declares: it is defined FROM the control range (twice it) and takes the same
                    // bonus, so a class that declares the one has the other whether it says so or
                    // not. Last because ordering it launches the wing, and a launched wing is not
                    // armed for the next measurement.
                    var reachIds = cls.Reach.Keys.ToList();
                    if (cls.Has(Fit.Wing)) reachIds.Add("strike_range");
                    foreach (var reachId in reachIds)
                    {
                        if (flownNotFired.Contains(reachId)) { skipped.Add($"{cls.Name}.{reachId}"); continue; }
                        await ProveReach(reachShip, cls, reachId);
                        reachShip.Position = VaryNear(afield, 700f); reachShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    }
                    foreach (var flag in new[] { Status.Disabled, Status.Evading, Status.Hardened, Status.Untargetable })
                        reachShip.Statuses.Clear(flag);
                    reachShip.RecallWing();
                }
                Check(skipped.Count == flownNotFired.Length && skipped.All(sk2 => flownNotFired.Any(fn2 => sk2.EndsWith(fn2)))
                      && Classes.All.Where(cd2 => cd2.Ready).SelectMany(cd2 => cd2.Reach.Keys).Distinct().Count() == 13
                      && Classes.All.Where(cd2 => cd2.Ready).Sum(cd2 => cd2.Reach.Count) == 31,
                      $"thirteen reach ids over 31 class-and-id pairs, and every one of them measured above except the three that are FLOWN rather than fired "
