                      + $"-- the fighters' approach, the bombers' run out and the torpedo's own run ({string.Join(", ", skipped)})");
            }

            await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);

            {   // ── A STATUS'S SHARE IS A ROW, AND ITS GAP IS SWEPT ─────────────────
                // Guarded read Stats["rush_guard"] -- a row only the warrior carries -- out of
                // the one path every class's damage goes through, so Hardened on any other hull
                // was a status that did nothing at all.
                var gd = Me; gd.Position = yonder; gd.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
                Check(gd.Stats["rush_guard"] == 0, "a battleship's sheet has no rush_guard row of its own at all");

                // The per-source gap kept one entry per attacker that had EVER touched the hull,
                // for the life of the world. Forty in one frame, and none of them a second later.
                var lastHitBy = (Dictionary<string, double>)typeof(PlayerShip)
                    .GetField("_lastHitBy", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(gd);
                for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) gd.Hit(1, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), $"test:swept{i}");
                int keptGap = lastHitBy.Count, tallied = gd.DamageBySource.Count;
                await Wait(1.2);                                       // well past the 0.52 s gap
                int swept = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) if (!lastHitBy.ContainsKey($"test:swept{i}")) swept++;
                Check(keptGap >= 40 && swept == 40 && tallied >= 40 && gd.DamageBySource.Count >= 40,
                      $"forty sources in one frame leave forty entries in the 0.52 s gap and none of them 1.2 s later ({keptGap} held, {swept} of 40 swept), and the tally the HUD reads still has all forty ({gd.DamageBySource.Count})");
                double gap0 = gd.Hp;
                gd.Hit(10, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:gap");
                gd.Hit(10, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:gap");
                Check(Math.Abs(gap0 - gd.Hp - 10) < 1e-6,
                      $"...and the gap itself still holds: the same source twice inside 0.52 s lands once, for 10 ({gap0 - gd.Hp:0})");

                // HARDENED on a hull with no row of its own: half of it, which is what Hardened
                // has always meant. (The warrior's own rush_guard row is 0.5, checked above.)
                gd.ApplyStatus(Status.Hardened, 0.4); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double hd0 = gd.Hp;
                gd.Hit(100, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:hardrow");
                Check(gd.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(hd0 - gd.Hp - 50) < 1e-6,
                      $"a 100 blow on a HARDENED battleship -- a class with no rush_guard row -- lands for 50 ({hd0 - gd.Hp:0})");
                await Wait(0.6);
                // and EVADING, the same table's other row, on a hull that cannot roll
                gd.ApplyStatus(Status.Evading, 0.4); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double ev0 = gd.Hp;
                gd.Hit(100, gd.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:evadrow");
                Check(!gd.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(gd.Hp - ev0) < 1e-6,
                      $"and an EVADING one takes none of that 100 at all ({ev0 - gd.Hp:0})");
                await Wait(0.6);
            }

            {   // ── FIVE STATUSES ON THE WIRE, AND ALL FIVE COME BACK ───────────────
                // Shielded was a row nothing set, read or implemented, and it still rode the
                // wire in StatusSet.Bits. The values that remain ARE the wire format: 1 2 4 8 16.
                var put = new StatusSet();
                put.Apply(Status.Pinned, 1); put.Apply(Status.Disabled, 1); put.Apply(Status.Untargetable, 1);
                put.Apply(Status.Hardened, 1); put.Apply(Status.Evading, 1);
                int bits = put.Bits;
                var got = new StatusSet(); got.FromBits(bits);
                Check(bits == 31 && System.Enum.GetValues(typeof(Status)).Length == 6
                      && (int)Status.Pinned == 1 && (int)Status.Disabled == 2 && (int)Status.Untargetable == 4
                      && (int)Status.Hardened == 8 && (int)Status.Evading == 16
                      && got.Has(Status.Pinned) && got.Has(Status.Disabled) && got.Has(Status.Untargetable)
                      && got.Has(Status.Hardened) && got.Has(Status.Evading),
                      $"five statuses and no sixth: 1 2 4 8 16 pack into {bits} and all five come back out of it");
            }
        }

        // ── buildings open menus; READY -> autopilot -> portal -> ARENA -> home ──
        // (Late in the run on purpose: the trip replaces the whole hub scene, so every
        //  node reference taken before it -- dummies, ships -- is stale afterwards.)
        {
            var tioHome = Me.Position;
            Me.Position = Hub.TioPos + new Vector2(260, 0); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.5);
            Click(Scr(Hub.TioPos)); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.TioOpen && Nodes<TioWindow>().Any(), "left-click on the TIO opens WARP TO TARGET");
            var tw = Nodes<TioWindow>().First();
            Check(!Nodes<Button>(tw).Any(b => b.Name == "Warp"), "no WARP button: READY drives the mission");
            H.Yard.Credits = 1000;
            // start well away from where the portal opens, so the autopilot has a real trip
            Me.Position = H.MissionPortalPos + new Vector2(300, 700); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
            Nodes<Button>(tw).First(b => b.Name == "Ready").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.AllReady && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Opening && Me.AutopilotTo == H.MissionPortalPos,
                  "READY: the party (just me) is ready, the portal starts opening, and my ship flies itself there");
            await Wait(Hub.PortalOpenTime + 0.2);
            var mp = H.GetNodeOrNull<Node2D>("MissionPortal");
            var tio = H.GetNode<Sprite2D>("TIO"); float halfW = tio.GetRect().Size.X * tio.Scale.X / 2f;
            Check(H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen && mp != null && mp.Position.X > Hub.TioPos.X + halfW && mp.Position.Y < Hub.TioPos.Y - Hub.TioHeight / 2f,
                  "after 3 s the portal opens off the TIO's top-right corner");
            // THE MARK THAT WAS PICKABLE AND INVISIBLE: the open portal is one row of the scope
            // table now, so the glyph and the pick are the same row or neither exists.
            var mpMark = H.ScopeMarks().Where(smk => smk.Name == "MISSION PORTAL").ToList();
            Check(mpMark.Count == 1 && mpMark[0].At.DistanceTo(H.MissionPortalPos) < 0.01f && mpMark[0].Pick > 0f
                  && mpMark[0].Size.X > 0f && mpMark[0].Colour.A > 0f,
                  $"the open mission portal is 1 scope mark with a glyph of its own ({mpMark.Count} row, pick {(mpMark.Count == 1 ? mpMark[0].Pick : -1):0} u)");
            float d0 = Me.Position.DistanceTo(H.MissionPortalPos);
            // The base as it LEAVES, marked: the only thing that can put this figure on disk is the
            // save on the way out (the 30 s autosave is held off). That save used to write nothing --
            // the sector had already become the arena -- and a quit out there lost the difference.
            typeof(Yard).GetField("_saveCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H.Yard, 1e9);
            // ...and a hauler with a load waiting on its pad: the trip must take it home, not lose it
            H.Yard.Hauler.ResetToPad(); H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo = 300;
            double heldAtLeaving = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 45 && !Hub.InArena; i++)
            {
                if (H?.Yard != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Yard))
                {
                    H.Yard.Credits = 424242;
                    heldAtLeaving = H.Yard.Stock("ore") + H.Yard.Stock("salvage") + H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo + H.Yard.Gatherers.Sum(g => g.Cargo);
                }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var tripRec = typeof(Yard).GetField("_trip", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null);
            // The trip's stock is one figure per resource row now, not two named fields.
            var tripBag = tripRec?.GetType().GetProperty("Stock").GetValue(tripRec) as System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, double>;
            double tripStock = tripBag == null ? -1 : Gathering.Resources.Sum(r => tripBag.GetValueOrDefault(r));
            Check(Math.Abs(tripStock - heldAtLeaving) < 1.0, $"leaving for the arena, the loads come home first: the base set aside holds the hauler's 300 too ({tripStock:0} of {heldAtLeaving:0})");
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena, $"the capital-ship autopilot flew {d0:0} u to the portal and the party went through");
            var leftWith = new ConfigFile(); leftWith.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
            Check(Math.Abs((double)leftWith.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - 424242) < 1e-6,
                  $"leaving for the arena writes the base to disk on the way out ({(double)leftWith.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0} of 424242)");
            var boss = H.Boss;
            Check(boss != null && boss.Alive && boss.Type.Id == "silver_lancer" && boss.Type.Name == "RUSTY BUCKET" && H.Yard == null && !Nodes<TargetDummy>().Any() && Combat.Hostiles.Contains(boss),
                  "the arena at level 1: the Silver Lancer, and no base or dummies");
            // THE LEVEL'S OWN COPY of every move. At level 1 the scale is exactly 1, so the boss
            // flies the build's rows unaltered -- which is also how this proves the copy is made
            // from them rather than instead of them.
            {
                var flown = boss.Type.Moves.First(m => m.Windup > 0 && m.Reach > 0);
                var slot = boss.S(flown.Id);
                double q1 = Missions.Quicken(Missions.Level);
                Check(Math.Abs(q1 - 1.0) < 1e-9 && slot != null
                      && Math.Abs(slot.M.Windup - flown.Windup / q1) < 1e-6
                      && Math.Abs(slot.M.Reach - flown.Reach * q1) < 0.01
                      && Math.Abs(slot.M.Live - flown.Live) < 1e-9,
                      $"the boss flies the level's own copy of its rows: at level {Missions.Level} the scale is x{q1:0.000}, so its {flown.Id} winds up in {slot?.M.Windup:0.00} s and reaches {slot?.M.Reach:0} u -- its row's own figures");
            }
            // ITS MOVES ARE ROWS, so what follows reads the SLOT a row drives, by that row's id.
            // A beam's phases: Opening -- its escorts are out; Winding -- the red line is charging;
            // Firing -- it is burning down that line. These were five private fields and a
            // reflection handle each.
            Boss.Slot BeamSlot() => boss.S("beam");
            bool EscortsOut() => BeamSlot().At == Boss.Phase.Opening;
            bool BeamCharging() => BeamSlot().At == Boss.Phase.Winding;
            bool BeamLive() => BeamSlot().At == Boss.Phase.Firing;
            bool Ramming() => boss.S("ram").At == Boss.Phase.Firing;
            // -- THE MOVES ARE A TABLE, AND THESE ARE ITS FIGURES --------------------------
            // Every number the two bosses fight with, against the literals of the design rather
            // than against the code's own constants. The behaviour checks below read the rows;
            // this is the one place that says what a row must SAY.
            {
                var lan = Missions.Bosses[0]; var dra = Missions.Bosses[1];
                BossMove L(string id) => lan.Moves.First(m => m.Id == id);
                BossMove D(string id) => dra.Moves.First(m => m.Id == id);
                Check(lan.Moves.Select(m => m.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "guns", "trident", "beam", "ram", "wave" })
                      && dra.Moves.Select(m => m.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "gun", "shotgun", "throw" }),
                      $"a boss's moves are a table of rows, in the order a frame resolves them ({string.Join(",", lan.Moves.Select(m => m.Id))} / {string.Join(",", dra.Moves.Select(m => m.Id))})");
                // its art, its shape and what it closes on are the row's too, as EnemyDef carries
                // a raider's -- they were abstract members of a boss class each
                Check(lan.Hull == 760 && lan.Length == 360f && lan.HalfWidth == 70f && lan.Sprite == "res://boss_raider.png"
                      && dra.Hull == 700 && dra.Length == 420f && dra.HalfWidth == 90f && dra.Sprite == "res://boss_drake.png"
                      && lan.HoldOff == 650f && lan.CloseSpeed == 30f && Math.Abs(lan.TurnRate - 0.3f) < 1e-6
                      && dra.HoldOff == 650f && dra.CloseSpeed == 30f && Math.Abs(dra.TurnRate - 0.3f) < 1e-6,
                      "a boss's hull, art and approach are its row's: 760 / 360 / 70 and 700 / 420 / 90, both closing to 650 u at 30 u/s and turning at 0.3 rad/s");
                var gn = L("guns");
                Check(gn.Way == MoveWay.Bolt && gn.Every == 1.2 && gn.First == 2.0 && gn.Damage == 3.6 && gn.Find == 900f
                      && gn.Waits == MoveWait.Nothing && !gn.Busy && !gn.Super && gn.Source == "boss:guns"
                      && Math.Abs(gn.Damage / gn.Every - 3) < 1e-9 && gn.Beam == Beam.Bolt,
                      $"the guns: {gn.Damage} every {gn.Every} s (3 DPS) at the nearest ship within 900 u, they wait on nothing, and they are seen and heard as a boss's bolt ({Beam.Of(gn.Beam).Id})");
                var tr = L("trident");
                Check(tr.Way == MoveWay.Shoot && tr.Shot == Shots.Seeker && tr.Every == 15 && tr.First == 13.5
                      && tr.Count == 3 && tr.Spread == 25f && tr.Damage == 15 && tr.Speed == 135f && tr.Range == 1920f
                      && tr.Turn == 1.4f && tr.Size == 2f && tr.Muzzle == 14f && tr.Cue == "boss_trident"
                      && tr.Waits == MoveWait.Nothing && !tr.Busy && tr.Source == "boss:missiles",
                      $"the trident: {tr.Count} seekers at -25 / 0 / +25 degrees, 15 each, 135 u/s out to 1920 u, twice the size");
                var bm = L("beam");
                Check(bm.Way == MoveWay.Beam && bm.Every == 30 && bm.First == 6.0 && bm.Windup == 6.0 && bm.Live == 3.0
                      && bm.Tick == 0.25 && bm.Damage == 50 && bm.Reach == 10000f && bm.Width == 70f
                      && bm.Busy && bm.Super && bm.Waits == MoveWait.Itself && bm.Source == "boss:beam"
                      && bm.Cue == "boss_beam_charge" && bm.Strike == "boss_beam"
                      && bm.Escorts == 2 && bm.EscortKind == Enemies.Webifier && bm.EscortAngle == 45f
                      && bm.EscortOut == 130f && bm.EscortHull == 3 && bm.WebGrace == 1.0 && bm.WebSlack == 1.0
                      && bm.ArmMax == 5.0 && bm.ArmMax < bm.Windup,
                      $"the beam: wind-up {bm.Windup} s, live {bm.Live} s judged every {bm.Tick} s for {bm.Damage}, 70 u wide down 10000 u; two escorts 45 degrees off at 3 hull, never stalling it past 5 s");
                var rm = L("ram");
                Check(rm.Way == MoveWay.Dash && rm.Every == 30 && rm.First == 21.0 && rm.Windup == 1.5
                      && rm.Damage == 40 && rm.Reach == 900f && rm.Speed == 1200f && rm.Width == 140f
                      && rm.Busy && rm.Super && rm.Shifts && rm.Waits == MoveWait.Everything && rm.Source == "boss:charge",
                      $"the ram: wind-up {rm.Windup} s, {rm.Reach} u at {rm.Speed} u/s, {rm.Damage} on contact -- and a cadence of its OWN ({rm.Every} s, where it read the beam's constant by accident)");
                var wv = L("wave");
                Check(wv.Way == MoveWay.Ring && wv.Every == 17.0 && wv.First == 10.0 && wv.Windup == 1.8
                      && wv.Damage == 45 && wv.Reach == 340f && wv.Busy && !wv.Super && !wv.Shifts
                      && wv.Waits == MoveWait.Movers && wv.Source == "boss:wave" && wv.Strike == "boss_shockwave",
                      $"the shockwave: every {wv.Every} s, wind-up {wv.Windup} s, {wv.Damage} within {wv.Reach} u -- the period and the damage were bare literals in a method body");
                var dg = D("gun"); var sg = D("shotgun"); var th = D("throw");
                Check(dg.Way == MoveWay.Shoot && dg.Shot == Shots.Slug && dg.Every == 2.5 && dg.First == 2.0
                      && dg.Find == 1100f && dg.Damage == 6 && dg.Speed == 220f && dg.Range == 1300f
                      && dg.Radius == 7f && dg.Waits == MoveWait.Nothing && !dg.Busy && dg.Source == "boss:gun",
                      $"the Drake's main gun: {dg.Damage} a shell (2.4 DPS) every {dg.Every} s at 220 u/s within 1100 u, through its own specials");
                Check(sg.Way == MoveWay.Shoot && sg.Shot == Shots.Scrap && sg.Every == 15 && sg.First == 17.0
                      && sg.Warp == 1.0 && sg.Standoff == 600f && sg.WarpRing == 117f && sg.WarpSound == "drake_warp"
                      && sg.Windup == 1.875 && sg.Count == 7 && sg.Spread == 10f && sg.Width == 28f
                      && sg.Damage == 18.75 && sg.Speed == 480f && sg.Range == 1400f && sg.Radius == 9f
                      && sg.Busy && sg.Super && sg.Shifts && sg.Waits == MoveWait.Everything && sg.Source == "boss:scrap",
                      $"the scrap shotgun: a {sg.Warp} s ring, a warp to {sg.Standoff} u off, {sg.Windup} s of fan, then {sg.Count} pieces of {sg.Damage} at {sg.Spread} degrees apart");
                // THE BODY IS 180 u and the row carries NO Width, because a thrown body's lane is
                // the body's own width and Boss.Warn works it out. Width == 0 is asserted here so
                // a Width put back on this row fails rather than quietly taking over; that the
                // lane really is twice the body is proved where the throw runs, on this peer and
                // on a guest, because only a live raise can show it.
                // The 310 u it is held at is the clearance the row's comment sets out: the hull's
                // own half-width, the body, and a 40 u gap -- 90 + 180 + 40.
                Check(th.Way == MoveWay.Throw && th.Every == 30 && th.First == 6.0 && th.Windup == 7.5
                      && th.Flight == 1.2 && th.Damage == 250 && th.Reach == 1800f && th.Width == 0f
                      && th.Warp == 1.0 && th.Standoff == 1300f && th.WarpSound == "drake_warp"
                      && th.Radius == 180f && th.Offset == 310f
                      && th.Offset == Missions.Bosses[1].HalfWidth + th.Radius + 40f && th.Busy && th.Super
                      && th.Waits == MoveWait.Everything && th.Cue == "drake_tractor" && th.Strike == "drake_throw",
                      $"the asteroid throw: a {th.Radius} u body {th.Offset} u off the flank, a {th.Windup} s lane {th.Reach} u long and as wide as the body ({th.Radius * 2f} u), {th.Flight} s of flight, {th.Damage} to a ship in it");
                // ...AND A BOSS'S MOVES COME FROM ITS ROW, NOT FROM ITS CLASS. There is one Boss
                // class now: given the Lancer's hull with the DRAKE'S Moves, the boss built from
                // that row schedules the Drake's moves and knows nothing of the Lancer's.
                var mixed = new Missions.BossType { Id = "mixed", Name = "MIXED", Hull = 100, Sprite = lan.Sprite,
                                                    Length = lan.Length, HalfWidth = lan.HalfWidth, Moves = dra.Moves };
                var proof = new Boss { Hub = H, Type = mixed, Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, -6000) };
                AddChild(proof);                                  // _Ready builds its slots from the row
                Check(boss.GetType() == typeof(Boss) && proof.GetType() == typeof(Boss)
                      && proof.S("beam") == null && proof.S("ram") == null && proof.S("throw") != null
                      && proof.S("throw").Due == 6.0 && proof.S("shotgun").Due == 17.0
                      && proof.Length == 360f && Math.Abs(proof.MaxHp - 100) < 1e-6,
                      $"a boss's moves come from its ROW, not its class: one Boss class, and the Lancer's hull handed the Drake's rows runs the Drake's moves ({string.Join(",", proof.Type.Moves.Select(m => m.Id))})");
                proof.QueueFree();
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            Check(Met("boss"), "THE BOSS: met on entering the arena");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            var bb = Nodes<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "BaseButton");
            Check(bb != null && !bb.IsVisibleInTree(), "in the arena the BASE button is hidden");
            bb?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);                 // and even pressed, there is nothing to open
            Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0, "neither B nor the button opens a base menu out there");
            Check(Music.CombatZone, "the arena counts as combat for the music");
            Me.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.Rotation = 0;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(0.1);                           // point defence up
            Check(Math.Abs(boss.MaxHp - 760) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(boss.S("guns").M.Damage / boss.S("guns").M.Every - 3) < 1e-9, "the boss: 760 hull at level 1 (solo); its guns 3 DPS");
            // stand in the beam on purpose with a thick hull (test only), and watch the rhythm
            // the boss's health bar: a chunk per hit, bouncing, whitening, fading; bursts merge
            var bar = Nodes<QuarryBar>().First();
            Check(bar.Visible, "in the arena the boss's health bar shows");
            double hpA = boss.Hp;
            boss.TakeDamage(60); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var ck = bar.Chunks.LastOrDefault();
            Check(ck != null && Math.Abs(ck.To - hpA / boss.MaxHp) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ck.From - (hpA - 60) / boss.MaxHp) < 1e-6,
                  "a hit leaves a chunk: exactly the slice of the bar it took");
            await Wait(0.06);
            float off = QuarryBar.Offset(ck.T), wh = QuarryBar.Whiteness(ck.T);
            Check(Math.Abs(off) > 1f && wh > 0f && wh < 1f, $"the chunk bobs ({off:0.0} px) and is turning white ({wh:0.00})");
            Check(QuarryBar.Offset(QuarryBar.BounceFor) == 0 && QuarryBar.Alpha(QuarryBar.Life) <= 0.001 && QuarryBar.Whiteness(QuarryBar.Life * 0.5) >= 0.999,
                  "the bounce dies away; white by half-life; gone by the end");
            await Wait(QuarryBar.Life + 0.1);
            Check(bar.Chunks.Count == 0, "and then it is gone from the bar");
            // one small hit a frame for three frames (50 ms): inside the 60 ms window, so one chunk
            // (the bar reads the hull once a frame, so hits within ONE frame could never test this)
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { boss.TakeDamage(1); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(bar.Chunks.Count == 1, $"a burst of small hits, frame after frame, shares one chunk ({bar.Chunks.Count})");
            var me2 = Me; me2.MaxHp = 5000; me2.TakeDamage(-(5000 - me2.Hp));
            double tBeam = -1, tCharge = -1, tVolley = -1, arenaT = 0; int trident = 0; float spread = 0;
            // THE LIVE BEAM, 3 s: it must hold the line it drew, and that line must still be drawn.
            // The boss used to turn after the pilot the whole time, with the telegraph already gone
            // -- an invisible line sweeping the arena. 2.2 s in, the pilot is moved clear to the
            // side (after five 50-hull ticks, which the damage check below counts on): a boss that
            // turns would now swing after it.
            double liveT = 0; float liveRot0 = 0, liveTurn = 0; bool liveDrawn = false, sidestep = false;
            async void Watch()
            {
                while (Hub.InArena && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(boss))
                {
                    arenaT += 1.0 / 60;
                    if (BeamLive())
                    {
                        if (liveT == 0) liveRot0 = boss.Rotation;
                        liveT += 1.0 / 60;
                        liveTurn = Math.Max(liveTurn, Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(liveRot0, boss.Rotation)));
                        if (liveT > 2.0 && Nodes<FxNode>(boss).Any(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane)) liveDrawn = true;
                        if (liveT > 2.2 && !sidestep) { sidestep = true; me2.Position += Vector2.Right.Rotated(boss.Rotation) * 400f; }
                    }
                    // The beam's place in the rhythm is where it COMMITS -- its escorts going out.
                    // Its red line comes later, when their web pins the pilot, so timing the rhythm
                    // off the telegraph would measure that wait, not the rhythm.
                    if (tBeam < 0 && H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort)) tBeam = arenaT;
                    foreach (var tl in Nodes<FxNode>().Where(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane))
                        if (tCharge < 0 && Math.Abs(tl.Radius - 140f) < 0.1f) tCharge = arenaT;   // the ram's lane, 140 u
                    if (tVolley < 0 && boss.S("trident").Fired > 0)
                    {
                        tVolley = arenaT;
                        var big = InFlight().Where(x => x.HostileFire && x.Size == 2f).ToList();
                        trident = big.Count;
                        if (big.Count == 3) spread = big.Max(x => x.Dir.Angle()) - big.Min(x => x.Dir.Angle());
                    }
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                }
            }
            Watch();
            // The death beam OPENS WITH ITS ESCORTS, well before any red line: two fragile lights
            // 45 degrees to port and starboard of the nose, shivering on the spot while they come
            // round onto the pilot. The beam starts charging once their web has pinned the pilot.
            var escorts = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Raider>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && escorts.Count < 2; i++) { escorts = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            var bnose = Vector2.Up.Rotated(boss.Rotation);
            var escAng = escorts.Select(r => Mathf.RadToDeg(bnose.AngleTo(r.Position - boss.Position))).OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
            Check(escorts.Count == 2 && Math.Abs(escAng[0] + 45) < 12 && Math.Abs(escAng[1] - 45) < 12
                  && escorts.All(r => r.Target == me2 && r.Hp > 0 && r.Hp <= 3 * Missions.S(Missions.Level) + 1e-6),
                  $"two escorts launch at the pilot, 45 degrees to port and starboard ({string.Join(", ", escAng.Select(x => x.ToString("0")))}), fragile");
            Check(escorts.All(r => r.Shivering), "the escorts shiver at the launch point before the run in");
            // ── A BOSS'S ESCORTS ARE NOT THE LANCER'S: one more every ten levels, to five ──
            // The count was the row's alone, so every boss that ever calls craft in would have had
            // to grow its own. It belongs to the move table: a move that calls nothing still calls
            // nothing, and the fan spreads across the same arc whatever the count, so five need no
            // new formation.
            var callsThem = Missions.ForLevel(1).Moves.First(m => m.Escorts > 0);
            var callsNone = Missions.ForLevel(1).Moves.First(m => m.Escorts == 0);
            Check(Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 1) == 2 && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 10) == 2
                  && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 11) == 3 && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 21) == 4
                  && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 31) == 5 && Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 99) == 5
                  && Boss.EscortsAt(callsNone, 99) == 0,
                  $"a move's escorts grow with the level and stop at five: {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 1)} at level 1, {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 11)} at 11, {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 21)} at 21, {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 31)} at 31, {Boss.EscortsAt(callsThem, 99)} at 99 -- and a move that calls none still calls none");
            // ESCORTS OUT, THE BOSS ALREADY HOLDS ITS POSITION -- and turns only to face the pilot. Pushed
            // out past its 650 u standoff (where it would otherwise close at 30 u/s) and twisted 0.6 rad
            // off the pilot first, so both "it held" and "it turned" are real claims.
            {
                var armHome = boss.Position;
                boss.Position += (boss.Position - me2.Position).Normalized() * 700f;
                var armAt = boss.Position;
                boss.Rotation += 0.6f;
                float Off() => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f));
                float armOff0 = Off();
                await Wait(0.35);
                float armOff1 = Off();
                Check(EscortsOut() && !BeamCharging() && boss.Locked && boss.Position.DistanceTo(armAt) < 0.01f && armOff0 - armOff1 > 0.07f,
                      $"escorts out, the boss holds its position already ({boss.Position.DistanceTo(armAt):0.000} u) and turns only to face the pilot ({armOff0:0.000} -> {armOff1:0.000} rad)");
                boss.Position = armHome;
            }
        // ON THE WIRE. Boosting and Shivering are read by _Draw, and both were host-only: a guest
        // drew neither the escorts' triple-length plume nor any raider's boost plume. They ride
        // the raider packet as two bits now. Bit values as literals, not Raider.FlagShiver -- a
        // check that reads the constant cannot catch the constant changing under it.
        //
        // An escort is boost-ARMED while it shivers (the boost is not spent sitting still), so
        // both bits are set here and only the shiver bit clears when it goes. Asserting the pair
        // BEFORE and AFTER is what makes this a check on the bits rather than on one snapshot:
        // a NetFlags stuck at any constant value fails one half or the other.
        var shiverFlags = escorts.Select(r => r.NetFlags).ToList();
        Check(escorts.All(r => (r.NetFlags & 2) != 0),
              $"a shivering escort says so on the wire ({string.Join(",", shiverFlags)})");
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && escorts.Any(r => r.Shivering); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var runFlags = escorts.Where(r => r.Alive).Select(r => r.NetFlags).ToList();
        Check(runFlags.Count > 0 && runFlags.All(f => (f & 2) == 0) && runFlags.Any(f => (f & 1) != 0),
              $"and once it goes, the shiver bit clears and the boost bit stands ({string.Join(",", runFlags)})");
            Check(!Nodes<FxNode>().Any(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane), "no beam line yet: the escorts go first, the charge follows their web");
            // THE CHARGE STARTS ON THE PIN -- not on a guess made at launch. Pinned now, well before the
            // web was predicted to land, it charges at once (the old boss waited out its estimate).
            if (EscortsOut())
            {
                double predicted = BeamSlot().Predict;
                me2.ApplyStatus(Status.Pinned, 0.3);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(BeamCharging() && BeamSlot().Cause == "pinned" && BeamSlot().ArmedFor < predicted - 0.2,
                      $"the charge starts the moment the pilot is pinned, before the web was predicted to land ({BeamSlot().Cause} at {BeamSlot().ArmedFor:0.00} s, predicted {predicted:0.00} s)");
            }
            else Check(BeamCharging() && BeamSlot().Cause == "pinned",
                       $"the charge starts the moment the pilot is pinned ({BeamSlot().Cause} at {BeamSlot().ArmedFor:0.00} s)");
            // the bar under the boss's hull resets as a super commits and counts the 15 s to the next
            Check(boss.SuperFill < 0.15 && Math.Abs(boss.NextSuperIn - 15) < 1.5,
                  $"the super-move bar resets as the beam commits, counting the 15 s to the ram ({boss.NextSuperIn:0.0} s, fill {boss.SuperFill:0.00})");
            FxNode beam = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && beam == null; i++) { beam = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Radius - 70f) < 0.1f); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(beam != null && boss.WarningsPending > 0 && beam.Warn && BeamSlot().M.Width == 70f,
                  "the death beam is telegraphed: a red lane 70 u wide -- one warning row of Fx.All -- before it fires");
            Check(Math.Abs(beam.Time - 6.0) < 1e-6 && BeamSlot().M.Windup == 6 && Math.Abs(beam.Hold - 3.0) < 1e-6 && BeamSlot().M.Live == 3.0,
                  "the beam charges 6 s -- its row's wind-up, drawn for exactly that long -- and its lane stays lit the 3 s it burns");
            // the warning is parented to the boss, so it swings with the hull instead of being
            // pinned to the spot the boss stood on: the line drawn IS the line fired
            Check(beam.GetParent() == boss && beam.Anchor == boss.NetId,
                  "the beam's warning rides the hull rather than the world: the raise carries the hull's own id");
            // Push it out past its 650 u standoff BEFORE measuring. Inside that range it would
            // not close anyway, so watching for drift there proves nothing -- a mutant that
            // ignored the lock completely still passed this. Out here it wants to close at
            // 30 u/s, which is 22 u over the window below, so holding still is a real claim.
            var bossWasAt = boss.Position;
            boss.Position += (boss.Position - me2.Position).Normalized() * 700f;
            var lockAt = boss.Position;
            double standoff = boss.Position.DistanceTo(me2.Position);
            await Wait(0.75);
            Check(BeamCharging() && boss.Locked && standoff > 650 && boss.Position.DistanceTo(lockAt) < 0.01f,
                  $"the boss is locked in place while the beam charges, {standoff:0} u out where it would otherwise close (drifted {boss.Position.DistanceTo(lockAt):0.000} u)");
            boss.Position = bossWasAt;   // put it back: left out there its tridents take so long to
                                         // arrive that the point-defence check below times out
            // Reach, not trigger: this is how far the beam carries, whatever started its charge (the
            // 30 s rhythm, the nearest pilot, the escorts' web). Compared with
            // the spec's number as a literal, never with BeamLength itself, and checked on the
            // real segment so the drawn indicator and the hit line are both covered.
            var (segA, segB) = boss.Segment("beam");
            var farOut = segA + (segB - segA).Normalized() * 5000f;
            Check(BeamSlot().M.Reach == 10000f && Math.Abs((beam.To - beam.Position).Length() - 10000f) < 1f
                  && Combat.DistToSegment(farOut, segA, segB) < 1f,
                  $"beam and indicator both reach 10000 u -- a point 5000 u out is inside it, where the old 1800 u fell short (indicator {(beam.To - beam.Position).Length():0} u)");
            // HARD LOCKED while it charges -- with the pilot FREE: the old boss froze too once the pilot
            // was pinned, and tracked it otherwise, so only a free pilot tells the two apart. Point
            // defence clears the escorts and the web lets go; then, twisted well off the pilot, the boss
            // does not turn back. A boss that tracked would close 0.15 rad of the 0.6 in the 0.5 s.
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && (me2.Pinned || H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort)); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            boss.Rotation += 0.6f;
            float heldAt = boss.Rotation;
            await Wait(0.5);
            Check(BeamCharging() && !me2.Pinned && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, heldAt)) < 1e-4f,
                  $"hard locked while the beam charges, the pilot free: twisted off it, the boss does not turn back (pinned {me2.Pinned}, turned {Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, heldAt):0.0000} rad in 0.5 s)");
            // The twist was a probe, and the aim is locked, so put it back on the pilot by hand: the
            // beam below has to be the real shot, and the damage check at the end of this scenario is
            // the only thing that proves the locked line still lands.
            boss.Rotation = (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f;
            int down0 = Shot.Intercepted;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && Shot.Intercepted == down0; i++)
            {
                if (!Me.PdActive && Me.PdReady) Key(global::Godot.Key.Q);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            Check(Shot.Intercepted > down0, "point defence shoots the boss's missiles down");
            Check(!H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort), "point defence, switched on, kills the beam's escorts");
            var aMissile = InFlight().FirstOrDefault(t => t.HostileFire);
            Check(Turret.Rank(boss) == 2 && (aMissile == null || Turret.Rank(aMissile) == 0), "point defence takes missiles before anything else");
            var tiny = new StandIn { Tags = Tag.Light };
            Check(Turret.Rank(tiny) == 1 && Turret.Rank(tiny) < Turret.Rank(boss)
                  && Targeting.PointDefence.Chooses(tiny) && !Targeting.PointDefence.Chooses(boss),
                  "then small craft (by their tag, not their size), then everything else -- and a boss is never a PD target");
            FxNode ring = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 18 && ring == null; i++) { ring = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(ring != null && ring.Radius == 340f && boss.S("wave").M.Reach == 340f && ring.Anchor == Fx.World,
                  "the shockwave is telegraphed: a red zone 340 u across, on the ground, before it lands");
            // without point defence, a pair of escorts reaches the pilot and pins it inside the charge
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Me.PdActive; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            boss.LaunchEscorts("beam", me2);
            bool pinnedInTime = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < (int)(60 * BeamSlot().M.Windup) && !pinnedInTime; i++) { pinnedInTime = me2.Pinned; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(pinnedInTime, "left alone (no point defence), the escorts pin the pilot before the charge ends");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 25 && (tCharge < 0 || Ramming() || tVolley < 0); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            GD.Print($"  boss rhythm: beam {tBeam:0.0} s, trident {tVolley:0.0} s, charge {tCharge:0.0} s; trident of {trident}, spread {Mathf.RadToDeg(spread):0} deg");
            // the watcher starts a moment after the boss's clock, so compare the gaps, not the times
            Check(tBeam > 0 && Math.Abs(tCharge - tBeam - 15) < 0.4 && Math.Abs(tVolley - tBeam - 7.5) < 0.4,
                  "the rhythm: the charge 15 s after the beam, a trident half-way between");
            Check(trident == 3 && Math.Abs(Mathf.RadToDeg(spread) - 50) < 3, "a trident: three double-size missiles, the outer two 25 degrees off the middle");
            double beamDmg = me2.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("boss:beam", out var bd) ? bd : 0, chargeDmg = me2.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("boss:charge", out var cd) ? cd : 0;
            // live 3 s, checked every 0.25 s for 50 -- one source lands at most once per 0.52 s, and
            // ticks fall on frames, so two ticks are sometimes 0.533 s apart: 5 to 7 hits of 50
            Check(beamDmg >= 200 && beamDmg <= 350 && Math.Abs(beamDmg % 50) < 1e-6, $"the beam, live 3 s at 50 a tick, through the 0.52 s guard: {beamDmg / 50:0} hits ({beamDmg:0})");
            Check(Math.Abs(chargeDmg - 40) < 1e-6, $"the charge rams for 40 ({chargeDmg:0})");
            Check(sidestep && liveTurn < 0.01f, $"the live beam holds the line it drew, with the pilot gone from it (turned {Mathf.RadToDeg(liveTurn):0.00} deg)");
            Check(liveDrawn, "and the line is still drawn 2 s into its 3 s burn");
            Check(Music.I == null || Math.Abs(boss.S("trident").M.Speed - 135f) < 1e-3, "trident missiles fly at 135 u/s (10% slower)");
            // THE FALLBACKS, each on a beam started by hand. The escorts shot down: the charge still comes,
            // but not before their web would have landed. Escorts that neither pin nor die (the pilot far
            // off): they cannot stall it past 5 s. And the ram waits out a beam rather than snapping round
            // in the middle of one.
            {
                // A MOVE IS ARMED BY ITS ROW'S CLOCK, so each fallback is started by putting that
                // clock on the point of coming round -- no reflection, and a renamed field can no
                // longer pass silently.
                bool RamLine() => Nodes<FxNode>().Any(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Radius - 140f) < 0.1f) || boss.S("ram").At != Boss.Phase.Idle;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && (boss.Locked || RamLine()); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                BeamSlot().Due = 0.01;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !EscortsOut(); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double predictD = BeamSlot().Predict;
                foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !BeamCharging(); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(BeamCharging() && BeamSlot().Cause == "escorts down" && BeamSlot().ArmedFor >= predictD - 0.02 && BeamSlot().ArmedFor < predictD + 0.1,
                      $"the escorts shot down, the beam still charges -- once their web would have landed, not before ({BeamSlot().Cause} at {BeamSlot().ArmedFor:0.00} s, web due {predictD:0.00} s)");
                // the ram falls due in the middle of this beam: it waits for the beam to end, then comes
                boss.S("ram").Due = 0.01;
                bool ramDuringBeam = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && (BeamCharging() || BeamLive()); i++)
                { ramDuringBeam |= RamLine(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                bool ramAfter = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !ramAfter; i++) { ramAfter = RamLine(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                Check(!ramDuringBeam && ramAfter, $"the ram, due mid-beam, waits for the beam to end and then comes (during: {ramDuringBeam}, after: {ramAfter})");
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && (boss.Locked || RamLine()); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                // the pilot 3000 u off: the escorts need longer than 5 s to reach it
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 3000); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                BeamSlot().Due = 0.01;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !BeamCharging(); i++) { me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                Check(BeamCharging() && BeamSlot().Cause == "overdue" && Math.Abs(BeamSlot().ArmedFor - 5.0) < 0.05,
                      $"escorts that neither pin nor die cannot stall it: it charges 5 s after they launch ({BeamSlot().Cause} at {BeamSlot().ArmedFor:0.00} s)");
                foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            }
            // ── STEALTH STOPS THE BOSS CHOOSING, NEVER HITTING, AND NEVER STOPS THE FIGHT ──
            // Its pilots were ONE list, the seen one: a solo pilot who went dark emptied it and the
            // boss stopped closing, turning and counting every clock it had until the pilot came
            // back -- and a dark pilot beside a seen one stood in its shockwave untouched. Every
            // clock is held and the beam the last fallback started is stood down, so the only move
            // that runs is the one each check brings round. The pilot is seen once at a seeded spot,
            // goes dark, and is moved a seeded angle round the hull.
            {
                var dkHeld = boss.Type.Moves.ToDictionary(dkMove => dkMove.Id, dkMove => boss.S(dkMove.Id).Due);
                foreach (var dkMove in boss.Type.Moves) { boss.S(dkMove.Id).Due = 999; boss.S(dkMove.Id).At = Boss.Phase.Idle; }
                Fx.ClearWarnings();
                var dkSeen = boss.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(300f, 500f);
                for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) { me2.Position = dkSeen; me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                float dkToSeen = Aim.Face(boss.Position, dkSeen);
                me2.ApplyStatus(Status.Untargetable, 10.0);
                float dkOff = Vary(0.8f, 2.4f) * (VaryAngle() < 0 ? -1f : 1f);
                var dkAt = boss.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(dkToSeen + dkOff) * boss.Position.DistanceTo(dkSeen);
                me2.Position = dkAt; me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                // the nose put half-way round from where it saw the pilot toward where the pilot now is
                boss.Rotation = dkToSeen + dkOff * 0.5f;
                double dkDue = boss.S("beam").Due;
                await Wait(1.0);
                double dkRan = dkDue - boss.S("beam").Due;
                Check(Targeting.Hidden(me2) && Math.Abs(dkRan - 1.0) < 0.1,
                      $"its only pilot dark, the boss's clocks run on: the beam's comes {dkRan:0.00} s nearer in 1 s -- it stood still for as long as the pilot was unseen");
                float dkBack = Mathf.Abs(dkOff * 0.5f) - Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(dkToSeen, boss.Rotation));
                Check(Mathf.Abs(dkBack - 0.3f) < 0.03f,
                      $"...and it comes back round onto where it last saw the pilot at its 0.3 rad/s ({dkBack:0.000} rad in 1 s), not on after the pilot it cannot see, {Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(dkOff)):0} degrees round");

                // A BLOW THAT LANDS IN AN AREA LANDS ON EVERYONE IN IT, SEEN OR NOT
                me2.Position = VaryNear(boss.Position, 300f); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                double dkWave0 = me2.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault("boss:wave");
                int dkWaves0 = boss.S("wave").Fired;
                boss.S("wave").Due = 0.01;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && (boss.S("wave").Fired == dkWaves0 || boss.S("wave").At != Boss.Phase.Idle); i++)
                { me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                double dkWaveHit = me2.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault("boss:wave") - dkWave0;
                Check(Targeting.Hidden(me2) && boss.S("wave").Fired == dkWaves0 + 1 && Math.Abs(dkWaveHit - 45) < 1e-6,
                      $"a pilot it cannot see, {me2.Position.DistanceTo(boss.S("wave").To):0} u inside its 340 u shockwave, still takes the whole 45 ({dkWaveHit:0})");

                // ...AND ITS MOVES STILL COME, AIMED WHERE IT LAST SAW ANYONE: not held back for want
                // of a pilot in sight, and not aimed at the one it cannot see
                me2.Position = dkAt; me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                boss.S("ram").Due = 0.01;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && boss.S("ram").At == Boss.Phase.Idle; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                float dkRamOff = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference((boss.S("ram").To - boss.Position).Angle(), (dkSeen - boss.Position).Angle()));
                Check(boss.S("ram").At == Boss.Phase.Winding && dkRamOff < 0.01f,
                      $"with nobody in sight its ram still comes, down the line to where it last saw the pilot ({Mathf.RadToDeg(dkRamOff):0.0} degrees off it; the pilot is {Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(dkOff)):0} degrees round from there)");
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && boss.S("ram").At != Boss.Phase.Idle; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);

                // A SEEKER DOES NOT FOLLOW A PILOT IT CANNOT SEE: thrown square across the line to the
                // dark pilot, beyond its point defence, it flies on straight -- homing at the
                // trident's 1.4 rad/s would swing it 0.7 rad in the half second
                var dkSkFrom = me2.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(1000f, 1400f);
                var dkSkDir = (me2.Position - dkSkFrom).Normalized().Rotated(Mathf.Pi / 2f);
                var dkSk = Combat.Fire(Shots.Seeker, dkSkFrom, dkSkDir, 135f, 1920f, 0.0, targetId: me2.NetId, turnRate: 1.4f);
                await Wait(0.5);
                float dkSwung = dkSk.Dir.AngleTo(dkSkDir);
                Check(Targeting.Hidden(me2) && dkSk.Alive && Mathf.Abs(dkSwung) < 1e-4f,
                      $"a seeker thrown at a pilot gone dark does not home on it: it flies on straight ({Mathf.RadToDeg(dkSwung):0.000} degrees turned in 0.5 s)");
                dkSk.Intercept();

                foreach (var dkMove in boss.Type.Moves) boss.S(dkMove.Id).Due = dkHeld[dkMove.Id];
                me2.Statuses.Clear(Status.Untargetable);
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            }
            // the kill: shared EXP for it and the mission, the bounty's credits, then home
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            // (hints off meanwhile: a card starting mid-way would move the batched save these checks time)
            Character.HintsOff = true;
            // clear of where the crates will fall, with the boss's escorts out (they must die with it)
            Me.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 1500); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            boss.LaunchEscorts("beam", Me); await Wait(0.1);
            int escortsOut = H.Raiders.Count;
            int HoldOnDisk() { var c = new ConfigFile(); if (c.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg") != Error.Ok || !c.HasSection("gear_hold")) return 0;
                               return c.GetSectionKeys("gear_hold").Sum(k => (int)c.GetValue("gear_hold", k, 0)); }
            int UnclaimedOnDisk() => ((string)OnDisk("loot", "unclaimed", "")).Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Length;
            int hold0 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum(), disk0 = HoldOnDisk();
            var bossAt = boss.Position;
            // killed mid-wind-up, a lane on its hull and a zone on the ground: its warnings go with
            // it. Raised here rather than by any boss move, because what is being proved is the
            // WARNING -- the raise's anchor puts the lane on the hull and leaves the zone on the
            // ground, and a boss that is down clears both (Fx.ClearWarnings).
            Fx.Warn(new FxRaise { Id = Fx.WarnLane, At = new Vector2(0, -100), To = new Vector2(0, -3000), Size = 70f, Time = 10.0, Anchor = boss.NetId });
            Fx.Warn(new FxRaise { Id = Fx.WarnZone, At = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 600), To = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 600), Size = 300f, Time = 10.0 });
            var warnings = Nodes<FxNode>().Where(t => t.Warn && t.Time == 10.0).ToList();
            bool ridesHull = warnings.Count(t => t.GetParent() == boss) == 1 && warnings.Count(t => t.GetParent() == H) == 1;
            // ...and a SEEKER of its own in the air at the pilot, from a seeded side 600-900 u out --
            // 0.6 s at 135 u/s closes 81 u of that, so nothing it could reach is what ends it
            var winFrom = Me.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(600f, 900f);
            var winSeeker = Combat.Fire(Shots.Seeker, winFrom, Me.Position - winFrom, 135f, 1920f, 1, targetId: Me.NetId, turnRate: 1.4f);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool winUp = winSeeker != null && winSeeker.Alive && Combat.Hostiles.Contains(winSeeker);
            int winDown0 = Shot.Intercepted;
            boss.TakeDamage(boss.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(warnings.Count == 2 && ridesHull && warnings.All(t => !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(t)),
                  $"a warning goes where its raise anchors it -- one on the hull, one on the ground -- and a boss killed mid-wind-up takes both with it: no beam charging over a DEFEATED boss ({warnings.Count(t => GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(t))} of {warnings.Count} left)");
            await Wait(0.4);
            Check(winUp && !winSeeker.Alive && !Combat.Hostiles.Contains(winSeeker) && Shot.Intercepted == winDown0,
                  $"a boss's seeker in the air at the kill goes with it: 0.6 s on it is down and out of the hostiles, and counted as nothing shot down (up {winUp}, alive {winSeeker?.Alive}, {Shot.Intercepted - winDown0} counted)");
            var dropped = H.Crates.ToList();
            Check(dropped.Count == 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 2 && UnclaimedOnDisk() == 2
                  && dropped.All(c => c.Position.DistanceTo(bossAt) < 300 && Equipment.ById(c.Item) is { Kit: false, Rarity: Rarity.Common }),
                  $"a level-1 boss drops this pilot 2 Common crates where it died, on disk at once ({dropped.Count} crates, {UnclaimedOnDisk()} on disk)");
            Check(escortsOut >= 2 && H.Raiders.Count == 0, $"the boss's escorts die with it ({escortsOut} out, {H.Raiders.Count} left)");
            // NO CLOCK any more: the failure timer is not armed on a win, and a RETURN button appears
            double solEndT = (double)typeof(Hub).GetField("_arenaEndT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H);
            Check(solEndT < 0 && H.MissionWon && !H.IReturned && H.ReturnTotal == 1 && Nodes<ReturnButton>().Any(),
                  $"after the kill: a RETURN button and no clock sending you home (_arenaEndT {solEndT:0.0}, ready {H.ReturnReady}/{H.ReturnTotal})");
            Priv(H, "ReceiveLoot", new[] { "no_such_part", "std_drive", "fr_flak_1", "fr_flak_2", "fr_flak_3" }, bossAt + new Vector2(0, 400), 1);
            Check(H.Crates.Count == 4 && H.Crates.Skip(2).Select(c => c.Item).SequenceEqual(new[] { "fr_flak_1", "fr_flak_2" }),
                  $"a drop off the wire: no unknown part, no kit, and no more than a boss gives -- 2 at level 1 ({string.Join(",", H.Crates.Skip(2).Select(c => c.Item))})");
            // FLY OVER A CRATE: in the hold at once; on disk 2.5 s after the LAST pickup
            var crA = H.Crates[0]; var crB = H.Crates[1];
            Me.Position = crA.Position - Vector2.Right.Rotated(Me.Rotation) * (Me.MyArt.HalfWidth + 38f); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            await Wait(0.5);
            bool besideLeft = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA) && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0;
            Check(besideLeft, "a ship beside a crate, not over it, leaves it where it lies");
            Me.Position = crA.Position; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool tookA = !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA) && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0 + 1;
            await Wait(1.5);
            Me.Position = crB.Position; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crB); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.5); int diskMid = HoldOnDisk();
            await Wait(1.3); int diskAfter = HoldOnDisk();
            Check(tookA && diskMid == disk0 && diskAfter == disk0 + 2 && UnclaimedOnDisk() == 2,
                  $"flying over a crate takes it into the hold at once; the file follows 2.5 s after the LAST pickup ({disk0}: {diskMid} 1.5 s after the second, {diskAfter} at 2.8 s)");
            // IN STASIS, THE POD COLLECTS: near is not enough -- it has to fly over the crate
            var crC = H.Crates[0]; var cAt = crC.Position;
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.3);
            var pod = (EscapePod)typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_pod", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Me);
            Me.Position = cAt + new Vector2(0, 900);
            pod.Position = cAt + new Vector2(60, 0); pod.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.0);
            bool stillThere = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC) && crC.Position == cAt;
            pod.Position = cAt; pod.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(!Me.Alive && stillThere && !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC),
                  $"a pilot in stasis collects with its escape pod: 60 u off leaves the crate where it lies, flying over it takes it");
            Check(Hub.InArena, "five seconds and more after the kill, the party is still in the arena");
            var crD = H.Crates[0];
            Check(H.MissionWon && Character.Level == 1 && Character.Exp == 650, $"the kill of a level-1 boss pays a level-1 pilot 300 + 250 first clear + 100 = 650 (exp {Character.Exp})");
            var onKill = new ConfigFile(); onKill.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
            Check(Math.Abs((double)onKill.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - (424242 + 2000)) < 1e-6,
                  $"the bounty is on disk at the kill, not on the trip home: a quit out there kept the EXP and lost the credits ({(double)onKill.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0})");
            // no clock: RETURN takes you home; solo, the one present pilot is enough
            H.SetMyReturn(true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && H.Yard != null, "pressing RETURN takes the pilot home (solo: no one else to wait for)");
            // the crate left lying came home too -- into the hold -- and its node stayed behind
            Check(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crD) && Count<LootCrate>() == 0 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0 + 4,
                  $"the crate left behind comes home in the hold, its node gone with the arena ({Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() - hold0} of 4 parts)");
            Character.HintsOff = false;
            await Wait(Raids.Delay + 0.5);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0, "a won mission brings no raid");
            // exactly the credits set aside at departure + the 2000 bounty (a hauler sale before
            // departure is part of what was set aside, so a fixed window was fragile)
            Check(Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - Yard.TripStartCredits - 2000) < 1e-6, $"the bounty's 2000 credits are paid at home ({Yard.TripStartCredits:0} + 2000 = {H.Yard.Credits:0})");
            // the literal 1/20 asked for -- not Yard.AwayShare, or the check could never fail
            double want = H.Yard.FleetRate(GatherKind.Miner) * Yard.LastAway / 20.0;
            Check(Yard.LastAway > 1 && Yard.LastAwayGained("ore") > 0 && Math.Abs(Yard.LastAwayGained("ore") - want) < 1e-6,
                  $"while away the base earned 1/20 of its rate ({Yard.LastAwayGained("ore"):0.0} ore for {Yard.LastAway:0} s)");
            // levels: the win cleared level 1; docking at the TIO selects level 2
            Check(Character.BossCleared.TryGetValue("silver_lancer", out var cleared) && cleared.Contains(1), "the win records level 1 as cleared");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.S(2) - Missions.LevelStep) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.S(3) - Missions.LevelStep * Missions.LevelStep) < 1e-9,
                  "each level is stronger, multiplicatively (the level-3 Lancer: its row x the level scale)");
            H.OpenTio(); await Wait(0.1);
            var tw2 = Nodes<TioWindow>().First();
            Check(Missions.Level == 2, "docking at the TIO selects the newest unlocked level (2)");
            Check(Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelUp").Disabled, "no level beyond the newest unlocked");
            Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelDown").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Missions.Level == 1, "the host can pick an easier, cleared level");
            Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelUp").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Missions.Level == 2, "and back up to the newest");
            // ONE SIDE WINDOW AT A TIME: they share a spot. B over the open TIO used to leave both up,
            // one drawn over the other; now it takes the spot, and B again gives it back empty.
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<TioWindow>() == 0 && Count<BasePanel>() == 1, $"B with the TIO open: the base takes the spot ({Count<TioWindow>()} TIO, {Count<BasePanel>()} base)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0 && Count<TioWindow>() == 0, "and B again closes it");
            H.OpenTio(); await Wait(0.1);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            // a wipe: every ship in stasis at once -> the mission fails, home with nothing
            H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Arena); await Wait(1.5);
            // THE DRAKE BASTION: level 2 is its. Checked here, in the arena the wipe below fails.
            {
                var dk = H.Boss;
                var me3 = Me; me3.MaxHp = 5000; me3.TakeDamage(-(5000 - me3.Hp)); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me3.Rotation = 0f;
                var dspr = dk?.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().FirstOrDefault();
                Check(dk != null && dk.Type.Id == "drake_bastion" && dk.Type.Name == "DRAKE BASTION" && Math.Abs(dk.MaxHp - 700 * Missions.S(2)) < 1e-6
                      && dspr != null && dspr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("boss_drake.png") && Math.Abs(dspr.Texture.GetHeight() * dspr.Scale.Y - 420f) < 0.5f,
                      $"level 2 is the Drake Bastion: 700 hull x the level's scale, 420 u long ({dk?.Type.Name}, {dk?.MaxHp:0})");
                if (dk != null)
                {
                    // ITS CLOCKS: a throw every 30 s from 6 s, the shotgun every 15 s from 17 s -- 11 s after a
                    // throw starts, clear of its 9.7 s (1 s of warp, 7.5 s of warning, 1.2 s of flight), so the
                    // two never run back to back (at the trident's own 13.5 s it fell inside the first throw).
                    // The warp is what makes this tight: the throw grew a phase and 11 s now clears it by 1.3.
                    double gap = dk.S("shotgun").Due - dk.S("throw").Due;
                    Check(Math.Abs(gap - 11) < 1e-6 && 11 > 1.0 + 7.5 + 1.2 && 30 - 11 - 15 > 0,
                          $"its specials never run back to back: the shotgun 11 s after each throw starts, clear of the throw's 9.7 s ({gap:0.00} s)");
                    // its own clocks out of the way: each special below is started by hand, alone
                    dk.S("throw").Due = 999.0; dk.S("shotgun").Due = 999.0;
                    // MAIN GUN: a slow shell every 2.5 s (2.4 DPS at 6 a shell), dodged by moving
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 700); await Wait(0.1);
                    var shellSeen = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shot>(Slugs());
                    var shellAt = new System.Collections.Generic.List<double>(); Shot shell = null; double clock = 0;
                    // EVERY FIRED SHOT IS ITS OWN SOURCE (Shots.SourceKey), so a weapon's tally is
                    // its family name, then "#", then the body's number. Summed by family here --
                    // and "boss:gun" is matched WHOLE as well, so the Lancer's "boss:guns", one
                    // character away, can never be counted as this boss's gun.
                    double Fired(string fam) => me3.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == fam || kv.Key.StartsWith(fam + "#")).Sum(kv => kv.Value);
                    double gun0 = Fired("boss:gun");
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 7 && shellAt.Count < 2; i++)
                    {
                        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); clock += GetProcessDeltaTime();
                        foreach (var s in Slugs().Where(s => s.Kind == Shots.Slug && shellSeen.Add(s))) { shellAt.Add(clock); shell = s; }
                    }
                    float sspd = 0;
                    if (shell != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(shell)) { var sp0 = shell.Position; for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); sspd = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(shell) ? (shell.Position - sp0).Length() / (10f / 60f) : 0; }
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && Fired("boss:gun") == gun0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double gunHit = Fired("boss:gun") - gun0;
                    Check(shellAt.Count == 2 && Math.Abs(shellAt[1] - shellAt[0] - 2.5) < 0.05 && Math.Abs(sspd - 220) < 4 && Math.Abs(gunHit - 6 * Missions.S(2)) < 1e-6,
                          $"its main gun: a slow shell (220 u/s) every 2.5 s, 6 a hit -- 2.4 DPS before the level's scale (here {6 * Missions.S(2):0.00}) ({sspd:0} u/s, {(shellAt.Count == 2 ? shellAt[1] - shellAt[0] : -1):0.00} s apart, {gunHit:0.00})");
                    // THE SCRAP SHOTGUN (started by hand): a warpRing where it will land -- 600 u from the pilot --
                    // then, held still, a fan of seven red lines for 1.875 s (1.25 x the Lancer's 1.5 s), then
                    // seven pieces of scrap down them: the same fixed fan, 10 degrees apart
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && dk.Locked; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 1400); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    dk.S("shotgun").Due = 0.01;
                    FxNode warpRing = null;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && warpRing == null; i++) { warpRing = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnZone); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && dk.S("shotgun").At != Boss.Phase.Winding; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var landed = dk.Position; float warpOff = landed.DistanceTo(me3.Position);
                    // by the move's OWN wind-up, which the level shortens (1.875 at level 1)
                    var fan = Nodes<FxNode>().Where(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Time - dk.S("shotgun").M.Windup) < 1e-3).ToList();
                    var scrapSeen = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shot>(Slugs());
                    await Wait(1.0);
                    bool heldStill = dk.Position.DistanceTo(landed) < 0.01f && dk.Locked;
                    var scrap = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Shot>();
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && scrap.Count < 7; i++) { scrap.AddRange(Slugs().Where(s => s.Kind == Shots.Scrap && scrapSeen.Add(s))); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    float aimTo = (me3.Position - landed).Angle();
                    var offs = scrap.Select(s => Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(aimTo, s.Dir.Angle()))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
                    bool fanned = offs.Count == 7 && offs.Zip(new[] { -30f, -20f, -10f, 0f, 10f, 20f, 30f }, (a, b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 0.5f).All(x => x);
                    // 606, not 600: this is the LEVEL-2 Drake, and a boss gains 1% a level on its
                    // reach (Missions.Quicken), so its 600 u standoff is 600 x 1.01.
                    // The FIGURES ARE THE BOSS'S OWN, scaled for its level -- 1% a level on wind-ups
                    // and reach, proved against literals where that scale is defined. What is
                    // checked here is the SHAPE: a ring, a standoff, seven lines, seven pieces.
                    var shot = dk.S("shotgun").M;
                    Check(warpRing != null && Math.Abs(warpRing.Time - shot.Warp) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(warpOff - shot.Standoff) < 6 && fan.Count == 7 && heldStill && fanned
                          && dk.S("shotgun").Fired == 1,
                          $"the scrap shotgun: a warp to its own standoff off the pilot ({warpOff:0} u of {shot.Standoff:0}, ring {warpRing?.Time:0.000} of {shot.Warp:0.000}), a fan of {fan.Count} lines for {shot.Windup:0.000} s held still ({heldStill}), then 7 pieces at -30..+30 in 10s ({string.Join(" ", offs.Select(a => a.ToString("0")))}), counted once ({dk.S("shotgun").Fired})");
                    // EACH PIECE ITS OWN HIT, by construction rather than by hand. The fan used to
                    // number its seven source keys itself (boss:scrap:0..6) so the pieces would not
                    // swallow one another inside a hull's 0.52 s per-source gap; every fired shot
                    // carries its own identity now, so the fan says "boss:scrap" once. The piece at
                    // 0 degrees is aimed dead at the pilot (`fanned`, above), and a hull as broad as
                    // the 302 u battleship's takes its neighbours too -- so what the tally must show
                    // is a WHOLE number of one piece, at least one: every piece that lands felt in
                    // full, and not one of them swallowed.
                    double all0 = Fired("boss:scrap");
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double allDmg = Fired("boss:scrap") - all0, pieces = allDmg / (18.75 * Missions.S(2));
                    Check(pieces >= 1 && Math.Abs(pieces - Math.Round(pieces)) < 1e-6,
                          $"the piece aimed at the pilot strikes it, and every piece that lands is felt in full -- 15 x1.25 = 18.75, x the level's scale ({18.75 * Missions.S(2):0.00} each) ({allDmg:0.000} taken, {pieces:0} of the 7 struck)");
                    // THE ASTEROID THROW (started by hand): a rock beside it in its tractor, a red lane of the
                    // rock's width for 7.5 s (1.25 x the Lancer's 6 s charge), the boss held still -- then the
                    // throw: slow, then very fast (the first half of the flight covers an eighth of the lane),
                    // 250 x the level's scale to a ship in the lane, and the rock breaks apart at the lane's end
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && dk.Locked; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 700); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    await Wait(0.2);
                    float beforeWarp = dk.Position.DistanceTo(me3.Position);
                    dk.S("throw").Due = 0.01;
                    // IT WARPS FIRST, so nothing may be sampled until that is over. The hull is
                    // still TRAVELLING during Phase.Opening -- reading its position then put the
                    // rock 424 u from a boss that was 313 u from it a moment later. Wait for
                    // Winding, exactly as the shotgun's own check does, and everything holds still.
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && dk.S("throw").At != Boss.Phase.Winding; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    float afterWarp = dk.Position.DistanceTo(me3.Position);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && dk.Rock == null; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var rock = dk.Rock;
                    // FOUND BY THE BODY IT IS WARNING ABOUT: the lane this move raises is twice
                    // the rock's radius at this level, which is the whole point of not writing a
                    // width down beside it. The row's 180 u body is proved against its literal in
                    // the Drake's row check above; this reads the row and the level's scale.
                    var heave = dk.S("throw").M;
                    var lane = Nodes<FxNode>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Radius - heave.Radius * 2f) < 0.5f);
                    var drakeAt = dk.Position; var rockAt = rock?.Position ?? Vector2.Zero;
                    await Wait(3.0);
                    bool heldRock = rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) && rock.Position.DistanceTo(rockAt) < 0.01f && !rock.Thrown
                                    && dk.Position.DistanceTo(drakeAt) < 0.01f && dk.Locked;
                    // 7.43 s and 1.58: the flight and the wind-up are the level-2 Drake's, one
                    // percent quicker than the row's 7.5 and 1.6 (Missions.Quicken). The 310 u the
                    // rock is held at is a clearance, so it goes the other way: 310 x 1.01 = 313 --
                    // and the body it clears grew by the same one percent, which is why the offset
                    // is scaled with it (Boss.Scaled) and the rock is never drawn inside the hull.
                    bool clearsHull = rockAt.DistanceTo(drakeAt) - heave.Radius > dk.HalfWidth;
                    Check(rock != null && lane != null && Math.Abs(lane.Time - heave.Windup) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(lane.Hold - heave.Flight) < 1e-3
                          && Math.Abs(rockAt.DistanceTo(drakeAt) - heave.Offset) < 1.5 && heave.Offset > 310f && clearsHull
                          // IT BACKED OFF FIRST: 700 u away when the throw was called, its own
                          // standoff away when the wind-up began, and further than it started.
                          && Math.Abs(afterWarp - heave.Standoff) < 8 && afterWarp > beforeWarp
                          && Math.Abs(rock.Radius - heave.Radius) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(lane.Radius - heave.Radius * 2f) < 0.5f
                          && heldRock && dk.S("throw").Fired == 1,
                          $"the throw: it backs off from {beforeWarp:0} u to {afterWarp:0} u, then a {heave.Radius:0} u rock beside it in its tractor ({heave.Offset:0} u off, clear of a {dk.HalfWidth:0} u flank), a lane of its own width ({lane?.Radius:0} u) for {heave.Windup:0.00} s then {heave.Flight:0.00} s of flight, the boss and the rock held still ({rockAt.DistanceTo(drakeAt):0} u, lane {lane?.Time:0.00} s), counted once ({dk.S("throw").Fired})");
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) && !rock.Thrown; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var lane0 = rock?.From ?? Vector2.Zero; float laneLen = rock == null ? 1 : rock.From.DistanceTo(rock.To);
                    // HALF OF THIS FLIGHT, not a fixed 0.8 s. The wait was written when the flight
                    // was 1.58 s; at 1.19 it sampled 0.67 of the way through, and the cube ease
                    // turned that into 0.30 of the lane against an expected 0.125.
                    await Wait(heave.Flight / 2);
                    double half = rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) ? rock.Position.DistanceTo(lane0) / laneLen : -1;
                    bool broke = false;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock); i++) { broke |= rock.Done; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    double rockDmg = me3.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault("boss:rock");
                    Check(Math.Abs(half - 0.125) < 0.04 && broke && Math.Abs(laneLen - heave.Reach) < 1 && Math.Abs(rockDmg - 250 * Missions.S(2)) < 1e-6,
                          $"thrown: half the flight covers an eighth of its {heave.Reach:0} u lane ({half:0.000}) -- the ease, whatever the level does to the length -- it strikes the pilot for its 250 at the level's scale ({rockDmg:0}), and breaks apart at the end ({broke})");
                }
            }
            // EVERY BOSS SPECIAL HAS ITS OWN SOUND, heard as it happens: the Lancer's beam (its charge, and
            // the growl as it fires), ram, shockwave and trident, fought above; the Drake's gun, warp, scrap
            // blast, tractor, throw and the rock breaking, here
            {
                var bossSounds = new[] { "boss_beam_charge", "boss_beam", "boss_ram", "boss_shockwave", "boss_trident",
                                         "drake_gun", "drake_warp", "drake_scrap", "drake_tractor", "drake_throw", "drake_rock" };
                var unheard = bossSounds.Where(n => Assets.Load<AudioStream>($"res://sfx/{n}.wav") == null || Sfx.Played.GetValueOrDefault(n) == 0).ToList();
                Check(unheard.Count == 0, $"every boss special has its own sound, and each was heard in its fight (unheard: {string.Join(", ", unheard)})");
            }
            // A PILOT IN STASIS RE-BOARDS AT 24 S (the owner's ruling), and while a partner is still
            // flying the mission goes on without it. The partner is a second ship in the party, made by
            // the one door a guest's ship comes through (Hub.SpawnFor), parked far off the boss with a
            // hull nothing here can empty. Timed off this ship's own clock, a frame at a time: not
            // ready on any frame short of 24 s, ready on the first frame at it, F refused at 20 s.
            {
                const int stPeer = 77;
                Priv(H, "SpawnFor", stPeer);
                var stPartner = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.OwnerId == stPeer);
                var stPartnerAt = (H.Boss?.Position ?? Hub.ArenaCentre) + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(2600f, 3200f);
                if (stPartner != null)
                {
                    stPartner.MaxHp = 1e7; stPartner.TakeDamage(-(1e7 - stPartner.Hp));
                    stPartner.Position = stPartnerAt;
                    typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_netPos", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(stPartner, stPartnerAt);
                }
                await Wait(0.2);
                var stShip = Me; var stEndT = typeof(Hub).GetField("_arenaEndT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
                stShip.TakeDamage(stShip.Hp + 1);
                double stDiedAt = stShip.Clock, stWaiting = -1, stReadyAt = -1;
