                bool stRefused = false, stPressed = false, stFought = true;
                while (stReadyAt < 0 && stShip.Clock - stDiedAt < 26)
                {
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double stSince = stShip.Clock - stDiedAt;
                    stFought &= Hub.InArena && stPartner is { Alive: true } && (double)stEndT.GetValue(H) < 0;
                    if (stShip.CanReboard) stReadyAt = stSince; else stWaiting = stSince;
                    if (!stPressed && stSince >= 20) { stPressed = true; stShip.UseAbility("reboard", 0); stRefused = !stShip.Alive; }
                }
                stShip.UseAbility("reboard", 0);
                bool stBack = stShip.Alive;
                Check(stPartner != null && PlayerShip.StasisTime == 24 && stWaiting < 24 && stReadyAt >= 24 - 1e-6 && stReadyAt < 24 + 0.05
                      && stRefused && stBack && stFought,
                      $"a pilot in stasis re-boards at 24 s and not before, while its partner flies on: still waiting {stWaiting:0.000} s in, ready {stReadyAt:0.000} s in, F refused at 20 s ({stRefused}), back aboard ({stBack}); the mission never began to fail ({stFought})");
                Priv(H, "DespawnFor", stPeer);
                await Wait(0.2);
            }
            int exp1 = Character.Exp; int failLevel = Missions.Level;
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Character.Exp == exp1 && Me.Alive, "the whole party in stasis: the mission fails, home with no reward");
            // ...and the boss sends its raiders after you: 2 patrols (solo), from the map's edge, at the failed level's strength
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0, "the raid is not there at once (every guest's world loads first)");
            // measured the frame they appear: a raider that spots a miner boosts inward at once
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && H.Raiders.Count == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            double sL = Math.Pow(Missions.LevelStep, failLevel - 1);
            var raid = H.Raiders.ToList();
            GD.Print($"  raid: {raid.Count} raiders, level {failLevel} (x{sL:0.00}), {raid.Min(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos)):0}-{raid.Max(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos)):0} u out");
            Check(raid.Count == 8 && raid.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 2 && raid.Select(r => r.Patrol).Distinct().Count() == 2,
                  "a failed mission: the boss sends 2 patrols (3 lights and a heavy each)");
            Check(raid.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - Hub.RaidEdge) < 110f), "in from the edge of the base's map (3200 u out)");
            Check(raid.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Strength - sL) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Hull * sL) < 1e-6),
                  $"at the failed boss's level: hull and damage x{sL:0.00} (LevelStep^(L-1), L = {failLevel})");
            foreach (var r in raid) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
            await Wait(0.3);
            // the fighter-class autopilot, flown on its own (no player fighter class yet)
            Vector2 fp = Vector2.Zero, fv = Vector2.Zero; float fh = 0f; var goal = new Vector2(800, -600);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12; i++)
            {
                var (turn, thrust) = Autopilot.Fighter(fp, fh, fv, goal, 300f, 30f);
                fh += turn * 4f / 60f; fv += Vector2.Right.Rotated(fh) * thrust * 600f / 60f; fv *= 0.985f;
                if (fv.Length() > 300f) fv = fv.Normalized() * 300f;
                fp += fv / 60f;
            }
            Check(fp.DistanceTo(goal) < 80f, $"the fighter-class autopilot reaches its point ({fp.DistanceTo(goal):0} u off)");
            Check(Settings.MusicOn, "music is on by default");

            // ── THE PIRATE BASE SIEGE: the first entry of RAIDS, the second thing a mission has
            //    ever been able to be, and a LADDER OF ITS OWN ─────────────────────────────────
            {
                Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
                Character.Unclaimed.Clear(); Character.Save();
                Check(Missions.KindOf(Missions.Bounty).Category == "BOSS" && Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege).Category == "RAIDS"
                      && Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege).Name == "PIRATE BASE SIEGE",
                      $"a mission kind is a ROW: RAIDS beside BOSS, the siege its first entry ({Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege).Category} · {Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege).Name})");
                // THE SELECTED LEVEL IS PER CATEGORY: stepping one never moves the other
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Bounty; Missions.Level = 2;
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Siege;  Missions.Level = 3;
                int raidSel = Missions.Level;
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Bounty; int bossSel = Missions.Level;
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Siege;
                Check(raidSel == 3 && bossSel == 2 && Missions.Level == 3,
                      $"the selected level is the CATEGORY's: RAIDS on 3 left BOSS on 2 ({raidSel} raids, {bossSel} boss)");

                H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Arena); await Wait(1.5);
                // outside the base's 4500 u missile reach: nothing is launched at the pilot while the
                // numbers below are read, and where it sits is not one of them
                var sme = Me; sme.MaxHp = 500000; sme.TakeDamage(-(500000 - sme.Hp));
                sme.Position = VaryNear(Hub.ArenaCentre + new Vector2(0, 5400), 500f); sme.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                await Wait(0.2);
                // THE SERVICE GATE IS SHUT IN THE ARENA, though the arena's coordinates run under the
                // equipment base's: a calm ship set on the pad's spot, at a seeded point of it, is
                // refused; EQUIPMENT opens to look, with no RECYCLER on its title line and no reason
                // on its foot line (there is no yard here to spend from). Dark while it is there: the
                // launcher is not what this looks at.
                {
                    var gtRow = Landmarks.ById("equipment");
                    var gtBack = sme.Position;
                    sme.ApplyStatus(Status.Untargetable, 2.0);
                    sme.Position = gtRow.At + new Vector2(Vary(-0.8f, 0.8f) * gtRow.Half.X, Vary(-0.8f, 0.8f) * gtRow.Half.Y); sme.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_combatT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(sme, 0.0);
                    Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
                    var gtWin = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
                    var gtSays = Landmarks.Serves(sme, Service.LevelGear);
                    var gtRecycler = gtWin == null ? null : Nodes<Button>(gtWin).FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Recycler");
                    string gtLine = gtWin == null ? "(no window)" : Nodes<Label>(gtWin).FirstOrDefault(l => l.Name == "Gate")?.Text ?? "(no gate line)";
                    Check(Hub.InArena && gtRow.Covers(sme.Position) && sme.CalmFor >= Landmarks.CalmNeeded && !gtSays.Ok
                          && gtWin != null && gtRecycler != null && !gtRecycler.Visible && gtLine == "",
                          $"the equipment base serves nobody in the arena: a calm ship on its spot ({sme.Position.X - gtRow.At.X:0}, {sme.Position.Y - gtRow.At.Y:0} off its centre, {sme.CalmFor:0.0} s calm) is refused ({gtSays.Why}); EQUIPMENT opens with RECYCLER {(gtRecycler == null ? "missing" : gtRecycler.Visible ? "shown" : "hidden")} and the foot line \"{gtLine}\"");
                    if (gtWin != null) { Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.1); }
                    sme.Statuses.Clear(Status.Untargetable);
                    sme.Position = gtBack; sme.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    await Wait(0.1);
                }
                var em = H.Emplacements.ToList();
                var pbase = em.FirstOrDefault(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Base);
                var pylons = em.Where(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Pylon).ToList();
                Check(em.Count == 5 && pbase != null && pylons.Count == 4 && H.Boss == null,
                      $"a siege builds a pirate base and FOUR pylons, and no boss at all ({em.Count} hulls, {pylons.Count} pylons, boss {(H.Boss == null ? "none" : "built")})");
                // 1200 u out on each diagonal -- the four ends of an invisible X, one per corner
                var pylonCorners = pylons.Select(p => (Math.Sign(p.Position.X - pbase.Position.X), Math.Sign(p.Position.Y - pbase.Position.Y))).ToHashSet();
                Check(pylons.All(p => Math.Abs(p.Position.DistanceTo(pbase.Position) - 1200) < 1f
                                   && Math.Abs(Math.Abs(p.Position.X - pbase.Position.X) - Math.Abs(p.Position.Y - pbase.Position.Y)) < 1f)
                      && pylonCorners.Count == 4,
                      $"four pylons 1200 u out on the diagonals, one to each corner ({string.Join(", ", pylons.Select(p => $"{p.Position.DistanceTo(pbase.Position):0}"))} u; {pylonCorners.Count} pylonCorners)");
                // THE BASE'S ONE WEAPON AND THE PYLONS' NONE, as literals, once: every check of the
                // launcher further down reads the row this proves
                var launcher = pbase.Def.Gun.GetValueOrDefault();
                Check(pbase.Def.Gun != null && pylons.All(p => p.Def.Gun == null)
                      && pbase.Siblings.Count == 1 && pylons.All(p => p.Siblings.Count == 0)
                      && launcher.Kind == Shots.Cruise && launcher.Interval == 15 && launcher.Windup == 1.5
                      && launcher.ShellSpeed == 120f && launcher.Range == 4500f && launcher.Homing == 1.2f && launcher.Size == 3f
                      && launcher.Damage == 126 && launcher.Hull == 30 && launcher.Source == "base:missile"
                      && launcher.Turn * launcher.Windup > Math.PI
                      && Math.Abs(pbase.Spec(false).Damage - 126 * Missions.DamageMult(3, 1)) < 1e-9
                      && Math.Abs(pbase.Spec(false).Hull - 30 * Missions.HullMult(3, 1)) < 1e-9,
                      $"the pirate base answers with ONE launcher and its pylons with nothing: a cruise missile every {launcher.Interval:0} s after a {launcher.Windup:0.0} s warning its barrel comes round inside ({launcher.Turn * launcher.Windup:0.00} rad), {launcher.ShellSpeed:0} u/s out to {launcher.Range:0} u, turning {launcher.Homing:0.0} rad/s, {launcher.Size:0}x a missile, {launcher.Damage:0} damage on the level's and party's scale and {launcher.Hull:0} hull on the level's alone ({pbase.Siblings.Count} mount on the base, {pylons.Sum(p => p.Siblings.Count)} on the pylons)");
                var cruiseRow = Shots.Of(Shots.Cruise);
                Check(cruiseRow.AtPlayers && cruiseRow.Guided && cruiseRow.Interceptable && cruiseRow.Tags == Tag.Hulled && cruiseRow.Label == "CRUISE MISSILE",
                      "its round is the cruise missile: fired at the pilots, guided, given an id -- and a body with a HULL, Tag.Hulled and not Tag.Missile");
                // TWICE THE BOSS OF THIS LEVEL, at the same scale -- one hull formula, not a second.
                // (The level's own scale is proved against its literal once, where it is defined.)
                double bossAt3 = Missions.ForLevel(3).Hull * Missions.HullMult(3, 1);
                Check(Math.Abs(pbase.MaxHp - 2 * bossAt3) < 1e-6,
                      $"the pirate base is 2x the boss of its own level: 2 x {bossAt3:0.0} = {pbase.MaxHp:0.0} at level 3");
                Check(pylons.All(p => Math.Abs(p.MaxHp - 400 * Missions.S(3)) < 1e-6),
                      $"each pylon starts at 400 hull and climbs the same level scale (level 3: 400 x {Missions.S(3):0.000} = {pylons[0].MaxHp:0})");

                // ITS GARRISON comes as an escort's hunters do -- the same one builder, a row of
                // Waves.All -- but at FULL hull and the mission's own strength: the escort halves
                // its hunters (Waves.HunterHull), and none of that reaches here.
                var siegeRow = Missions.KindOf(Missions.Siege);
                Check(siegeRow.WaveEvery > 0 && Missions.KindOf(Missions.Bounty).WaveEvery == 0,
                      $"the wave clock is the mission ROW's: a siege every {siegeRow.WaveEvery:0} s from {siegeRow.FirstWave:0} s, a bounty none");
                int raid0 = H.Raiders.Count;
                H.GarrisonWave(Hub.ArenaCentre, 0); await Wait(0.3);
                var wave = H.Raiders.Skip(raid0).ToList();
                double siegeLv = Math.Pow(Missions.LevelStep, 3 - 1);
                Check(wave.Count == 4 && wave.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1 && Math.Abs(Waves.HunterHull - 0.5) < 1e-9
                      && wave.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Strength - siegeLv) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Hull * siegeLv) < 1e-6),
                      $"a siege wave is 3 pinners and a standoff at FULL hull and x{siegeLv:0.00} -- not the escort's half ({wave.Count} raiders, {wave.Count(r => r.Heavy)} heavy)");
                foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) H.RaiderDown(r);
                await Wait(0.2);

                // THE SHIELD: the four pylons hold it up, and nothing touches the base until every
                // one of them is down. All in one frame -- a dropped hull leaves H.Emplacements at
                // the call, not at the frame's end.
                double baseHp0 = pbase.Hp;
                pbase.TakeDamage(500);
                bool allFour = pbase.Shielded && Math.Abs(pbase.Hp - baseHp0) < 1e-9;
                for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) pylons[i].TakeDamage(pylons[i].MaxHp * 2);
                pbase.TakeDamage(500);
                bool lastOne = pbase.Shielded && Math.Abs(pbase.Hp - baseHp0) < 1e-9
                               && H.Emplacements.Count(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Pylon) == 1;
                Check(allFour && lastOne,
                      $"the base takes NOTHING while a pylon stands: 500 with four up and 500 with one, {baseHp0 - pbase.Hp:0} taken of 1000");
                pylons[3].TakeDamage(pylons[3].MaxHp * 2);
                pbase.TakeDamage(500);
                Check(!pbase.Shielded && Math.Abs(baseHp0 - pbase.Hp - 500) < 1e-6
                      && H.Emplacements.Count(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Pylon) == 0,
                      $"all four pylons down, the base takes damage: 500 off {baseHp0:0} leaves {pbase.Hp:0}");
                // ── THE BASE'S ONE WEAPON, OVER ONE MINUTE ─────────────────────────────────
                // A battleship parked in reach at a seeded bearing and distance, facing a seeded way,
                // not firing until the second missile is close. Over the minute: the base launches
                // nothing but cruise missiles; the first within one interval and one warning of the
                // pilot coming into reach, the rest 15 s apart, each 1.5 s after a lane that rides the
                // base's hull out to the pilot, and each leaving down it; the round flies at its row's
                // speed; the first, left alone, lands for its damage; the second falls to the MAIN
                // GUNS (point defence off) before it arrives. Every time here is read off an effect --
                // a round as it is launched, a lane the frame it is up -- never off a call.
                {
                    var classWas = Character.Class;
                    await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
                    var bs = Me; bs.MaxHp = 500000; bs.TakeDamage(-(500000 - bs.Hp)); bs.AutopilotTo = null;
                    var gun = pbase.Def.Gun.GetValueOrDefault();
                    var gunsKey = Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "guns");
                    foreach (var c in Nodes<Shot>().Where(x => x.Kind == Shots.Cruise && x.Alive).ToList()) c.Intercept();
                    var post = pbase.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(1700f, 2300f);
                    var toward = (pbase.Position - post).Normalized();
                    bs.Position = post; bs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; bs.Rotation = VaryAngle();

                    // ── THE LAUNCHER, BEFORE THE MINUTE: three things the minute cannot see ──
                    // Each is started by making the base's clock due by hand (its _cd), with any warning
                    // already up spent first and nothing of its own left in the air, and each is timed
                    // off effects on this ship's clock: a lane the frame it is seen under the base, a
                    // round as it is fired (Combat.ShotFired).
                    var lf = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
                    var lCd = typeof(Emplacement).GetField("_cd", lf); var lMarked = typeof(Emplacement).GetField("_marked", lf);
                    var lAngle = typeof(Turret).GetField("_angle", lf);
                    var lMount = pbase.Siblings[0];
                    var lFired = new System.Collections.Generic.List<(double t, Shot s, Vector2 at, Vector2 dir)>();
                    var lHook = Combat.ShotFired;
                    Combat.ShotFired = s =>
                    {
                        lHook?.Invoke(s);
                        if (s.Kind == Shots.Cruise && s.HostileFire && s.Position.DistanceTo(pbase.Position) < 400f) lFired.Add((bs.Clock, s, s.Position, s.Dir));
                    };
                    FxNode LNewLane(System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<FxNode> old) => Nodes<FxNode>(pbase).FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane && !old.Contains(x));
                    void LHold(Vector2 at) { foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) H.RaiderDown(r); bs.Position = at; bs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                    async Task LSettle(Vector2 at)
                    {
                        foreach (var c in Nodes<Shot>().Where(x => x.Kind == Shots.Cruise && x.Alive).ToList()) c.Intercept();
                        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && (int)lMarked.GetValue(pbase) != 0; i++) { LHold(at); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                        foreach (var c in Nodes<Shot>().Where(x => x.Kind == Shots.Cruise && x.Alive).ToList()) c.Intercept();
                        LHold(at); lFired.Clear();
                    }

                    // IT COMES ROUND FROM ANY BEARING AND LEAVES DOWN ITS LANE. At three seeded bearings
                    // the barrel is turned square away from the pilot as the clock comes due: the lane
                    // goes up with the barrel still pointing away, and the round leaves 1.5 s later
                    // within 0.02 rad of the pilot -- the barrel came round inside the warning.
                    {
                        int brPass = 0; string brLast = "";
                        for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++)
                        {
                            var brAt = pbase.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(1700f, 2300f);
                            await LSettle(brAt);
                            var brOld = Nodes<FxNode>(pbase).Where(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane).ToHashSet();
                            float brAway = (brAt - lMount.GlobalPosition).Angle() + Mathf.Pi;
                            lAngle.SetValue(lMount, brAway); lMount.GlobalRotation = brAway;
                            lCd.SetValue(pbase, 0.0);
                            double brLaneT = -1; float brOffAtLane = 0; Vector2 brLaneTo = Vector2.Zero;
                            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && lFired.Count == 0; i++)
                            {
                                LHold(brAt);
                                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                                if (brLaneT < 0 && LNewLane(brOld) is { } brLane)
                                {
                                    brLaneT = bs.Clock; brLaneTo = pbase.ToGlobal(brLane.To);
                                    brOffAtLane = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(lMount.GlobalRotation, (brAt - lMount.GlobalPosition).Angle()));
                                }
                            }
                            var brShot = lFired.FirstOrDefault();
                            float brOff = brShot.s == null ? 9f : Mathf.Abs(brShot.dir.AngleTo(brAt - brShot.at));
                            if (brLaneT > 0 && brShot.s != null && Math.Abs(brShot.t - brLaneT - gun.Windup) < 0.1
                                && brLaneTo.DistanceTo(brAt) < 1f && brOffAtLane > 2.5f && brOff < 0.02f) brPass++;
                            else brLast = $"bearing {k + 1}: lane {(brLaneT < 0 || brShot.s == null ? "or round never seen" : $"{brShot.t - brLaneT:0.00} s ahead of the round")}, barrel {brOffAtLane:0.00} rad off the pilot as it went up, the round {brOff:0.000} rad off it";
                            brShot.s?.Intercept();
                        }
                        Check(brPass == 3,
                              $"the launcher comes round from any bearing and its round leaves down its lane: the barrel square away from the pilot as the clock comes due, the lane up, and {gun.Windup:0.0} s later the round away within 0.02 rad of the pilot ({brPass} of 3 seeded bearings{(brLast.Length > 0 ? "; " + brLast : "")})");
                    }

                    // A PILOT GONE DARK WHILE ITS WARNING IS UP IS NOT FOLLOWED: aiming is choosing
                    // (Targeting.cs). At three seeded bearings, the lane up, the pilot goes dark for 3 s
                    // and moves 300 u square to the line: the round still leaves AT that pilot (stealth
                    // stops choosing, never hitting) but down the line to where it was last seen, not
                    // where it went -- and flies on straight for as long as the pilot stays dark.
                    {
                        int dkPass = 0; string dkLast = "";
                        for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++)
                        {
                            var dkAt = pbase.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(1700f, 2300f);
                            await LSettle(dkAt);
                            var dkOld = Nodes<FxNode>(pbase).Where(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane).ToHashSet();
                            lCd.SetValue(pbase, 0.0);
                            bool dkLane = false;
                            for (int i = 0; i < 60 && !dkLane; i++)
                            {
                                LHold(dkAt);
                                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                                dkLane = LNewLane(dkOld) != null;
                            }
                            bs.ApplyStatus(Status.Untargetable, 3.0);
                            var dkTo = dkAt + (dkAt - pbase.Position).Normalized().Orthogonal() * (Vary(0f, 1f) < 0.5f ? -300f : 300f);
                            float dkTurned = 0; double dkLaunched = -1, dkDark = 0;
                            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && Targeting.Hidden(bs); i++)
                            {
                                LHold(dkTo);
                                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                                if (lFired.Count > 0 && Targeting.Hidden(bs) && lFired[0].s.Alive)
                                {
                                    if (dkLaunched < 0) dkLaunched = bs.Clock;
                                    dkTurned = Mathf.Max(dkTurned, Mathf.Abs(lFired[0].s.Dir.AngleTo(lFired[0].dir)));
                                    dkDark = bs.Clock - dkLaunched;
                                }
                            }
                            var dkShot = lFired.FirstOrDefault();
                            float dkToSeen = dkShot.s == null ? 9f : Mathf.Abs(dkShot.dir.AngleTo(dkAt - dkShot.at));
                            float dkToWent = dkShot.s == null ? 0f : Mathf.Abs(dkShot.dir.AngleTo(dkTo - dkShot.at));
                            if (dkLane && dkShot.s != null && dkShot.s.TargetId == bs.NetId && dkToSeen < 0.02f && dkToWent > 0.1f
                                && dkTurned < 1e-4f && dkDark > 1.0) dkPass++;
                            else dkLast = $"bearing {k + 1}: lane {dkLane}, {(dkShot.s == null ? "no round" : $"the round {dkToSeen:0.000} rad off where the pilot was seen and {dkToWent:0.000} off where it went, turned {dkTurned:0.00000} rad in {dkDark:0.0} s of it dark")}";
                            dkShot.s?.Intercept();
                            bs.Statuses.Clear(Status.Untargetable);
                        }
                        Check(dkPass == 3,
                              $"a pilot gone dark while the launcher's warning is up is not followed: the round still leaves at it, but down the line to where it was last seen -- 300 u short of where it went -- and never turns while it stays dark ({dkPass} of 3 seeded bearings{(dkLast.Length > 0 ? "; " + dkLast : "")})");
                    }

                    // AN EMP'S HOLD ON THE LAUNCHER (Status.Disabled). Held with its clock due, it raises
                    // nothing and fires nothing for its 3 s; let go, the warning goes up at once; held again
                    // part way through that warning, the warning runs out with no round -- and the clock,
                    // restarted as that warning went up, brings the next one 15 s after it. That last is
                    // read off the minute's first lane, below: the pilot stays where the minute holds it.
                    double spentLaneAt = -1;
                    {
                        await LSettle(post);
                        var hdOld = Nodes<FxNode>(pbase).Where(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane).ToHashSet();
                        lCd.SetValue(pbase, 0.0); pbase.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 3.0);
                        double hdFrom = bs.Clock, hdFor = 0; bool hdLane = false;
                        while (pbase.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled) && bs.Clock - hdFrom < 4)
                        {
                            LHold(post);
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                            if (pbase.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled)) hdLane |= LNewLane(hdOld) != null;   // (the frame it lets go may raise one: that is the next line's)
                            hdFor = bs.Clock - hdFrom;
                        }
                        int hdFiredHeld = lFired.Count;
                        double hdFreeAt = bs.Clock, hdUpAt = -1;
                        for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++)
                        {
                            if (LNewLane(hdOld) != null) { hdUpAt = bs.Clock; break; }
                            LHold(post);
                            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                        }
                        double hdMid = Vary(0.3f, 1.0f);
                        while (hdUpAt > 0 && bs.Clock - hdUpAt < hdMid) { LHold(post); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                        pbase.ApplyStatus(Status.Disabled, 2.0);
                        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && (int)lMarked.GetValue(pbase) != 0; i++) { LHold(post); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                        double hdSpentAt = bs.Clock;
                        for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) { LHold(post); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                        Check(!hdLane && hdFiredHeld == 0 && hdFor >= 2.9 && hdUpAt > 0 && hdUpAt - hdFreeAt < 0.1
                              && (int)lMarked.GetValue(pbase) == 0 && lFired.Count == 0 && hdSpentAt - hdUpAt > gun.Windup - 0.1,
                              $"an EMP's hold on the launcher: held {hdFor:0.0} s with its clock due it raises no lane and fires nothing ({(hdLane ? "a lane went up" : "no lane")}, {hdFiredHeld} rounds); let go, the warning is up {hdUpAt - hdFreeAt:0.00} s later; held again {hdMid:0.00} s into it, the warning runs out {hdSpentAt - hdUpAt:0.00} s after it went up with {lFired.Count} rounds fired");
                        spentLaneAt = hdUpAt;
                    }
                    Combat.ShotFired = lHook;

                    double dealt0 = bs.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault(DamageSource.BaseMissile);
                    int downIn = Shot.Intercepted, ownShells = 0;
                    var launches = new System.Collections.Generic.List<(double t, Shot s, Vector2 at, Vector2 dir)>();
                    var others = new System.Collections.Generic.List<int>();
                    var lanes = new System.Collections.Generic.List<(double t, Vector2 to, float wide)>();
                    // (a lane of the checks above still fading is not one of the minute's)
                    var seenLanes = Nodes<FxNode>(pbase).Where(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane).ToHashSet();
                    var hook = Combat.ShotFired;
                    Combat.ShotFired = s =>
                    {
                        hook?.Invoke(s);
                        if (s.Source == bs) { if (s.Kind == Shots.Shell) ownShells++; return; }
                        if (s.Position.DistanceTo(pbase.Position) > 400f) return;        // the base's own, not a raider's
                        if (s.Kind == Shots.Cruise && s.HostileFire) launches.Add((bs.Clock, s, s.Position, s.Dir)); else others.Add(s.Kind);
                    };
                    double flown = 0, flownFor = 0, dealtFirst = -1, dealtAtSecond = -1, fellAt = -1;
                    int downAtFirst = -1, downAtSecond = -1; bool firing = false;
                    double tIn = bs.Clock;
                    while (bs.Clock - tIn < 60.0)
                    {
                        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) H.RaiderDown(r);       // the garrison is not what this measures
                        bs.Position = post; bs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                        var firstUp = launches.Count > 0 ? launches[0].s : null;
                        var secondUp = launches.Count > 1 ? launches[1].s : null;
                        Vector2? firstWas = firstUp is { Alive: true } ? firstUp.Position : null;
                        double clockWas = bs.Clock;
                        if (secondUp is { Alive: true } && firstUp is { Alive: false } && secondUp.Position.DistanceTo(post) <= 550f)
                        { AimWorld(secondUp.Position); if (!firing) { KeyDown(gunsKey); firing = true; } }
                        else AimWorld(post + toward * 400f);                          // swung toward the base, not firing
                        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                        foreach (var lane in Nodes<FxNode>(pbase).Where(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane))
                            if (seenLanes.Add(lane)) lanes.Add((bs.Clock, pbase.ToGlobal(lane.To), lane.Radius));
                        if (firstUp != null && firstWas is { } fw && firstUp.Alive)
                        {
                            double age = bs.Clock - launches[0].t;
                            if (age > 0.5 && age < 2.5) { flown += firstUp.Position.DistanceTo(fw); flownFor += bs.Clock - clockWas; }
                        }
                        if (firstUp != null && !firstUp.Alive && dealtFirst < 0)
                        { dealtFirst = bs.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault(DamageSource.BaseMissile) - dealt0; downAtFirst = Shot.Intercepted - downIn; }
                        if (secondUp != null && !secondUp.Alive && fellAt < 0)
                        {
                            fellAt = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(secondUp) ? secondUp.Position.DistanceTo(post) : 0;
                            downAtSecond = Shot.Intercepted - downIn;
                            dealtAtSecond = bs.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault(DamageSource.BaseMissile) - dealt0;
                            if (firing) { KeyUp(gunsKey); firing = false; }
                        }
                    }
                    if (firing) KeyUp(gunsKey);
                    Combat.ShotFired = hook;
                    double firstIn = launches.Count > 0 ? launches[0].t - tIn : -1;
                    var gaps = launches.Zip(launches.Skip(1), (a, b) => b.t - a.t).ToList();
                    // (the count read with 0.1 s either side of the minute's end: a launch due right on
                    // it may land a frame either way)
                    Check(others.Count == 0 && firstIn >= 0 && firstIn <= gun.Interval + gun.Windup + 0.1
                          && launches.Count >= 1 + (int)((59.9 - firstIn) / gun.Interval)
                          && launches.Count <= 1 + (int)((60.1 - firstIn) / gun.Interval),
                          $"a minute in the base's reach: it launches nothing but its missiles -- {launches.Count} cruise missiles, the first {firstIn:0.0} s after the pilot came into reach, and {others.Count} shots of anything else");
                    Check(gaps.Count >= 2 && gaps.All(g => Math.Abs(g - 15) < 0.1),
                          $"one missile every 15 s: {string.Join(", ", gaps.Select(g => $"{g:0.00}"))} s apart");
                    float laneWide = 2f * Shot.BodyRadius * gun.RoundSize;
                    Check(launches.All(l => lanes.Any(w => Math.Abs(l.t - w.t - gun.Windup) < 0.1 && w.to.DistanceTo(post) < 1f
                                                         && Math.Abs(w.wide - laneWide) < 1e-3)
                                            && Mathf.Abs(l.dir.AngleTo(post - l.at)) < 0.02f),
                          $"each goes {gun.Windup:0.0} s after a {laneWide:0} u red lane riding the base out to the pilot it is fired at, and leaves down it ({lanes.Count} lanes for {launches.Count} launches; worst {launches.Select(l => Mathf.Abs(l.dir.AngleTo(post - l.at))).DefaultIfEmpty(0f).Max():0.000} rad off)");
                    Check(flownFor > 1.5 && Math.Abs(flown / flownFor - gun.ShellSpeed) < gun.ShellSpeed * 0.01,
                          $"it flies at its row's {gun.ShellSpeed:0} u/s ({flown / Math.Max(1e-9, flownFor):0.0} u/s over {flownFor:0.0} s)");
                    double oneHit = gun.Damage * Missions.DamageMult(3, 1);
                    Check(Math.Abs(dealtFirst - oneHit) < 1e-6 && downAtFirst == 0,
                          $"left alone it lands for its damage: {dealtFirst:0.00} of the {gun.Damage:0} x {Missions.DamageMult(3, 1):0.000} a level-3 missile carries, and nothing counted it shot down");
                    Check(fellAt > 0 && downAtSecond == 1 && Math.Abs(dealtAtSecond - dealtFirst) < 1e-9 && ownShells > 0 && !bs.PdActive,
                          $"the next falls to the battleship's MAIN GUNS at a seeded bearing, {fellAt:0} u short of the hull, point defence off ({ownShells} shells, {downAtSecond} down, nothing more taken)");
                    // ...and the warning after the one the EMP's hold spent went up 15 s after it: the
                    // clock restarts as a warning goes up, round or no round
                    Check(spentLaneAt > 0 && lanes.Count > 0 && Math.Abs(lanes[0].t - spentLaneAt - gun.Interval) < 0.1,
                          $"the warning after one an EMP's hold spent goes up {gun.Interval:0} s after it, round or no round ({(lanes.Count > 0 && spentLaneAt > 0 ? $"{lanes[0].t - spentLaneAt:0.00} s" : "never")})");
                    if (classWas != ShipClass.Battleship) await SetClass(classWas);
                }
                await Wait(0.2);
                // ONLY THE BASE DROPS: four pylons have fallen and left nothing at all
                Check(H.CratesDropped == 0 && Count<LootCrate>() == 0 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0,
                      $"four pylons down and not one crate: only the base drops in a siege ({H.CratesDropped} crates, {Character.Unclaimed.Count} unclaimed)");
                // A MISSILE IN THE AIR, AND A WARNING UP, WHEN THE BASE FALLS go with it: one of the
                // base's own rounds is put up on purpose and its mount raises a warning at the pilot,
                // so the check never rests on where the minute's last launch or lane is
                var lateRound = pbase.Spec(false);
                var late = Combat.Fire(lateRound.Kind, pbase.Position, Me.Position - pbase.Position, lateRound.ShellSpeed, lateRound.Range,
                                       lateRound.Damage, targetId: Me.NetId, turnRate: lateRound.Homing, size: lateRound.RoundSize, hull: lateRound.Hull);
                pbase.Siblings[0].Warn(Me, pbase.NetId);
                var laneUp = Nodes<FxNode>(pbase).FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane);
                int downLate = Shot.Intercepted;
                var wreck = pbase.Position;
                pbase.TakeDamage(pbase.Hp + 1);
                await Wait(0.6);
                Check(late != null && !late.Alive && Shot.Intercepted == downLate && !Nodes<Shot>().Any(x => x.HostileFire && x.Alive)
                      && laneUp != null && !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(laneUp),
                      $"the base's fall takes what it had in the air and its warning down with it, counted as nothing shot down ({Nodes<Shot>().Count(x => x.HostileFire && x.Alive)} still up; lane {(laneUp == null ? "never raised" : GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(laneUp) ? "still up" : "gone")})");
                Check(H.MissionWon && H.CratesDropped == Loot.CratesFor(3) && Count<LootCrate>() == Loot.CratesFor(3)
                      && H.Crates.All(c => Math.Abs(c.Position.DistanceTo(wreck) - Loot.Ring) < 1f),
                      $"the base's fall ends the siege and drops ITS crates, on the {Loot.Ring:0} u ring ({H.CratesDropped} of {Loot.CratesFor(3)})");

                // ── TWO LADDERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
                // The siege clear opens the next RAID and leaves the bosses exactly where they
                // were; a boss clear then opens the next BOSS and leaves the raids where they are.
                Check(Missions.Cleared(Missions.Siege, 3) && Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Siege) == 3 && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege) == 4
                      && Character.BossCleared.TryGetValue("siege", out var sieged) && sieged.Contains(3),
                      $"a siege clear counts on the RAID ladder: level 3 cleared, {Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege)} unlocked, filed under its own id");
                Check(!Missions.Cleared(Missions.Bounty, 3) && Missions.HighestBeaten(Missions.Bounty) == 0 && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty) == 1,
                      $"...and it unlocked NO boss level: the bounties are still at {Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty)} with raids at {Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege)}");
                Progression.AwardClear(Missions.Bounty, 1);
                Check(Missions.Cleared(Missions.Bounty, 1) && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty) == 2
                      && !Missions.Cleared(Missions.Siege, 1) && Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege) == 4,
                      $"a boss clear opens the next BOSS and no raid ({Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Bounty)} boss, {Missions.Unlocked(Missions.Siege)} raids)");

                H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Home); await Wait(1.5);
                // the TIO's ◀ ▶ are capped by whichever ladder the selected operation climbs
                H.SelectMission(Missions.Siege); H.SelectLevel(99); int raidTop = Missions.Level;
                H.SelectMission(Missions.Bounty); H.SelectLevel(99); int bossTop = Missions.Level;
                Check(raidTop == 4 && bossTop == 2,
                      $"the level selector is capped by the OPERATION's own ladder: raids stop at {raidTop}, bosses at {bossTop}");
                Missions.Kind = Missions.Bounty; Missions.Level = 1;
                Character.Unclaimed.Clear();
            }

            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; Character.Save();
            Me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 330); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.5);
            Click(Scr(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Nodes<BasePanel>().Any(), "left-click on the base station opens the BASE menu");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        }

        // ── radar and the Esc menu ──
        {
            var radar = Nodes<Radar>().First();
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[Settings.RadarSize]), "the radar sits top right at the chosen size");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.EscMenuOpen && Hub.ControlsLocked, "Esc (nothing else open) opens the menu; the helm is locked");
            var menu = Nodes<EscMenu>().First();
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "LARGE").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Settings.RadarSize == 2 && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[2]), "LARGE radar: saved and applied");
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "SMALL").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[0]), "SMALL radar applied");
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "MEDIUM").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "50%").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(Settings.MusicVolume, 0.5f), "music volume set from the menu");
            // ...and the row LIGHTS the live step. The default used to be 0.6, which is not one of
            // the five the menu offers, so the match that picks the lit button found nothing and
            // the row opened with none of them pressed. Both rows are checked: a settings row where
            // nothing is selected looks broken whichever row it is.
            foreach (var g in new[] { "Radar", "Music" })
            {
                var opts = Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(menu).First(r => r.Name == g)).ToList();
                Check(opts.Count > 1 && opts.Count(b => b.ButtonPressed) == 1,
                      $"the {g} row shows exactly one live choice ({opts.Count(b => b.ButtonPressed)} of {opts.Count} lit)");
            }
            // ...and the harness cap SURVIVES the menu re-applying the volume. Checked here, after
            // a menu has just written the audio settings, rather than at startup where nothing has
            // had a chance to overwrite it. 5% as a literal, never Settings.TestVolume: a check
            // that reads the value it is testing cannot catch the value being wrong.
            int mbus = AudioServer.GetBusIndex("Master");
            float lin = mbus < 0 ? -1f : (float)Mathf.DbToLinear(AudioServer.GetBusVolumeDb(mbus));
            Check(mbus >= 0 && !AudioServer.IsBusMute(mbus) && Mathf.Abs(lin - 0.05f) < 0.001f && Settings.Volume > 0.05f,
                  $"the harness runs at 5% master volume whatever the settings say ({lin * 100:0.0}%, setting {Settings.Volume * 100:0}%)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!H.EscMenuOpen, "Esc closes the menu");
        }

        // ── music by mood: ambient, 20% combat on a selected enemy, 100% in combat ──
        {
            var sh = Me; var mus = Music.I;
            Check(mus != null, "the music player is running");
            H.ClearSelection();
            await Wait(9.0);
            Check(mus.Target == Music.Mood.Ambient && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.AmbientLevel, 1f) && mus.CombatLevel == 0f, "nothing selected: ambient only");
            H.SelectTarget(H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"));
            await Wait(2.5);
            Check(mus.Target == Music.Mood.Alert && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.CombatLevel, 0.2f), $"enemy selected: combat music at 20% ({mus.CombatLevel:0.00})");
            Check(Math.Abs(Music.I.AmbientLevel - 0.35f) < 0.02, $"and the ambient at half its alert level under it (0.35: {Music.I.AmbientLevel:0.00})");
            sh.Hit(1, sh.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * 80f); await Wait(2.0);
            Check(sh.InCombat && mus.Target == Music.Mood.Combat && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.CombatLevel, 1f) && mus.AmbientLevel == 0f,
                  "taking damage: combat music at 100%");
            H.ClearSelection();
            await Wait(PlayerShip.CombatHold + 3.0);
            Check(!sh.InCombat && mus.Target == Music.Mood.Ambient, "8 s after the last damage, back to ambient");
            sh.TakeDamage(-1);
        }

        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 0 && H != null, "Esc closes the K window first");
        H.ClearSelection();
        // a part dropped and not flown over when the pilot quits (as in a victory window)
        Character.Unclaimed.Add("drv_surge_2"); Character.Save(); int surge0 = Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("drv_surge_2");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Nodes<Button>(Nodes<EscMenu>().First()).First(b => b.Name == "Quit").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.3);
        Check(GetTree().CurrentScene is MainMenu, "QUIT TO MAIN MENU returns to the main menu");
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn"); await Wait(0.4);
        Check(H != null && Count<PlayerShip>() == 1, "hub re-enters cleanly");
        Check(Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("drv_surge_2") == surge0 + 1 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0,
              "a part not flown over before a quit is in the hold the next time the pilot enters a world");

        // ── sound: the music 35% quieter; effects by the camera's distance and zoom ──
        Check(Math.Abs(Music.Master - 0.65f) < 1e-6, "the music plays 35% quieter than it started");
        Check(Math.Abs(Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1)) < 1e-4 && Sfx.VolumeAt(1000, 1) < Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1) - 3
              && Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 0.5f) < Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1) - 3 && float.IsNegativeInfinity(Sfx.VolumeAt(6000, 1)),
              "effects: 0 dB at the view's centre, quieter with distance and when zoomed out, silent far away");
        Check(new[] { "laser_light", "laser_fighter", "laser_boss", "laser_hit", "missile_whoosh", "impact_thunk", "cannon" }
                  .All(n => Assets.Load<AudioStream>($"res://sfx/{n}.wav") != null),
              "all seven effects load");
        int P(string n) => Sfx.Played.TryGetValue(n, out var v) ? v : 0;
        GD.Print($"  sounds played: {string.Join(", ", Beam.All.Select(b => $"{b.Id} {P(b.Id)}"))}, hit {P("laser_hit")}, whoosh {P("missile_whoosh")}, thunk {P("impact_thunk")}, cannon {P("cannon")}");
        Check(P("point_defence") > 0 && P("laser_hit") > 0, $"point defence buzzes where it fires and crackles where it hits ({P("point_defence")} / {P("laser_hit")})");
        Check(P("bolt") > 0, $"the boss's guns are heard as a boss's bolt, their own note ({P("bolt")})");
        Check(P("missile_whoosh") > 0 && P("impact_thunk") > 0, "missiles and torpedoes whoosh at launch and thunk when they hit");
        Check(P("fighter") > 0, $"a carrier's fighters are heard as their own beam, not the capital ship's ({P("fighter")})");
        // THE RAIL WAS SILENT ON EVERY GUEST: its report was played inside FireRail, which runs on
        // the host alone. It comes through the door every beam comes through now, and nothing but
        // that door counts a "rail" -- so this, read before the block below fires one of every row
        // by hand, is the sniper's own railgun (fired earlier in this run) arriving by Combat.Flash.
        Check(P("rail") > 0, $"a sniper's railgun is heard, through the door every beam comes through ({P("rail")})");
        Check(P("cannon") > 0, "the battleship's guns have their own file, not the impact played quietly");

        // ── EVERY BEAM IS ITS OWN NOTE (the owner: "different beams have different frequency
        // sounds"). Every beam the game fires is a row of Beam.All; the ones a boss holds for
        // seconds -- the death beam charging and burning, the Drake's tractor -- are their moves'
        // own files. Each resolves to a file and a pitch: two on one file must be at least a
        // semitone apart (2^(1/12)), or "different" is a difference nobody hears. Each row is pinned
        // here to literals -- its id, its tint (the colour that beam was always drawn in), its file
        // and its pitch -- so a beam added, renamed, doubled, moved to another file or retuned
        // without being accounted for here goes red, and the check below that plays every row can
        // read the table this proves. An id is what Sfx gates and counts a beam under, so none may be
        // a sound file's name.
        {
            var pinned = new Dictionary<string, (Color tint, string file, float pitch)>
            {
                ["point_defence"] = (new(0.7f, 0.95f, 1f), "laser_light", 1.00f),  ["base"] = (new(0.6f, 0.9f, 1f), "laser_light", 1.12f),
                ["deployed"] = (new(0.7f, 0.95f, 1f), "laser_light", 1.26f),       ["hauler"] = (new(0.7f, 0.95f, 1f), "laser_light", 1.41f),
                ["light_raider"] = (new(1f, 0.3f, 0.25f), "laser_light", 0.84f),   ["heavy_raider"] = (new(1f, 0.35f, 0.25f), "laser_light", 0.71f),
                ["fighter"] = (new(0.7f, 0.95f, 1f), "laser_fighter", 1.00f),      ["bolt"] = (new(1f, 0.5f, 0.35f), "laser_boss", 0.90f),
                ["rail"] = (new(0.45f, 0.70f, 1f), "laser_boss", 0.71f),
            };
            var ids = Beam.All.Select(b => b.Id).OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
            var beamMove = Lancer.Moves.First(m => m.Id == "beam");
            var notes = Beam.All.Select(b => (who: b.Id, file: b.Report, pitch: b.Pitch))
                .Append((who: "death beam charging", file: beamMove.Cue, pitch: 1f)).Append((who: "death beam", file: beamMove.Strike, pitch: 1f))
                .Append((who: "tractor", file: Drake.Moves.First(m => m.Id == "throw").Cue, pitch: 1f)).ToList();
            var clash = notes.SelectMany((x, k) => notes.Skip(k + 1)
                    .Where(y => y.file == x.file && Math.Max(x.pitch, y.pitch) / Math.Min(x.pitch, y.pitch) < 1.0595f)
                    .Select(y => $"{x.who}/{y.who}")).ToList();
            var unloaded = notes.Where(x => Assets.Load<AudioStream>($"res://sfx/{x.file}.wav") == null).Select(x => x.who).ToList();
            var unpinned = Beam.All.Where(b => !pinned.TryGetValue(b.Id, out var pin) || !b.Tint.IsEqualApprox(pin.tint)
                                               || b.Report != pin.file || Mathf.Abs(b.Pitch - pin.pitch) > 1e-6).Select(b => b.Id).ToList();
            var asFile = Beam.All.Where(b => ResourceLoader.Exists($"res://sfx/{b.Id}.wav")).Select(b => b.Id).ToList();
            Check(ids.SequenceEqual(pinned.Keys.OrderBy(x => x)) && unpinned.Count == 0 && asFile.Count == 0 && clash.Count == 0 && unloaded.Count == 0,
                  $"every beam is its own note, drawn in the colour it always was: {string.Join(", ", notes.Select(x => $"{x.who} {x.file}@{x.pitch:0.00}"))} (ids {string.Join(",", ids)}; too close: {string.Join(",", clash)}; no file: {string.Join(",", unloaded)}; tint, file or pitch not the literal here, or not accounted for: {string.Join(",", unpinned)}; id is a file's name: {string.Join(",", asFile)})");
            // WHO FIRES WHICH. An enemy row that forgot its beam would fire row 0 -- your point
            // defence, a friendly buzz from a raider -- so the six are pinned in table order.
            var enemyBeams = string.Join(",", Enemies.All.Select(e => Beam.Of(e.Beam).Id));
            var dropped = new DeployedTurret(); int dropBeam = dropped.Spec(true).Beam; dropped.Free();
            int haulBeam = H.Yard.Hauler?.Spec(true).Beam ?? -1;
            Check(enemyBeams == "light_raider,heavy_raider,light_raider,light_raider,heavy_raider,heavy_raider"
                  && Me.Spec(true).Beam == Beam.Point && dropBeam == Beam.Deployed && haulBeam == Beam.Hauler,
                  $"every gun fires its own beam: the enemies {enemyBeams}; a warship's PD {Beam.Of(Me.Spec(true).Beam).Id}, a dropped turret {Beam.Of(dropBeam).Id}, the hauler {(haulBeam < 0 ? "none" : Beam.Of(haulBeam).Id)}");
        }

        // ...AND EACH IS PLAYED AS ITS ROW SAYS, through the one door every beam comes through
        // (Combat.Flash -> the world's hook -> Sfx.Beam): counted once under its own id, on a voice
        // playing its file at its pitch -- at the camera's own level with NO trim on top, on the
        // master bus. The quarter the owner took off is in the file (below); a second cut here or
        // on a bus would make the beams 0.56. From a varied spot near the view's centre, down a
        // varied line, both drawn AFTER each pause (the camera moves while it waits). UP TO THREE
        // TRIES, a pause before each (past the longest beam gate, laser_boss's 0.08 s): a shot of
        // the same row the world fires in the same frame holds that row's gate and would swallow
        // this one. The pause decides WHEN, never what is asserted.
        {
            var cam = GetTree().Root.GetViewport().GetCamera2D();
            var wrong = new List<string>();
            for (int k = 0; k < Beam.All.Length; k++)
            {
                var row = Beam.All[k];
                string miss = "never tried";
                for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 3 && miss != null; attempt++)
                {
                    await Wait(0.1);
                    var muzzle = VaryNear(cam.GetScreenCenterPosition(), 150f);
                    var landing = muzzle + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(200f, 900f);
                    int was = P(row.Id);
                    Combat.Flash(muzzle, landing, k);
                    float db = Sfx.VolumeAt(cam.GetScreenCenterPosition().DistanceTo(muzzle), cam.Zoom.X);
                    var voice = GetTree().Root.GetNodeOrNull("SfxPool")?.GetChildren().OfType<AudioStreamPlayer>()
                        .FirstOrDefault(p => p.Stream?.ResourcePath == $"res://sfx/{row.Report}.wav"
                                             && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(p.PitchScale, row.Pitch) && Mathf.Abs(p.VolumeDb - db) < 0.01f);
                    miss = P(row.Id) == was + 1 && voice != null && voice.Bus == "Master" ? null
                         : $"{row.Id}: +{P(row.Id) - was}, {(voice == null ? "no voice" : $"{voice.PitchScale:0.00} at {voice.VolumeDb:0.00} dB on {voice.Bus}")}";
                }
                if (miss != null) wrong.Add(miss);
            }
            Check(wrong.Count == 0, $"every beam is heard as its row says, through Combat.Flash, at no level of its own in code (zoom {cam.Zoom.X:0.00}; wrong: {string.Join("; ", wrong)})");
        }

        // ── THE LEVEL IS IN THE FILE, and these read the files to prove it ──────────────
        // Every trim in Sfx is 0 now: a sound's loudness is a property of the sound, not of
        // whoever plays it. That only means anything if the files actually carry those levels,
        // so this measures them. Ratios as literals -- 0.65 is "35% quieter" as a person means
        // it -- never as Sfx constants, which no longer exist to read.
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/laser_fighter.wav") / Peak("res://sfx/laser_light.wav") - 0.65) < 0.02,
              $"a carrier fighter's report is 35% quieter than a capital ship's, IN THE FILE ({Peak("res://sfx/laser_fighter.wav") / Peak("res://sfx/laser_light.wav"):0.000})");
        // The bomber's launch whoosh, 35% quieter than it used to be heard. The old file is in
        // retired/, and what is compared is the LEVEL IT PLAYS AT: the old file times its old
        // -8 dB trim, which is what the ear was actually getting.
        double oldWhoosh = Peak("res://retired/sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") * Math.Pow(10, -8 / 20.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") / oldWhoosh - 0.65) < 0.02,
              $"a missile launch is 35% quieter than it was heard before ({Peak("res://sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") / oldWhoosh:0.000})");
        // ...and the impact is NOT, because it was already right. Same level as before, to the
        // sample: the old file at its old -2 dB trim.
        double oldThunk = Peak("res://retired/sfx/impact_thunk.wav") * Math.Pow(10, -2 / 20.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/impact_thunk.wav") / oldThunk - 1.0) < 0.02,
              $"the impact is exactly as loud as it was, because it was already right ({Peak("res://sfx/impact_thunk.wav") / oldThunk:0.000})");
        // BEAMS A QUARTER QUIETER, IN THE FILE (the owner: "lower the volume default of beams by
        // 25%"). Every file a beam is heard by -- the ones Beam.All names, the crackle where one
        // lands, and the death beam's and the tractor's -- against the peak it shipped with before
        // the cut: literals measured from the files at 242b1aa, never a figure the code holds. The
        // list must be exactly the files the beams use, so a beam on a new file is accounted for
        // here. The player's master and music settings are not touched, so nothing saved moves.
        {
            var shipped = new Dictionary<string, double>
            {
                ["laser_light"] = 0.45105, ["laser_fighter"] = 0.29321, ["laser_boss"] = 0.89996, ["laser_hit"] = 0.56787,
                ["boss_beam"] = 0.84998, ["boss_beam_charge"] = 0.54999, ["drake_tractor"] = 0.44998,
            };
            var beamMove = Lancer.Moves.First(m => m.Id == "beam");
            var used = Beam.All.Select(b => b.Report).Append("laser_hit").Append(beamMove.Cue).Append(beamMove.Strike)
                .Append(Drake.Moves.First(m => m.Id == "throw").Cue).Distinct().OrderBy(f => f).ToList();
            var cut = shipped.Select(kv => (file: kv.Key, ratio: Peak($"res://sfx/{kv.Key}.wav") / kv.Value)).ToList();
            Check(used.SequenceEqual(shipped.Keys.OrderBy(f => f)) && cut.All(x => Math.Abs(x.ratio - 0.75) < 0.01),
                  $"every beam's file is 25% quieter than it shipped ({string.Join(", ", cut.Select(x => $"{x.file} {x.ratio:0.000}"))}; the beams use {string.Join(",", used)})");
            // ...and nothing that is not a beam moved, against the same shipped peaks
            var rest = new Dictionary<string, double>
            {
                ["cannon"] = 0.31931, ["missile_whoosh"] = 0.23291, ["impact_thunk"] = 0.71484, ["boss_ram"] = 0.74997,
                ["boss_shockwave"] = 0.89996, ["boss_trident"] = 0.69998, ["drake_gun"] = 0.59998, ["drake_warp"] = 0.59998,
                ["drake_scrap"] = 0.84998, ["drake_throw"] = 0.74997, ["drake_rock"] = 0.89996,
            };
            var moved = rest.Where(kv => Math.Abs(Peak($"res://sfx/{kv.Key}.wav") / kv.Value - 1) > 0.01).Select(kv => kv.Key).ToList();
            Check(moved.Count == 0, $"...and no sound that is not a beam moved ({string.Join(",", moved)})");
        }

        await WebRtcPairs();
        // leaks: leaving and re-entering the hub must not grow the orphan-node or object
        // counts (the first cycle warms caches; compare the next two against it)
        async Task Cycle()
        {
            GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://MainMenu.tscn"); await Wait(0.5);
            GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn"); await Wait(1.0);
        }
        // Collect .NET garbage first, so the count shows real leaks, not objects merely
        // waiting for the collector (it runs whenever it likes).
        async Task<double> Settled()
        {
            for (int g = 0; g < 2; g++) { System.GC.Collect(); System.GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers(); }
            for (int f = 0; f < 3; f++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            return Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectCount);
        }
        await Cycle();
        double objA = await Settled(), orphA = Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectOrphanNodeCount);
        await Cycle(); await Cycle();
        double objB = await Settled(), orphB = Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectOrphanNodeCount);
        GD.Print($"  leak check: orphan nodes {orphA:0} -> {orphB:0}; objects {objA:0} -> {objB:0} (after collection)");
        Check(orphB <= orphA, "no orphan nodes accumulate across leaving and re-entering the hub");
        Check(objB <= objA + 20, "no objects accumulate across leaving and re-entering the hub");
        HeldFiles();
        // THE LEVEL COMES WITH THE SECTOR: a peer sent into a world is told the level in the same call,
        // set before the world -- and an arena's boss -- is built from it, rather than left to the
        // mission report that follows. (Sent home again here, where the hub has just been rebuilt.)
        {
            int lv0 = Missions.Level;
            Priv(H, "NetSector", (int)Hub.SectorKind.Home, Missions.Kind, 7);   // the kind rides beside the level now
            int lvSet = Missions.Level;
            await Wait(1.0);                                   // the home world, rebuilt
            Missions.Level = lv0;
            Check(lvSet == 7 && H != null && !Hub.InArena, $"a peer sent into a world is told the level with it, before the world is built ({lvSet})");
        }

        // ── LAST, because it ends a session and that resets the fleet ────────────────────
        // A session can end without anything else moving. Quitting to the menu changes scene and
        // Yard._ExitTree writes on the way out; pressing PLAY OFFLINE, or a host closing the lobby
        // while everyone stands at home, changes nothing but the socket -- and nothing wrote the
        // character on that route, with the autosave up to thirty seconds away.
        //
        // Hosted first so there is a session to leave, and the figure is changed WHILE in it, so a
        // stale file cannot pass: the value on disk has to be the one from inside the session.
        // Placed at the very end on purpose -- going offline re-parks the base and resets the
        // ships, which quietly broke four later checks when this sat in the middle of the run.
        var yard = H.Yard;
        Net.I.Host(27124);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 && !Net.IsOnline; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        bool hosted = Net.IsOnline;
        yard.Credits = 4242;
        // a place held for a pilot that dropped, as the session ends
        var soloHeld = (System.Collections.IDictionary)Session.Places;   // Session.Held is a public record now
        soloHeld["ghost"] = new Session.Held { OldPeer = 515151, Name = "Ghost", Until = Time.GetTicksMsec() + 60000 };
        Priv(H, "RefreshAway");
        bool ghostInParty = H.PartyIds.Contains(515151);
        Net.I.GoOffline();
        await Wait(0.15);
        Check(ghostInParty && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Idle && H.PartyIds.SequenceEqual(new[] { Net.LocalId }) && soloHeld.Count == 0,
              $"a session ended with a place held: the solo world's party is this pilot alone -- its portal is not kept shut ({string.Join(",", H.PartyIds)})");
        var afterLeaving = new ConfigFile();
        afterLeaving.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
        Check(hosted && Math.Abs((double)afterLeaving.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - 4242) < 1e-6,
              $"leaving a session writes this peer's character, with no scene change to do it for them ({(double)afterLeaving.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0})");
    }

    // ── A FILE'S C# OBJECT OUTLIVES EVERY NODE THAT DREW IT (Assets.cs) ─────────────────────────
    // "Handle is not initialized", twice, in a solo run that passed every check (and in an arena
    // guest's): Godot swapping the GC handle of a texture whose C# object the collector had taken,
    // because the same path was being loaded again while that object's finalizer ran. It needs a
    // collection and a finalizer to land inside one load, so it is proved here by the two things
    // that close the gap for good, not by waiting for it. The build calls the engine's loader nowhere but
    // Assets: read from the build's own code, so a GD.Load added anywhere -- a lambda, an async body,
    // this harness -- is found; and the scan must find Assets' own calls, or it is reading nothing.
    // And a file whose last sprite has gone (the title screen's foe, left behind by the scene
    // changes above) keeps the very C# object it had through a full collection and its finalizers.
    void HeldFiles()
    {
        var calls = LoaderCalls();
        int inAssets = calls.Count(c => c.StartsWith("Assets."));
        var stray = calls.Where(c => !c.StartsWith("Assets.")).ToList();
        Check(inAssets >= 2 && stray.Count == 0,
              $"every file is loaded through Assets, which holds it: the engine's loader is called there {inAssets} times and nowhere else ({string.Join(", ", stray)})");
        const string foe = "res://enemy_light_tier_2.png";                // the title screen's foe: nothing in the hub draws it
        var (weak, refs) = DrawnAndDropped(foe);
        for (int g = 0; g < 2; g++) { GC.Collect(); GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers(); }
        var again = Assets.Load<Texture2D>(foe);
        Check(refs == 1 && weak.IsAlive && ReferenceEquals(weak.Target, again) && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(again),
              $"a file whose last sprite has gone keeps its C# object through a full collection, and is handed out again as that same object -- the gap the engine's handle swap failed in cannot open ({refs} reference left, the C# object {(weak.IsAlive ? "kept" : "collected")})");
    }
    // One sprite draws the file and goes, the way a scene or a raider does. Its own method, so no
    // local of the caller's keeps the C# object alive: the count left is the C# object's alone.
    [System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImpl(System.Runtime.CompilerServices.MethodImplOptions.NoInlining)]
    static (WeakReference weak, int refs) DrawnAndDropped(string path)
    {
        var tex = Assets.Load<Texture2D>(path);
        new Sprite2D { Texture = tex }.Free();
        return (new WeakReference(tex), tex.GetReferenceCount());
    }
    // Every call to the engine's loader in this build, named by the type it was written in (a
    // lambda's or an async body's compiler-made type counts as the one that holds it). Read from
    // the IL: a call, a callvirt or an ldftn whose token is a member reference or a generic
    // instance -- the loader lives in GodotSharp, so a call to it is always one of those two.
    static List<string> LoaderCalls()
    {
        static bool Loader(MethodBase m) => (m.DeclaringType == typeof(GD) && m.Name == nameof(GD.Load))
                                            || (m.DeclaringType == typeof(ResourceLoader) && m.Name.StartsWith("Load"));
        const BindingFlags every = BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly;
        Type[] types;
        try { types = typeof(Assets).Assembly.GetTypes(); }
        catch (ReflectionTypeLoadException e) { types = e.Types.Where(t => t != null).ToArray(); }
        var found = new List<string>();
        foreach (var t in types)
        {
            var home = t; while (home.DeclaringType != null) home = home.DeclaringType;
            foreach (var m in t.GetMethods(every).Cast<MethodBase>().Concat(t.GetConstructors(every)))
            {
                byte[] il;
                try { il = m.GetMethodBody()?.GetILAsByteArray(); } catch (Exception) { continue; }
                if (il == null) continue;
                for (int i = 0; i + 4 < il.Length; i++)
                {
                    bool call = il[i] == 0x28 || il[i] == 0x6F || (il[i] == 0x06 && i > 0 && il[i - 1] == 0xFE);
                    if (!call || (il[i + 4] != 0x0A && il[i + 4] != 0x2B)) continue;
                    MethodBase callee;
                    try { callee = t.Module.ResolveMethod(BitConverter.ToInt32(il, i + 1), t.IsGenericType ? t.GetGenericArguments() : null,
                                                          m.IsGenericMethod ? m.GetGenericArguments() : null); }
                    catch (Exception) { continue; }
                    if (callee != null && Loader(callee)) found.Add($"{home.Name}.{m.Name}");
                }
            }
        }
        return found;
    }

    // ── multiplayer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // ── a dedicated two-player arena run (its own port, after the three-player run) ──
    // Mission 1: both READY, both at the portal, into the arena together; the boss dies;
    // both home. Mission 2: into the arena again; the host drops -- the guest must land
    // home, offline, with its OWN base (a marker credit total set before it joined).
    async Task ArenaMp(string role)
    {
        const int Port = 27125;
        // Where the guest parks once its own stall is over, for the host to see: 400 u from where it
        // was put back, clear of it.
        var stallDone = new Vector2(900, -100);
        async Task ToArena(string who, string mission)
        {
            // the home world must be BUILT first: the scene swap is deferred, and for a frame
            // or two the old one is still current
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && (H == null || Hub.InArena || H.GetNodeOrNull("TIO") == null || !H.IsNodeReady()); i++)
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            H.SetMyReady(true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && H.Mission != Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && !Hub.InArena; i++)
            {
                if (H != null && Me != null && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen) { Me.Position = H.MissionPortalPos; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            // what the mission is won against, built: a boss, or a pirate base (Missions.Kinds[].Quarry)
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.IsNodeReady() && H.Quarry != null && Me != null, 5);
            Check(Hub.InArena, $"{who}: into the arena with the other pilot ({mission})");
        }
        if (role == "ahost")
        {
            Character.Name = "Arena Host";
            Net.I.Host(Port);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && Net.I.Players.Count < 2; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Net.I.Players.Count == 2, "arena host: the guest joined");
            await Wait(1.0);
            // ── TWO OF THE NINE, IN A SESSION ─────────────────────────────────────
            // Everything the nine classes put on the wire had only ever flown alone: the status
            // bits, the slot array a class names itself, a turret dropped into the world, an
            // effect, and a bubble that covers SOMEBODY ELSE. At home before the first arena trip
            // the host flies a WARRIOR and the guest a FREIGHTER.
            // Each process seeds its own varied geometry, so the rendezvous is a LITERAL and
            // everything varied is the HOST's -- the guest reads it off the wire.
            var mpSpot = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2400, 2400);   // clear of the belt, the wreck, the dummies and the lane
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior);
            var hw = Me; hw.AutopilotTo = null;
            Check(hw.Class == ShipClass.HeavyWarrior && Math.Abs(hw.MaxHp - 140) < 1e-6 && Abilities.For(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior).Length == 9,
                  $"arena host: hosting as a WARRIOR -- its stock 140 hull, no chips, three abilities and six open slots ({hw.MaxHp:0})");
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { Class: ShipClass.FreightHauler } gg
                                 && gg.Position.DistanceTo(mpSpot) < 60, Wan ? 25 : 15);
            var fg = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine);
            Check(fg != null && fg.Class == ShipClass.FreightHauler && Math.Abs(fg.MaxHp - 400) < 1e-6
                  && fg.Position.DistanceTo(mpSpot) < 60,
                  $"arena host: the guest refitted to a FREIGHTER and parked -- its stock 400 hull ({fg?.Class}, {fg?.MaxHp:0}, {fg?.Position.DistanceTo(mpSpot):0} u off)");
            hw.Position = VaryNear(fg.Position, 230f); hw.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hw.Rotation = 0f;   // inside its 260 u, any bearing
            await Wait(0.3);
            // ITS TURRET, MADE BY THE HOST: the guest presses T; only the host may put one in the
            // world, and only the host may take it away again.
            await RealTime(() => H.Deployed.Count(t => t.OwnerId == fg.OwnerId) == 1, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            var t1 = H.Deployed.FirstOrDefault(t => t.OwnerId == fg.OwnerId);
            Check(t1 != null && H.Deployed.Count == 1 && fg.TurretsOut == 1
                  && Math.Abs(t1.Hp - 120) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(t1.Spec(true).Damage - fg.Stats["deploy_damage"]) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(fg.Stats["deploy_damage"] - 6) < 1e-9,     // the stock 6: the guest's freighter carries no chips
                  $"arena host: a guest's T reached the host -- one 120-hull turret firing the GUEST's sheet, {t1?.Spec(true).Damage:0.0} a shot, owned by the guest ({H.Deployed.Count} out)");
            H.Drop(fg, VaryNear(fg.Position, 420f), fg.Stats["deploy_hull"]);
            H.Drop(fg, VaryNear(fg.Position, 420f), fg.Stats["deploy_hull"]);
            await Wait(0.3);
            Check(fg.TurretsOut == 3, $"arena host: three of the guest's turrets standing ({fg.TurretsOut})");
            // WHICH ARM HOLDS WHICH SHIP IS THE HOST'S WORD. Put the first gatherer in arm 3 of the
            // base's 5 for the guest to read off the wire: the hologram bars are drawn from
            // Arm.Occupant on every peer, and nothing had ever written it anywhere but here.
            var armShip = H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault();
            H.Yard.SetArm(armShip, 3);
            Check(armShip != null && H.Yard.ArmOf(armShip) == 3 && H.Yard.Arms[3].Occupant == armShip
                  && H.Yard.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null) == 1,
                  $"arena host: its first gatherer stands in arm 3 of the base's 5, and no other arm is taken ({(armShip == null ? -1 : H.Yard.ArmOf(armShip))}, {H.Yard.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null)} busy)");
            // 45 into the LAST of them (the first is the one shot off its base below): nothing
            // carried a turret's hull after the drop, so its owner watched a pristine turret take
            // a whole raid and then vanish in a burst.
            var hurtT = H.Deployed.Last(t => t.OwnerId == fg.OwnerId);
            hurtT.TakeDamage(45);
            Check(Math.Abs(hurtT.Hp - 75) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(hurtT.MaxHp - 120) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena host: 45 into one of the guest's turrets leaves 75 of 120 ({hurtT.Hp:0})");
            // THE BUBBLE IS OVER SOMEBODY ELSE. The pool is the GUEST's and the blow lands on the
            // HOST's hull: PlayerShip.ThroughBubbles reads Combat.Players, and until now no check
            // had ever had two pilots in it.
            await RealTime(() => fg.BubbleUp, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            Check(fg.BubbleUp && Math.Abs(fg.BubbleLeft - 400) < 1e-6 && hw.Position.DistanceTo(fg.Position) < 260f,
                  $"arena host: the guest's F raised its 400 bubble, and this hull is inside its 260 u ({hw.Position.DistanceTo(fg.Position):0} u)");
            double hwHull0 = hw.Hp;
            hw.Hit(150, hw.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:mpbubble");
            Check(Math.Abs(hw.Hp - hwHull0) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(fg.BubbleLeft - 250) < 1e-6,
                  $"...and a 150 blow on the HOST's hull comes out of the GUEST's pool ({fg.BubbleLeft:0} of 400 left, hull {hw.Hp:0} of {hwHull0:0})");
            // THE RUSH: a status only the host decides, and an EMP whose ring is an Fx row.
            hw.UseAbility("rush", 0);
            double rushLeft = hw.Sl("rush").Left, rushCool = hw.Sl("rush").Cool;
            Check(hw.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(rushLeft - 2.5) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(rushCool - 18) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(hw.SpeedMult - 2.5f) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena host: the warrior's rush -- hardened, 2.5 s up, 18 s cooling, 2.5x top speed ({rushLeft:0.0} s, {rushCool:0.0} s)");
            // ...and one of the guest's turrets shot off its base, for the guest to see the gap and the burst
            var doomed = H.Deployed.First(t => t.OwnerId == fg.OwnerId);
            int doomedId = doomed.NetId;
            doomed.TakeDamage(1e12); await Wait(0.4);
            Check(H.Deployed.All(t => t.NetId != doomedId) && fg.TurretsOut == 2,
                  $"arena host: a turret shot off its base is gone here ({fg.TurretsOut} left out)");
            await RealTime(() => fg.TurretsOut == 1, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            Check(fg.TurretsOut == 1 && H.Deployed.Count == 1,
                  $"arena host: the guest's C picked one up again -- one of its three left standing ({fg.TurretsOut})");
            // THE RUSH MUST LAND BEFORE THE REFIT. A refit rebuilds the ship's slots, so a rush
            // still running is discarded with them and its EMP never fires -- which is right (it is
            // a different ship now), but it would leave the guest waiting for a ring that never came.
            await RealTime(() => !hw.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && hw.Sl("rush").Left <= 0, Wan ? 12 : 6);
            await Wait(0.4);
            foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);   // the world clear for the arena
            await Wait(0.3);
            // A SNIPER'S RAILGUN IS HEARD ON A GUEST. Its report is a flash of Beam.All (Beam.Rail),
            // sent to every peer through the one door every flash goes through; the guest counts it
            // (its side is in the aguest branch). The real railgun, from a seeded spot 240-600 u off
            // the guest, its line pointed straight away from it.
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavySniper);
            if (H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { } railEar)
            {
                var railShip = Me; railShip.AutopilotTo = null;
                var railAt = VaryNear(railEar.Position, 600f);
                railShip.Position = railAt; railShip.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                railShip.Rotation = (railAt - railEar.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f;      // its nose (Up) away from the guest
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                railShip.UseAbility("railgun", 0);
                await RealTime(() => railShip.Sl("railgun").Cool > 0, 6);
                Check(railShip.Sl("railgun").Cool > 0 && railShip.Position.DistanceTo(railEar.Position) < 800f,
                      $"arena host: a sniper's railgun fired {railShip.Position.DistanceTo(railEar.Position):0} u from the guest, for it to hear");
                await Wait(0.3);
            }
            await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);                            // the hull the rest of this scenario flies
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { Class: ShipClass.Destroyer }, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            await ToArena("arena host", "mission 1");
