            // Built after a scene change, so from what is REMEMBERED of each pilot, not a fresh
            // announcement. It used to be only names and colours: every boss a solo boss (it counted
            // the ships before there were any), and every guest at default gear.
            Check(Math.Abs(H.Boss.HullMult - 1.6) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(H.Boss.DamageMult - 1.2) < 1e-9,
                  $"arena host: a party of two faces a boss scaled for two (hull x1.6, damage x1.2; x{H.Boss.HullMult:0.00}, x{H.Boss.DamageMult:0.00})");
            var gship = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine);
            Check(gship != null && gship.Class == ShipClass.Destroyer && Math.Abs(gship.MaxHp - 290) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(gship.Stats["missile_mag"] - 6) < 1e-9,
                  $"arena host: the guest's destroyer and its own gear came into the arena with it (290 hull from two Armour Chips I, a Salvo Rack III's 6 bursts; {gship?.Class}, {gship?.MaxHp:0.0}, {gship?.Stats["missile_mag"]:0})");
            // A CRUISE MISSILE CROSSES PEERS: the base's own round (Shots.Cruise) through the one
            // launch on the wire, a hull only the host keeps, and a fall every peer hears of. At the
            // guest from 2600 u at a seeded bearing -- 240 u of that is all it closes before it is
            // brought down -- once the guest is here to be told of it.
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.Any(s => !s.Mine && Hub.PeerSector(s.OwnerId) == Hub.SectorKind.Arena), 6);
            var hostRound = Emplacements.Of(Emplacements.RowOf(Emplacements.Base)).Gun.GetValueOrDefault();
            if (gship != null)
            {
                var cruiseFrom = gship.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * 2600f;
                int cruiseDown0 = Shot.Intercepted;
                var hostCruise = Combat.Fire(hostRound.Kind, cruiseFrom, gship.Position - cruiseFrom, hostRound.ShellSpeed, hostRound.Range, 1,
                                             targetId: gship.NetId, turnRate: hostRound.Homing, size: hostRound.RoundSize, hull: hostRound.Hull);
                await Wait(1.5);
                hostCruise.TakeDamage(hostRound.Hull / 2); bool cruiseStood = hostCruise.Alive && Shot.Intercepted == cruiseDown0;
                await Wait(0.5);
                hostCruise.TakeDamage(hostRound.Hull / 2);
                Check(cruiseStood && !hostCruise.Alive && Shot.Intercepted == cruiseDown0 + 1,
                      $"arena host: a cruise missile at the guest stands half its {hostRound.Hull:0} hull and falls to the other half ({Shot.Intercepted - cruiseDown0} down)");
            }
            // Long enough for the guest to watch the super-move bar move, and no longer: this
            // scenario has a second arena visit after it, and a stay stretched to fit a whole beam
            // cycle pushed that off the end of every wait that follows. (Two of its four seconds are
            // the cruise missile's, above.)
            await Wait(2.0);
            // the host in stasis at the moment of the kill: a pilot shot down in the fight still
            // helped win it, and gets its own crates -- only its own
            int ahHold0 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum();
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1);
            H.Boss.TakeDamage(H.Boss.Hp + 1);
            await Wait(0.3);
            var ahCrates = H.Crates.ToList();
            Check(!Me.Alive && ahCrates.Count == 2 && Count<LootCrate>() == 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 2,
                  $"arena host: in stasis at the kill, still its own 2 crates, and none of the guest's ({ahCrates.Count} here, {Count<LootCrate>()} nodes)");
            // NO CLOCK: the party goes home only once EVERY present pilot presses RETURN. The guest
            // presses first (it collects, then readies); one pilot ready is not enough on its own.
            // (generously: the guest presses only after its own checks of the fight and its crates,
            // which can run past the kill)
            await RealTime(() => H.ReturnReady == 1, Wan ? 60 : 45);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena && H.ReturnReady == 1 && H.ReturnTotal == 2,
                  $"arena host: one pilot's RETURN is not enough -- the party waits ({H.ReturnReady} of {H.ReturnTotal})");
            H.SetMyReturn(true);                                  // now both (a stasis pilot readies too); home together
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 30);
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady(), 5);   // the home world is built a frame after the sector changes
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline, "arena host: the boss down, both pilots home -- still hosting");
            Check(Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == ahHold0 + 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0 && ahCrates.All(c => !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(c)),
                  "arena host: the crates it never reached came home in its hold; their nodes did not");
            // ── THE SIEGE, AS A GUEST SEES IT (the aguest branch checks what it is shown) ──
            // Between the two bounties, a siege at level 1. This host parks beyond the launcher's
            // 4500 u; the guest parks at a LITERAL spot inside it. The launcher's clock is made due,
            // so its warning goes up at the guest and its round follows; the next warning is made due
            // at once, so a lane is up and a round is in the air at the guest when the pylons and then
            // the base come down. Its garrison is put down as it comes: it is not what this shows.
            await Wait(2.0);
            H.SelectMission(Missions.Siege); H.SelectLevel(1);
            await ToArena("arena host", "the siege");
            var sgBase = H.Emplacements.FirstOrDefault(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Base);
            var sgSpot = Hub.ArenaCentre + new Vector2(0, 2200);             // the guest's spot, as a literal
            Me.Position = VaryNear(Hub.ArenaCentre + new Vector2(0, -5400), 300f); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.AutopilotTo = null;
            bool SgClear() { foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) H.RaiderDown(r); return true; }
            await RealTime(() => SgClear() && H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { } sgAt && sgAt.Position.DistanceTo(sgSpot) < 60, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            var sgGuest = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine);
            Shot sgRound = null; bool sgLaneUp = false, sgRoundUp = false;
            if (sgBase != null && sgGuest != null)
            {
                var sgCd = typeof(Emplacement).GetField("_cd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
                var sgHook = Combat.ShotFired;
                Combat.ShotFired = s => { sgHook?.Invoke(s); if (s.Kind == Shots.Cruise && s.TargetId == sgGuest.NetId) sgRound ??= s; };
                sgCd.SetValue(sgBase, 0.0);                                  // due: the warning goes up at the one pilot in reach
                await RealTime(() => SgClear() && sgRound != null, 6);
                Combat.ShotFired = sgHook;
                var sgOld = Nodes<FxNode>(sgBase).Where(f => f.Id == Fx.WarnLane).ToHashSet();
                sgCd.SetValue(sgBase, 0.0);                                  // ...and the next one at once
                await RealTime(() => SgClear() && Nodes<FxNode>(sgBase).Any(f => f.Id == Fx.WarnLane && !sgOld.Contains(f)), 3);
                await Wait(0.3);                                             // the lane on its way to the guest
                sgLaneUp = Nodes<FxNode>(sgBase).Any(f => f.Id == Fx.WarnLane);
                sgRoundUp = sgRound != null && sgRound.Alive;
                foreach (var pl in H.Emplacements.Where(e => e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Pylon).ToList()) pl.TakeDamage(pl.MaxHp * 2);
                sgBase.TakeDamage(sgBase.Hp + 1);
            }
            Check(H.MissionWon && sgLaneUp && sgRoundUp,
                  $"arena host: the pirate base brought down with a warning up at the guest and a round in the air at it (lane {sgLaneUp}, round {sgRoundUp}, won {H.MissionWon})");
            await RealTime(() => H.ReturnReady == 1, Wan ? 30 : 20);
            H.SetMyReturn(true);
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 30);
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady(), 5);
            await Wait(2.0);
            H.SelectMission(Missions.Bounty);
            H.SelectLevel(2);                                    // mission 2 is the Drake Bastion's level
            await ToArena("arena host", "mission 2");
            Check(H.Boss is { Type.Id: "drake_bastion" }, $"arena host: mission 2, level 2, is the Drake Bastion ({H.Boss?.Type.Name})");
            H.Boss.DamageMult = 0;                               // nobody is lost while the rest is checked
            H.Boss.HullMult = 2.2;                               // a party-of-3 scale: the guest must show the host's
            // in its gun's reach, and a rock thrown as soon as the guest is in the arena to be told of it
            // (a boss's events go to the peers already there), for the guest to see both
            if (H.Boss is { Type.Id: "drake_bastion" } hd)
            {
                Me.Position = hd.Position + new Vector2(0, 600); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                await RealTime(() => H.Ships.Any(s => !s.Mine && Hub.PeerSector(s.OwnerId) == Hub.SectorKind.Arena), 6);
                // ONE SHELL FIRST, while the guest is there to be sent it. The throw now warps the
                // Drake back to 1300 u from its target -- past its gun's 1100 u -- so a throw called
                // the moment the guest arrived left it nothing in reach to shoot for the whole throw,
                // the guest waited out its watch for a shell, and every step after it ran late.
                int gunFired = hd.S("gun").Fired;
                await RealTime(() => hd.S("gun").Fired > gunFired, 4);
                hd.S("throw").Due = 0.01;
            }
            await Wait(3.5);
            // A GUEST DROPPED MID-ARENA (no goodbye): its place is held, and it comes back into the
            // arena, where it was
            // (only once this host sees the guest where it parked: the place held must be that one)
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { } p && p.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50, 8);
            var ag = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine); int agOld = ag?.OwnerId ?? 0;
            Check(ag != null && ag.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50, $"arena host: the guest parked at (900, 300) before its drop ({ag?.Position})");
            Drop(agOld);
            await RealTime(() => !H.Ships.Any(s => s.OwnerId == agOld), 3);
            Check(H.Ships.Count() == 1 && H.PartyIds.Count() == 2, $"arena host: a guest dropped mid-arena keeps its place in the party ({H.Ships.Count()} ship, {H.PartyIds.Count()} in the party)");
            // THE BOSS DIES WHILE IT IS AWAY: it helped win it, so the kill is owed to it
            var arenaHeld = (System.Collections.IDictionary)Session.Places;
            H.Boss.TakeDamage(H.Boss.Hp + 1);
            var owedA = arenaHeld.Values.Cast<object>().Select(x => (System.Collections.IList)x.GetType().GetField("Owed").GetValue(x)).FirstOrDefault();
            Check(H.MissionWon && owedA?.Count == 1, $"arena host: the boss killed while the guest is away -- the kill is owed to it ({owedA?.Count})");
            // (no need to prop the arena open any more: with no victory clock it stays until a RETURN)
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { } back && Hub.PeerSector(back.OwnerId) == Hub.SectorKind.Arena
                                 && back.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50, Wan ? 25 : 15);
            var ag2 = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine);
            Check(ag2 != null && ag2.OwnerId != agOld && ag2.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50,
                  $"arena host: it came back into the arena, where it was ({ag2?.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)):0} u off; its world {(ag2 == null ? "-" : Hub.PeerSector(ag2.OwnerId)?.ToString())}; at {ag2?.Position})");
            // A STALL IS NOT A DROP. The guest checks what it was paid, its whole world freezes for 6 s
            // (a first load, a garbage-collection pause, a laptop lid), and then it parks at stallDone.
            // This host must keep it through the silence: ENet gave a silent peer up after 4 s.
            int agId = ag2?.OwnerId ?? 0, agLeft = 0; float agQuiet = 0;
            Action<int, Net.PlayerInfo, bool> agGone = (peer, _, _) => { if (peer == agId) agLeft++; };
            Net.I.PlayerLeft += agGone;
            var agWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                if (H?.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.OwnerId == agId) is { } g) agQuiet = Math.Max(agQuiet, Quiet(g));
                return false;
            } };
            AddChild(agWatch);
            await RealTime(() => H?.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.OwnerId == agId) is { } g && g.Position.DistanceTo(stallDone) < 60, Wan ? 40 : 30);
            Net.I.PlayerLeft -= agGone; agWatch.QueueFree();
            Check(agQuiet >= 5 && agLeft == 0 && Net.I.Players.ContainsKey(agId),
                  $"arena host: the guest's world stalled and this host heard nothing from it for {agQuiet:0.0} s (at least 5), and kept it -- the same pilot, never dropped ({agLeft} leaves)");
            // ...and then THIS host's world freezes for 6 s, for the guest to outlast in turn
            OS.DelayMsec(6000);
            Net.SkipGoodbye = true;                              // then the HOST vanishes -- no goodbye, as in a crash
            Net.I.GoOffline();
            await Wait(2.0);
        }
        else
        {
            Character.Name = "Arena Guest";
            // Gear the host can only know from this pilot's announcement: two Armour Chips I on a
            // destroyer (250 x 1.16 = 290 hull, not the stock 250). The host checks it survives the
            // move into the arena.
            Character.Class = ShipClass.Destroyer;
            var agl = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Destroyer); agl[5] = "chip_armour_1"; agl[6] = "chip_armour_1";
            agl[4] = "dd_salvo_3";                      // and a dropped rack: a magazine of 6 bursts
            H.Yard.Credits = 12345;                               // its own base, before it visits
            // Level 2 against the level-1 boss: its OWN share is 100 for the kill (the host's is 200)
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 2; Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            await Wait(0.8);
            int railHeard0 = Sfx.Played.GetValueOrDefault("rail");   // what this peer had heard of a railgun before it joined
            var joinWatch = new JoinWatch(); AddChild(joinWatch);
            Net.I.Join(HostAt(Port));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && !(Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != 1); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != 1, "arena guest: joined");
            // ── TWO OF THE NINE, IN A SESSION (the host's side is in the ahost branch) ──
            // A FREIGHTER: the class whose abilities MAKE things in the world. Every press below
            // is a request -- nothing here is made on this peer.
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightHauler);
            var fgm = Me; fgm.AutopilotTo = null;
            fgm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2400, 2400); fgm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            // ITS HULL IS THE HOST'S WORD. A guest applies the refit locally and announces it; the
            // hull it then flies comes back in the host's report (ApplyHostState), a round trip
            // later -- so this waits for the host to have refitted its copy too.
            await RealTime(() => Math.Abs(fgm.MaxHp - 400) < 1e-6, Wan ? 20 : 10);
            Check(fgm.Class == ShipClass.FreightHauler && Math.Abs(fgm.MaxHp - 400) < 1e-6
                  && Abilities.For(ShipClass.FreightHauler).Length == 12,
                  $"arena guest: flying a FREIGHTER in a session -- its stock 400 hull, no chips, and the host's word for it, six abilities and six open slots ({fgm.MaxHp:0})");
            // Watched from before the host does any of it: an effect lives well under a second and
            // a status bit is held for a packet, so neither survives a single sample.
            bool sawEmp = false, sawBurst = false; int mostBursts = 0;
            var fxWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                sawEmp   |= Nodes<FxNode>().Any(f => f.Id == Fx.Emp);
                sawBurst |= Nodes<FxNode>().Any(f => f.Id == Fx.Burst);
                // ...and ONCE. The host raises an effect and tells its guests through one RPC, but
                // a guest also ran the code that raised it -- the burst on a turret it was told to
                // remove -- and drew the same burst twice, a frame apart.
                mostBursts = Math.Max(mostBursts, Nodes<FxNode>().Count(f => f.Id == Fx.Burst));
                return sawEmp && sawBurst;
            } };
            AddChild(fxWatch);
            var hardWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
                H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.OwnerId == 1) is { } hs && hs.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) };
            AddChild(hardWatch);
            // ONLY THE HOST MAY PUT ONE IN THE WORLD, asked at the door itself rather than by
            // pressing T and looking a frame later: on a loopback the host's answer can beat that
            // frame, and a check that depends on losing a race is a check that hides a bug.
            bool ownDrop = H.Drop(fgm, fgm.Position, 120) != null || fgm.TurretsOut > 0;
            fgm.UseAbility("deploy", 0);                       // T
            await RealTime(() => H.Deployed.Count == 1, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            Check(!ownDrop && H.Deployed.Count == 1 && H.Deployed[0].OwnerId == Net.LocalId && fgm.TurretsOut == 1
                  && Math.Abs(H.Deployed[0].Hp - 120) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena guest: its T is the host's -- nothing made here ({ownDrop}), then a 120-hull turret of its own arrives ({H.Deployed.Count} out)");
            await RealTime(() => H.Deployed.Count == 3, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            Check(H.Deployed.Count == 3 && H.Deployed.All(t => t.OwnerId == Net.LocalId),
                  $"arena guest: every turret the host put out for it stands here ({H.Deployed.Count})");
            // ...AND ON ITS OWN SCOPE. A freighter's turrets were the one thing a pilot places by
            // hand that the radar refused to draw, so three left out could not be found again.
            var dpMarks = H.ScopeMarks().Where(smk => H.Deployed.Any(dp => dp.Position.DistanceTo(smk.At) < 0.01f)).ToList();
            Check(dpMarks.Count == 3 && dpMarks.All(smk => smk.Name.Length > 0 && smk.Pick > 0f && smk.Size.X > 0f && smk.Colour.A > 0f),
                  $"arena guest: all 3 of its turrets are marks on its own scope, each with a name and a glyph ({dpMarks.Count} of {H.Deployed.Count} standing)");
            // THE ARM INDEX IS ON THE WIRE: the arms used to be host-only state, so on a guest the
            // hologram bars never brightened for a ship it could plainly see standing in one.
            await RealTime(() => H.Yard.Gatherers.Count > 0 && H.Yard.ArmOf(H.Yard.Gatherers[0]) == 3, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            int gArm = H.Yard.Gatherers.Count > 0 ? H.Yard.ArmOf(H.Yard.Gatherers[0]) : -1;
            Check(gArm == 3 && H.Yard.Arms[3].Occupant == H.Yard.Gatherers[0] && H.Yard.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null) == 1,
                  $"arena guest: arm 3 of the base's 5 holds the host's first gatherer here too, the other 4 free ({gArm}, {H.Yard.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null)} busy)");
            // ...and what the host puts INTO one of them arrives too
            await RealTime(() => H.Deployed.Any(t => t.Hp < 119), Wan ? 20 : 12);
            var hurtHere = H.Deployed.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Hp < 119);
            Check(hurtHere != null && Math.Abs(hurtHere.Hp - 75) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(hurtHere.MaxHp - 120) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena guest: the 45 the host put into one of its turrets arrives -- {hurtHere?.Hp:0} of {hurtHere?.MaxHp:0}, and a bar to draw it");
            fgm.UseAbility("bubble", 0);                       // F
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool ownBubble = fgm.BubbleUp;
            await RealTime(() => fgm.BubbleUp && fgm.BubbleLeft < 400, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            Check(!ownBubble && fgm.BubbleUp && Math.Abs(fgm.BubbleLeft - 250) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(fgm.BubbleRadius - 260f) < 1e-3,
                  $"arena guest: its bubble is the host's ({ownBubble}), and what the host spent of it on ANOTHER pilot's hull comes back on the wire ({fgm.BubbleLeft:0} of 400)");
            await RealTime(() => H.Deployed.Count == 2, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            Check(H.Deployed.Count == 2, $"arena guest: the turret the host shot off its base is gone here too ({H.Deployed.Count} left)");
            var takeBack = H.Deployed.First();
            fgm.Position = takeBack.Position; fgm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            await Wait(0.6);                                   // the host must see it over the turret before C
            fgm.UseAbility("collect", 0);                      // C
            await RealTime(() => H.Deployed.Count == 1, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            Check(H.Deployed.Count == 1 && fgm.TurretsOut == 1,
                  $"arena guest: C over one picked it up, and the host's word of it is here ({H.Deployed.Count} left)");
            await RealTime(() => hardWatch.Seen && sawEmp && sawBurst, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            Check(hardWatch.Seen, "arena guest: the host's warrior reads HARDENED here -- a status bit is the host's word, counted down on no other peer");
            Check(sawEmp && sawBurst && mostBursts == 1,
                  $"arena guest: the rush's EMP ring and the turret's burst both reached this peer, each drawn ONCE (emp {sawEmp}, burst {sawBurst}, most bursts at once {mostBursts})");
            fxWatch.QueueFree(); hardWatch.QueueFree();
            await RealTime(() => H.Deployed.Count == 0, Wan ? 15 : 8);
            await SetClass(ShipClass.Destroyer);               // back to the hull and the rack the rest of this scenario flies
            await Wait(0.5);

            // THE HOST'S CRUISE MISSILE (the ahost branch fires it once this pilot is in the arena): a
            // copy of the base's round, aimed at THIS ship, in its hostiles and pickable, that goes when
            // the host's does -- before the win, which takes every hostile body down as well
            int cruiseSeen = 0; bool cruiseGone = false; float cruiseSize = 0;
            var cruiseWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                var cw = Nodes<Shot>().FirstOrDefault(x => x.Kind == Shots.Cruise && x.Cosmetic);
                var mine = Me;
                if (cruiseSeen == 0 && cw is { Alive: true } && mine != null && cw.TargetId == mine.NetId
                    && Combat.Hostiles.Contains(cw) && Combat.Pickable(cw)) { cruiseSeen = cw.NetId; cruiseSize = cw.Size; }
                if (cruiseSeen != 0 && !cruiseGone && (cw == null || !cw.Alive) && H != null && !H.MissionWon) cruiseGone = true;
                return cruiseGone;
            } };
            AddChild(cruiseWatch);
            // THE HOST'S RAILGUN, HEARD HERE: the ahost branch fires a sniper's railgun within 600 u of
            // this ship before it leaves for the arena. The report is counted under the beam's own id
            // ("rail"), on this peer, from the flash the host sends every peer.
            await RealTime(() => Sfx.Played.GetValueOrDefault("rail") > railHeard0, Wan ? 20 : 12);
            Check(Sfx.Played.GetValueOrDefault("rail") > railHeard0,
                  $"arena guest: a railgun the host fired near this ship is heard here ({Sfx.Played.GetValueOrDefault("rail") - railHeard0} reports since joining)");
            await ToArena("arena guest", "mission 1");
            await GuestSeesTheFight();
            if (Wan) Check(Net.Arriving(1.5) < 1.45 && Net.Arriving(1.5) >= 0.6,
                           $"over the internet, a 1.5 s warning is shown a round trip short, so it ends when this pilot is judged ({Net.Arriving(1.5):0.00} s)");
            int agHold0 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum();
            await RealTime(() => H.MissionWon, 15);
            var guestRound = Emplacements.Of(Emplacements.RowOf(Emplacements.Base)).Gun.GetValueOrDefault();
            Check(cruiseSeen != 0 && cruiseGone && Math.Abs(cruiseSize - guestRound.RoundSize) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena guest: the host's cruise missile flew here as the base's round -- aimed at this ship, {cruiseSize:0}x a missile, in its hostiles and pickable -- and fell here when the host brought it down, before the win (id {cruiseSeen}, fell {cruiseGone})");
            cruiseWatch.QueueFree();
            Check(H.MissionWon && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) && H.Boss.Hp == 0,
                  $"arena guest: the boss the host killed reads zero here too ({(GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) ? H.Boss.Hp : -1):0})");
            Check(H.MissionWon && !H.IReturned && H.ReturnTotal == 2 && Nodes<ReturnButton>().Any(),
                  $"arena guest: after the win a RETURN button, no countdown ({H.ReturnReady} of {H.ReturnTotal} ready)");
            await RealTime(() => H.Crates.Count == 2, 5);
            var agCrates = H.Crates.ToList();
            Check(agCrates.Count == 2 && Count<LootCrate>() == 2 && ((string)OnDisk("loot", "unclaimed", "")).Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Length == 2,
                  $"arena guest: its own 2 crates, and on its own file at once ({agCrates.Count} crates)");
            Check(Met("loot") && !Met("equipment"), "arena guest: LOOT is met with its first crates");
            FlyOver(agCrates[0].Position);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(agCrates[0]) && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == agHold0 + 1 && H.Crates.Count == 1,
                  "arena guest: flying over its own crate takes it -- no host involved");
            Check(Met("equipment"), "arena guest: EQUIPMENT is met with the first part taken");
            FlyOver(agCrates[1].Position + new Vector2(0, 1500));      // and well clear of the other: that one is left behind
            await Wait(6.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena, "arena guest: still in the arena 6 s after the kill -- no clock");
            H.SetMyReturn(true);                                  // done collecting; the host readies once it sees this
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 30);
            // the sector changes at once, the world a frame later: wait for the home world to be built
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady(), 5);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline, "arena guest: home with the host after the win");
            Check(Character.Exp == 500, $"arena guest: its OWN EXP for the kill -- 150 at level 2 (300 x the boss's level over its own), +250 first clear, +100 (500; exp {Character.Exp})");
            var agFile = new ConfigFile(); agFile.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
            int agDisk = agFile.HasSection("gear_hold") ? agFile.GetSectionKeys("gear_hold").Sum(k => (int)agFile.GetValue("gear_hold", k, 0)) : 0;
            Check(agDisk == agHold0 + 2 && (string)agFile.GetValue("id", "name", "") == "Arena Guest",
                  $"arena guest: both parts reached its OWN file's hold -- the one it took, and the one it left ({agDisk - agHold0} of 2; in memory {Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() - agHold0}, unclaimed {Character.Unclaimed.Count})");
            // ── THE SIEGE, SEEN FROM HERE (the ahost branch runs it) ─────────────
            // Every warning lane this peer is shown rides the pirate base -- a child of the
            // Emplacement with the base's NetId -- and the host brings the base down with a lane up
            // and a round in the air at this ship: within 0.6 s of the host's word of the win
            // (NetWon), no lane of it is left here and no cruise missile flies. Watched from before
            // the arena is built, at the end of every frame.
            int sgBaseId = 0, sgLanesSeen = 0, sgLanesElsewhere = 0; bool sgRoundSeen = false;
            double sgWonAt = -1, sgClearAt = -1;
            var sgLanes = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<FxNode>();
            var sgWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                if (H == null || !Hub.InArena || Missions.Kind != Missions.Siege) return false;
                if (H.Emplacements.FirstOrDefault(e => GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(e) && e.Def.Id == Emplacements.Base) is { } sgB) sgBaseId = sgB.NetId;
                foreach (var lane in Nodes<FxNode>().Where(x => x.Id == Fx.WarnLane).ToList())
                    if (sgLanes.Add(lane))
                    {
                        sgLanesSeen++;
                        if (!(sgBaseId != 0 && lane.GetParent() is Emplacement rode && rode.Def.Id == Emplacements.Base && rode.NetId == sgBaseId)) sgLanesElsewhere++;
                    }
                var mine = Me;
                if (!H.MissionWon && mine != null && Nodes<Shot>().Any(x => x.Kind == Shots.Cruise && x.Alive && x.TargetId == mine.NetId)) sgRoundSeen = true;
                double now = Time.GetTicksMsec() / 1000.0;
                if (H.MissionWon && sgWonAt < 0) sgWonAt = now;
                if (sgWonAt >= 0 && sgClearAt < 0 && sgLanes.All(x => !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(x))
                    && !Nodes<Shot>().Any(x => x.Kind == Shots.Cruise && x.Alive)) sgClearAt = now;
                return sgClearAt >= 0;
            } };
            AddChild(sgWatch);
            await ToArena("arena guest", "the siege");
            Me.Position = Hub.ArenaCentre + new Vector2(0, 2200); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.AutopilotTo = null;   // the host's literal spot
            await RealTime(() => sgWatch.Seen, Wan ? 30 : 20);
            Check(sgLanesSeen > 0 && sgLanesElsewhere == 0 && sgRoundSeen && sgWonAt > 0 && sgClearAt >= 0 && sgClearAt - sgWonAt <= 0.6,
                  $"arena guest: the siege's warning lanes ride the pirate base here ({sgLanesSeen} seen, {sgLanesElsewhere} on anything else) and its round flew at this ship ({sgRoundSeen}); within 0.6 s of the win no lane is left and no cruise missile flies ({(sgClearAt < 0 || sgWonAt < 0 ? "never" : $"{sgClearAt - sgWonAt:0.00} s")})");
            sgWatch.QueueFree();
            H.SetMyReturn(true);
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 30);
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady(), 5);
            // what this guest is shown of the Drake Bastion, watched from before the arena is built
            bool sawDrake = false, sawShell = false, sawRock = false;
            var drakeWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                if (H == null || !Hub.InArena || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss)) return false;
                sawDrake |= H.Boss is { Type.Id: "drake_bastion" };
                sawShell |= Slugs().Any(s => s.Cosmetic && s.Kind == Shots.Slug);
                // WHERE THE HOST HELD IT, not a figure written down here: the body is held
                // Offset off the flank, and Offset is scaled by the level with the body it clears
                // (Boss.Scaled), so a literal here would have to be re-chosen every time either
                // the rock or the level's scale moved. Read the row and assert the spot exactly.
                double heldAt = H.Boss.S("throw")?.M.Offset ?? 0;
                sawRock |= heldAt > 0 && Nodes<ThrownRock>().Any(r => r.Cosmetic && r.Hold <= 7.5
                                                                      && Math.Abs(r.From.DistanceTo(H.Boss.Position) - heldAt) < 2f);
                return sawDrake && sawShell && sawRock;
            } };
            AddChild(drakeWatch);
            await ToArena("arena guest", "mission 2");
            int agOldId = Net.LocalId;
            int agHold1 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum();
            Me.Position = new Vector2(900, 300); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.AutopilotTo = null;
            // its boss is the host's boss, scaled as the host scaled it (the host sets a party-of-3 x2.2)
            double seenMult = 0;
            await RealTime(() => { if (H?.Boss != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss)) seenMult = H.Boss.HullMult; return Math.Abs(seenMult - 2.2) < 1e-9; }, 5);
            Check(Math.Abs(seenMult - 2.2) < 1e-9, $"arena guest: its boss is the host's boss, scaled as the host scaled it (x2.2; x{seenMult:0.00})");
            // A PEER ARRIVING MID-THROW is sent the rock and its lane as they stand (Boss.CatchUp, from
            // the host's catch-up for a peer that reports its world, as a rejoining or slow-loading guest
            // does) -- asked for here by reporting again, a second into the throw's 7.5 s warning by the
            // rock's own clock, well before the host drops this peer
            await RealTime(() => Nodes<ThrownRock>().Any(r => r.Cosmetic && !r.Done && r.Elapsed >= 1.0), 8);   // a shell, a 1 s warp, then the rock
            typeof(Hub).GetMethod("ReportSector", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).Invoke(H, null);
            await RealTime(() => Nodes<ThrownRock>().Any(r => r.Cosmetic && !r.Done && !r.IsQueuedForDeletion() && r.Hold < 6.9), 3);
            var lateRocks = Nodes<ThrownRock>().Where(r => r.Cosmetic && !r.Done && !r.IsQueuedForDeletion()).ToList();
            // ...AT THE HOST'S SIZE. The lane a guest is sent is the body's own width (twice its
            // radius, Boss.Warn) and the body is the host's, so this proves the two agree ACROSS
            // PEERS -- which is the one thing rung 5 can see and rung 3 cannot.
            float lateBody = H.Boss.S("throw")?.M.Radius ?? 0;
            Check(lateRocks.Count == 1 && lateRocks[0].Hold < 6.9 && lateBody > 0
                  && Math.Abs(lateRocks[0].Radius - lateBody) < 1e-3
                  && Nodes<FxNode>().Any(t => t.Id == Fx.WarnLane && Math.Abs(t.Radius - lateBody * 2f) < 0.5f && t.Time < 6.9 && t.Since > 0),
                  $"arena guest: a peer arriving mid-throw is sent the rock and its lane as they stand -- one {lateRocks.FirstOrDefault()?.Radius:0} u rock in a lane of its own width, with what was left of its warning ({lateRocks.FirstOrDefault()?.Hold:0.00} s)");
            await RealTime(() => drakeWatch.Seen, 4);
            Check(sawDrake && sawShell && sawRock,
                  $"arena guest: mission 2 is the Drake Bastion here too, and its slow shells and its thrown rock reach this guest (Drake {sawDrake}, shell {sawShell}, rock {sawRock})");
            drakeWatch.QueueFree();
            // DROPPED MID-ARENA: to its own world at once, then back in -- into the arena
            await RealTime(() => !Net.IsOnline, Wan ? 20 : 10);
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 2);
            Check(!Net.IsOnline && !Hub.InArena && Net.I.Reconnecting, "arena guest: dropped mid-arena, home to its own world, trying to get back in");
            await RealTime(() => Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != 1, 12);
            Check(Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != agOldId, "arena guest: back in under a new id -- into the arena, where the party is");
            await RealTime(() => Me != null && Me.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50, 5);
            Check(Me != null && Me.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50,
                  $"arena guest: put back where it was when it dropped ({Me?.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)):0} u off; at {Me?.Position})");
            // the boss died while it was away: it helped win it, so it is paid on its return -- its EXP
            // (the level-2 Drake at pilot level 3 -- the siege's 1000 took it from 2 with 500 to 3 with
            // 500 -- is 200, +250 its first clear of level 2, +100 = 550 on its 500: 1050, a level and 50
            // over -- level 4) and both its parts (straight to the hold if they reach it at home, else as
            // crates)
            await RealTime(() => Character.Level == 4 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() + Character.Unclaimed.Count == agHold1 + 2, 6);
            Check(Character.Level == 4 && Character.Exp == 50 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() + Character.Unclaimed.Count == agHold1 + 2,
                  $"arena guest: the kill made while it was away is paid on its return: its EXP and its 2 parts (level {Character.Level}, exp {Character.Exp}; {Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() + Character.Unclaimed.Count - agHold1} parts)");
            await RealTime(() => H.MissionWon && H.Boss != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) && H.Boss.Hp == 0, 5);
            Check(H.MissionWon && H.Boss != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) && H.Boss.Hp == 0, "arena guest: back in an arena already won -- the boss reads zero");
            // A STALL IS NOT A DROP, both ways. This world freezes for 6 s (the host must keep this pilot
            // through it) and parks at stallDone to say it is over; then the HOST's world freezes for 6 s,
            // and this pilot must keep the host through that. ENet gave a silent peer up after 4 s.
            await Wait(1.0);
            OS.DelayMsec(6000);
            // The next frame carries the whole 6 s as its step, and a remote ship ages by its step: the
            // host's would read 6 s quiet here with the host never quiet at all. So listen only from
            // the host's first report after that frame.
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await RealTime(() => H?.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.OwnerId == 1) is { } h0 && Quiet(h0) < 0.5, 3);
            int idThroughStall = Net.LocalId; bool keptHost = true; float hostQuiet = 0;
            var hostWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                if (!Net.IsOnline) return false;
                if (Net.LocalId != idThroughStall) keptHost = false;
                if (H?.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.OwnerId == 1) is { } h1) hostQuiet = Math.Max(hostQuiet, Quiet(h1));
                return false;
            } };
            AddChild(hostWatch);
            if (Me != null) { Me.AutopilotTo = null; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.Position = stallDone; }
            // THE HOST VANISHES (no goodbye) once its stall is over: 3 tries over about 20 s, then RECONNECT
            await RealTime(() => !Net.IsOnline, Wan ? 40 : 20);
            hostWatch.QueueFree();
            Check(hostQuiet >= 5 && keptHost,
                  $"arena guest: the host's world stalled and this pilot heard nothing from it for {hostQuiet:0.0} s (at least 5), and kept it -- the same session, not dropped");
            ulong vanished = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            var tries = new List<double>(); bool wasTrying = false; PlayerShip homeShip = null;
            while (!Net.I.CanReconnect && Time.GetTicksMsec() - vanished < 25000)
            {
                double since = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - vanished) / 1000.0;
                if (Net.I.Connecting && !wasTrying) tries.Add(since);
                wasTrying = Net.I.Connecting;
                if (homeShip == null && since > 1.0 && H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady()) homeShip = Me;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            double toButton = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - vanished) / 1000.0;
            Check(Net.I.CanReconnect && Net.I.Attempt == 3 && tries.Count == 3 && Math.Abs(tries[0] - 2) < 0.3 && Math.Abs(tries[1] - 8) < 0.3 && Math.Abs(tries[2] - 14) < 0.3
                  && Math.Abs(toButton - 19) < 0.7 && !Net.IsOnline,
                  $"arena guest: a vanished host is tried at 2, 8 and 14 s, 5 s each, then RECONNECT is offered at 19 s ({string.Join(", ", tries.Select(t => t.ToString("0.0")))}; {toButton:0.0} s)");
            Check(homeShip != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(homeShip) && Me == homeShip, "arena guest: the failed tries left its own world and ship alone");
            await Wait(1.0);
            // its own base, plus its shares of three clears with two pilots -- the level-1 bounty,
            // 2000 x 1.5 / 2 = 1500; the level-1 siege, the same 1500; the level-2 bounty at the level's
            // scale, 1500 x 1.025 = 1537.5 -- the last made while it was away, but split two ways all
            // the same. (The scale is 1.025 a level because a boss that doubled every seven levels
            // outran any pilot; the step itself is proved against its own literal where the curve is.)
            double want = 12345 + 1500 + 1500 + 1537.5;
            Check(!Hub.InArena && !Net.IsOnline && H.Yard != null && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - want) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena guest: the host gone mid-arena, home offline with its own base and its bounty share ({H.Yard?.Credits:0} of {want:0})");
            // ...AND IT IS ON DISK, in THIS peer's own file. It used to read the host's: every
            // process shares one user:// and only solo made a character, so host and guest wrote
            // the same file and the loser's numbers were simply gone. Each role makes its own now,
            // which is what makes this assertable at all.
            var guestFile = new ConfigFile();
            Check(guestFile.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg") == Error.Ok
                  && (string)guestFile.GetValue("id", "name", "") == "Arena Guest"
                  && Math.Abs((double)guestFile.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - want) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena guest: its own base reached its OWN file ({(string)guestFile.GetValue("id", "name", "?")}, {(double)guestFile.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0} of {want:0})");
            int agDisk2 = guestFile.HasSection("gear_hold") ? guestFile.GetSectionKeys("gear_hold").Sum(k => (int)guestFile.GetValue("gear_hold", k, 0)) : 0;
            Check(agDisk2 == agHold1 + 2 && (int)guestFile.GetValue("progress", "level", 0) == 4 && (int)guestFile.GetValue("progress", "exp", -1) == 50,
                  $"arena guest: the kill paid on its return is on its own file: its EXP, both parts in the hold ({agDisk2 - agHold1} of 2, exp {(int)guestFile.GetValue("progress", "exp", 0)})");
            // PRESSING RECONNECT: a try (5 s: the host was here a moment ago, or it is gone), and when
            // that fails too, the button again
            var rcSm = Nodes<SessionMenu>().First(); var rcB = Nodes<Button>(rcSm).First(b => b.Name == "Reconnect");
            bool offered = rcB.IsVisibleInTree() && !rcB.Disabled;
            rcB.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            bool trying = Net.I.Connecting && !rcB.Visible;
            ulong pressedAt = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            await RealTime(() => Net.I.CanReconnect, 9);
            double tryFor = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - pressedAt) / 1000.0 + 0.2;
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(offered && trying && Net.I.CanReconnect && rcB.IsVisibleInTree() && !Net.IsOnline && Math.Abs(tryFor - 5) < 0.6 && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("RECONNECT to try again"),
                  $"arena guest: RECONNECT pressed tries once more, for 5 s, and fails back to the button ({offered}, {trying}, {tryFor:0.0} s; {Net.I.LastStatus})");
            Check(joinWatch.Frames > 0 && joinWatch.Online == 0 && joinWatch.Hosting == 0,
                  $"arena guest: through every frame of every join and try it is not online, and its panel does not read hosting, until the host has let it in ({joinWatch.Frames} frames joining; online on {joinWatch.Online}, 'hosting' on {joinWatch.Hosting})");
            joinWatch.QueueFree();
        }
    }

    // WHAT A GUEST IS SHOWN. Both of these were host-only state read by drawing code, so on a
    // guest they were simply wrong -- and nothing said so, because the solo run is the host.
    async Task GuestSeesTheFight()
    {
        Boss boss = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && boss == null; i++)
        {   // InArena goes true before the boss node exists on this peer
            boss = H?.Boss;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(boss != null && !Net.Sim, "arena guest: the boss is here, and this peer is not the host");
        if (boss == null) return;

        // THE SUPER-MOVE BAR. _beam and _charge only tick under Net.Sim, so a guest's bar sat at
        // zero for the whole fight. Watched over time rather than sampled once: the failure was a
        // value that never moved, and a single reading cannot tell a stopped clock from a slow one.
        // Sampled only once a figure has actually ARRIVED. Before the first packet a guest's
        // countdown is -1, which reads as 0 s left, and a first sample of 0 makes "it went down"
        // impossible to tell from "it has not started".
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && boss.NextSuperIn <= 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        double firstFill = boss.SuperFill, firstLeft = boss.NextSuperIn;
        Check(firstLeft > 0, $"arena guest: the host's super-move countdown arrives at all ({firstLeft:0.0} s)");
        await Wait(1.5);
        Check(boss.NextSuperIn > 0 && boss.NextSuperIn < firstLeft - 0.5 && boss.SuperFill > firstFill,
              $"arena guest: the boss's super-move bar runs ({firstLeft:0.0} -> {boss.NextSuperIn:0.0} s, fill {firstFill:0.00} -> {boss.SuperFill:0.00})");
    }

    // ── A GUEST AGAINST ITS OWN WALLS (level_walls checks 13, 14, 16, 17), played by the third player: a
    // battleship at level 1 whose peak is 3. The host decides every press; this ship's slots show only
    // what the host's report says, so what they show here is the host's word. PEND walls until its class
    // carries an ability 2 and 3 (lane A, slice 6). Returns the game seconds it spent.
    async Task<double> GuestWalls()
    {
        var me = Me; var open = Unlocks.Nth(me.Class, 2); var shut = Unlocks.Nth(me.Class, 3);
        if (open == null || shut == null)
        {
            GD.Print($"PEND walls: the {me.Class} has no ability {(open == null ? 2 : 3)} yet (level_walls checks 13, 14 and 17: a guest's press past its own refusal, the peak on the wire)");
            return 0;
        }
        double t0 = me.Clock;
        // 13: ability 3 (level 6) sent past its own refusal, straight to the host: nothing comes back
        string before = Sn(me, shut.Id);
        Net.AskHost(me, "RequestAbility", shut.Id, 0);
        await Wait(0.6);
        string after = Sn(me, shut.Id);
        Check(me.Peak == 3 && after == before,
              $"third player at peak 3: its {shut.Name} (ability 3, level 6) sent past its own refusal is refused by the host -- nothing comes back ({before} -> {after})");
        // 14 and 17: at level 1 with a peak of 3, ability 2 (level 3) acts -- the host reads the peak it was
        // sent, not the level
        string b2 = Sn(me, open.Id);
        me.UseAbility(open.Id, 0);
        double t1 = me.Clock; bool acted = false;
        while (me.Clock - t1 < 0.8 && !acted) { await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); acted = Sn(me, open.Id) != b2; }
        Check(Character.Level == 1 && acted && me.FailNote(open.Id) == null,
              $"third player at level 1, peak 3: its {open.Name} (ability 2, level 3) acts on the host and comes back -- the wall reads the peak on the wire, not the level ({b2} -> {Sn(me, open.Id)}; {me.FailNote(open.Id)})");
        return me.Clock - t0;
    }

    // 16: a level up and a press in the same frame. The identity (the new peak) and the request go on one
    // reliable channel, in that order, so the host has the peak before the press: ability 3 acts.
    async Task GuestLevelsAndPresses()
    {
        var me = Me; var a3 = me == null ? null : Unlocks.Nth(me.Class, 3);
        if (a3 == null) { GD.Print("PEND walls: no ability 3 to press in the frame its level opens it (level_walls check 16)"); return; }
        int peakBefore = me.Peak;
        string before = Sn(me, a3.Id);
        if (Character.Level < 6) Progression.AddExp((6 - Character.Level) * Progression.ExpToNext - Character.Exp);   // to level 6, exactly
        me.UseAbility(a3.Id, 0);
        double t0 = me.Clock; bool acted = false;
        while (me.Clock - t0 < 1.0 && !acted) { await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); acted = Sn(me, a3.Id) != before; }
        Check(peakBefore < 6 && Character.Peak == 6 && acted,
              $"third player: levelled from peak {peakBefore} to 6 and {a3.Name} (ability 3) pressed in the same frame -- the identity reaches the host first, so it acts ({before} -> {Sn(me, a3.Id)})");
    }

    async Task Mp(string role)
    {
        await Wait(0.1);
        if (role == "host")
        {
            Character.Name = "HostPilot"; Character.Class = ShipClass.Battleship; Priv(H, "ApplyLocalIdentity"); Priv(H, "SendIdentity", 0);
            // THIS HOST'S OWN LEVEL for the part the guest has levelled, at the ceiling: none of it may
            // reach the guest's ship. (Its own battleship does not wear one.)
            Character.GearLevel["drv_racing_1"] = 40;
            H.GetNode<PlayerShip>("Ship_1").Position = new Vector2(-900, 900);   // out of the way
            int refusals = 0;
            // THE HOST SENDS A GUEST NOTHING UNRELIABLE UNTIL IT HAS ANSWERED THE WELCOME: read the moment
            // each guest is let in (this handler runs after Net's), and again once both are here
            int heardAtLetIn = 0, letIn = 0;
            void AtLetIn(long id) { letIn++; if (Net.Hears((int)id)) heardAtLetIn++; }
            Multiplayer.PeerConnected += AtLetIn;
            Net.I.Status += s => { if (s.StartsWith("Refused")) refusals++; };
            Net.I.Host(27115);
            _streaming = true;                         // ten a second to every guest let in, for the guest to count
            // A raider that exists BEFORE either guest joins: they can only learn of it from the
            // catch-up a joining guest is sent (Hub.NetMySector). Far out, and gone at 9 s.
            var early = H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(-6000, -6000));
            H.Yard.Credits = 1000;                     // for the guest's purchase request
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            Check(Net.I.Reachability == Net.Reach.Checking && Net.I.LanAddress.EndsWith(":27115"), "hosting starts at once; the router is asked in the background");
            await Wait(9.0);
            // this harness's network has no router and no internet (NoRouterNoInternet): it must
            // say so, and how to get friends in anyway
            Check(Net.I.Reachability == Net.Reach.LanOnly && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("forward UDP 27115") && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Tailscale"),
                  $"no UPnP router: LAN hosting stands, and the status explains the fix");
            Check(Router.IsShared("100.72.1.5") && Router.IsShared("192.168.1.20") && !Router.IsShared("81.2.3.4"),
                  "carrier-grade and private addresses are recognised as unreachable from outside");
            // THE BUILD HANDSHAKE. The save format is versioned (3, as a literal: a check that reads
            // the number it tests cannot catch the number being wrong); a SESSION is matched on the
            // build's fingerprint. The refusal is proved on a real connection by the third player,
            // who first knocks as another build: exactly one refusal here, and no ship for it.
            Check(Game.Version == 3 && Net.Accepts(Net.Protocol) && !Net.Accepts(Net.Protocol ^ 1),
                  $"a session admits this build and refuses any other (save format {Game.Version}, build {Net.Protocol:x8})");
            Check(refusals == 1, $"the host refused the one peer that knocked as another build ({refusals})");
            var ships = Nodes<PlayerShip>().ToList();
            Check(ships.Count == 3, $"host sees 3 ships: itself and two guests ({ships.Count})");
            Multiplayer.PeerConnected -= AtLetIn;
            // A guest just let in may not have the host's own "done" yet (lost on the way, resent), and
            // Godot throws away with an engine error anything else that reaches it first. Its answer to
            // the welcome, which travels behind that "done", is what opens the unreliable reports to it.
            Check(letIn == 2 && heardAtLetIn == 0 && Multiplayer.GetPeers().All(Net.Hears),
                  $"host: a guest just let in is sent nothing unreliable until it answers the welcome -- neither had at the moment it was let in, both have now ({letIn} let in, {heardAtLetIn} heard at once, {Multiplayer.GetPeers().Count(Net.Hears)} of {Multiplayer.GetPeers().Length} heard)");
            // EVERY LINK KEEPS EVERY UNRELIABLE PACKET: ENet's throttle, at its defaults, falls with
            // every slow round trip and throws ship, raider and boss reports away before they are sent
            var links = Multiplayer.GetPeers().Select(id => (Multiplayer.MultiplayerPeer as ENetMultiplayerPeer)?.GetPeer(id)).ToList();
            Check(links.Count == 2 && links.All(l => l != null && Throttle(l) == (5000, 2, 0)),
                  $"host: its link to each guest keeps every unreliable packet -- ENet's throttle falls by 0 (interval 5000 ms, up 2, down 0; {string.Join(" / ", links.Select(l => l == null ? "none" : Throttle(l).ToString()))})");
            Check(ships.Any(s => s.Pilot == "Third"), "the second guest's identity reached the host");
            // A GUEST'S CHIPS ARE HELD TO ITS OWN WALLS (level_walls check 15): the third player claims
            // peak 3 -- one chip slot open -- with five chips fitted, and the host's copy of its
            // battleship flies the first alone, an Armour Chip I: 300 x 1.08 = 324 hull (all five would be 318).
            var thirdW = ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Third");
            Check(thirdW != null && thirdW.Peak == 3 && Math.Abs(thirdW.MaxHp - 324) < 1e-6,
                  $"host: a guest at peak 3 with five chips fitted flies one: 324 hull on the host's copy (peak {thirdW?.Peak}, {thirdW?.MaxHp:0.##})");
            // Each guest is known by its CHARACTER: a returning pilot gets a new peer id, not a new self
            var gInfo = Net.I.Players.Values.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Name == "Guesty"); var tInfo = Net.I.Players.Values.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Name == "Third");
            Check(gInfo != null && gInfo.CharacterId.EndsWith("-guest") && tInfo != null && tInfo.CharacterId.EndsWith("-guest2"),
                  $"the host knows each guest's character, not just its peer id ({gInfo?.CharacterId}, {tInfo?.CharacterId})");
            var third = ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Third");
            third?.Hit(250, third.Position, "test:reconnect");
            if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(early)) H.RaiderDown(early);
            // pinned by the HOST, the way a raider's web does it: the guest must feel it (it checks)
            ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Guesty")?.ApplyStatus(Status.Pinned, 3.0);
            // and a salvager lost, the way a raid takes one: the guest must see its rebuild run
            H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager)?.TakeDamage(1e6);
            // hosting here (no router, no internet): both answers come back empty -> network only
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 9 && Net.I.Reachability == Net.Reach.Checking; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var smh = Nodes<SessionMenu>().First(); var rev = Nodes<Button>(smh).First(b => b.Name == "RevealAddress");
            Check(Net.I.Reachability == Net.Reach.LanOnly && !rev.Visible, $"no router or internet here: network only, nothing to reveal ({Net.I.Reachability})");
            Net.I.Reachable(Net.Reach.Manual, "203.0.113.7:27015", "Hosting (test).");
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(rev.Visible && !rev.Text.Contains("203.0.113.7") && !Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("203.0.113.7"), "the address friends need is hidden until clicked");
            rev.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(rev.Text.Contains("203.0.113.7:27015"), "click: it shows the public address and port");
            rev.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(!rev.Text.Contains("203.0.113.7"), "click again: hidden");
            Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            // every pilot: its own EXP by its own level, and its own drops -- each guest ITS OWN, and only its own
            H.AnnounceClear(Missions.Bounty, 1, Vector2.Zero);
            // what the host rolled for whom, by character, for each guest to hold its own parts against
            var rolled = Session.Kills[Session.Kills.Count - 1].Drops;   // Session.Kill is a public record now
            var dropsFile = new ConfigFile();
            foreach (var kv in rolled) dropsFile.SetValue("drops", kv.Key == Net.LocalId ? Character.Id : Net.I.Players[kv.Key].CharacterId, string.Join(",", kv.Value.OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.Ordinal)));
            dropsFile.Save("user://smoke_drops.cfg");
            Check(rolled.Count == 3 && rolled.Values.All(d => d.Length == 2), $"the host rolls a kill's drops for each of the 3 pilots, 2 each ({rolled.Count})");
            // ...and the base owner's record is the level-3 boss: AUTO-SELL opens on THIS base for everyone
            Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1, 2, 3 };
            H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(-3500, 3500));   // far off: the guests must see it
            H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(-3700, 3700), Enemies.Gunship);
            Check(!H.AllReady, "the host cannot warp until every pilot is ready");
            H.SetMyReady(true); Me.AutopilotTo = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && !H.AllReady; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(H.AllReady && H.PartyIds.Count() == 3, "both guests' READY reached the host: the party of three is ready");
            Check(Multiplayer.GetPeers().All(id => Hub.PeerSector(id) == Hub.SectorKind.Home),
                  "every guest has told the host it is home (the yard talks only to guests at home)");
            H.StartMission();
            Check(Character.Exp == 650, $"the host gets its own EXP for the kill (level 1: 650; exp {Character.Exp})");
            Check(H.Yard.Level("salvage_rate") == 1 && H.Yard.Credits == 900, "a guest's BUY request was honoured by the host");
            var g = ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Guesty");
            Check(g != null && g.Name == PlayerShip.NodeName(g.OwnerId), "guest ship named by its id");
            Check(g != null && g.Pilot == "Guesty" && g.Class == ShipClass.Carrier && g.Main == new Color(1, 0, 0), $"guest identity reached host ({g?.Pilot}/{g?.Class}/{g?.Main})");
            // THE GUEST'S PEAK, NOT ITS LEVEL: every role's pilot is level 1 with every wall open (peak
            // 14), and the host's copy of its ship is held to the peak it announced (Unlocks).
            Check(g != null && g.Peak == 14 && Net.I.Players.TryGetValue(g.OwnerId, out var gPeakInfo) && gPeakInfo.Peak == 14 && gPeakInfo.Level == 1,
                  $"a guest's highest level reached comes with its identity: the host's copy of its ship reads peak 14 at level 1 (peak {g?.Peak})");
            Check(g != null && g.Loadout[0] == "cv_swarm_3" && g.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter) == 9 && g.WingCount(WingKind.Bomber) == 2,
                  $"the guest's dropped hangar reached the host: its carrier flies 9 fighters and 2 bombers here ({g?.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter)} / {g?.WingCount(WingKind.Bomber)})");
            // ITS PILOT'S LEVELS, NOT THIS HOST'S: the guest levelled its Racing Drive (+25% top speed)
            // twice, +10% of it; this host holds the same part at 40. Lifted by the host's levels the
            // sheet read x1.75, by none at all x1.25.
            double gTop = g == null ? 0 : g.Stats["max_speed"] / new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier)["max_speed"];
            Check(Math.Abs(gTop - 1.275) < 1e-9,
                  $"a guest's gear levels come with its identity, and the host's own do not leak into its ship: top speed x{gTop:0.0000} here (level 2: 1 + 0.25 x 1.10 = 1.275; the host's level 40 would be 1.75)");
            var dummy = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Check(g != null && g.WingTarget == dummy, "guest's Space order reached the host");
            Check(dummy.Total > 5, $"guest's wing hit the dummy on the host ({dummy.Total:0.0})");
            Check(g != null && g.PdActive, "guest's PD activation reached the host");
            Check(InFlight().Any(x => !x.Cosmetic) || dummy.Total > 12, "guest's bomber strike launched real torpedoes on the host");
            // stay up while the guest watches its bombers come home and dock (it checks ~22 s in), and
            // send the hauler on an escort half way through for it to see
            ulong w0 = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            H.Yard.Credits = 100000;                  // enough for AUTO-SELL: only its owner rule can refuse a guest
            GetTree().CreateTimer(3.0).Timeout += () => { if (H?.Yard != null) { H.Yard.Hauler.ResetToPad(); H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo = 300; } };
            GetTree().CreateTimer(6.0).Timeout += () =>
            {
                if (H?.Yard == null) return;
                H.Yard.Hauler.ResetToPad(); H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo = 300; H.Yard.RequestDispatch(escorted: true);
            };
            // two seconds into the escort, on to its fourth leg and just short of the south-west outpost:
            // the guest must see it hold there and offload
            GetTree().CreateTimer(8.0).Timeout += () =>
            {
                if (H?.Yard?.Hauler is not { Escorted: true } eh) return;
                typeof(Hauler).GetField("_leg", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(eh, 3);
                eh.Position = Hub.EscortRoute[3] + new Vector2(15, -15);
            };
            await Wait(2.0);
            // A DROP: Third's connection cut by the engine, no goodbye -- what a Wi-Fi drop looks like
            var heldD = (System.Collections.IDictionary)Session.Places;
            int thirdOld = third?.OwnerId ?? 0;
            Drop(thirdOld);
            await RealTime(() => !Multiplayer.GetPeers().Contains(thirdOld) && !H.Ships.Any(s => s.OwnerId == thirdOld), 3);
            var hEntry = heldD.Values.Cast<object>().FirstOrDefault();
            int owedOnDrop = hEntry == null ? -1 : ((System.Collections.IList)hEntry.GetType().GetField("Owed").GetValue(hEntry)).Count;
            double hullOnDrop = hEntry == null ? -1 : (double)hEntry.GetType().GetField("Hp").GetValue(hEntry);
            Check(owedOnDrop == 1, $"the kill 2 s before the drop is owed to the dropped pilot too: the host may not have reached it ({owedOnDrop})");
            double holdLeft = hEntry == null ? 0 : ((long)(ulong)hEntry.GetType().GetField("Until").GetValue(hEntry) - (long)Time.GetTicksMsec()) / 1000.0;
            Check(H.PartyIds.Count() == 3 && H.PilotName(thirdOld).Contains("Third") && H.PilotName(thirdOld).Contains("reconnecting") && H.IsReady(thirdOld)
                  && holdLeft > 85 && holdLeft <= 90.5,
                  $"a dropped pilot's place is held for 90 s: still in the party of 3, still READY ({H.PilotName(thirdOld)}, {holdLeft:0.0} s left)");
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.Any(s => s.Pilot == "Third" && s.OwnerId != thirdOld), Wan ? 25 : 12);
            // the hull as it comes back: its place is restored with its identity, and it regenerates from then on
            double hullBack = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Third" && s.OwnerId != thirdOld)?.Hp ?? -1;
            await Wait(0.5);
            var third2 = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Third" && s.OwnerId != thirdOld);
            Check(third2 != null && H.IsReady(third2.OwnerId) && !H.PartyIds.Contains(thirdOld) && H.PartyIds.Count() == 3 && hullOnDrop < 300 && Math.Abs(hullBack - hullOnDrop) < 1 && heldD.Count == 0,
                  $"back under a new id: READY and the damaged hull move to it, the place is released ({third2?.OwnerId}, {hullBack:0.0} hull back of {hullOnDrop:0.0} held)");
            // ...and when it leaves (it presses HOST), how long this host had heard nothing from it
            float thirdQuiet = -1;
            var thirdWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                if (H?.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Third" && s.OwnerId != thirdOld) is { } t3) thirdQuiet = Quiet(t3);
                return false;
            } };
            AddChild(thirdWatch);
            // A HOLD THAT LAPSES: a held pilot that was not READY keeps the portal shut until its time is up
            object Ghost(int peer, ulong ms) =>
                new Session.Held { OldPeer = peer, Name = "Ghost", Until = Time.GetTicksMsec() + ms };
            heldD["ghost"] = Ghost(424242, 300); Priv(H, "RefreshAway");
            bool shut = !H.AllReady;
            await Wait(0.6);
            Check(shut && heldD.Count == 0 && H.AllReady, "a held pilot that was not READY keeps the portal shut -- until its place lapses");
            // Fifteen seconds at least (the guest watches its bombers dock and the escort hold), and then
            // until the third player has gone: over the simulated internet a hang-up can be lost, and
            // then it notices the drop only by its own timeout before it comes back, finishes and leaves.
            await RealTime(() => Time.GetTicksMsec() - w0 >= 15000 && !Net.I.Players.Values.Any(p => p.Name == "Third"), Wan ? 45 : 20);
            thirdWatch.QueueFree();
            // the third player has LEFT, saying goodbye: that is not a drop, and no place is held for it
            Check(heldD.Count == 0 && H.PartyIds.Count() == 2 && !Net.I.Players.Values.Any(p => p.Name == "Third"),
                  $"a pilot that leaves on purpose is gone from the party: no place held ({heldD.Count} held, a party of {H.PartyIds.Count()})");
            // ...and its goodbye got out though it left by pressing HOST, which cut a guest's goodbye
            // off: this host heard it go inside the 2 s a goodbye is given to get out, plus a one-way
            // trip -- under 3 s -- not after the 8 s a silent peer is given (16-20 s over the simulated
            // internet, where resends run out later). The same bound on either link: this role is not
            // told which one it is on.
            Check(thirdQuiet >= 0 && thirdQuiet < 3,
                  $"a guest that leaves by pressing HOST still says goodbye: this host heard it go {thirdQuiet:0.00} s after its last report -- inside the 2 s a goodbye is given, plus the trip, under 3 s -- not by timeout (8 s)");
            // ...and the level that guest bought MID-SESSION, once this host's portal had started to open
            // (so after the check above): it comes with the identity again, and a ship whose parts have
            // not changed is refitted for it all the same.
            var gNow = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Guesty");
            double gTopNow = gNow == null ? 0 : gNow.Stats["max_speed"] / new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier)["max_speed"];
            Check(Math.Abs(gTopNow - 1.2875) < 1e-9,
                  $"a level a guest buys mid-session reaches the host's copy of its ship: top speed x{gTopNow:0.0000} (level 3: 1 + 0.25 x 1.15 = 1.2875; still level 2 would be 1.275)");
            heldD["ghost2"] = Ghost(424243, 60000);          // a place still held when the host leaves
            Net.I.GoOffline();   // drop the guest
            await Wait(2.0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && !Net.I.NetworkIdle; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Net.I.NetworkIdle && heldD.Count == 0, "back offline: no socket, no lookup, no router job left running, and no place still held");
        }
        else if (role == "guest2")
        {   // a third player: joins, sees everyone, leaves by hosting a world of its own
            var joinWatch = new JoinWatch(); AddChild(joinWatch);
            await Wait(1.6);
            // FIRST, AS ANOTHER BUILD. It must be refused -- and learn why, on its own screen, which
            // the old handshake never managed: its refusal went out in the same breath as the
