                  "guest sees the host's miner out working and the hauler on its lane");
            Check(host != null && host.Pilot == "HostPilot", $"host identity reached guest ({host?.Pilot})");
            Check(H.Yard.Stock("ore") < 1000, $"guest shows host's totals ({H.Yard.Stock("ore"):0})");
            Check(dummy.Total > 0 && dummy.LastSecond > 0, $"dummy readout reached the guest ({dummy.LastSecond:0.00} / {dummy.Total:0.0})");
            int near = Nodes<Wing>().Count(w => w.Carrier == mine && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter && w.Position.DistanceTo(dummy.Position) < 400);
            Check(near >= 3, $"guest sees its fighters at the target, as the host flies them ({near})");
            // a flash lives 0.1 s, so watch for one over two seconds rather than sampling once
            var flashes = (System.Collections.IList)H.GetType().GetField("_flashes", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H);
            int seen = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) { if (flashes.Count > 0) seen++; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(seen > 0, $"guest sees the host's hit flashes ({seen} of 60 frames)");
            // ...and HEARS them as their own rows. The host sends a flash's row index and nothing
            // else about it; a guest that dropped or misread it would hear every beam as row 0
            // (point defence) and never count a fighter. Nothing on a guest fires a fighter's shot
            // itself (Wing.Tick returns under !Net.Sim), so every one counted since it joined came
            // over the wire.
            int heardFighters = Sfx.Played.GetValueOrDefault("fighter") - heardFighters0;
            Check(heardFighters > 0, $"guest hears its fighters' beams as the fighter's own row, sent by index ({heardFighters} heard since it joined)");
            Check(mine != null && Math.Abs(mine.MaxHp - 248) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(mine.Hp - 248) < 1e-6,
                  $"hull replicated from the host, which applied this pilot's own gear held to three of a kind (three of its four Armour Chips I: 200 x 1.24 = 248; {mine?.Hp:0})");
            int streamed0 = _streamGot; ulong streamAt = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            await Wait(12.0);                      // strike done, bombers home and docked
            // THE HOST'S TEN A SECOND ARRIVE, over the 12 s just waited: nothing thrown away unsent. The
            // floor, from the link: 100 ms apart on a channel of its own, the simulated internet's jitter
            // (+/- 25 ms, +/- 40 on its worst day: 80 ms of spread at most) cannot reorder the stream, so
            // only its loss takes packets -- 2%, 5% on its worst day: 9.8 and 9.5 a second. Under 8 is a
            // fifth of them gone, which the link cannot do and a falling throttle does.
            double streamRate = (_streamGot - streamed0) / ((Time.GetTicksMsec() - streamAt) / 1000.0);
            Check(streamRate >= 8,
                  $"guest: the host's unreliable stream, sent ten a second on a channel of its own, arrives at {streamRate:0.0} a second over 12 s -- at least 8, as the link loses at most 5% (ENet's throttle throws none away before sending)");
            Check(pinWatch.Seen, "a guest's own ship is pinned when the host pins it (a raider's web works on guests too)");
            var lostSv = H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager);
            Check(lostSv != null && lostSv.Lost && lostSv.RebuildIn > 5 && lostSv.RebuildIn < 29,
                  $"a salvager the host lost is lost here too, its 30 s rebuild counting down -- not stuck at 0 s ({lostSv?.RebuildIn:0.0} s)");
            var gb = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == mine && w.Def.Parks).ToArray();
            Check(gb.Length == 2 && gb.All(w => w.Docked && w.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(mine.ToGlobal(mine.Bay(w))) < 0.5f),
                  "guest sees its bombers parked in their bays on the carrier's deck (the state from the host, placed in its carrier's frame)");
            Check(gLift < 0.67f && gBow && gLand < 0.67f && gOff < 0.05f,
                  $"guest places its own bombers' deck moves on its own clock, in its carrier's frame: on the runway at deck size, up to the bow, down on the centre at deck size ({gLift:0.00}, {gLand:0.00}; {gOff:0.000} u from the deck clock's pose)");
            deckWatch.QueueFree();
            Check(numberWatch.Seen, "guest sees damage numbers where the hits the host resolves land (from the dummy's replicated figures)");
            numberWatch.QueueFree();
            // TAKEN WHEN IT IS TRUE: the host closes its session as soon as the third player has
            // gone, so a sample after the waits below could find the portal already shut with it.
            bool portalWasOpen = H.GetNodeOrNull("MissionPortal") != null && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen;
            Check(portalWasOpen, "the host's mission portal opened here too (the party was ready)");
            // AUTO-SELL is the base owner's to buy: this guest's request changes nothing
            double hostCredits = H.Yard.Credits;
            H.Yard.BuyUpgrade("hauler_autosell"); await Wait(1.5);
            Check(H.Yard.Level("hauler_autosell") == 0 && hostCredits >= 4462 && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - hostCredits) < 1e-6,
                  $"a guest cannot buy AUTO-SELL on the host's base: it is the owner's (level {H.Yard.Level("hauler_autosell")}, {hostCredits:0} -> {H.Yard.Credits:0} cr)");
            // the host put its hauler at the south-west outpost (the last quarter aboard): this guest saw
            // it hold there and the pods drain -- never more than that quarter shown
            await RealTime(() => stopWatch.Seen && shownMin < 0.15, 12);
            Check(stopWatch.Seen && stopLeg == 3 && stopMax > 3 && stopMax <= 4 && shownMin < 0.15 && shownMax <= 0.26
                  && Nodes<Sprite2D>().Count(s => s.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Outpost")) == 4,
                  $"a guest sees the host's hauler hold at the south-west outpost and offload -- its outposts built here too (leg {stopLeg}, {stopMax:0.0} s, shown {shownMax:0.00} -> {shownMin:0.00} of the load)");
            Check(dispatchWatch.Seen && !Met("hauler"), "the hauler hint is the base owner's: a guest watching the host's loaded hauler is not shown buttons it cannot press");
            // the host closes the session once its 15 s are up and the third player has gone (~24.5 s in)
            await RealTime(() => !Net.IsOnline, Wan ? 40 : 10);
            Check(!Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId == 1 && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Host closed") && !Net.I.Reconnecting,
                  $"guest fell back offline when host left -- told it was closed, so nothing is retried ({Net.I.LastStatus})");
            Check(heldWatch.Seen, "while the third player was away, this guest's party list showed it as reconnecting");
            // THE WORLD'S NEXT FRAME, not the moment the session ended: the wait above wakes in the very
            // frame the host's hang-up is read, before this world has ticked once, and the portal follows
            // the mission state on the world's own tick (Hub.SyncMissionPortal). Given a second, not a frame.
            await RealTime(() => H.GetNodeOrNull("MissionPortal") == null, 1.0);
            Check(portalWasOpen && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Idle && H.GetNodeOrNull("MissionPortal") == null && H.PartyIds.SequenceEqual(new[] { 1 }),
                  $"the host gone, its open portal and its party went with it: this pilot's own world has neither ({H.Mission}; party {string.Join(",", H.PartyIds)}; portal {(H.GetNodeOrNull("MissionPortal") == null ? "gone" : "still up")})");
            // Back to its OWN world, with nothing lost: everything held -- stock, the
            // ships' cargo, and credits from a sale -- is at least the 5000 it had, and
            // nowhere near the host's few hundred.
            await midSession;
            Check(Math.Abs(levelPaid - 156) < 1e-9 && Equipment.LevelOf("drv_racing_1") == 3,
                  $"a guest levels a part mid-session out of its OWN salvage, parked at home: level 2 -> 3 for 156 (100 x 1.25 x 1.25) ({levelPaid:0.##} paid, level {Equipment.LevelOf("drv_racing_1")})");
            double own = Held();
            double share = Missions.BountyEach(Missions.Bounty, 1, 3);   // the host's announced kill, a party of three
            // ...and that level is the one thing it spent while away
            Check(own - share + levelPaid >= held0 - 1e-6 && own - share + levelPaid < held0 + 400,
                  $"guest's own totals restored, nothing lost but the {levelPaid:0} salvage its level cost, plus its bounty share ({own:0.00} held, {share:0.00} of it the share, {held0:0.00} on leaving)");
            Check(H.Yard.Level("salvage_rate") == 0, "guest's own upgrade levels restored (the host's level 1 stays in the host's world)");
            Check(H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo <= H.Yard.Capacity && H.Yard.Gatherers.All(x => x.Cargo == 0 || x.State == Gatherer.St.Working),
                  "economy ships restart docked and empty in your own world");
            var ships = Nodes<PlayerShip>().ToList();
            Check(ships.Count == 1 && ships[0].Mine && ships[0].Name == "Ship_1", "guest rebuilt to its own single ship");
            Check(joinWatch.Frames > 0 && joinWatch.Online == 0 && joinWatch.Hosting == 0,
                  $"guest: through every frame of its join it is not online, and its panel does not read hosting, until the host has let it in ({joinWatch.Frames} frames joining; online on {joinWatch.Online}, 'hosting' on {joinWatch.Hosting})");
            joinWatch.QueueFree();
        }
    }
}

// Samples at the END of a frame, after every other node's _Process -- the one place a value that
// something overwrites every frame can be seen as it really stands.
public partial class LateSampler : Node
{
    public System.Func<bool> Probe;
    public bool Seen;
    public override void _Ready() => ProcessPriority = int.MaxValue;
    public override void _Process(double delta) { if (Probe?.Invoke() == true) Seen = true; }
}

// A JOIN, EVERY FRAME OF IT, at the end of each frame: while this pilot is joining it is not online,
// and its MULTIPLAYER panel does not read "hosting". Both were true for every frame of the handshake:
// ENet calls a link connected before the host has let the pilot in, and the pilot, still the host of
// its own world until then, sent into a host that threw it all away.
public partial class JoinWatch : Node
{
    public int Frames, Online, Hosting;
    public override void _Ready() => ProcessPriority = int.MaxValue;
    public override void _Process(double delta)
    {
        if (Net.I == null || !Net.I.Connecting) return;
        Frames++;
        if (Net.IsOnline) Online++;
        if (GetTree().Root.FindChild("NetToggle", true, false) is Button b && b.Text.Contains("hosting")) Hosting++;
    }
}

// A third host-spawned replicated kind, registered by the check that proves a third kind is a ROW
// of Spawns.All and not a third copy of the six methods each one used to need. The game builds
// none of these; only the check does.
public partial class SpawnProbe : Node2D { public int NetId; }

// A STAND-IN THAT SAYS WHAT IT IS, for a rule written in tags (Turret.Rank, a TargetFilter): the
// rule reads the tag and nothing else -- never a hit radius -- so the check hands it one.
public class StandIn : IHittable, ITagged
{
    public int NetId => 0;
    public Vector2 Position => Vector2.Zero;
    public float HitRadius => 8f;
    public bool Alive => true;
    public Tag Tags { get; set; }
    public void TakeDamage(double d) { }
}
